# v1.7.0 W2 — identity, operations, verification engine: implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Every document has an id and a content revision; COM refuses to write
into a document it was not bound to; every call leaves an operation record with
an honest state, and a client can retry with its own operation id; requirements
are typed; a change can be previewed and applied only if nothing moved; a
requirement is bound to the real feature, measured on CAD geometry, reported
pass / fail / unknown, and measured again on the saved file.

**Architecture:** Identity and revision in the backends; an in-memory operation
journal minted by middleware; the pure package `engineering/verification/`
(types → requirements → observe → binding → measure → report → artifact) over the
snapshot reader; `engineering/changes/preview.py` for exact preconditions; one
new core tool, `drawing_verify_requirements`. One new contract member,
`document_identity()`, on both engines.

**Tech Stack:** as W1. **Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-24-v1.7.0-design.md`
§5, §7, §8.

**Drafted after W1 merged:** read the merged W1 code on `release/1.7.0` before
writing a task. Where this skeleton's Interfaces disagree with what W1 landed
(`call_outcomes`, `qa_snapshot`, `EntityRecord.visible`, `_async(readonly=)`),
the landed code wins and the deviation is recorded in the task.

## Global Constraints

The W1 plan's Global Constraints apply unchanged (branch base `release/1.7.0`,
`uv run --frozen`, `--basetemp=out/pytest/<key>`, commit identity and trailer,
known red, ruff, registration gates, live AutoCAD rules and COM pitfalls,
deviations, confidentiality, triage assignments from
`docs/analysis/backlog-1.7.0-triage.json`). In addition:

- **Exact numbers in tests come from analytic oracles** written in the test (hand
  geometry), never from the generator or module under test.
- **No silent pass:** a requirement with no binding, an ambiguous binding or an
  unsupported measurement is `unknown` / `ambiguous` / `unsupported`, never `pass`.
- **EntityRecord stays hashable and backward compatible:** new fields have
  defaults and are tuples (or `field(compare=False, hash=False)`); readers that
  ignore them see no change; `diff.signature` does not change.
- **Live probes in groups** are raw pywin32 probes on their own `Document` objects
  (never `ActiveDocument`, never `SendCommand`); code that drives `ComBackend`
  against the live seat runs only in the foundation (Tasks 9–11) and the final
  (Tasks 16–17).

## Review Focus

4. **The operator switches AutoCAD's active document between two calls.** The
   second mutating call is refused with `active_document_changed`; nothing is
   written into the newly active document. *(Task 9)*
5. **An entity edited between preview and apply** — through the server and
   directly in the document. Apply refuses with `stale_revision` and changes
   nothing. *(Task 12)*
6. **A circle inside a mirrored, non-uniformly scaled nested block.** Observed as
   an ellipse with the approximation note; a mirrored arc keeps its true sweep.
   *(Task 13)*
7. **Two identical holes where the requirement names one; a copied feature tag.**
   `ambiguous` with both candidates, never a pick. *(Task 14)*
8. **A dimension whose text says 40 over geometry that measures 42; a DIMLFAC of
   2.** The geometric value fails; the displayed text and the dimension's own
   measurement are reported separately. *(Tasks 14, 16)*
9. **A save that renumbers handles; an artifact edited after its manifest was
   written.** Re-binding by tag then geometry, `unknown` when ambiguous;
   `verify_manifest` reports `stale`. *(Task 17)*

## Interfaces (the contract between tasks)

```python
# backends/contracts/identity.py (Task 9) — new abstract member, both engines
def document_identity(self) -> dict
    # {"document_id": str | None, "revision": int | None, "name": str | None,
    #  "path": str | None, "backend": "ezdxf" | "com"}
# backends/ezdxf_backend.py (Task 9)
_DocState.document_id: str                 # uuid4().hex minted in _register_document
_DocState.revision: int = 0                # bumped wherever content changes: dirty set,
                                           # undo, redo, transaction_rollback, snapshot
                                           # restore; never by save / export / deliver
# backends/document_guard.py (Task 9)
class ActiveDocumentChangedError(RuntimeError):
    def to_dict(self) -> dict              # {"ok": False, "kind": "active_document_changed",
                                           #  "error": str, "bound": {...}, "active": {...}}
# backends/com_backend.py (Task 9)
self._bound: dict | None                   # {"document_id", "name", "full_name"}
def _guard(self) -> Any                    # inside the executor job: the bound Document, or raise
def _mutating(method)                      # decorator: bump revision after the call, success or failure

# backends/operations.py (Task 10)
STATES = ("running", "committed", "partial", "failed", "refused",
          "quarantined", "cancelled", "unknown_commit")
@dataclass
class OperationRecord:
    operation_id: str; tool: str; request_hash: str; state: str
    document_id: str | None; revision_before: int | None; revision_after: int | None
    started: float; ended: float | None = None; error_kind: str | None = None
    client_supplied: bool = False
def request_hash(tool: str, arguments: dict) -> str     # sha256 of canonical JSON
class OperationConflictError(RuntimeError): ...         # kind "operation_conflict"
class RequiresReconcileError(RuntimeError): ...         # kind "requires_reconcile"
class OperationJournal:
    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 1000): ...
    def begin(self, tool, arguments, *, operation_id=None, document=None)
        -> tuple[OperationRecord, dict | None]          # (record, replayed result or None)
    def finish(self, record, state, *, result=None, error_kind=None, document=None) -> None
    def recent(self, n: int = 20) -> list[dict]
CURRENT_OPERATION: contextvars.ContextVar           # reused when the search proxy re-enters

# engineering/verification/types.py (Task 11) — used by Tasks 13-17
Status = Literal["pass", "fail", "unknown", "unsupported", "ambiguous"]
Provenance = Literal["explicit", "derived", "assumed", "conflicting", "unknown", "raster_estimate"]
Quantity = Literal["distance", "radius", "diameter", "angle", "count", "absence",
                   "concentric", "coincident", "parallel", "perpendicular",
                   "tangent", "horizontal", "vertical"]
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FeatureSelector:
    kind: Literal["handle", "feature_tag", "arch_element", "predicate", "dimension"]
    value: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...]               # sorted key/value pairs (hashable)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Requirement:
    id: str; quantity: Quantity; target: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...]
    features: tuple[FeatureSelector, ...]; provenance: Provenance
    source: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...]; datum: str | None = None
    manufacturing_tolerance: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...] | None = None
    verification_epsilon: float | None = None
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RequirementSet:
    requirements: tuple[Requirement, ...]; units: str
    legacy: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...] | None = None
    def digest(self) -> str                             # sha256 of the canonical form
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ObservedFeature:
    handle: str; type: Literal["line", "circle", "arc", "ellipse", "polyline", "point"]
    space: str; layer: str; block_path: tuple[str, ...]
    geometry: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...]            # WCS, drawing units
    tags: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()              # (app id, payload json)
    approximation: str | None = None
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ObservedGeometry:
    features: tuple[ObservedFeature, ...]; declared_unit: str | None
    inferred_unit: str | None; document_id: str | None; revision: int | None; source: str
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FeatureRef:
    document_id: str | None; revision: int | None; space: str
    block_path: tuple[str, ...]; handles: tuple[str, ...]; feature: str | None
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Binding:
    requirement_id: str; status: Literal["bound", "ambiguous", "unknown"]
    refs: tuple[FeatureRef, ...]; candidates: tuple[FeatureRef, ...]; reason: str
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Measurement:
    requirement_id: str; value: float | None; unit: str; method: str
    residual: float | None; status: Status; detail: tuple[tuple[str, object], ...] = ()
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AcceptanceItem:
    requirement: Requirement; binding: Binding; measurement: Measurement
    delta: float | None; tolerance_basis: str
# engineering/verification/requirements.py (Task 11)
def parse_requirements(raw) -> RequirementSet   # typed items, or a legacy {"dimensions": {...}} kept read-only
async def store_requirements(backend, rs: RequirementSet) -> dict   # ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS XRECORD
async def load_requirements(backend) -> RequirementSet | None

# engineering/changes/preview.py (Task 12)
async def entity_fingerprint(backend, handle: str) -> str   # exact; same reader at preview and apply
async def region_fingerprint(backend, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> str
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChangePreview:
    document_id: str | None; revision: int | None
    preconditions: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]   # {"handle"|"region", "fingerprint"}
    planned: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]
    def to_dict(self) -> dict
class StaleRevisionError(RuntimeError): ...   # kind "stale_revision": expected, actual, changed
async def check_preconditions(backend, *, expected_revision=None, preconditions=None) -> None

ellipse: tuple | None = None            # (center, major_axis, ratio, start_param, end_param)

hatch_loops: tuple = ()                 # per loop: (kind, flags, depth, vertices_or_edges, bulges)
```

  - `backends/ezdxf_backend.py`: `_DocState.document_id: str` (uuid4 hex minted in `_register_document`; `""` only on the empty `_void` entry), `_DocState.revision: int = 0` (moved by every `self._dirty = True`).
  - `backends/document_guard.py`: `class ActiveDocumentChangedError(RuntimeError)` with `kind = "active_document_changed"`, `bound: dict`, `active: dict | None`, `def to_dict(self) -> dict` → `{"ok": False, "kind": "active_document_changed", "error": str, "bound": {"document_id", "name", "path"}, "active": {"document_id", "name", "path"} | None}`.

  - Beyond the skeleton (Tasks 10 and 11 use them): `ComBackend._known_documents: dict[tuple[str, str], dict]` (`(Name, FullName)` → `{"document_id", "revision"}`); `ComBackend._bind_key(key: tuple[str, str]) -> bool`; `ComBackend._bump_revision() -> None`; `async ComBackend._rebind() -> None`; `ComBackend._reactivate_bound_sync(app) -> bool`; `backends.com_backend._MUTATING_METHODS: frozenset[str]` (98 names); `DrawingInfo.document_id: str | None = None`, `DrawingInfo.revision: int | None = None`; `system_status()` keys `document_id`, `revision` on both engines plus `bound_document`, `active_document_changed` on COM; `document_list()` rows gain `document_id`, `revision`.
  - Deviation recorded: `_guard` takes the Application (`_guard(self, app)`), because it runs from `_acad_doc()`, which already holds it; the skeleton wrote `_guard(self)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing headless identity tests**

Create `tests/test_document_identity.py`:

```python
"""B11 - every open document has an id and a content revision.

``document_id`` is a uuid4 hex minted when a document is registered: not
``$FINGERPRINTGUID`` (every template-born drawing shares it) and not
``$VERSIONGUID`` (every internal snapshot save rewrites it). ``revision`` is a
*content* revision: it moves wherever the content changes - every point that
sets ``dirty``, undo, redo, ``transaction_rollback`` - and never on a save, an
export, a finalize or a delivery, so a delivery manifest is not stale the
moment it is written. Every expected revision below is counted by hand from
the calls the test makes (one ``_mark_dirty`` per primitive).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastmcp import Client

import config
import server
from backends.ezdxf_backend import EzdxfBackend
from engineering.delivery import deliver_drawing

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _is_uuid4_hex(value) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, str)
        and len(value) == 32
        and set(value) <= set("0123456789abcdef")
        and value[12] == "4"
    )


async def test_a_registered_document_has_a_uuid4_id_and_revision_zero(backend):
    identity = backend.document_identity()
    assert set(identity) == {"document_id", "revision", "name", "path", "backend"}
    assert _is_uuid4_hex(identity["document_id"])
    assert identity["revision"] == 0
    assert identity["backend"] == "ezdxf"
    assert identity["name"] == "untitled-1" and identity["path"] is None


async def test_each_document_keeps_its_own_identity(backend):
    first = backend.document_identity()
    created = await backend.drawing_new()
    second = backend.document_identity()
    assert _is_uuid4_hex(second["document_id"])
    assert second["document_id"] != first["document_id"]
    assert second["revision"] == 0
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 1, 0)
    await backend.document_activate(first["name"])
    assert backend.document_identity() == first, "switching back finds the first one untouched"
    await backend.document_activate(created["document"])
    assert backend.document_identity() == {**second, "revision": 1}


async def test_no_document_means_no_identity():
    bare = EzdxfBackend()
    await bare.connect()
    try:
        assert bare.document_identity() == {
            "document_id": None,
            "revision": None,
            "name": None,
            "path": None,
            "backend": "ezdxf",
        }
    finally:
        await bare.disconnect()


async def test_revision_moves_with_content_and_only_with_content(backend, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(config.settings, "ezdxf_undo_depth", 5)
    await backend.drawing_new()  # a fresh history baseline at depth 5
    document_id = backend.document_identity()["document_id"]

    def revision() -> int:
        return backend.document_identity()["revision"]

    assert revision() == 0
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    assert revision() == 1
    await backend.entity_create_circle(5, 5, 2)
    assert revision() == 2
    assert (await backend.drawing_undo())["ok"] is True
    assert revision() == 3, "an undo restores a snapshot: content changed"
    assert (await backend.drawing_redo())["ok"] is True
    assert revision() == 4
    assert (await backend.transaction_begin())["ok"] is True
    assert revision() == 4, "a checkpoint changes nothing"
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 10, 10, 10)
    assert revision() == 5
    assert (await backend.transaction_rollback())["ok"] is True
    assert revision() == 6, "a rollback restores a snapshot: content changed"

    await backend.drawing_save_as(str(tmp_path / "a.dxf"))
    await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(tmp_path / "b.dxf"))
    await backend.drawing_save()
    assert revision() == 6, "saving and exporting never move the content revision"
    delivered = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, tmp_path, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False
    )
    assert delivered.status in ("success", "failed_validation")
    assert revision() == 6, "a delivery reads and exports; it never changes the content"
    assert backend.document_identity()["document_id"] == document_id


async def test_reopening_a_file_is_a_new_registration(backend, tmp_path):
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 1, 0)
    path = tmp_path / "part.dxf"
    await backend.drawing_save_as(str(path))
    before = backend.document_identity()
    assert before["revision"] == 1
    opened = await backend.drawing_open(str(path))
    assert opened["reloaded"] is True
    after = backend.document_identity()
    assert _is_uuid4_hex(after["document_id"]) and after["document_id"] != before["document_id"]
    assert after["revision"] == 0 and after["path"] == str(path)


async def test_list_info_and_status_carry_id_and_revision(backend):
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 1, 0)
    identity = backend.document_identity()
    (row,) = await backend.document_list()
    assert (row["document_id"], row["revision"]) == (identity["document_id"], 1)
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    assert (info.document_id, info.revision) == (identity["document_id"], 1)
    status = await backend.system_status()
    assert (status["document_id"], status["revision"]) == (identity["document_id"], 1)


async def test_finalize_leaves_the_revision_where_it_was(tmp_path):
    async with Client(server.mcp) as client:
        await client.call_tool("drawing_new", {})
        await client.call_tool(
            "entity_create_line", {"x1": 0, "y1": 0, "x2": 40, "y2": 0, "layer": "GEOMETRY"}
        )
        before = (await client.call_tool("system_status", {})).structured_content
        await client.call_tool(
            "drawing_finalize", {"save_path": str(tmp_path / "final.dxf")}, raise_on_error=False
        )
        after = (await client.call_tool("system_status", {})).structured_content
        info = (await client.call_tool("drawing_info", {})).structured_content
        rows = (await client.call_tool("document_list", {})).structured_content["documents"]
    assert _is_uuid4_hex(before["document_id"])
    assert after["document_id"] == before["document_id"]
    assert after["revision"] == before["revision"], "finalize saves and checks; it never edits"
    assert (info["document_id"], info["revision"]) == (before["document_id"], before["revision"])
    assert [r["document_id"] for r in rows] == [before["document_id"]]
```

```python
class _DirtyField(_ActiveField):
    """``_dirty`` that also moves the content revision (v1.7.0, B11).

    Every content change in this engine ends in ``self._dirty = True`` -
    ``_mark_dirty`` after each mutation, ``_restore_snapshot`` for undo and
    redo, ``transaction_rollback`` - and saving writes ``False``. So the
    assignment is the one place that sees every content change and nothing
    else: saving, exporting, finalizing and delivering never assign ``True``.
    """

    def __set__(self, instance, value):
        state = instance._state
        state.dirty = value
        if value:
            state.revision += 1
```

```python
    def document_identity(self) -> dict:
        """The bound document's ``{document_id, revision, name, path, backend}`` (B11).

        ``revision`` counts this server's own content-changing calls on the
        document (``_mutating``: on success and on failure, never on a refusal
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the failing live-engine tests**

Create `tests/test_bound_document_com.py`:

```python
"""B11 on the live engine: the bound document, checked inside every call's own job.

``_acad_doc()`` re-read ``ActiveDocument`` on every call, so an operator who
switched AutoCAD's active document between two calls had the second call
write into the document they had just switched to. ``ComBackend`` now binds
``(Name, FullName)`` and an id on new / open / activate / close (and at the
first call that touches a document), and the first ``_acad_doc()`` of every
executor job compares AutoCAD's active document with the bound one before any
work: a mismatch is refused with ``ActiveDocumentChangedError`` and nothing is
written - reads used as evidence included.

No AutoCAD is touched: ``_run`` is the real one on a plain daemon thread (no
CoInitialize) and the seat models only members measured on AutoCAD 2026 -
Documents.Count / Item / Open, ActiveDocument, Name, FullName, Activate,
Close, SaveAs, Save, GetVariable / SetVariable, SendCommand,
StartUndoMark / EndUndoMark, SelectionSets.Add / Select / Delete, Export.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
import os

import ezdxf
import pytest
from fastmcp import Client

import server
from backends import com_backend as cb
from backends.document_guard import ActiveDocumentChangedError
from backends.ezdxf_backend import EzdxfBackend
from backends.quarantine import DaemonExecutor


class _Space:
    Count = 0

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(())


class _Selection:
    def Select(self, mode):
        self.mode = mode

    def Delete(self):
        pass


class _SelectionSets:
    def Add(self, name):
        return _Selection()


class _Doc:
    def __init__(self, seat, name, full_name):
        self._seat = seat
        self.Name = name
        self.FullName = full_name
        self.Saved = True
        self.vars = {"LTSCALE": 1.0, "CMDACTIVE": 0, "OSMODE": 0, "SNAPMODE": 0, "CMDECHO": 1}
        self.writes: list[tuple[str, object]] = []
        self.commands: list[str] = []
        self.undo_marks: list[str] = []
        self.ModelSpace = _Space()
        self.SelectionSets = _SelectionSets()
        self.refuse_writes = False

    def GetVariable(self, name):
        return self.vars.get(name, 0)

    def SetVariable(self, name, value):
        if self.refuse_writes:
            raise RuntimeError("SetVariable refused")
        self.writes.append((name, value))
        self.vars[name] = value

    def SendCommand(self, text):
        self.commands.append(text)

    def StartUndoMark(self):
        self.undo_marks.append("start")

    def EndUndoMark(self):
        self.undo_marks.append("end")

    def Activate(self):
        self._seat.ActiveDocument = self

    def Close(self, save_changes):
        docs = self._seat.Documents._docs
        docs.remove(self)
        self._seat.ActiveDocument = docs[-1] if docs else None

    def SaveAs(self, path, save_as_type=None):
        self.FullName = str(path)
        self.Name = os.path.basename(str(path))

    def Save(self):
        self.Saved = True

    def Export(self, base, extension, selection):
        ezdxf.new("R2010").saveas(f"{base}.dxf")


class _Docs:
    def __init__(self, seat):
        self._seat = seat
        self._docs: list[_Doc] = []

    @property
    def Count(self):
        return len(self._docs)

    def Item(self, index):
        return self._docs[index]

    def Open(self, path, read_only=False):
        doc = _Doc(self._seat, os.path.basename(str(path)), os.path.abspath(str(path)))
        self._docs.append(doc)
        self._seat.ActiveDocument = doc
        return doc


class _Seat:
    Version = "25.1s (test seat)"

    def __init__(self):
        self.Documents = _Docs(self)
        self.first = _Doc(self, "First.dwg", "C:\\work\\First.dwg")
        self.second = _Doc(self, "Second.dwg", "C:\\work\\Second.dwg")
        self.Documents._docs.extend([self.first, self.second])
        self.ActiveDocument = self.first


@pytest.fixture
def seat(monkeypatch):
    fake = _Seat()
    monkeypatch.setattr(cb, "_acad_app", lambda: fake)
    return fake


@pytest.fixture
def com(seat):
    backend = cb.ComBackend()
    backend._executor = DaemonExecutor(max_workers=1, name="test-sta")  # no CoInitialize
    backend._connected = True
    yield backend
    if backend._executor is not None:
        backend._executor.shutdown(wait=False)
        backend._executor = None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_first_call_binds_the_active_document(com, seat):
    assert com.document_identity() == {
        "document_id": None,
        "revision": None,
        "name": None,
        "path": None,
        "backend": "com",
    }
    assert await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE") == 1.0
    identity = com.document_identity()
    assert identity["name"] == "First.dwg" and identity["path"] == "C:\\work\\First.dwg"
    assert len(identity["document_id"]) == 32 and identity["revision"] == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_switch_between_two_calls_is_refused_and_writes_nothing(com, seat):
    """Review Focus 4: the operator switches AutoCAD's active document between two calls."""
    await com.system_set_variable("LTSCALE", 2.0)
    bound = com.document_identity()
    assert seat.first.writes == [("LTSCALE", 2.0)] and bound["revision"] == 1

    seat.ActiveDocument = seat.second  # the operator clicks the other drawing tab
    with pytest.raises(ActiveDocumentChangedError) as refused:
        await com.system_set_variable("LTSCALE", 3.0)
    assert seat.second.writes == [], "nothing may land in the newly active document"
    assert seat.first.writes == [("LTSCALE", 2.0)]
    payload = refused.value.to_dict()
    assert payload["ok"] is False and payload["kind"] == "active_document_changed"
    assert payload["bound"] == {
        "document_id": bound["document_id"],
        "name": "First.dwg",
        "path": "C:\\work\\First.dwg",
    }
    assert payload["active"] == {
        "document_id": None,
        "name": "Second.dwg",
        "path": "C:\\work\\Second.dwg",
    }
    assert "document_activate('Second.dwg')" in payload["error"]

    with pytest.raises(ActiveDocumentChangedError):
        await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")  # a read used as evidence is refused too
    assert com.document_identity() == bound, "a refused call moves no revision"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_document_activate_binds_the_document_the_operator_chose(com, seat):
    await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")
    first = com.document_identity()
    com._plan_spec = object()
    seat.ActiveDocument = seat.second
    result = await com.document_activate("Second.dwg")
    assert result["active"] == "Second.dwg"
    second = com.document_identity()
    assert second["name"] == "Second.dwg" and second["document_id"] != first["document_id"]
    assert com._plan_spec is None, "drawing-scoped state belongs to the document it was made for"
    await com.system_set_variable("LTSCALE", 4.0)
    assert seat.second.writes == [("LTSCALE", 4.0)]
    await com.document_activate("First.dwg")
    assert com.document_identity()["document_id"] == first["document_id"], "a known id is kept"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_transaction_rollback_addresses_the_bound_document(com, seat):
    assert (await com.transaction_begin())["ok"] is True
    assert seat.first.commands == ["_.UNDO _M\n"] and seat.first.undo_marks == ["start"]
    seat.ActiveDocument = seat.second
    with pytest.raises(ActiveDocumentChangedError):
        await com.transaction_rollback()
    assert seat.second.commands == [] and seat.second.undo_marks == []
    assert com._transaction_active is True, "the mark is still open in First.dwg"
    seat.ActiveDocument = seat.first
    assert (await com.transaction_rollback())["ok"] is True
    assert seat.first.undo_marks == ["start", "end"]
    assert seat.first.commands[-1] == "_.UNDO _B\n"
    assert com._transaction_active is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_revision_moves_on_writes_success_or_failure_never_on_saves(com, seat, tmp_path):
    await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")

    def revision() -> int:
        return com.document_identity()["revision"]

    assert revision() == 0
    await com.system_set_variable("LTSCALE", 2.0)
    assert revision() == 1
    seat.first.refuse_writes = True
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="SetVariable refused"):
        await com.system_set_variable("LTSCALE", 3.0)
    assert revision() == 2, "a failed write may have landed in part: the revision moves anyway"
    seat.first.refuse_writes = False
    await com.drawing_undo()
    assert revision() == 3
    await com.drawing_save()
    await com.drawing_save_as(str(tmp_path / "Saved.dwg"), "dwg")
    assert revision() == 3, "saving changes no content"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_our_own_save_as_keeps_the_binding_and_the_id(com, seat, tmp_path):
    await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")
    before = com.document_identity()
    target = tmp_path / "Renamed.dwg"
    await com.drawing_save_as(str(target), "dwg")
    after = com.document_identity()
    assert after["document_id"] == before["document_id"]
    assert after["name"] == "Renamed.dwg" and after["path"] == str(target)
    assert await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE") == 1.0, "the renamed document is still bound"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_dwg_snapshot_hands_the_seat_back_to_the_bound_document(com, seat, tmp_path):
    from engineering.understand.snapshot import take_snapshot

    await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")  # bound to First.dwg
    part = tmp_path / "part.dwg"
    part.write_bytes(b"AC1032 stand-in: the seat's Export writes the DXF")
    snapshot = await take_snapshot(com, str(part))
    assert snapshot.source == "live:part.dwg" and snapshot.records == ()
    assert seat.ActiveDocument is seat.first, "Close(False) left Second.dwg active; it is undone"
    assert [d.Name for d in seat.Documents._docs] == ["First.dwg", "Second.dwg"]


def test_every_content_changing_method_is_mutating_on_the_live_engine():
    """The oracle is the headless engine: whatever sets ``dirty`` there changes content."""
    dirty = set()
    for name, fn in vars(EzdxfBackend).items():
        if not inspect.iscoroutinefunction(fn):
            continue
        source = inspect.getsource(fn)
        if "_mark_dirty()" in source or "self._dirty = True" in source:
            dirty.add(name)
    own = {name for name, fn in vars(cb.ComBackend).items() if inspect.iscoroutinefunction(fn)}
    expected = (dirty & own) | {
        "drawing_undo",  # the headless twins restore a snapshot instead of setting dirty inline
        "drawing_redo",
        "solid_box",  # live-only content writers
        "solid_cylinder",
        "solid_extrude",
        "solid_revolve",
        "solid_boolean",
        "system_run_command",  # escape hatches: assume they write
        "system_run_lisp",
    }
    assert cb._MUTATING_METHODS == expected
    assert len(cb._MUTATING_METHODS) == 98
    for name in cb._MUTATING_METHODS:
        assert getattr(vars(cb.ComBackend)[name], "__acadmcp_mutating__", False) is True, name
    never = {
        "drawing_save",
        "drawing_save_as",
        "drawing_export_dxf",
        "drawing_export_dwg",
        "drawing_export_pdf",
        "drawing_template_save",
        "drawing_info",
        "entity_get",
        "entity_list",
        "document_list",
        "system_status",
        "transaction_begin",
        "transaction_commit",
        "preferences_set",
    }
    assert not (never & cb._MUTATING_METHODS)

async def test_the_refusal_reaches_the_client_as_a_typed_payload(com, seat, monkeypatch):
    await com.system_get_variable("LTSCALE")
    seat.ActiveDocument = seat.second

    async def make():
        return com

    monkeypatch.setattr(server, "_make_backend", make)
    async with Client(server.mcp) as client:
        result = await client.call_tool("drawing_info", {}, raise_on_error=False)
    assert result.is_error is True
    payload = result.structured_content
    assert payload["kind"] == "active_document_changed"
    assert payload["tool"] == "drawing_info" and payload["backend"] == "com"
    assert payload["bound"]["name"] == "First.dwg" and payload["active"]["name"] == "Second.dwg"
```

Run: `uv run --frozen pytest tests/test_bound_document_com.py -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp=C:/Users/VECTOR/AppData/Local/Temp/acadmcp-pytest/task-9`
Expected: FAIL — collection error `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'backends.document_guard'`.

- [ ] **Step 8: Create the refusal**

Create `backends/document_guard.py`:

```python
"""The bound document on the live engine (v1.7.0, B11).

``_acad_doc()`` used to re-read ``ActiveDocument`` on every call, so a click on
another drawing tab between two calls sent the second call's writes into the
drawing the operator had just switched to. ``ComBackend`` now binds one
document - ``(Name, FullName)`` and a uuid4 ``document_id`` - when the session
creates, opens, activates or closes one (and at the first call that touches a
document), and every executor job checks AutoCAD's active document against it
before any document work. A mismatch is this refusal; nothing was written.

Operator edits are not counted in the revision. The document events that
could count them (``ObjectAdded`` / ``ObjectModified`` / ``ObjectErased``
through ``win32com.client.WithEvents``) were probed on AutoCAD 2026 on
2026-10-24 on a scratch ``Documents.Add()`` document: once subscribed, the very
next ``ModelSpace.AddLine`` from the subscribing STA thread raised
``RPC_E_SERVERFAULT`` (-2147417851) although the line was added, and every
later ``Documents.Add`` the probe tried on that seat was refused with "Filer
error". They are not used: the live revision moves on this server's own writes
only, and staleness on the live engine rests on the entity fingerprints of the
change-preview contract (Task 12).
"""

from __future__ import annotations


class ActiveDocumentChangedError(RuntimeError):
    """AutoCAD's active document is not the one this session is bound to.

    Raised inside the executor job, before any document work, so nothing was
    written - not into the bound document and not into the active one. Carries
    both documents as ``{"document_id", "name", "path"}`` (``active`` is None
    when AutoCAD has no document open).
    """

    kind = "active_document_changed"

    def __init__(self, bound: dict, active: dict | None):
        self.bound = dict(bound)
        self.active = None if active is None else dict(active)
        bound_name = self.bound.get("name") or "the bound document"
        if self.active is None:
            message = (
                f"AutoCAD has no document open, but this session is bound to {bound_name!r}; "
                f"nothing was written. Open it again and call document_activate({bound_name!r}), "
                "or drawing_new / drawing_open to bind another document."
            )
        else:
            active_name = self.active.get("name") or "another document"
            message = (
                f"AutoCAD's active document changed: this session is bound to {bound_name!r} "
                f"but {active_name!r} is active, so nothing was written. Switch AutoCAD back to "
                f"{bound_name!r}, or call document_activate({active_name!r}) to bind this "
                "session to it."
            )
        super().__init__(message)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "kind": self.kind,
            "error": str(self),
            "bound": dict(self.bound),
            "active": None if self.active is None else dict(self.active),
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Install the guard in `_acad_doc()` and in every executor job**

In `backends/com_backend.py`:

1. Imports: add `import functools` (after `import concurrent.futures`) and `import threading` (after `import sys`) to the standard-library block, and add `from .document_guard import ActiveDocumentChangedError` between `from .contracts.settings import SUMMARY_FIELDS, validate_drawing_properties` and `from .quarantine import DaemonExecutor, real_call_name` (the isort order `ruff check` enforces).

2. After `_COM_STATE: dict[str, Any] = {}  # keys: "app"`, add:

```python
#: Per-thread state of the executor job running now (B11): ``guard`` is the
#: ``_JobGuard`` that ``_acad_doc()`` consults. Thread-local, not in
#: ``_COM_STATE``: a stuck job on an abandoned executor thread must never
#: clear the guard of the job running on its replacement.
_JOB_STATE = threading.local()


class _JobGuard:
    """One job's check of the bound document, run at its first ``_acad_doc()``.

    The checked Document is reused by the rest of the job and dropped on the
    COM thread when the job ends (a COM proxy must not be released elsewhere).
    """

    __slots__ = ("backend", "doc")

    def __init__(self, backend):
        self.backend = backend
        self.doc = None

    def __call__(self, app):
        if self.doc is None:
            self.doc = self.backend._guard(app)
        return self.doc
```

3. Replace `_acad_doc` with:

```python
def _acad_doc():
    """The active AutoCAD document - inside a ComBackend call, the bound one.

    ``ComBackend._run`` installs a per-job ``_JobGuard`` (B11): the first call
    here in a job checks that AutoCAD's active document is still the one this
    session is bound to, and refuses with ``ActiveDocumentChangedError`` before
    any work when the operator switched; later calls in the same job reuse the
    checked document. Outside a job (a test calling a ``_sync`` directly) it is
    the plain active document, as before.
    """
    app = _acad_app()
    if app.Documents.Count == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("No drawing is open in AutoCAD. Open or create a .dwg file first.")
    guard = getattr(_JOB_STATE, "guard", None)
    if guard is not None:
        return guard(app)
    return app.ActiveDocument
```

4. In `ComBackend._run`, replace

```python
        started = time.time()
        document = self._document_label()
        work = self._executor.submit(lambda: func(*args, **kwargs))
```

with

```python
        started = time.time()
        document = self._document_label()
        guard = _JobGuard(self)

        def _job():
            # B11: the first _acad_doc() of this job checks the bound document,
            # inside the job, before any document work.
            _JOB_STATE.guard = guard
            try:
                return func(*args, **kwargs)
            finally:
                _JOB_STATE.guard = None
                guard.doc = None

        work = self._executor.submit(_job)
```

and add this paragraph at the end of `_run`'s docstring:

```
        **The bound document (v1.7.0, B11).** Each job carries a
        ``_JobGuard``: the first ``_acad_doc()`` of the job compares AutoCAD's
        active document with the bound one inside this same job, before any
        work, and refuses with ``ActiveDocumentChangedError`` when the
        operator switched. A call that never asks for the document
        (``Documents.Add`` / ``Open``, ``document_list``, ``system_status``)
        writes into none and is not guarded.
```

5. Replace the whole `_ensure_document_state` method (from `    async def _ensure_document_state(self) -> None:` through its `        await self._run(_sync)`) with:

```python
    # ── the bound document (v1.7.0, B11) ─────────────────────────────────────
    #
    # One document per session: (Name, FullName) plus a uuid4 id, bound by
    # ``_rebind`` after drawing_new / drawing_open / document_activate /
    # document_close / system_launch, and by ``_guard`` at the first call that
    # touches a document. backends/document_guard.py says why the operator's
    # own edits are not counted.

    async def _ensure_document_state(self) -> None:
        """The guard as a call of its own: bind on first contact, else refuse a switch.

        Before v1.7.0 this reset drawing-scoped metadata whenever AutoCAD's
        active document had changed - silently following the operator to
        another drawing. A switch is now refused with
        ``ActiveDocumentChangedError``; ``document_activate`` is how a session
        moves. The read waits out ``RPC_E_CALL_REJECTED`` (the window right
        after Documents.Add / Open / Close).
        """
        await self._run(self._check_bound_sync)

    def _check_bound_sync(self) -> None:
        """``_guard`` on the COM thread; returns nothing (a proxy must not leave the STA)."""
        self._guard(_acad_app())

    def _active_key_sync(self, app) -> tuple[Any, tuple[str, str] | None]:
        """``(ActiveDocument, (Name, FullName))``, or ``(None, None)`` with nothing open."""

        def _read():
            if int(app.Documents.Count) == 0:
                return None, None
            doc = app.ActiveDocument
            return doc, (str(doc.Name), str(doc.FullName))

        return self._wait_out_rejected_call(_read)

    def _guard(self, app) -> Any:
        """The bound Document, checked against AutoCAD's active one (COM thread).

        Unbound: the active document is adopted - the first call that touches
        a document binds it. Bound: a different active document, or none, is
        refused with ``ActiveDocumentChangedError`` before anything is written.
        """
        doc, key = self._active_key_sync(app)
        if self._bound is None:
            if key is None:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "No drawing is open in AutoCAD. Open or create a .dwg file first."
                )
            self._bind_key(key)
            return doc
        if key != (self._bound["name"], self._bound["full_name"]):
            raise ActiveDocumentChangedError(self._bound_view(), self._view_of(key))
        return doc

    def _bind_key(self, key: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        """Bind ``key``; True when that is a different document than before."""
        entry = self._known_documents.get(key)
        if entry is None:
            entry = {"document_id": uuid.uuid4().hex, "revision": 0}
            self._known_documents[key] = entry
        previous = None if self._bound is None else self._bound["document_id"]
        self._bound = {"document_id": entry["document_id"], "name": key[0], "full_name": key[1]}
        self._document_scope_key = key
        return previous != entry["document_id"]

    def _bind_active_sync(self) -> bool:
        """Bind whatever AutoCAD now has active (COM thread); True when that changed."""
        _doc, key = self._active_key_sync(_acad_app())
        if key is None:
            changed = self._bound is not None
            self._bound = None
            self._document_scope_key = None
            return changed
        return self._bind_key(key)

    async def _rebind(self) -> None:
        """After our own document operation: bind the active one, reset state on a change."""
        changed = await self._run(self._bind_active_sync)
        if changed:
            self._transaction_active = False
            self._reset_document_state()

    def _rekey_bound_sync(self, doc) -> None:
        """Our own SaveAs renamed the bound document: same document, same id (COM thread)."""
        bound = self._bound
        if bound is None:
            return
        old = (bound["name"], bound["full_name"])
        new = self._wait_out_rejected_call(lambda: (str(doc.Name), str(doc.FullName)))
        if new == old:
            return
        entry = self._known_documents.pop(old, None) or {
            "document_id": bound["document_id"],
            "revision": 0,
        }
        self._known_documents[new] = entry
        self._bound = {"document_id": entry["document_id"], "name": new[0], "full_name": new[1]}
        self._document_scope_key = new

    def _reactivate_bound_sync(self, app) -> bool:
        """Make the bound document active again after a job opened and closed another.

        COM thread. True when it had to activate it; False when it already was,
        when nothing is bound, or when the bound document is gone (the next
        guarded call then refuses by name).
        """
        bound = self._bound
        if bound is None:
            return False
        key = (bound["name"], bound["full_name"])

        def _find():
            count = int(app.Documents.Count)
            if count == 0:
                return None
            active = app.ActiveDocument
            if (str(active.Name), str(active.FullName)) == key:
                return None
            for index in range(count):
                doc = app.Documents.Item(index)
                if (str(doc.Name), str(doc.FullName)) == key:
                    return doc
            return None

        doc = self._wait_out_rejected_call(_find)
        if doc is None:
            return False
        doc.Activate()
        self._wait_out_rejected_call(lambda: str(app.ActiveDocument.Name))
        return True

    def _bump_revision(self) -> None:
        """One more content revision on the bound document (see ``_mutating``)."""
        bound = getattr(self, "_bound", None)
        if bound is None:
            return
        entry = self._known_documents.get((bound["name"], bound["full_name"]))
        if entry is not None:
            entry["revision"] += 1

    def _identity_of_key(self, key: tuple[str, str]) -> dict:
        entry = self._known_documents.get(key)
        return {
            "document_id": entry["document_id"] if entry else None,
            "revision": entry["revision"] if entry else None,
        }

    def _bound_view(self) -> dict:
        bound = self._bound
        return {
            "document_id": bound["document_id"],
            "name": bound["name"],
            "path": bound["full_name"] or None,
        }

    def _view_of(self, key: tuple[str, str] | None) -> dict | None:
        if key is None:
            return None
        return {
            "document_id": self._identity_of_key(key)["document_id"],
            "name": key[0],
            "path": key[1] or None,
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Add the `_mutating` decorator and wrap the 98 content-changing methods**

In `backends/com_backend.py`, before `class ComBackend(AutoCADBackend):` add:

```python
def _mutating(method):
    """Bump the bound document's content revision after ``method`` (B11).

    On success and on failure - a write that raised may have landed in part,
    so the revision moves - and never on ``ActiveDocumentChangedError``, which
    is raised before anything reaches AutoCAD. Applied by name from
    ``_MUTATING_METHODS``, never from a tool's cost class: that class counts
    save and deliver as mutations, and ``cad_batch`` steps bypass it.
    """

    @functools.wraps(method)
    async def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
        refused = False
        try:
            return await method(self, *args, **kwargs)
        except ActiveDocumentChangedError:
            refused = True
            raise
        finally:
            if not refused:
                self._bump_revision()

    wrapper.__acadmcp_mutating__ = True
    return wrapper
```

7. Replace the whole `system_status` method with:

```python
    async def system_status(self) -> dict:
        tx_active = self._transaction_active

        def _sync():
            try:
                app = _acad_app()
                version = app.Version
                doc_count = app.Documents.Count
                active = app.ActiveDocument if doc_count > 0 else None
                active_key = None if active is None else (str(active.Name), str(active.FullName))
                bound = self._bound
                return {
                    "backend": "com",
                    "connected": True,
                    # Which application this actually is. Without it a
                    # GstarCAD/ZWCAD operator reads an "autocad_version" key
                    # naming a product they do not own.
                    "cad_progid": config.settings.cad_progid,
                    "autocad_version": version,
                    "open_documents": doc_count,
                    "active_document": None if active_key is None else active_key[0],
                    # B11: the document this session writes into, and whether
                    # the operator has switched away from it (the next guarded
                    # call would be refused with active_document_changed).
                    "bound_document": None
                    if bound is None
                    else {"name": bound["name"], "path": bound["full_name"] or None},
                    "active_document_changed": bound is not None
                    and active_key != (bound["name"], bound["full_name"]),
                    "transaction_active": tx_active,
                    # Accurate capability list: the backend authors 2D entities
                    # (lines/arcs/circles/polylines/text/hatch/dims/blocks). It
                    # does NOT create 3D solids, so the old "all_entity_types"
                    # claim was false — report "entities_2d" instead.
                    "capabilities": [
                        "live_control",
                        "screenshot",
                        "transactions",
                        "com_api",
                        "lisp_execution",
                        "entities_2d",
                    ],
                }
            except Exception as exc:
                log.debug("system_status: _acad_app check failed: %s", exc)
                return {
                    "backend": "com",
                    "connected": False,
                    "cad_progid": config.settings.cad_progid,
                    "error": str(exc),
                }

        status = await self._run(_sync)
        identity = self.document_identity()
        status["document_id"] = identity["document_id"]
        status["revision"] = identity["revision"]
        status["call_outcomes"] = [dict(row) for row in self.call_outcomes][
            -self._STATUS_CALL_OUTCOMES :
        ]
        return status
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Type the refusal at the wire and document the new fields**

In `server.py`:

1. After `_batch_quarantine_of`, add:

```python
def _document_changed_of(exc: BaseException) -> dict | None:
    """The ``ActiveDocumentChangedError`` payload on ``exc``'s cause chain, or None (B11).

    Recognised by type; the error lives in ``backends.document_guard``, which
    imports nothing, so this resolves on any box.
    """
    from backends.document_guard import ActiveDocumentChangedError

    for error in _batch_cause_chain(exc):
        if isinstance(error, ActiveDocumentChangedError):
            return error.to_dict()
    return None
```

```python
                payload = {
                    **changed,
                    "tool": context.message.name,
                    "backend": _backend_name_or_none(context),

                    content=payload["error"], structured_content=payload, is_error=True

            capability = _batch_capability_of(exc)
```

1. In `tests/test_com_backend.py`, replace `test_active_document_switch_clears_com_document_state` with:

```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_active_document_switch_is_refused_and_keeps_the_bound_state(monkeypatch):
    """v1.7.0 (B11): a switch used to reset drawing-scoped state and follow the
    operator to the other document. It is now refused, and the state stays with
    the document it was made for; document_activate is how a session moves."""
    from backends.document_guard import ActiveDocumentChangedError

    backend = ComBackend()
    documents = SimpleNamespace(Count=1)
    app = SimpleNamespace(
        Documents=documents,
        ActiveDocument=SimpleNamespace(Name="First.dwg", FullName="C:/First.dwg"),
    )

    async def run_inline(function, *args, **kwargs):
        return function(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(backend, "_run", run_inline)
    monkeypatch.setattr("backends.com_backend._acad_app", lambda: app)

    await backend._ensure_document_state()
    plan = object()
    backend._plan_spec = plan
    backend._gdt_datums_defined = {"A"}
    app.ActiveDocument = SimpleNamespace(Name="Second.dwg", FullName="C:/Second.dwg")

    with pytest.raises(ActiveDocumentChangedError):
        await backend._ensure_document_state()

    assert backend._gdt_datums_defined == {"A"}
```

```bash
Every open document gets a uuid4 document_id when it is registered and a
content revision. Headless, the revision moves wherever dirty is set (every
mutation, undo, redo, transaction_rollback, snapshot restore) and never on a
save, an export, a finalize or a delivery. Live, a _mutating decorator on the

never on a refusal or a save; the set is held to the headless engine's
dirty-setting methods by a test.

drawing_open, document_activate, document_close and system_launch, and at the

executor job before any document work: an active document that is not the
bound one is refused with ActiveDocumentChangedError (kind
```

- Modify: `server.py` (lifespan journal; `AuditMiddleware` mints / reuses ids, replays, refuses `operation_conflict` / `requires_reconcile`, records the outcome; `CapabilityRefusalMiddleware` payloads carry `operation_id`; `system_status` lists `operations`; `cad_batch(operation_id)`, one record per step, `BATCH_ERROR_KINDS`, `_classify_batch_error` types `active_document_changed`)

- Produces (the skeleton's Interfaces, exactly):

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing journal tests**

Create `tests/test_operations.py`:

```python
"""B12 - the operation journal: one record per call, honest states, client ids.

Pure: no backend and no server. The request hash's oracle is the canonical-JSON
string written out below; every state transition is driven by hand.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import pytest

from backends.operations import (
    CURRENT_OPERATION,
    STATES,
    OperationConflictError,
    OperationJournal,
    OperationRecord,
    RequiresReconcileError,
    request_hash,
)

_LINE = {"x1": 0, "y1": 0, "x2": 10, "y2": 0}
_DOC = {
    "document_id": "a" * 32,
    "revision": 4,
    "name": "untitled-1",
    "path": None,
    "backend": "ezdxf",
}


def test_the_states_are_the_documented_vocabulary():
    assert STATES == (
        "running",
        "committed",
        "partial",
        "failed",
        "refused",
        "quarantined",
        "cancelled",
        "unknown_commit",
    )


def test_request_hash_is_the_sha256_of_the_canonical_json():
    canonical = '{"arguments":{"x1":0,"x2":10,"y1":0,"y2":0},"tool":"entity_create_line"}'
    expected = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    assert request_hash("entity_create_line", _LINE) == expected
    shuffled = {"y2": 0, "x2": 10, "y1": 0, "x1": 0}
    assert request_hash("entity_create_line", shuffled) == expected, "key order is no part of it"
    assert request_hash("entity_create_circle", _LINE) != expected


def test_a_server_minted_record_runs_and_finishes():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, replay = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, document=_DOC)
    assert isinstance(record, OperationRecord) and replay is None
    assert len(record.operation_id) == 32
    assert set(record.operation_id) <= set("0123456789abcdef")
    assert (record.state, record.client_supplied) == ("running", False)
    assert (record.document_id, record.revision_before, record.revision_after) == (
        "a" * 32,
        4,
        None,
    )
    assert record.request_hash == request_hash("entity_create_line", _LINE)
    journal.finish(record, "committed", document={**_DOC, "revision": 5})
    assert (record.state, record.revision_after, record.error_kind) == ("committed", 5, None)
    assert record.ended is not None and record.ended >= record.started
    assert journal.recent() == [record.to_dict()]
    assert set(record.to_dict()) == {
        "operation_id",
        "tool",
        "request_hash",
        "state",
        "document_id",
        "revision_before",
        "revision_after",
        "started",
        "ended",
        "error_kind",
        "client_supplied",
    }


def test_a_client_id_with_the_same_request_replays_the_recorded_result():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-1", document=_DOC)
    assert record.client_supplied is True and record.operation_id == "op-1"
    result = {"handle": "2A"}
    journal.finish(record, "committed", result=result, document=_DOC)
    again, replay = journal.begin("entity_create_line", dict(_LINE), operation_id="op-1")
    assert again is record and replay is result
    assert len(journal) == 1, "a replay records nothing new"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("state", ["partial", "failed", "refused", "quarantined"])
def test_every_finished_state_but_cancelled_replays(state):
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("cad_batch", {"steps": []}, operation_id="op-2")
    journal.finish(record, state, result="the recorded answer", error_kind="failed")
    assert (
        journal.begin("cad_batch", {"steps": []}, operation_id="op-2")[1] == "the recorded answer"
    )


def test_a_cancelled_operation_runs_again_under_its_id():
    """Cancelled while queued: nothing ran, so nothing can be replayed - or doubled."""
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-3")
    journal.finish(record, "cancelled", error_kind="cancelled")
    rerun, replay = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-3")
    assert replay is None and rerun is not record and rerun.state == "running"
    assert len(journal) == 1


def test_the_same_id_for_another_request_is_an_operation_conflict():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-4")
    journal.finish(record, "committed", result={"handle": "2A"})
    with pytest.raises(OperationConflictError) as conflict:
        journal.begin("entity_create_line", {**_LINE, "x2": 11}, operation_id="op-4")
    payload = conflict.value.to_dict()
    assert payload["ok"] is False and payload["kind"] == "operation_conflict"
    assert payload["operation_id"] == "op-4" and payload["reason"] == "different_request"


def test_an_id_still_running_is_an_operation_conflict():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-5")
    with pytest.raises(OperationConflictError) as conflict:
        journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-5")
    assert conflict.value.to_dict()["reason"] == "in_progress"


def test_an_unknown_commit_must_be_reconciled():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-6")
    journal.finish(record, "unknown_commit", error_kind="cancelled")
    with pytest.raises(RequiresReconcileError) as reconcile:
        journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id="op-6")
    payload = reconcile.value.to_dict()
    assert payload["kind"] == "requires_reconcile" and payload["operation_id"] == "op-6"
    assert payload["record"]["state"] == "unknown_commit"
    assert "new operation_id" in payload["error"]


def test_the_journal_is_bounded_and_forgets_the_oldest():
    journal = OperationJournal(maxlen=3)
    ids = []
    for index in range(4):
        record, _ = journal.begin(
            "entity_create_line", {**_LINE, "x2": index}, operation_id=f"op-{index}"
        )
        journal.finish(record, "committed", result=index)
        ids.append(record.operation_id)
    assert [row["operation_id"] for row in journal.recent(10)] == ids[1:]
    assert journal.get("op-0") is None
    _, replay = journal.begin("entity_create_line", {**_LINE, "x2": 0}, operation_id="op-0")
    assert replay is None, "an evicted id runs again: the journal is bounded memory (B13)"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "bad, message",
    [
        ("", "must not be empty"),
        (" op", "leading or trailing"),
        ("x" * 129, "at most 128"),
        ("op\n1", "printable"),
        (17, "expected a string"),
    ],
)
def test_a_malformed_client_id_is_refused_by_name(bad, message):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message):
        OperationJournal().begin("entity_create_line", _LINE, operation_id=bad)


def test_finish_takes_only_a_final_state():
    journal = OperationJournal()
    record, _ = journal.begin("entity_create_line", _LINE)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="state must be one of"):
        journal.finish(record, "running")


def test_the_current_operation_starts_unset():
    assert CURRENT_OPERATION.get() is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the journal**

Create `backends/operations.py`:

```python
"""Operation records: one per tool call, held in memory (v1.7.0, B12).

Every tool call gets an ``operation_id``: minted by ``AuditMiddleware`` into
``CURRENT_OPERATION`` (a call that re-enters the middleware - the search
proxy's ``call_tool`` - reuses the id already set), one per step by
``cad_batch`` (its steps skip the middleware), and carried into the COM
executor explicitly because a thread pool copies no context. A record says
what happened, honestly:

    committed       the call returned its result
    partial         a batch whose steps partly landed (on_error stop/continue)
    failed          attempted and broke, or a read that outran its deadline
    refused         declined before doing its work (capability, guard, args)
    quarantined     refused because the document is quarantined
    cancelled       cancelled before its worker ran - nothing happened
    unknown_commit  a mutating call cancelled or timed out while its worker ran:
                    it may or may not have landed

A client may bring its own id (``_meta.operation_id`` on any request, or
``cad_batch(operation_id=...)``). The same id with the same request hash gets
the recorded result back without running again (``replayed: true``); after an
``unknown_commit`` it is refused with ``requires_reconcile``; with a different
request it is refused with ``operation_conflict``. The journal is in memory and
bounded: a server restart forgets every id (B13 makes it durable in 1.8.0),
and an id pushed out of the bound runs again.

Pure: no backend, no server import.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import contextvars
import hashlib
import json
import time
import uuid
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Any

__all__ = [
    "CURRENT_OPERATION",
    "FINAL_STATES",
    "REPLAYABLE_STATES",
    "STATES",
    "OperationConflictError",
    "OperationJournal",
    "OperationRecord",
    "RequiresReconcileError",
    "request_hash",
    "validate_operation_id",
]

STATES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "running",
    "committed",
    "partial",
    "failed",
    "refused",
    "quarantined",
    "cancelled",
    "unknown_commit",
)
FINAL_STATES: tuple[str, ...] = STATES[1:]
#: A finished operation whose recorded answer is final. ``cancelled`` is not:
#: nothing ran, so the same id runs again. ``unknown_commit`` is not either: it
#: is refused with ``requires_reconcile``.
REPLAYABLE_STATES: tuple[str, ...] = ("committed", "partial", "failed", "refused", "quarantined")
MAX_OPERATION_ID_CHARS = 128

#: The operation the running code belongs to. Set by AuditMiddleware (one per
#: request), by cad_batch (one per step) and inside the COM job (explicitly).
CURRENT_OPERATION: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    "acadmcp_operation_id", default=None
)


@dataclass
class OperationRecord:
    operation_id: str
    tool: str
    request_hash: str
    state: str
    document_id: str | None
    revision_before: int | None
    revision_after: int | None
    started: float
    ended: float | None = None
    error_kind: str | None = None
    client_supplied: bool = False

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


def request_hash(tool: str, arguments: dict | None) -> str:
    """``sha256:`` + the hex digest of the canonical JSON of tool + arguments."""
    canonical = json.dumps(
        {"tool": str(tool), "arguments": arguments or {}},
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        default=str,
    )
    return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def validate_operation_id(value: Any) -> str:
    """A client's id: 1-128 printable characters, no surrounding whitespace."""
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError(f"operation_id: expected a string, got {type(value).__name__}")
    if not value:
        raise ValueError("operation_id: must not be empty")
    if len(value) > MAX_OPERATION_ID_CHARS:
        raise ValueError(
            f"operation_id: at most {MAX_OPERATION_ID_CHARS} characters, got {len(value)}"
        )
    if value != value.strip():
        raise ValueError("operation_id: no leading or trailing whitespace")
    if not value.isprintable():
        raise ValueError("operation_id: printable characters only")
    return value


class OperationConflictError(RuntimeError):
    """A client id already names another request, or a request still running."""

    kind = "operation_conflict"

    def __init__(self, operation_id: str, *, reason: str, recorded_tool: str):
        self.operation_id = operation_id
        self.reason = reason
        if reason == "in_progress":
            message = (
                f"operation {operation_id!r} ({recorded_tool}) is still running; wait for it, "
                "then send the same id again to get its result"
            )
        else:
            message = (
                f"operation {operation_id!r} was already used for a different request "
                f"({recorded_tool}); an operation id names one request - send a new id"
            )
        super().__init__(message)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "kind": self.kind,
            "error": str(self),
            "operation_id": self.operation_id,
            "reason": self.reason,
        }


class RequiresReconcileError(RuntimeError):
    """The id's first run ended ``unknown_commit``: whether it landed is unknown."""

    kind = "requires_reconcile"

    def __init__(self, record: OperationRecord):
        self.record = record
        super().__init__(
            f"operation {record.operation_id!r} ({record.tool}) ended unknown_commit: it was "
            "cancelled or timed out while its worker was writing, so whether it landed is "
            "unknown. Check the drawing (system_status revision, entity_list) before doing it "
            "again, and retry under a new operation_id."
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "kind": self.kind,
            "error": str(self),
            "operation_id": self.record.operation_id,
            "record": self.record.to_dict(),
        }


class OperationJournal:
    """The bounded, in-memory record of recent operations, oldest first."""

    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 1000):
        if int(maxlen) < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"maxlen must be at least 1, got {maxlen!r}")
        self._maxlen = int(maxlen)
        self._records: collections.OrderedDict[str, OperationRecord] = collections.OrderedDict()
        self._results: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._records)

    def begin(
        self,
        tool: str,
        arguments: dict | None,
        *,
        operation_id: str | None = None,
        document: dict | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[OperationRecord, Any | None]:
        """Open a record; ``(record, replayed result or None)``.

        A client id (``operation_id`` given) is checked against what the
        journal holds: a different request is ``OperationConflictError``
        (``different_request``), one still running is ``OperationConflictError``
        (``in_progress``), an ``unknown_commit`` is ``RequiresReconcileError``,
        and a finished one in ``REPLAYABLE_STATES`` returns its recorded result
        with the record it belongs to - nothing new is recorded then.
        """
        digest = request_hash(tool, arguments)
        identity = document or {}
        client_supplied = operation_id is not None
        if client_supplied:
            operation_id = validate_operation_id(operation_id)
            prior = self._records.get(operation_id)
            if prior is not None:
                if prior.request_hash != digest:
                    raise OperationConflictError(
                        operation_id, reason="different_request", recorded_tool=prior.tool
                    )
                if prior.state == "running":
                    raise OperationConflictError(
                        operation_id, reason="in_progress", recorded_tool=prior.tool
                    )
                if prior.state == "unknown_commit":
                    raise RequiresReconcileError(prior)
                if prior.state in REPLAYABLE_STATES and operation_id in self._results:
                    return prior, self._results[operation_id]
        else:
            operation_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        record = OperationRecord(
            operation_id=operation_id,
            tool=str(tool),
            request_hash=digest,
            state="running",
            document_id=identity.get("document_id"),
            revision_before=identity.get("revision"),
            revision_after=None,
            started=time.time(),
            client_supplied=client_supplied,
        )
        self._store(record)
        return record, None

    def finish(
        self,
        record: OperationRecord,
        state: str,
        *,
        result: Any = None,
        error_kind: str | None = None,
        document: dict | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Close ``record``; a client id's ``result`` is kept for replay."""
        if state not in FINAL_STATES:
            raise ValueError(f"state must be one of {FINAL_STATES}, got {state!r}")
        record.state = state
        record.error_kind = error_kind
        record.ended = time.time()
        if document:
            record.revision_after = document.get("revision")
            if record.document_id is None:
                record.document_id = document.get("document_id")
        if result is not None and record.client_supplied:
            if self._records.get(record.operation_id) is record:
                self._results[record.operation_id] = result

    def get(self, operation_id: str) -> OperationRecord | None:
        return self._records.get(operation_id)

    def recent(self, n: int = 20) -> list[dict]:
        """The last ``n`` records as dicts, newest last."""
        if n <= 0:
            return []
        return [record.to_dict() for record in list(self._records.values())[-int(n) :]]

    def _store(self, record: OperationRecord) -> None:
        self._records.pop(record.operation_id, None)
        self._results.pop(record.operation_id, None)
        self._records[record.operation_id] = record
        while len(self._records) > self._maxlen:
            evicted, _ = self._records.popitem(last=False)
            self._results.pop(evicted, None)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing worker-context tests**

Create `tests/test_operation_context.py`:

```python
"""B12 - the operation id reaches the worker threads and the call_outcomes rows.

ezdxf's ``_async`` runs its worker in ``contextvars.copy_context()`` (W1); the
COM executor copies no context, so ``_run`` carries the id into the STA job
explicitly. Every cancellation and abandonment row names the operation it
belongs to - that is what lets the middleware call a cancelled write
``unknown_commit`` and a cancelled queue entry ``cancelled``. No AutoCAD is
touched: the COM executor runs plain callables on a daemon thread.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import threading
import time

import pytest

import config
from backends.com_backend import ComBackend
from backends.operations import CURRENT_OPERATION
from backends.quarantine import DaemonExecutor

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _wait_on(entered: threading.Event, release: threading.Event) -> str:
    entered.set()
    assert release.wait(10), "the test never released the worker"
    return "late"


async def _until(predicate, timeout: float = 10.0) -> None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while not predicate():
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise AssertionError(f"condition not met within {timeout:.1f}s")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)


@pytest.fixture
def com_backend():
    b = ComBackend()
    b._executor = DaemonExecutor(max_workers=1, name="test-sta")  # no CoInitialize
    b._connected = True
    yield b
    if b._executor is not None:
        b._executor.shutdown(wait=False)
        b._executor = None


async def test_the_com_job_sees_the_callers_operation_id(com_backend):
    token = CURRENT_OPERATION.set("op-com-1")
    try:
        seen = await com_backend._run(CURRENT_OPERATION.get)
    finally:
        CURRENT_OPERATION.reset(token)
    assert seen == "op-com-1"
    assert await com_backend._run(CURRENT_OPERATION.get) is None, "nothing leaks into the next job"


async def test_a_cancelled_com_call_records_its_operation_id(com_backend, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(config.settings, "com_call_timeout", 30)
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    token = CURRENT_OPERATION.set("op-com-2")
    try:
        task = asyncio.ensure_future(com_backend._run(_wait_on, entered, release))
    finally:
        CURRENT_OPERATION.reset(token)
    try:
        assert await asyncio.to_thread(entered.wait, 10)
        task.cancel()
        with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
            await task
        row = com_backend.call_outcomes[-1]
        assert (row["call"], row["outcome"]) == ("_wait_on", "cancelled_running")
        assert row["operation_id"] == "op-com-2"
    finally:
        release.set()


async def test_a_com_deadline_overrun_leaves_an_abandoned_deadline_row(com_backend, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(config.settings, "com_call_timeout", 0.2)
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    token = CURRENT_OPERATION.set("op-com-3")
    try:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="did not respond"):
            await com_backend._run(_wait_on, entered, release)
    finally:
        CURRENT_OPERATION.reset(token)
        release.set()
    row = com_backend.call_outcomes[-1]
    assert (row["call"], row["outcome"], row["ended"]) == ("_wait_on", "abandoned_deadline", None)
    assert row["operation_id"] == "op-com-3"


async def test_a_cancelled_ezdxf_call_records_its_operation_id(backend):
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    token = CURRENT_OPERATION.set("op-ezdxf-1")
    try:
        task = asyncio.ensure_future(backend._async(_wait_on, entered, release))
    finally:
        CURRENT_OPERATION.reset(token)
    try:
        assert await asyncio.to_thread(entered.wait, 10)
        task.cancel()
        with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
            await task
        row = backend.call_outcomes[-1]
        assert (row["call"], row["outcome"]) == ("_wait_on", "cancelled_running")
        assert row["operation_id"] == "op-ezdxf-1"
    finally:
        release.set()
    await _until(lambda: backend.call_outcomes[-1]["ended"] is not None)


async def test_the_ezdxf_worker_sees_the_callers_operation_id(backend):
    token = CURRENT_OPERATION.set("op-ezdxf-2")
    try:
        assert await backend._async(CURRENT_OPERATION.get) == "op-ezdxf-2"
    finally:
        CURRENT_OPERATION.reset(token)
```

```python
    def _record_cancel(
        self,
        func,
        work,
        started: float,
        document: str | None,
        operation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        queued = work.cancel()
        row = {
            "call": real_call_name(func),
            "outcome": "cancelled_queued" if queued else "cancelled_running",
            "started": started,
            "ended": time.time() if queued else None,
            "document": document,
            "operation_id": operation_id,
        }
        self.call_outcomes.append(row)
        if not queued:
            # Runs on the worker thread when the call returns (at once if it
            # already has); a dict store is atomic under the GIL.
            work.add_done_callback(lambda _work: row.__setitem__("ended", time.time()))
```
