- [ ] **Step 7: Write the parser**

Create `engineering/verification/requirements.py`:

```python
"""Typed requirements: parse, validate, conflicts, the stored record (v1.7.0, B15 residue).

A requirement set arrives as::

    {"units": "mm",
     "items": [{"id": "D1", "quantity": "diameter",
                "target": {"nominal": 40, "unit": "mm"},
                "features": [{"kind": "feature_tag", "part": "flange", "feature": "bore"}],
                "provenance": "explicit",
                "source": {"document": "flange.pdf", "page": 1, "label": "Ø40"}}],
     "dimensions": {...}}          # optional legacy object, kept read-only

Every refusal names its path through ``engineering.validation``
(``items[2].target.nominal: must be greater than zero, got -1.0.``).
Conflicting provenance is kept and reported (``requirement_conflicts``), never
collapsed to one value. A legacy ``dimensions`` object is accepted as given,
stored as given and never measured.

``store_requirements`` writes the set into the drawing - an XRECORD named
``CURRENT`` inside the ``ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS`` dictionary, JSON in
255-character 1000-code chunks: the layer-states pattern
(``engineering/environment/layer_states.py``). ActiveX can add only a
dictionary to the named object dictionary (``IAcadDictionaries`` has ``Add``
and no ``AddXRecord`` - read off the AutoCAD 2026 type library), so the record
sits one level down on both engines. It is the server's own record; AutoCAD
shows it nowhere.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

import config
from engineering.validation import (
    SpecError,
    finite,
    int_in_range,
    non_negative,
    positive,
    short_repr,
    within_budget,
)
from engineering.verification.types import (
    FEATURE_TYPES,
    PROVENANCES,
    QUANTITIES,
    SELECTOR_KINDS,
    FeatureSelector,
    Requirement,
    RequirementSet,
    freeze,
)

__all__ = [
    "ANGLE_UNITS",
    "CHUNK_SIZE",
    "DICT_NAME",
    "LENGTH_UNITS",
    "RECORD_NAME",
    "RequirementError",
    "decode_requirements",
    "encode_requirements",
    "parse_requirements",
    "requirement_conflicts",
]

DICT_NAME = "ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS"
RECORD_NAME = "CURRENT"
FORMAT_VERSION = 1
#: AutoCAD's ``SetXRecordData`` string limit; the chunks are ASCII, so a
#: boundary never splits a code point.
CHUNK_SIZE = 255

LENGTH_UNITS: tuple[str, ...] = ("mm", "cm", "m", "in", "ft")
ANGLE_UNITS: tuple[str, ...] = ("deg", "rad")
UNIT_ALIASES = {
    "millimetre": "mm",
    "millimeter": "mm",
    "millimetres": "mm",
    "millimeters": "mm",
    "centimetre": "cm",
    "centimeter": "cm",
    "metre": "m",
    "meter": "m",
    "inch": "in",
    "inches": "in",
    "foot": "ft",
    "feet": "ft",
    "degree": "deg",
    "degrees": "deg",
    "radian": "rad",
    "radians": "rad",
}

ITEM_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "id",
    "quantity",
    "target",
    "features",
    "provenance",
    "source",
    "datum",
    "manufacturing_tolerance",
    "verification_epsilon",
)
SOURCE_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = ("document", "page", "view", "label", "region")
#: A geometric predicate's optional keys: the vocabulary Task 14's binder reads
#: (a size window is a radius window for circles / arcs, a length window for lines).
PREDICATE_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "layer",
    "space",
    "region",
    "radius_min",
    "radius_max",
    "length_min",
    "length_max",
)
#: ``(required, optional)`` keys per selector kind, besides ``kind``.
SELECTOR_KEYS: dict[str, tuple[tuple[str, ...], tuple[str, ...]]] = {
    "handle": (("handle",), ("block_path",)),
    "feature_tag": (("part", "feature"), ("view", "type", "fallback")),
    "arch_element": (("element",), ("type",)),
    "predicate": (("type",), PREDICATE_KEYS),
    "dimension": (("handle",), ()),
}
#: How many feature selectors each quantity takes: ``(min, max)``, None = no maximum.
FEATURE_ARITY: dict[str, tuple[int, int | None]] = {
    "distance": (1, 2),
    "radius": (1, 1),
    "diameter": (1, 1),
    "angle": (1, 2),
    "count": (1, None),
    "absence": (1, None),
    "concentric": (2, 2),
    "coincident": (2, 2),
    "parallel": (2, 2),
    "perpendicular": (2, 2),
    "tangent": (2, 2),
    "horizontal": (1, 1),
    "vertical": (1, 1),
}
MEASURED: tuple[str, ...] = ("distance", "radius", "diameter", "angle")
MAX_COUNT = 1_000_000
_FIT = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z]{1,2}\d{1,2}")
_HEX = set("0123456789abcdefABCDEF")


class RequirementError(SpecError):
    """A requirement refused before anything is stored; the message starts with its path."""


def _mapping(value: Any, path: str) -> Mapping:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: expected an object, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    return value


def _only(mapping: Mapping, path: str, allowed: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
    unknown = sorted(str(key) for key in mapping if key not in allowed)
    if unknown:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: unknown key(s) {unknown}; allowed: {list(allowed)}.")


def _text(value: Any, path: str, *, max_chars: int = 200) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: expected a non-empty string, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    text = value.strip()
    if len(text) > max_chars:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: at most {max_chars} characters, got {len(text)}.")
    return text


def _choice(value: Any, path: str, choices: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    if value not in choices:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: must be one of {list(choices)}, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    return value


def _unit(value: Any, path: str, allowed: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: expected a unit name, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    folded = value.strip().lower()
    unit = UNIT_ALIASES.get(folded, folded)
    if unit not in allowed:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: must be one of {list(allowed)}, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    return unit


def _region(value: Any, path: str) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    if not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) or len(value) != 4:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: expected [x1, y1, x2, y2], got {short_repr(value)}.")
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = (finite(item, f"{path}[{index}]") for index, item in enumerate(value))
    if x1 == x2 or y1 == y2:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}: a region needs a width and a height, got {short_repr(value)}."
        )
    return (min(x1, x2), min(y1, y2), max(x1, x2), max(y1, y2))


def _handle(value: Any, path: str) -> str:
    text = _text(value, path, max_chars=16)
    if not set(text) <= _HEX:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: a handle is hexadecimal, got {short_repr(value)}.")
    return text.upper()


def _parse_target(value: Any, quantity: str, units: str, path: str) -> dict:
    if quantity in MEASURED:
        target = _mapping(value, path)
        _only(target, path, ("nominal", "unit"))
        if "nominal" not in target:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}.nominal: required for a {quantity}.")
        where = f"{path}.nominal"
        if quantity == "distance":
            nominal = non_negative(target["nominal"], where)
        elif quantity == "angle":
            nominal = finite(target["nominal"], where)
        else:
            nominal = positive(target["nominal"], where)
        if quantity == "angle":
            unit = _unit(target.get("unit", "deg"), f"{path}.unit", ANGLE_UNITS)
        else:
            unit = _unit(target.get("unit", units), f"{path}.unit", LENGTH_UNITS)
        return {"nominal": nominal, "unit": unit}
    if quantity == "count":
        target = _mapping(value, path)
        _only(target, path, ("exact", "min", "max"))
        out = {
            key: int_in_range(target[key], f"{path}.{key}", 0, MAX_COUNT)
            for key in ("exact", "min", "max")
            if key in target
        }
        if not out:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: a count needs 'exact', 'min' or 'max'.")
        if "exact" in out and len(out) > 1:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: 'exact' excludes 'min' and 'max'.")
        if "min" in out and "max" in out and out["min"] > out["max"]:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: min {out['min']} is greater than max {out['max']}.")
        return out
    # absence and the geometric relations carry no target
    if value is None:
        return {}
    target = _mapping(value, path)
    if target:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}: a {quantity} requirement takes no target, got {short_repr(dict(target))}."
        )
    return {}


def _parse_selector(value: Any, path: str) -> FeatureSelector:
    selector = _mapping(value, path)
    kind = _choice(selector.get("kind"), f"{path}.kind", SELECTOR_KINDS)
    required, optional = SELECTOR_KEYS[kind]
    _only(selector, path, ("kind", *required, *optional))
    for key in required:
        if key not in selector:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}.{key}: required for a {kind} selector.")
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if kind in ("handle", "dimension"):
        out["handle"] = _handle(selector["handle"], f"{path}.handle")
        if "block_path" in selector:
            out["block_path"] = _block_path(selector["block_path"], f"{path}.block_path")
    elif kind == "feature_tag":
        for key in ("part", "feature", "view"):
            if key in selector:
                out[key] = _text(selector[key], f"{path}.{key}")
        if "type" in selector:
            out["type"] = _choice(selector["type"], f"{path}.type", FEATURE_TYPES)
        if "fallback" in selector:
            fallback = _mapping(selector["fallback"], f"{path}.fallback")
            _only(fallback, f"{path}.fallback", ("type", *PREDICATE_KEYS))
            if "type" not in fallback:
                raise RequirementError(f"{path}.fallback.type: required for a fallback predicate.")
            out["fallback"] = _parse_predicate(fallback, f"{path}.fallback")
    elif kind == "arch_element":
        out["element"] = _text(selector["element"], f"{path}.element")
        if "type" in selector:
            out["type"] = _choice(selector["type"], f"{path}.type", FEATURE_TYPES)
    else:  # predicate
        out = _parse_predicate(selector, path)
    return FeatureSelector(kind=kind, value=freeze(out))


def _block_path(value: Any, path: str) -> list[str]:
    """The INSERT handles above an exploded feature, outermost first."""
    if not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) or not value:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}: expected a non-empty list of handles, got {short_repr(value)}."
        )
    return [_handle(item, f"{path}[{index}]") for index, item in enumerate(value)]


def _parse_predicate(selector: Mapping, path: str) -> dict:
    """A geometric predicate: a feature type, a layer / space, a region and a size
    window (``radius_*`` for circles and arcs, ``length_*`` for lines)."""
    out: dict[str, Any] = {"type": _choice(selector["type"], f"{path}.type", FEATURE_TYPES)}
    for key in ("layer", "space"):
        if key in selector:
            out[key] = _text(selector[key], f"{path}.{key}")
    for low, high in (("radius_min", "radius_max"), ("length_min", "length_max")):
        for key in (low, high):
            if key in selector:
                out[key] = non_negative(selector[key], f"{path}.{key}")
        if low in out and high in out and out[low] > out[high]:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: {low} {out[low]} is greater than {high} {out[high]}.")
    if "region" in selector:
        out["region"] = _region(selector["region"], f"{path}.region")
    return out


def _parse_source(value: Any, path: str) -> dict:
    if value is None:
        return {}
    source = _mapping(value, path)
    _only(source, path, SOURCE_KEYS)
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in ("document", "view", "label"):
        if key in source:
            out[key] = _text(source[key], f"{path}.{key}")
    if "page" in source:
        out["page"] = int_in_range(source["page"], f"{path}.page", 1, 100_000)
    if "region" in source:
        out["region"] = _region(source["region"], f"{path}.region")
    return out


def _parse_tolerance(value: Any, path: str) -> dict | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    tolerance = _mapping(value, path)
    _only(tolerance, path, ("upper", "lower", "fit", "source"))
    if "source" not in tolerance:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}.source: a manufacturing tolerance names its own source (a drawing note, "
            "a standard, a fit table); it is never derived."
        )
    out: dict[str, Any] = {"source": _text(tolerance["source"], f"{path}.source")}
    has_fit = "fit" in tolerance
    has_band = "upper" in tolerance or "lower" in tolerance
    if has_fit and has_band:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: give a fit or an upper/lower band, not both.")
    if has_fit:
        fit = _text(tolerance["fit"], f"{path}.fit", max_chars=8)
        if not _FIT.fullmatch(fit):
            raise RequirementError(
                f"{path}.fit: an ISO 286 fit such as H7 or g6, got {short_repr(tolerance['fit'])}."
            )
        out["fit"] = fit
    elif has_band:
        if "upper" not in tolerance or "lower" not in tolerance:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: a band needs both 'upper' and 'lower'.")
        upper = finite(tolerance["upper"], f"{path}.upper")
        lower = finite(tolerance["lower"], f"{path}.lower")
        if lower > upper:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}: lower {lower} is greater than upper {upper}.")
        out["upper"] = upper
        out["lower"] = lower
    else:
        raise RequirementError(f"{path}: give a 'fit' or an 'upper'/'lower' band.")
    return out


def _parse_item(value: Any, index: int, units: str) -> Requirement:
    path = f"items[{index}]"
    item = _mapping(value, path)
    _only(item, path, ITEM_KEYS)
    for key in ("id", "quantity", "features", "provenance"):
        if key not in item:
            raise RequirementError(f"{path}.{key}: required.")
    requirement_id = _text(item["id"], f"{path}.id", max_chars=64)
    quantity = _choice(item["quantity"], f"{path}.quantity", QUANTITIES)
    provenance = _choice(item["provenance"], f"{path}.provenance", PROVENANCES)
    target = _parse_target(item.get("target"), quantity, units, f"{path}.target")
    raw_features = item["features"]
    if not isinstance(raw_features, (list, tuple)) or not raw_features:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}.features: expected a non-empty list of selectors, got {short_repr(raw_features)}."
        )
    features = tuple(
        _parse_selector(selector, f"{path}.features[{position}]")
        for position, selector in enumerate(raw_features)
    )
    low, high = FEATURE_ARITY[quantity]
    if len(features) < low or (high is not None and len(features) > high):
        wanted = (
            str(low) if low == high else (f"at least {low}" if high is None else f"{low} to {high}")
        )
        raise RequirementError(
            f"{path}.features: a {quantity} takes {wanted} selector(s), got {len(features)}."
        )
    datum = (
        None if item.get("datum") is None else _text(item["datum"], f"{path}.datum", max_chars=16)
    )
    epsilon = item.get("verification_epsilon")
    if epsilon is not None:
        epsilon = positive(epsilon, f"{path}.verification_epsilon")
    tolerance = _parse_tolerance(
        item.get("manufacturing_tolerance"), f"{path}.manufacturing_tolerance"
    )
    return Requirement(
        id=requirement_id,
        quantity=quantity,
        target=freeze(target),
        features=features,
        provenance=provenance,
        source=freeze(_parse_source(item.get("source"), f"{path}.source")),
        datum=datum,
        manufacturing_tolerance=None if tolerance is None else freeze(tolerance),
        verification_epsilon=epsilon,
    )


def parse_requirements(raw: Any) -> RequirementSet:
    """A ``RequirementSet`` from typed ``items`` and/or a legacy ``dimensions`` object.

    Keys other than ``units``, ``items``, ``dimensions`` / ``legacy`` (the
    preflight's ``part_type``, ``tolerance_policy``, ``constraints``) are left
    to their own readers. Items come back sorted by id. Refusals are
    ``RequirementError`` (a ``SpecError``, so a ``ValueError``) naming the path.
    """
    if isinstance(raw, RequirementSet):
        return raw
    data = _mapping(raw, "requirements")
    if "dimensions" in data and "legacy" in data:
        raise RequirementError(
            "requirements: give 'dimensions' or 'legacy', not both (they are the same legacy object)."
        )
    legacy_raw = data.get("dimensions", data.get("legacy"))
    items_raw = data.get("items")
    if items_raw is None and legacy_raw is None:
        raise RequirementError(
            "requirements: nothing to read - give typed 'items' (a list of requirements) or a "
            "legacy 'dimensions' object."
        )
    if data.get("units") is None:
        raise RequirementError(
            f"units: required - a length means nothing without it ({', '.join(LENGTH_UNITS)})."
        )
    units = _unit(data["units"], "units", LENGTH_UNITS)
    requirements: tuple[Requirement, ...] = ()
    if items_raw is not None:
        if not isinstance(items_raw, (list, tuple)):
            raise RequirementError(f"items: expected a list, got {short_repr(items_raw)}.")
        within_budget(len(items_raw), config.settings.max_spec_items, "items", "MAX_SPEC_ITEMS")
        parsed = [_parse_item(value, index, units) for index, value in enumerate(items_raw)]
        seen: dict[str, int] = {}
        for index, requirement in enumerate(parsed):
            if requirement.id in seen:
                raise RequirementError(
                    f"items[{index}].id: {requirement.id!r} is already items[{seen[requirement.id]}].id; "
                    "an id names one requirement."
                )
            seen[requirement.id] = index
        requirements = tuple(sorted(parsed, key=lambda requirement: requirement.id))
    legacy = None if legacy_raw is None else freeze(_mapping(legacy_raw, "dimensions"))
    return RequirementSet(requirements=requirements, units=units, legacy=legacy)


def requirement_conflicts(rs: RequirementSet) -> list[dict]:
    """Conflicts kept in the set and reported: ``{code, field, values, message}``.

    ``CONFLICTING_PROVENANCE`` for an item marked ``conflicting``;
    ``CONFLICTING_REQUIREMENT`` for two items asking for the same quantity of
    the same feature(s) with different targets. Nothing is dropped or merged.
    """
    out: list[dict] = []
    for requirement in rs.requirements:
        if requirement.provenance == "conflicting":
            out.append(
                {
                    "code": "CONFLICTING_PROVENANCE",
                    "field": f"items[{requirement.id}]",
                    "values": [requirement.id],
                    "message": (
                        f"requirement {requirement.id!r} is marked conflicting: its sources "
                        "disagree. It is kept as given; resolve the source before it can pass."
                    ),
                }
            )
    groups: dict[tuple, list[Requirement]] = {}
    for requirement in rs.requirements:
        groups.setdefault((requirement.quantity, requirement.features), []).append(requirement)
    for (quantity, _features), members in groups.items():
        if len({member.target for member in members}) > 1:
            ids = [member.id for member in members]
            out.append(
                {
                    "code": "CONFLICTING_REQUIREMENT",
                    "field": "items",
                    "values": ids,
                    "message": (
                        f"requirements {ids} ask for the {quantity} of the same feature(s) with "
                        "different targets; all are kept - say which one controls."
                    ),
                }
            )
    return out


def encode_requirements(rs: RequirementSet) -> list[str]:
    """The stored form: ``{format, digest, set}`` as ASCII JSON in ``CHUNK_SIZE`` pieces."""
    payload = {"format": FORMAT_VERSION, "digest": rs.digest(), "set": rs.to_dict()}
    text = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True)
    return [text[i : i + CHUNK_SIZE] for i in range(0, len(text), CHUNK_SIZE)] or [""]


def decode_requirements(chunks) -> RequirementSet:
    """The set ``encode_requirements`` stored; an edited or damaged record is refused."""
    text = "".join(str(chunk) for chunk in chunks)
    try:
        payload = json.loads(text)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{DICT_NAME}: the stored record is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or payload.get("format") != FORMAT_VERSION:
        found = payload.get("format") if isinstance(payload, dict) else type(payload).__name__
        raise ValueError(f"{DICT_NAME}: not a format {FORMAT_VERSION} record (got {found!r})")
    rs = parse_requirements(payload.get("set"))
    if rs.digest() != payload.get("digest"):
        raise ValueError(
            f"{DICT_NAME}: the stored digest {payload.get('digest')!r} does not match the stored "
            f"set ({rs.digest()!r}); the record was edited or damaged"
        )
    return rs
```

def test_typed_items_make_a_ready_preflight_without_legacy_dimensions():
    result = preflight_drawing("flange", requirements=_TYPED)
    assert result.ready is True
    assert result.questions == [] and result.conflicts == [] and result.legacy == []
    expected = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    assert result.requirement_set == {**expected.to_dict(), "digest": expected.digest()}
    items = result.normalized_spec["requirements"]["items"]
    assert items == expected.to_dict()["items"]
    assert items[0]["target"] == {"nominal": 40.0, "unit": "mm"}, "the set's unit, filled in"
    assert result.to_dict()["requirement_set"]["digest"] == expected.digest()


def test_a_malformed_item_is_a_conflict_named_by_its_path():
    bad = {**_TYPED, "items": [{**_ITEMS[0], "target": {"nominal": -1}}]}
    result = preflight_drawing("flange", requirements=bad)
    assert result.ready is False
    (conflict,) = [c for c in result.conflicts if c.code == "INVALID_REQUIREMENT"]
    assert conflict.field == "items[0].target.nominal"
    assert "must be greater than zero, got -1.0" in conflict.message
    assert result.requirement_set is None


def test_conflicting_sources_are_kept_and_reported_never_collapsed():
    items = [
        {**_ITEMS[0], "source": {"view": "front"}},
        {
            **_ITEMS[0],
            "id": "D2",
            "target": {"nominal": 42},
            "provenance": "conflicting",
            "source": {"view": "section A-A"},
        },
    ]
    result = preflight_drawing("flange", requirements={**_TYPED, "items": items})
    assert result.ready is False
    found = {(c.code, tuple(c.values)) for c in result.conflicts}
    assert ("CONFLICTING_PROVENANCE", ("D2",)) in found
    assert ("CONFLICTING_REQUIREMENT", ("D1", "D2")) in found
    kept = result.requirement_set["items"]
    assert [(item["id"], item["target"]["nominal"]) for item in kept] == [
        ("D1", 40.0),
        ("D2", 42.0),
    ]


def test_items_wait_for_units_and_the_question_asks_for_them():
    no_units = {key: value for key, value in _TYPED.items() if key != "units"}
    result = preflight_drawing("flange", requirements=no_units)
    assert {q.code for q in result.questions} == {"MISSING_UNITS"}
    assert not any(c.code == "INVALID_REQUIREMENT" for c in result.conflicts)
    assert result.requirement_set is None


def test_the_legacy_spec_and_its_hash_are_unchanged():
    result = preflight_drawing(
        "L bracket",
        requirements={
            "units": "MM",
            "part_type": "Bracket",
            "dimensions": {"width": 80, "height": 50},
            "tolerance_policy": "ISO 2768-m",
        },
    )
    expected_spec = {
        "intent": "L bracket",
        "requirements": {
            "dimensions": {"height": 50.0, "width": 80.0},
            "part_type": "bracket",
            "tolerance_policy": "ISO 2768-m",
            "units": "mm",
        },
        "sheet_size": "A3",
        "scale": 1.0,
        "layer_set_id": "mech",
        "view_count": 1,
        "dim_style": "chain",
    }
    text = json.dumps(expected_spec, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    assert result.normalized_spec == expected_spec
    assert result.spec_hash == "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    assert result.ready is True and result.requirement_set is None
    assert [(entry["field"], entry["status"]) for entry in result.legacy] == [
        ("dimensions", "read_only")
    ]

Create `tests/test_requirements_store.py`:

```python
"""B15 residue - drawing_plan(persist_requirements=True) and the ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS record.

The set is written into the drawing (an XRECORD ``CURRENT`` inside the
``ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS`` dictionary, the layer-states pattern), so a saved and
reopened file carries its requirements. The live path runs against a fake
ActiveX surface that models only members measured on AutoCAD 2026 (the
layer-states fakes: ``Dictionaries.Item`` / ``Add``, ``AddXRecord``,
``SetXRecordData`` / ``GetXRecordData``, ``Count`` / ``Item`` / ``GetName``,
and the makepy narrowing of ``Dictionaries.Item``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ezdxf
import pytest
from fastmcp import Client

import server
from engineering.verification.requirements import (
    load_requirements,
    parse_requirements,
    store_requirements,
)
from engineering.verification.types import thaw

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

_ITEMS = [
    {
        "id": "D1",
        "quantity": "diameter",
        "target": {"nominal": 40},
        "features": [{"kind": "feature_tag", "part": "flange", "feature": "bore"}],
        "provenance": "explicit",
        "manufacturing_tolerance": {"fit": "H7", "source": "drawing note 2"},
    },
    {
        "id": "N1",
        "quantity": "count",
        "target": {"exact": 6},
        "features": [{"kind": "predicate", "type": "circle", "radius_min": 9, "radius_max": 11}],
        "provenance": "explicit",
    },
]
_RAW = {"units": "mm", "part_type": "flange", "tolerance_policy": "ISO 2768-m", "items": _ITEMS}


async def test_plan_accepts_the_hash_of_a_typed_preflight(backend):
    preflight = await backend.drawing_preflight("flange", _RAW)
    plan = await backend.drawing_plan(
        "flange",
        requirements=preflight.normalized_spec["requirements"],
        spec_hash=preflight.spec_hash,
    )
    assert plan.preflight_status == "ready"
    expected = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    assert plan.requirement_digest == expected.digest()
    assert plan.requirements_persisted is False
    assert await load_requirements(backend) is None, "nothing is written without the flag"


async def test_a_persisted_set_travels_with_the_saved_file(backend, tmp_path):
    preflight = await backend.drawing_preflight("flange", _RAW)
    assert preflight.ready is True
    before = backend.document_identity()["revision"]
    plan = await backend.drawing_plan(
        "flange",
        requirements=preflight.normalized_spec["requirements"],
        spec_hash=preflight.spec_hash,
        persist_requirements=True,
    )
    expected = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    assert plan.requirements_persisted is True
    assert plan.requirement_digest == expected.digest()
    assert backend.document_identity()["revision"] == before + 1, "the record is content"

    path = tmp_path / "flange.dxf"
    await backend.drawing_save_as(str(path))
    await backend.drawing_open(str(path))
    assert await load_requirements(backend) == expected

    record = ezdxf.readfile(str(path)).rootdict.get("ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS").get("CURRENT")
    chunks = [tag.value for tag in record.tags if tag.code == 1000]
    assert chunks and all(len(chunk) <= 255 and chunk.isascii() for chunk in chunks)


async def test_storing_again_replaces_the_one_record(backend):
    rs = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    first = await store_requirements(backend, rs)
    second = await store_requirements(backend, rs)
    assert (first["replaced"], second["replaced"]) == (False, True)
    assert (first["digest"], first["items"], first["backend"]) == (rs.digest(), 2, "ezdxf")
    holder = backend._doc.rootdict.get("ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS")
    assert list(holder.keys()) == ["CURRENT"]


async def test_persisting_nothing_typed_is_refused_by_name(backend):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="nothing to read"):
        await backend.drawing_plan(
            "flange", requirements={"units": "mm"}, persist_requirements=True
        )
    assert backend.get_plan_spec() is None
    assert await load_requirements(backend) is None


async def test_a_legacy_only_set_is_stored_as_given(backend):
    plan = await backend.drawing_plan(
        "bracket",
        requirements={"units": "mm", "dimensions": {"width": 80.0}},
        persist_requirements=True,
    )
    loaded = await load_requirements(backend)
    assert loaded.requirements == () and thaw(loaded.legacy) == {"width": 80.0}
    assert plan.requirement_digest == loaded.digest()


# ── live engine, against a fake ActiveX surface ──────────────────────────────


class _FakeXRecord:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.name = name
        self.types = None
        self.values = None

    def SetXRecordData(self, types_, values):
        self.types, self.values = list(types_), list(values)

    def GetXRecordData(self):
        return self.types, self.values


class _Narrowed:
    """``IAcadDictionaries.Item`` / ``IAcadDictionary.Item`` with a makepy cache:
    the declared ``IAcadObject`` wrapper, the live object only via ``_oleobj_``."""

    def __init__(self, obj):
        self._oleobj_ = obj

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        raise AttributeError(f"'IAcadObject' object has no attribute {name!r}")


class _FakeDict:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.Name = name
        self.entries: list[_FakeXRecord] = []
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, tuple]] = []

    @property
    def Count(self):
        return len(self.entries)

    def Item(self, index):
        return _Narrowed(self.entries[index])

    def GetName(self, obj):
        return obj.name

    def AddXRecord(self, keyword):
        self.calls.append(("AddXRecord", (keyword,)))
        record = _FakeXRecord(keyword)
        self.entries.append(record)
        return record


class _FakeDictionaries:
    def __init__(self):
        self.dicts: dict[str, _FakeDict] = {}
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, tuple]] = []

    def Item(self, name):
        self.calls.append(("Item", (name,)))
        if name not in self.dicts:
            raise RuntimeError(f"no dictionary {name}")
        return _Narrowed(self.dicts[name])

    def Add(self, name):
        self.calls.append(("Add", (name,)))
        self.dicts[name] = _FakeDict(name)
        return self.dicts[name]


class _FakeDocument:
    def __init__(self):
        self.Dictionaries = _FakeDictionaries()


@pytest.fixture
def com_store(monkeypatch):
    pytest.importorskip("win32com.client", reason="pywin32 not installed")
    from backends import com_backend as module

    document = _FakeDocument()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_acad_doc", lambda: document)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_ai", lambda values: list(values))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_avar", lambda values: list(values))
    backend = module.ComBackend()

    async def _run_inline(func, *args, **kwargs):
        return func(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(backend, "_run", _run_inline)
    backend._bind_key(("Part.dwg", "C:/work/Part.dwg"))
    return backend, document


async def test_the_live_engine_writes_one_xrecord_and_reads_it_back(com_store):
    backend, document = com_store
    rs = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    stored = await store_requirements(backend, rs)
    assert (stored["replaced"], stored["backend"]) == (False, "com")
    assert document.Dictionaries.calls == [
        ("Item", ("ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS",)),
        ("Add", ("ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS",)),
    ]
    holder = document.Dictionaries.dicts["ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS"]
    (record,) = holder.entries
    assert record.name == "CURRENT" and record.types == [1000] * len(record.values)
    assert all(len(value) <= 255 for value in record.values)
    assert backend.document_identity()["revision"] == 1, "a stored record is a content change"
    assert await load_requirements(backend) == rs
    again = await store_requirements(backend, rs)
    assert again["replaced"] is True and len(holder.entries) == 1


async def test_the_tool_takes_persist_requirements():
    async with Client(server.mcp) as client:
        await client.call_tool("drawing_new", {})
        plan = (
            await client.call_tool(
                "drawing_plan",
                {
                    "intent": "flange",
                    "requirements": {"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS},
                    "persist_requirements": True,
                },
            )
        ).structured_content
    expected = parse_requirements({"units": "mm", "items": _ITEMS})
    assert plan["requirements_persisted"] is True
    assert plan["requirement_digest"] == expected.digest()
```

- [ ] **Step 15: Write the stored record on both engines**

1. Append to `engineering/verification/requirements.py`:

```python
# ── the stored record (both engines) ─────────────────────────────────────────


async def store_requirements(backend, rs: RequirementSet) -> dict:
    """Write ``rs`` into the drawing as ``ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS/CURRENT``, replacing it.

    A content change: the headless revision moves through ``_mark_dirty``, the
    live one through ``_bump_revision`` (not on an ``active_document_changed``
    refusal, which writes nothing). Returns ``{ok, dictionary, record, items,
    legacy, digest, chunks, replaced, backend}``.
    """
    chunks = encode_requirements(rs)
    if getattr(backend, "name", None) == "com":
        from backends.document_guard import ActiveDocumentChangedError

        refused = False
        try:
            replaced = await backend._run(_com_store, chunks)
        except ActiveDocumentChangedError:
            refused = True
            raise
        finally:
            if not refused:
                backend._bump_revision()
    else:
        replaced = await backend._async(_ezdxf_store, backend, chunks)
    return {
        "ok": True,
        "dictionary": DICT_NAME,
        "record": RECORD_NAME,
        "items": len(rs.requirements),
        "legacy": rs.legacy is not None,
        "digest": rs.digest(),
        "chunks": len(chunks),
        "replaced": replaced,
        "backend": backend.name,
    }


async def load_requirements(backend) -> RequirementSet | None:
    """The stored set, or None when the drawing carries no ``ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS`` record."""
    if getattr(backend, "name", None) == "com":
        chunks = await backend._run(_com_chunks)
    else:
        chunks = await backend._async(_ezdxf_chunks, backend, readonly=True)
    return None if chunks is None else decode_requirements(chunks)


def _ezdxf_store(backend, chunks: list[str]) -> bool:
    doc = backend._require_doc()
    holder = doc.rootdict.get_required_dict(DICT_NAME)
    replaced = RECORD_NAME in holder
    if replaced:
        holder.remove(RECORD_NAME)
    record = holder.add_xrecord(RECORD_NAME)
    record.extend([(1000, chunk) for chunk in chunks])
    backend._mark_dirty()
    return replaced


def _ezdxf_chunks(backend) -> list[str] | None:
    doc = backend._require_doc()
    if DICT_NAME not in doc.rootdict:
        return None
    holder = doc.rootdict.get(DICT_NAME)
    if RECORD_NAME not in holder:
        return None
    return [str(tag.value) for tag in holder.get(RECORD_NAME).tags if tag.code == 1000]


def _com_holder(doc, *, create: bool):
    from backends import com_backend as com

    try:
        return com._com_unnarrow(doc.Dictionaries.Item(DICT_NAME))
    except Exception:  # absent: Dictionaries.Item raises for an unknown key
        if not create:
            return None
    return com._com_unnarrow(doc.Dictionaries.Add(DICT_NAME))


def _com_record(holder):
    from backends.com_backend import ComBackend

    if holder is None:
        return None
    for key, obj in ComBackend._com_dict_entries(holder):
        if key == RECORD_NAME:
            return obj
    return None


def _com_store(chunks: list[str]) -> bool:
    """COM thread: write the chunks into the bound document's record."""
    from backends import com_backend as com

    doc = com._acad_doc()
    holder = _com_holder(doc, create=True)
    record = _com_record(holder)
    replaced = record is not None
    if record is None:
        record = holder.AddXRecord(RECORD_NAME)
    record.SetXRecordData(com._ai([1000] * len(chunks)), com._avar(chunks))
    return replaced


def _com_chunks() -> list[str] | None:
    """COM thread: the record's chunks, or None."""
    from backends import com_backend as com
    from backends.com_backend import ComBackend

    record = _com_record(_com_holder(com._acad_doc(), create=False))
    return None if record is None else ComBackend._com_xrecord_chunks(record)
```

feat(verification): typed requirements and the shared verification types (B15 residue)

engineering/verification/types.py defines, once, the types Tasks 13-17 use:
FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet (with a canonical digest),
ObservedFeature, ObservedGeometry, FeatureRef, Binding, Measurement,
AcceptanceItem and the closed vocabularies (statuses pass / fail / unknown /
unsupported / ambiguous, provenances, quantities, selector kinds, feature
types). Frozen and hashable; an unknown word is refused at construction.

engineering/verification/requirements.py parses typed items - id, quantity,
target (nominal + unit, or exact / min / max for counts), feature selectors,
provenance, source, datum, a manufacturing tolerance with its own source kept
apart from verification_epsilon - refusing every bad value by its path
through engineering/validation.py, budgeted by MAX_SPEC_ITEMS. Conflicting
provenance and two targets for one feature are kept and reported, never
collapsed. A legacy dimensions object is accepted read-only.

drawing_preflight validates typed items (INVALID_REQUIREMENT conflicts by
path) and reports legacy dimensions; the legacy spec and its hash are
unchanged. drawing_plan carries the set's digest and, with
persist_requirements=True, writes it into the drawing as the
ACADMCP_REQUIREMENTS / CURRENT XRECORD (the layer-states pattern, both
engines; ActiveX adds only dictionaries to the named object dictionary), so a
reopened file carries its requirements.
No backlog row of docs/analysis/backlog-1.7.0-triage.json is owned by Task 11.

Known red: tests/test_release_consistency.py::test_document_numbers_match_the_tree
(README's collected-test figure; Task 17 retakes it).

Deviation recorded: the skeleton wrote `ComBackend._guard() -> Any`; Task 9 landed `_guard(self, app)` and installs it behind `_acad_doc()`, so this task calls `_acad_doc()` and never `_guard` directly.
  - Task 10: `server.BATCH_ERROR_KINDS` holding `"stale_revision"` (Step 19 adds it if absent).

- Produces (the skeleton's Interfaces, exactly):

  - `async def region_fingerprint(backend, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> str` - the current layout's name plus the sorted `(handle, fingerprint)` of every entity whose WCS box meets `bbox` (closed intervals).
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class ChangePreview: document_id: str | None; revision: int | None; preconditions: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]; planned: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]; def to_dict(self) -> dict` - precondition rows `{"handle", "fingerprint"}` or `{"region": [x0, y0, x1, y1], "fingerprint"}`.
  - `class StaleRevisionError(RuntimeError)` - `kind = "stale_revision"`, `.expected`, `.actual`, `.changed` (`[{handle | region, expected, actual}]`), `to_dict() -> {"ok": False, "kind": "stale_revision", "error", "expected_revision", "actual_revision", "changed"}`.
  - `async def check_preconditions(backend, *, expected_revision=None, preconditions=None) -> None` - reads fingerprints first and the revision last; raises `StaleRevisionError` on any mismatch, `SpecError` (by path) on a malformed argument; changes nothing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Cut the group branch and confirm the foundation it builds on**

- [ ] **Step 2: Probe the ActiveX members of the live text walk and the export fingerprint**

Raw pywin32 on our own `Documents.Add()` document (never `ActiveDocument`, never `SendCommand`), closed with `Close(False)` in a `finally:`; `RPC_E_CALL_REJECTED` retried for up to 90 s. Save it as `out/probes/task12_probe.py` (`out/` is git-ignored) and run it:

```python
"""Task 12 live probe: the ActiveX members the COM text walk and the export
fingerprint read. Raw pywin32 on our OWN Documents.Add() document - never
ActiveDocument, never SendCommand - closed with Close(False) in a finally."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
import uuid

import ezdxf
import pythoncom
import win32com.client
from ezdxf.lldxf.tagwriter import TagCollector

RPC_E_CALL_REJECTED = -2147418111


def retry(fn, *args):
    deadline, delay = time.time() + 90, 0.2
    while True:
        try:
            return fn(*args)
        except pythoncom.com_error as exc:
            if exc.args and exc.args[0] == RPC_E_CALL_REJECTED and time.time() < deadline:
                time.sleep(delay)
                delay = min(delay * 2, 3.0)
                continue
            raise


def pt(x, y):
    return win32com.client.VARIANT(pythoncom.VT_ARRAY | pythoncom.VT_R8, [float(x), float(y), 0.0])


def export(doc):
    folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="acadmcp_probe12_")
    selection = doc.SelectionSets.Add(f"_P12{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}")
    try:
        selection.Select(5)  # acSelectionSetAll
        doc.Export(os.path.join(folder, "snap"), "DXF", selection)
    finally:
        selection.Delete()
    (name,) = os.listdir(folder)
    return os.path.join(folder, name), folder


def digest(path, handle):
    dxf = ezdxf.readfile(path)
    entity = dxf.entitydb.get(handle)
    if entity is None:
        return None
    collector = TagCollector(dxfversion=dxf.dxfversion)
    entity.export_dxf(collector)
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for tag in collector.tags:
        h.update(f"{tag[0]}\x1f{tag[1]!r}\x1e".encode())
    return h.hexdigest()


out = {}
folders = []
app = win32com.client.GetActiveObject("AutoCAD.Application")
doc = retry(app.Documents.Add)
try:
    model = doc.ModelSpace
    line = retry(model.AddLine, pt(0, 0), pt(10, 0))
    block = doc.Blocks.Add(pt(0, 0), "PROBE12_B")
    attdef = block.AddAttribute(2.5, 0, "Tag?", pt(0, 0), "TAG", "OLD-D")
    block.AddText("OLD in block", pt(0, 5), 2.5)
    plain = doc.Blocks.Add(pt(0, 0), "PROBE12_PLAIN")
    plain.AddLine(pt(0, 0), pt(1, 0))
    ref = model.InsertBlock(pt(50, 50), "PROBE12_B", 1, 1, 1, 0)
    plain_ref = model.InsertBlock(pt(80, 50), "PROBE12_PLAIN", 1, 1, 1, 0)
    paper = doc.Layouts.Item("Layout1").Block
    paper_text = paper.AddText("OLD on paper", pt(10, 10), 2.5)
    paper_ref = paper.InsertBlock(pt(30, 30), "PROBE12_B", 1, 1, 1, 0)

    layouts = doc.Layouts
    out["layouts"] = sorted(
        (int(layouts.Item(i).TabOrder), str(layouts.Item(i).Name), int(layouts.Item(i).Block.Count))
        for i in range(int(layouts.Count))
    )
    blocks = doc.Blocks
    out["blocks"] = [
        [str(blocks.Item(i).Name), bool(blocks.Item(i).IsLayout), bool(blocks.Item(i).IsXRef)]
        for i in range(int(blocks.Count))
    ]
    out["reference_objectname"] = str(ref.ObjectName)
    out["reference_attributes"] = [
        [str(a.ObjectName), str(a.TagString), str(a.TextString), str(a.Layer)]
        for a in ref.GetAttributes()
    ]
    try:
        out["plain_reference_attributes"] = list(plain_ref.GetAttributes())
    except pythoncom.com_error as exc:
        out["plain_reference_attributes"] = f"raised {exc.args[0]}"
    try:
        out["has_attributes"] = [bool(ref.HasAttributes), bool(plain_ref.HasAttributes)]
    except (AttributeError, pythoncom.com_error) as exc:
        out["has_attributes"] = f"unreadable: {exc!r}"
    out["attdef"] = [str(attdef.ObjectName), str(attdef.TextString)]
    out["paper_text"] = [str(paper_text.ObjectName), str(paper_text.Handle)]
    paper_attr = paper_ref.GetAttributes()[0]
    paper_attr.TextString = "NEW-1"
    out["paper_attribute_after_write"] = str(paper_ref.GetAttributes()[0].TextString)
    attdef.TextString = "NEW-D"
    out["attdef_after_write"] = str(attdef.TextString)

    first, folder = export(doc)
    folders.append(folder)
    second, folder = export(doc)
    folders.append(folder)
    out["export_keeps_live_handles"] = all(
        ezdxf.readfile(first).entitydb.get(str(h)) is not None
        for h in (line.Handle, paper_text.Handle, paper_attr.Handle, attdef.Handle)
    )
    out["export_twice_same_fingerprint"] = digest(first, line.Handle) == digest(second, line.Handle)
    line.color = 3
    third, folder = export(doc)
    folders.append(folder)
    out["colour_change_moves_fingerprint"] = digest(second, line.Handle) != digest(third, line.Handle)
finally:
    doc.Close(False)
    for folder in folders:
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)

print(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
```

uv run --frozen python out/probes/task12_probe.py

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing tests for the preview module**

Create `tests/test_change_preview.py`:

```python
"""The change-preview contract: exact fingerprints, region fingerprints, refusals.

Spec section 5, Task 12 (B14 residue). A precondition is an EXACT fingerprint:
the sha256 of the entity's exported DXF tags, read the same way at preview and
at apply. ``engineering/understand/diff.py::signature`` is the counter-example
every test here leans on: it rounds to 0.01 and leaves out colour, linetype,
XDATA, ellipse axes, dimension values and MTEXT formatting, so each edit below
leaves the signature equal and must still move the fingerprint.

Expected fingerprints are recomputed in this file from ezdxf's own
``TagCollector`` (``_oracle``), never read back from the module under test.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import tempfile

import ezdxf
import pytest
from ezdxf.lldxf.tagwriter import TagCollector

from engineering.understand.diff import signature
from engineering.understand.snapshot import records_from_doc
from engineering.validation import SpecError

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _oracle(entity, dxfversion: str) -> str:
    """sha256 over the exported tags, written independently of the module."""
    collector = TagCollector(dxfversion=dxfversion)
    entity.export_dxf(collector)
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for tag in collector.tags:
        digest.update(f"{tag[0]}\x1f{tag[1]!r}\x1e".encode())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _signature_of(doc, handle: str) -> tuple:
    snap = records_from_doc(doc, source="test")
    (record,) = [r for r in snap.records if r.handle == handle]
    return signature(record)


# -- exact entity fingerprints ----------------------------------------------------


async def test_an_untouched_entity_reads_the_same_fingerprint_twice(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)

    first = await entity_fingerprint(backend, line.handle)
    second = await entity_fingerprint(backend, line.handle.lower())

    raw = backend._doc.entitydb[line.handle]
    assert first == second == _oracle(raw, backend._doc.dxfversion)
    assert len(first) == 64


def _edit_colour(doc, entity):
    entity.dxf.color = 1


def _edit_linetype(doc, entity):
    entity.dxf.linetype = "DASHED"


def _edit_xdata(doc, entity):
    if "PREVIEWTEST" not in doc.appids:
        doc.appids.new("PREVIEWTEST")
    entity.set_xdata("PREVIEWTEST", [(1000, "edited")])


def _edit_far_digit(doc, entity):
    end = entity.dxf.end
    entity.dxf.end = (end.x + 1e-12, end.y, end.z)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "edit",
    [_edit_colour, _edit_linetype, _edit_xdata, _edit_far_digit],
    ids=["colour", "linetype", "xdata", "1e-12 coordinate"],
)
async def test_an_edit_the_signature_cannot_see_moves_a_line_fingerprint(backend, edit):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    doc = backend._doc
    raw = doc.entitydb[line.handle]
    before_sig = _signature_of(doc, line.handle)
    before = await entity_fingerprint(backend, line.handle)

    edit(doc, raw)

    assert _signature_of(doc, line.handle) == before_sig, "diff.signature misses this edit"
    after = await entity_fingerprint(backend, line.handle)
    assert after != before
    assert after == _oracle(raw, doc.dxfversion)


async def test_an_ellipse_axis_ratio_moves_the_fingerprint(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    doc = backend._doc
    ellipse = doc.modelspace().add_ellipse((0, 0), major_axis=(10, 0), ratio=0.5)
    before_sig = _signature_of(doc, ellipse.dxf.handle)
    before = await entity_fingerprint(backend, ellipse.dxf.handle)

    ellipse.dxf.ratio = 0.5000001

    assert _signature_of(doc, ellipse.dxf.handle) == before_sig
    assert await entity_fingerprint(backend, ellipse.dxf.handle) != before


async def test_a_dimension_measurement_moves_the_fingerprint(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    doc = backend._doc
    builder = doc.modelspace().add_linear_dim(base=(0, 5), p1=(0, 0), p2=(40, 0))
    builder.render()
    dimension = builder.dimension
    before_sig = _signature_of(doc, dimension.dxf.handle)
    before = await entity_fingerprint(backend, dimension.dxf.handle)

    dimension.dxf.actual_measurement = 42.0

    assert _signature_of(doc, dimension.dxf.handle) == before_sig
    assert await entity_fingerprint(backend, dimension.dxf.handle) != before


async def test_mtext_formatting_moves_the_fingerprint(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    doc = backend._doc
    mtext = doc.modelspace().add_mtext("OLD")
    before_sig = _signature_of(doc, mtext.dxf.handle)
    before = await entity_fingerprint(backend, mtext.dxf.handle)

    mtext.text = "{\\LOLD}"

    assert _signature_of(doc, mtext.dxf.handle) == before_sig, "plain text is still OLD"
    assert await entity_fingerprint(backend, mtext.dxf.handle) != before


async def test_an_attribute_value_moves_its_reference_fingerprint(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import entity_fingerprint

    doc = backend._doc
    doc.blocks.new("TAGGED").add_attdef("TAG", (0, 0), text="")
    ref = doc.modelspace().add_blockref("TAGGED", (0, 0))
    ref.add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "P-101"})
    before = await entity_fingerprint(backend, ref.dxf.handle)

    ref.attribs[0].dxf.text = "P-102"

    assert await entity_fingerprint(backend, ref.dxf.handle) != before


async def test_a_handle_the_drawing_does_not_hold_reads_missing(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import MISSING, entity_fingerprint, entity_fingerprints

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    await backend.entity_delete(line.handle)

    assert await entity_fingerprint(backend, line.handle) == MISSING
    assert await entity_fingerprints(backend, ["FFFFF"]) == {"FFFFF": MISSING}
    assert await entity_fingerprints(backend, []) == {}


# -- region fingerprints ----------------------------------------------------------

REGION = (40.0, -5.0, 60.0, 5.0)


async def test_a_region_fingerprint_moves_only_for_entities_that_meet_it(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import region_fingerprint

    await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 0, 2)  # box (48, -2, 52, 2): inside
    first = await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION)

    await backend.entity_create_circle(500, 500, 1)  # box (499, 499, 501, 501): outside
    assert await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION) == first

    await backend.entity_create_circle(62, 0, 2)  # box x0 = 60.0 = the region's x1
    touching = await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION)
    assert touching != first, "closed intervals: a box touching the edge meets the region"

    await backend.entity_create_circle(62.5, 0, 2)  # box x0 = 60.5 > 60.0
    assert await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION) == touching


async def test_a_region_fingerprint_is_bound_to_the_current_layout(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import region_fingerprint

    model = await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION)
    await backend.layout_set_current("Layout1")

    assert await region_fingerprint(backend, REGION) != model


async def test_a_malformed_region_is_refused_by_path(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import region_fingerprint

    with pytest.raises(SpecError, match=r"^bbox: x0 <= x1"):
        await region_fingerprint(backend, (10.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0))
    with pytest.raises(SpecError, match=r"^bbox\[2\]: must be finite"):
        await region_fingerprint(backend, (0.0, 0.0, float("nan"), 1.0))


# -- the preview ------------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_preview_carries_identity_preconditions_and_plan(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import make_preview

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 0, 2)

    preview = await make_preview(
        backend,
        handles=[line.handle],
        region=REGION,
        planned=[{"i": 0, "tool": "entity_move", "args": {"handle": line.handle, "dx": 5}}],
    )
    payload = preview.to_dict()

    identity = backend.document_identity()
    assert payload["document_id"] == identity["document_id"]
    assert payload["revision"] == identity["revision"]
    raw = backend._doc.entitydb[line.handle]
    assert payload["preconditions"][0] == {
        "handle": line.handle,
        "fingerprint": _oracle(raw, backend._doc.dxfversion),
    }
    assert payload["preconditions"][1]["region"] == list(REGION)
    assert payload["planned"] == [
        {"i": 0, "tool": "entity_move", "args": {"handle": line.handle, "dx": 5}}
    ]
    assert hash(preview) == hash(preview), "a frozen, hashable value"


# -- check_preconditions ----------------------------------------------------------


async def test_unchanged_preconditions_pass(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import check_preconditions, make_preview

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    payload = (await make_preview(backend, handles=[line.handle], region=REGION)).to_dict()

    await check_preconditions(
        backend, expected_revision=payload["revision"], preconditions=payload["preconditions"]
    )


async def test_an_edit_through_the_server_is_a_stale_revision(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import StaleRevisionError, check_preconditions, make_preview

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    payload = (await make_preview(backend, handles=[line.handle])).to_dict()

    await backend.entity_set_properties(line.handle, color=1)

    with pytest.raises(StaleRevisionError) as caught:
        await check_preconditions(
            backend, expected_revision=payload["revision"], preconditions=payload["preconditions"]
        )
    refusal = caught.value.to_dict()
    assert refusal["ok"] is False and refusal["kind"] == "stale_revision"
    assert refusal["expected_revision"] == payload["revision"]
    assert refusal["actual_revision"] == backend.document_identity()["revision"]
    assert refusal["actual_revision"] != payload["revision"]
    assert [row["handle"] for row in refusal["changed"]] == [line.handle]
    assert "nothing was changed" in refusal["error"]


async def test_a_direct_edit_in_the_document_is_caught_by_the_fingerprint(backend):
    """An edit that never passes through the server leaves the revision where
    it was; the exact fingerprint is what refuses the apply."""
    from engineering.changes.preview import StaleRevisionError, check_preconditions, make_preview

    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    payload = (await make_preview(backend, handles=[line.handle])).to_dict()

    backend._doc.entitydb[line.handle].dxf.color = 5

    with pytest.raises(StaleRevisionError) as caught:
        await check_preconditions(
            backend, expected_revision=payload["revision"], preconditions=payload["preconditions"]
        )
    refusal = caught.value.to_dict()
    assert refusal["expected_revision"] == refusal["actual_revision"] == payload["revision"]
    (row,) = refusal["changed"]
    assert row["handle"] == line.handle
    assert row["expected"] == payload["preconditions"][0]["fingerprint"]
    assert row["actual"] == _oracle(backend._doc.entitydb[line.handle], backend._doc.dxfversion)


async def test_an_entity_added_inside_a_region_is_stale(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import StaleRevisionError, check_preconditions, make_preview

    payload = (await make_preview(backend, region=REGION)).to_dict()
    backend._doc.modelspace().add_circle((45, 0), 1)

    with pytest.raises(StaleRevisionError) as caught:
        await check_preconditions(backend, preconditions=payload["preconditions"])
    assert caught.value.changed[0]["region"] == list(REGION)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw, message",
    [
        ("x", r"^preconditions: expected a list"),
        ([1], r"^preconditions\[0\]: expected an object"),
        ([{"fingerprint": "missing"}], r"^preconditions\[0\]: carries exactly one"),
        ([{"handle": "1A", "fingerprint": "abc"}], r"^preconditions\[0\]\.fingerprint"),
        ([{"handle": "1A", "fingerprint": "missing", "x": 1}], r"^preconditions\[0\]: unknown"),
        ([{"region": [1, 0, 0, 1], "fingerprint": "missing"}], r"^preconditions\[0\]\.region"),
    ],
)
async def test_a_malformed_precondition_is_refused_by_path(backend, raw, message):
    from engineering.changes.preview import check_preconditions

    with pytest.raises(SpecError, match=message):
        await check_preconditions(backend, preconditions=raw)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [-1, True, 1.5, "3"])
async def test_a_malformed_expected_revision_is_refused(backend, bad):
    from engineering.changes.preview import check_preconditions

    with pytest.raises(SpecError, match=r"^expected_revision:"):
        await check_preconditions(backend, expected_revision=bad)


async def test_a_backend_without_a_revision_cannot_vouch_for_one(backend, monkeypatch):
    """No silent pass: a revision nobody can read is not the expected one."""
    from engineering.changes.preview import StaleRevisionError, check_preconditions

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        backend, "document_identity", lambda: {"document_id": None, "revision": None}
    )
    with pytest.raises(StaleRevisionError):
        await check_preconditions(backend, expected_revision=0)


# -- interleaving -----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_the_watch_reports_a_change_between_two_of_our_calls(backend):
    from engineering.changes.preview import RevisionWatch

    watch = await RevisionWatch.begin(backend)
    start = backend.document_identity()["revision"]

    assert await watch.before("step 0") is False
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 1, 0)  # our step
    await watch.after()
    ours = backend.document_identity()["revision"]
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 1, 1, 1)  # somebody else's call
    interleaved = backend.document_identity()["revision"]

    assert await watch.before("step 1") is True
    assert watch.report() == {
        "checked": start,
        "after": interleaved,
        "interleaved": [{"before": "step 1", "expected": ours, "actual": interleaved}],
    }


# -- the live engine reads its own export ---------------------------------------------


class _LiveBackend:
    """The surface the preview module touches on ComBackend: ``name``,
    ``_run(func, *args)``, ``layout_list()``, ``document_identity()``."""

    name = "com"

    def __init__(self):
        self.ran: list[str] = []

    async def _run(self, func, *args, **kwargs):
        self.ran.append(getattr(func, "__name__", str(func)))
        return func(*args, **kwargs)

    async def layout_list(self):
        return {"ok": True, "layouts": ["Model", "Layout1"], "current": "Model"}

    def document_identity(self):
        return {"document_id": "f" * 32, "revision": 7, "name": "A.dwg", "path": None}


async def test_the_live_engine_fingerprints_its_dxf_export(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from engineering.changes import preview

    doc = ezdxf.new("R2018")
    handle = doc.modelspace().add_line((0, 0), (10, 0)).dxf.handle
    folders: list[str] = []

    def _fake_export(backend, path):
        assert path is None, "the active (bound) document, never a file"
        folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="acadmcp_snap_", dir=str(tmp_path))
        folders.append(folder)
        written = os.path.join(folder, "snap.DXF")
        doc.saveas(written)
        return written, folder, "live:A.dwg"

    monkeypatch.setattr(preview, "_com_export", _fake_export)
    live = _LiveBackend()

    found = await preview.entity_fingerprints(live, [handle])

    assert live.ran == ["_fake_export"]
    assert not os.path.exists(folders[0]), "the export is deleted once read"
    exported = tmp_path / "oracle.dxf"
    doc.saveas(exported)
    oracle = ezdxf.readfile(exported)
    assert found == {handle: _oracle(oracle.entitydb[handle], oracle.dxfversion)}
    payload = (await preview.make_preview(live, handles=[handle])).to_dict()
    assert payload["revision"] == 7 and payload["document_id"] == "f" * 32
```

Create `engineering/changes/preview.py`:

```python
"""Change previews and exact preconditions (spec section 5, Task 12; B14 residue).

A dry run of a mutating tool returns a :class:`ChangePreview`: the document's
id and content revision, the preconditions an apply will check, and the
planned edits. An apply that carries ``expected_revision`` and/or
``preconditions`` re-reads both first; any mismatch raises
:class:`StaleRevisionError` (kind ``stale_revision``) before anything is
written.

**Exact fingerprints.** An entity's fingerprint is the sha256 of its own DXF
tags as ezdxf exports them (``export_dxf`` into a ``TagCollector``): every
group code and value - layer, colour, linetype, lineweight, geometry, XDATA,
an ellipse's axes, a DIMENSION's measurement and override text, MTEXT
formatting codes - each value hashed through ``repr``, so a float compares bit
for bit. An INSERT's tags carry its ATTRIBs. ``diff.signature`` is not used: it
rounds to 0.01 and leaves most of that out.

**The same reader at preview and at apply.** Headless, the tags come from the
in-memory document under the backend's lock (``_async(readonly=True)``); live,
from ``Document.Export`` of the active document (the snapshot exporter, which
keeps the live handles - ``scripts/smoke_understand_com.py`` moves an exported
handle with ``entity_move``), read with ezdxf. An engine is only ever compared
with itself.

**A region precondition** is the sha256 of the current layout's name and the
sorted ``(handle, fingerprint)`` pairs of every entity of that layout whose WCS
box meets the region (closed intervals). An entity with no finite box (XLINE,
RAY, a reference to an empty block) is not in it.

**Revision** is ``document_identity()["revision"]`` (Task 9): it moves when
content changes through the server. A direct edit of the document that does not
pass through the server leaves it where it was; the fingerprints catch that.

**Interleaving.** An apply made of several locked calls (``cad_batch`` steps,
``drawing_refine`` repairs, ``pid_from_spec`` items) lets other calls run
between its own. :class:`RevisionWatch` reads the revision before each of them
and reports a move since the previous one as an interleaved change. A change
made inside one of our calls cannot be told from our own and is not reported.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import inspect
import math
import shutil
from collections.abc import Iterable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.lldxf.tagwriter import TagCollector

from engineering.understand.snapshot import _box, _com_export
from engineering.validation import SpecError

__all__ = [
    "MISSING",
    "STALE_REVISION",
    "ChangePreview",
    "RevisionWatch",
    "StaleRevisionError",
    "check_preconditions",
    "current_revision",
    "entity_fingerprint",
    "entity_fingerprints",
    "entity_tags_digest",
    "make_preview",
    "parse_preconditions",
    "read_identity",
    "region_fingerprint",
]

#: The fingerprint of a handle the document does not hold (deleted, never there).
MISSING = "missing"
#: The refusal kind every stale apply carries.
STALE_REVISION = "stale_revision"
_HEX = frozenset("0123456789abcdef")
_ROW_KEYS = frozenset({"handle", "region", "fingerprint"})


# -- fingerprints ---------------------------------------------------------------


def entity_tags_digest(entity, dxfversion: str) -> str:
    """sha256 of ``entity``'s exported DXF tags, every value through ``repr``."""
    collector = TagCollector(dxfversion=dxfversion)
    entity.export_dxf(collector)
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for code, value in collector.tags:
        digest.update(f"{code}\x1f{value!r}\x1e".encode())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _handle(value, path: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise SpecError(f"{path}: expected a handle (hex text), got {value!r}")
    return value.strip().upper()


def _region(value, path: str) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    if not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) or len(value) != 4:
        raise SpecError(f"{path}: expected [x0, y0, x1, y1], got {value!r}")
    numbers = []
    for index, item in enumerate(value):
        if isinstance(item, bool) or not isinstance(item, (int, float)):
            raise SpecError(f"{path}[{index}]: expected a number, got {item!r}")
        if not math.isfinite(float(item)):
            raise SpecError(f"{path}[{index}]: must be finite, got {item!r}")
        numbers.append(float(item))
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = numbers
    if x0 > x1 or y0 > y1:
        raise SpecError(f"{path}: x0 <= x1 and y0 <= y1 are required, got {value!r}")
    return x0, y0, x1, y1


def _digests(doc, handles: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for handle in handles:
        entity = doc.entitydb.get(handle)
        if entity is None or not entity.is_alive:
            out[handle] = MISSING
        else:
            out[handle] = entity_tags_digest(entity, doc.dxfversion)
    return out


def _meets(box, region) -> bool:
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
    rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1 = region
    return not (x1 < rx0 or x0 > rx1 or y1 < ry0 or y0 > ry1)


def _region_digest(doc, space: str, region) -> str:
    try:
        layout = doc.layouts.get(space)
    except KeyError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"region fingerprint: the drawing has no layout {space!r}") from exc
    rows = []
    for entity in layout:
        box = _box(entity)
        if box is not None and _meets(box, region):
            rows.append((str(entity.dxf.handle), entity_tags_digest(entity, doc.dxfversion)))
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"space\x1f{space}\x1e".encode())
    for handle, value in sorted(rows):
        digest.update(f"{handle}\x1f{value}\x1e".encode())
    return digest.hexdigest()


async def _read(backend, job):
    """``job(doc)`` on the document this engine's preview and apply both read."""
    if backend.name == "com":
        written, folder, _source = await backend._run(_com_export, backend, None)
        try:
            return await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: job(ezdxf.readfile(written)))
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)
    return await backend._async(lambda: job(backend._require_doc()), readonly=True)


async def _current_space(backend) -> str:
    listing = await backend.layout_list()
    return str((listing or {}).get("current") or "Model")


async def entity_fingerprints(backend, handles: Iterable[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """``{handle: fingerprint}`` for every handle, in one read; ``MISSING`` for
    a handle the document does not hold. Handles are upper-cased."""
    wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(_handle(value, f"handles[{i}]") for i, value in enumerate(handles)))
    if not wanted:
        return {}
    return await _read(backend, lambda doc: _digests(doc, wanted))


async def entity_fingerprint(backend, handle: str) -> str:
    """The exact fingerprint of one entity (``MISSING`` when it is not there)."""
    key = _handle(handle, "handle")
    return (await entity_fingerprints(backend, [key]))[key]


async def region_fingerprint(backend, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> str:
    """The fingerprint of every entity of the current layout whose box meets ``bbox``."""
    region = _region(bbox, "bbox")
    space = await _current_space(backend)
    return await _read(backend, lambda doc: _region_digest(doc, space, region))


# -- identity and revision ------------------------------------------------------


async def read_identity(backend) -> dict:
    """``backend.document_identity()``, awaited when the engine made it a coroutine."""
    reader = getattr(backend, "document_identity", None)
    if reader is None:
        return {"document_id": None, "revision": None}
    value = reader()
    if inspect.isawaitable(value):
        value = await value
    return dict(value or {})


async def current_revision(backend) -> int | None:
    revision = (await read_identity(backend)).get("revision")
    return None if revision is None else int(revision)


# -- the preview ------------------------------------------------------------------


def _freeze(value):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return ("{}", tuple(sorted((str(k), _freeze(v)) for k, v in value.items())))
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return ("[]", tuple(_freeze(v) for v in value))
    return value


def _thaw(value):
    if isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2 and value[0] == "{}":
        return {k: _thaw(v) for k, v in value[1]}
    if isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2 and value[0] == "[]":
        return [_thaw(v) for v in value[1]]
    return value


def _row(row: dict) -> tuple[tuple[str, object], ...]:
    return tuple(sorted((str(k), _freeze(v)) for k, v in row.items()))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChangePreview:
    """What a dry run promises: the document, its revision, the preconditions an
    apply checks and the planned edits. Hashable; ``to_dict`` is the wire form."""

    document_id: str | None
    revision: int | None
    preconditions: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]
    planned: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, object], ...], ...]

    @classmethod
    def build(
        cls,
        document_id: str | None,
        revision: int | None,
        preconditions: Iterable[dict],
        planned: Iterable[dict],
    ) -> ChangePreview:
        return cls(
            document_id,
            revision,
            tuple(_row(row) for row in preconditions),
            tuple(_row(row) for row in planned),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "document_id": self.document_id,
            "revision": self.revision,
            "preconditions": [{k: _thaw(v) for k, v in row} for row in self.preconditions],
            "planned": [{k: _thaw(v) for k, v in row} for row in self.planned],
        }


async def make_preview(
    backend,
    *,
    handles: Iterable[str] = (),
    region: tuple[float, float, float, float] | None = None,
    planned: Iterable[dict] = (),
) -> ChangePreview:
    """The preview of a planned change: fingerprints first, the identity last,
    so the revision is never older than the content it vouches for."""
    rows: list[dict] = [
        {"handle": handle, "fingerprint": value}
        for handle, value in (await entity_fingerprints(backend, handles)).items()
    ]
    if region is not None:
        box = _region(region, "region")
        rows.append({"region": list(box), "fingerprint": await region_fingerprint(backend, box)})
    identity = await read_identity(backend)
    revision = identity.get("revision")
    return ChangePreview.build(
        identity.get("document_id"),
        None if revision is None else int(revision),
        rows,
        list(planned),
    )


# -- the apply ------------------------------------------------------------------


class StaleRevisionError(RuntimeError):
    """The document moved since the preview; nothing was changed.

    ``to_dict()`` is the refusal: ``{ok: False, kind: "stale_revision", error,
    expected_revision, actual_revision, changed}``; ``changed`` holds one row per
    precondition that no longer holds (``{handle | region, expected, actual}``).
    """

    kind = STALE_REVISION

    def __init__(
        self,
        message: str,
        *,
        expected: int | None = None,
        actual: int | None = None,
        changed: Iterable[dict] = (),
    ):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.expected = expected
        self.actual = actual
        self.changed = [dict(row) for row in changed]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "kind": self.kind,
            "error": str(self),
            "expected_revision": self.expected,
            "actual_revision": self.actual,
            "changed": [dict(row) for row in self.changed],
        }


def parse_preconditions(raw) -> list[dict]:
    """Validated precondition rows: ``{handle, fingerprint}`` or
    ``{region: [x0, y0, x1, y1], fingerprint}``. Refused by path."""
    if raw is None:
        return []
    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
        raise SpecError(f"preconditions: expected a list, got {type(raw).__name__}")
    rows = []
    for index, item in enumerate(raw):
        path = f"preconditions[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise SpecError(f"{path}: expected an object, got {type(item).__name__}")
        extra = sorted(set(item) - _ROW_KEYS)
        if extra:
            raise SpecError(f"{path}: unknown key(s) {', '.join(extra)}")
        if ("handle" in item) == ("region" in item):
            raise SpecError(f"{path}: carries exactly one of 'handle' or 'region'")
        value = item.get("fingerprint")
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not (
            value == MISSING or (len(value) == 64 and set(value) <= _HEX)
        ):
            raise SpecError(f"{path}.fingerprint: expected 64 hex digits or {MISSING!r}")
        if "handle" in item:
            rows.append({"handle": _handle(item["handle"], f"{path}.handle"), "fingerprint": value})
        else:
            rows.append(
                {"region": list(_region(item["region"], f"{path}.region")), "fingerprint": value}
            )
    return rows


def _expected_revision(value) -> int | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 0:
        raise SpecError(f"expected_revision: expected a revision (an integer >= 0), got {value!r}")
    return value


async def check_preconditions(backend, *, expected_revision=None, preconditions=None) -> None:
    """Refuse with :class:`StaleRevisionError` unless every precondition still
    holds and the revision is ``expected_revision``. Reads the fingerprints
    first and the revision last, so a change racing the check is caught by one
    or the other. Changes nothing."""
    expected = _expected_revision(expected_revision)
    rows = parse_preconditions(preconditions)
    if expected is None and not rows:
        return
    changed: list[dict] = []
    handle_rows = [row for row in rows if "handle" in row]
    if handle_rows:
        now = await entity_fingerprints(backend, [row["handle"] for row in handle_rows])
        for row in handle_rows:
            actual = now[row["handle"]]
            if actual != row["fingerprint"]:
                changed.append(
                    {"handle": row["handle"], "expected": row["fingerprint"], "actual": actual}
                )
    for row in rows:
        if "region" in row:
            actual = await region_fingerprint(backend, row["region"])
            if actual != row["fingerprint"]:
                changed.append(
                    {"region": row["region"], "expected": row["fingerprint"], "actual": actual}
                )
    actual_revision = await current_revision(backend)
    revision_moved = expected is not None and actual_revision != expected
    if not changed and not revision_moved:
        return
    reasons = []
    if revision_moved:
        reasons.append(f"revision {expected} expected, {actual_revision} now")
    if changed:
        names = ", ".join(
            row["handle"] if "handle" in row else f"region {row['region']}" for row in changed[:5]
        )
        reasons.append(f"{len(changed)} precondition(s) no longer hold: {names}")
    raise StaleRevisionError(
        "stale_revision: the document changed since the preview ("
        + "; ".join(reasons)
        + "); nothing was changed. Run the dry run again and apply its new preview.",
        expected=expected,
        actual=actual_revision,
        changed=changed,
    )


class RevisionWatch:
    """The revision across an apply made of several locked calls.

    ``before(label)`` before each of our calls records a move since our
    previous call as an interleaved change and returns True; ``after()``
    after each call takes the revision our call left. ``report()`` is
    ``{checked, after, interleaved: [{before, expected, actual}]}``.
    """

    def __init__(self, backend, start: int | None):
        self._backend = backend
        self.start = start
        self.last = start
        self.interleaved: list[dict] = []

    @classmethod
    async def begin(cls, backend) -> RevisionWatch:
        return cls(backend, await current_revision(backend))

    async def before(self, label: str) -> bool:
        now = await current_revision(self._backend)
        moved = self.last is not None and now is not None and now != self.last
        if moved:
            self.interleaved.append({"before": label, "expected": self.last, "actual": now})
        self.last = now
        return moved

    async def after(self) -> None:
        self.last = await current_revision(self._backend)

    def report(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "checked": self.start,
            "after": self.last,
            "interleaved": [dict(row) for row in self.interleaved],
        }
```
