- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing tests for the shared text-search scope**

Create `tests/test_text_find_replace_scope.py` (the fakes carry only the members Step 2 measures):

```python
"""``text_find_replace`` searches the same places on both engines (spec section 5, Task 12).

The live engine walked ``ModelSpace`` alone: an attribute on a sheet, a title
block field or a block definition's ATTDEF default was replaced headlessly and
never found live. The headless engine had its own gap: it read the ATTRIBs of
model-space references only, so an attribute on a paper layout or on a
reference nested in a block definition was missed. Both now search
``TEXT_SEARCH_SCOPE``: model space, every paper layout, every block definition
that is not a layout block or an xref, and the ATTRIBs of every reference
found in any of them.

The live engine is exercised through fakes that carry only members measured on
AutoCAD 2026 (Task 12's probe): ``Layouts.Count / Item(i)``, ``Layout.TabOrder``
/ ``Block``, ``Blocks.Count / Item(i)``, ``Block.IsLayout / IsXRef / Count /
Item(j)``, entity ``ObjectName / Handle / Layer / TextString``,
``GetAttributes()``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import pytest

from backends.contracts.entity_modification import TEXT_SEARCH_SCOPE

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

#: The one logical drawing both engines are given, as (DXF type, value): model
#: space TEXT, the ATTRIB of a model-space reference, paper-layout MTEXT, the
#: ATTRIB of a paper-layout reference, the ATTDEF default and a TEXT inside
#: block B, the ATTRIB of a reference to B nested inside block C.
EXPECTED = sorted(
    [
        ("TEXT", "OLD m", "NEW m"),
        ("ATTRIB", "OLD a1", "NEW a1"),
        ("MTEXT", "OLD p", "NEW p"),
        ("ATTRIB", "OLD a2", "NEW a2"),
        ("ATTDEF", "OLD d", "NEW d"),
        ("TEXT", "OLD b", "NEW b"),
        ("ATTRIB", "OLD n", "NEW n"),
    ]
)

_DXF_NAME = {
    "AcDbText": "TEXT",
    "AcDbMText": "MTEXT",
    "AcDbAttribute": "ATTRIB",
    "AcDbAttributeDefinition": "ATTDEF",
}


def _rows(result: dict, names: dict | None = None) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    names = names or {}
    return sorted(
        (names.get(row["type"], row["type"]), row["before"], row["after"])
        for row in result["entities"]
    )


# -- the headless engine --------------------------------------------------------


def _build_headless(backend):
    doc = backend._doc
    block_b = doc.blocks.new("B")
    block_b.add_attdef("TAG", (0, 0), text="OLD d")
    block_b.add_text("OLD b", dxfattribs={"insert": (0, 5)})
    model = doc.modelspace()
    model.add_text("OLD m", dxfattribs={"insert": (0, 0)})
    model.add_blockref("B", (10, 0)).add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "OLD a1"})
    paper = doc.layouts.get("Layout1")
    paper.add_mtext("OLD p", dxfattribs={"insert": (5, 5)})
    paper.add_blockref("B", (20, 20)).add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "OLD a2"})
    block_c = doc.blocks.new("C")
    block_c.add_blockref("B", (0, 0)).add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "OLD n"})


async def test_a_paper_layout_attribute_is_found_headlessly(backend):
    doc = backend._doc
    doc.blocks.new("P").add_attdef("TAG", (0, 0), text="")
    ref = doc.layouts.get("Layout1").add_blockref("P", (0, 0))
    ref.add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "OLD sheet"})

    result = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW")

    assert result["replaced"] == 1
    assert ref.attribs[0].dxf.text == "NEW sheet"


async def test_a_nested_reference_attribute_is_found_headlessly(backend):
    doc = backend._doc
    doc.blocks.new("INNER").add_attdef("TAG", (0, 0), text="")
    nested = doc.blocks.new("OUTER").add_blockref("INNER", (0, 0))
    nested.add_auto_attribs({"TAG": "OLD nested"})

    result = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW")

    assert result["replaced"] == 1
    assert nested.attribs[0].dxf.text == "NEW nested"


async def test_the_headless_engine_covers_the_whole_scope(backend):
    _build_headless(backend)

    result = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW", dry_run=True)

    assert _rows(result) == EXPECTED
    assert result["searched_scope"] == list(TEXT_SEARCH_SCOPE)


async def test_an_xref_block_is_not_searched_headlessly(backend):
    doc = backend._doc
    doc.add_xref_def("other.dxf", "XR")
    xref = doc.blocks.get("XR")
    xref.add_text("OLD x", dxfattribs={"insert": (0, 0)})
    assert xref.block_record.is_xref

    result = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW")

    assert result["replaced"] == 0


# -- the live engine, through measured fakes --------------------------------------


class _Text:
    def __init__(self, object_name, handle, text, layer="0"):
        self.ObjectName, self.Handle, self.TextString, self.Layer = (
            object_name,
            handle,
            text,
            layer,
        )


class _Reference:
    def __init__(self, handle, attributes, layer="0"):
        self.ObjectName, self.Handle, self.Layer = "AcDbBlockReference", handle, layer
        self._attributes = tuple(attributes)

    def GetAttributes(self):  # noqa: N802 - the ActiveX member name
        return self._attributes


class _Block:
    def __init__(self, name, items, *, is_layout=False, is_xref=False):
        self.Name, self.IsLayout, self.IsXRef = name, is_layout, is_xref
        self._items = list(items)

    @property
    def Count(self):  # noqa: N802
        return len(self._items)

    def Item(self, index):  # noqa: N802
        return self._items[index]


class _Layout:
    def __init__(self, tab_order, block):
        self.TabOrder, self.Block = tab_order, block


class _Collection:
    def __init__(self, items):
        self._items = list(items)

    @property
    def Count(self):  # noqa: N802
        return len(self._items)

    def Item(self, index):  # noqa: N802
        return self._items[index]


class _Doc:
    def __init__(self):
        model = _Block(
            "*Model_Space",
            [
                _Text("AcDbText", "10", "OLD m"),
                _Reference("11", [_Text("AcDbAttribute", "12", "OLD a1")]),
                _Text("AcDbLine", "13", ""),
            ],
            is_layout=True,
        )
        paper = _Block(
            "*Paper_Space",
            [
                _Text("AcDbMText", "20", "OLD p"),
                _Reference("21", [_Text("AcDbAttribute", "22", "OLD a2")]),
            ],
            is_layout=True,
        )
        block_b = _Block(
            "B",
            [_Text("AcDbAttributeDefinition", "30", "OLD d"), _Text("AcDbText", "31", "OLD b")],
        )
        block_c = _Block("C", [_Reference("40", [_Text("AcDbAttribute", "41", "OLD n")])])
        xref = _Block("XR", [_Text("AcDbText", "50", "OLD x")], is_xref=True)
        # Collection order puts Layout1 first: the walk sorts on TabOrder.
        self.Layouts = _Collection([_Layout(1, paper), _Layout(0, model)])
        self.Blocks = _Collection([model, paper, block_b, block_c, xref])


async def test_the_live_walk_covers_the_same_scope():
    from backends.com_backend import _com_text_find_replace

    doc = _Doc()
    pattern = re.compile(re.escape("OLD"))

    changed = _com_text_find_replace(doc, pattern, "NEW", None, True)

    rows = sorted((_DXF_NAME[r["type"]], r["before"], r["after"]) for r in changed)
    assert rows == EXPECTED
    assert [r["handle"] for r in changed][:2] == ["10", "12"], "model space (TabOrder 0) first"
    assert "50" not in {r["handle"] for r in changed}, "an xref's text is another file's"
    assert doc.Layouts.Item(1).Block.Item(0).TextString == "OLD m", "a dry run writes nothing"


async def test_the_live_walk_writes_through_textstring_and_honours_the_layer():
    from backends.com_backend import _com_text_find_replace

    doc = _Doc()
    doc.Blocks.Item(2).Item(1).Layer = "NOTES"
    pattern = re.compile(re.escape("OLD"))

    changed = _com_text_find_replace(doc, pattern, "NEW", "NOTES", False)

    assert [(r["handle"], r["after"]) for r in changed] == [("31", "NEW b")]
    assert doc.Blocks.Item(2).Item(1).TextString == "NEW b"
    assert doc.Blocks.Item(2).Item(0).TextString == "OLD d", "other layers untouched"


async def test_the_live_tool_reads_the_bound_document_and_reports_its_scope(monkeypatch):
    from backends import com_backend as module

    doc = _Doc()
    backend = module.ComBackend()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_acad_doc", lambda: doc)

    async def _inline(func, *args, **kwargs):
        return func(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(backend, "_run", _inline)

    result = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW", dry_run=True)

    assert result["ok"] is True and result["replaced"] == len(EXPECTED)
    assert _rows(result, _DXF_NAME) == EXPECTED
    assert result["searched_scope"] == list(TEXT_SEARCH_SCOPE)


async def test_both_engines_answer_the_same_rows(backend):
    from backends.com_backend import _com_text_find_replace

    _build_headless(backend)
    headless = await backend.text_find_replace("OLD", "NEW", dry_run=True)
    live = _com_text_find_replace(_Doc(), re.compile(re.escape("OLD")), "NEW", None, True)

    assert _rows(headless) == sorted((_DXF_NAME[r["type"]], r["before"], r["after"]) for r in live)
```

Edit 2 - in `backends/com_backend.py`, replace:

```python
def _int_member(obj, name: str) -> int | None:
```

with:

```python
#: The ActiveX object names ``text_find_replace`` reads and writes
#: (``TextString``): TEXT, MTEXT, ATTRIB, ATTDEF.
_COM_TEXT_OBJECTS = ("AcDbText", "AcDbMText", "AcDbAttribute", "AcDbAttributeDefinition")
#: A block reference and a MINSERT (``block_explode`` measured both names).
_COM_REFERENCE_OBJECTS = ("AcDbBlockReference", "AcDbMInsertBlock")


def _com_text_candidates(doc):
    """Every object ``text_find_replace`` reads on the live engine, in the
    headless engine's scope (``TEXT_SEARCH_SCOPE``): model space and every paper
    layout (``Layouts.Item(i).Block``, in ``TabOrder``), every block definition
    that is neither a layout block nor an xref (``Blocks.Item(i).IsLayout`` /
    ``IsXRef``), and the ATTRIBs of every block reference found in any of them
    (``GetAttributes()``, which answers an empty array for a reference without
    attributes). It searched ``ModelSpace`` alone, so an attribute on a sheet,
    a title-block field or a block definition's ATTDEF default was never found
    on the live engine while the headless one replaced it.

    Members measured on AutoCAD 2026 by Task 12's probe (a scratch
    ``Documents.Add()`` document closed with ``Close(False)``).
    """
    layouts = doc.Layouts
    spaces = []
    for index in range(int(layouts.Count)):
        layout = layouts.Item(index)
        spaces.append((int(layout.TabOrder), layout.Block))
    containers = [block for _order, block in sorted(spaces, key=lambda row: row[0])]
    blocks = doc.Blocks
    for index in range(int(blocks.Count)):
        block = blocks.Item(index)
        if bool(block.IsLayout) or bool(block.IsXRef):
            continue
        containers.append(block)
    for container in containers:
        for index in range(int(container.Count)):
            entity = container.Item(index)
            yield entity
            if str(entity.ObjectName) in _COM_REFERENCE_OBJECTS:
                yield from entity.GetAttributes()


def _com_text_find_replace(doc, pattern, replace: str, layer: str | None, dry_run: bool) -> list:
    """``[{handle, type, before, after}]`` for every match in the live scope;
    written through ``TextString`` unless ``dry_run``. Each handle once."""
    changed: list[dict] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for entity in _com_text_candidates(doc):
        object_name = str(entity.ObjectName)
        if object_name not in _COM_TEXT_OBJECTS:
            continue
        handle = str(entity.Handle)
        if handle in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(handle)
        if layer and str(entity.Layer) != layer:
            continue
        current = str(entity.TextString or "")
        if not current or not pattern.search(current):
            continue
        updated = pattern.sub(replace, current)
        if not dry_run:
            entity.TextString = updated
        changed.append({"handle": handle, "type": object_name, "before": current, "after": updated})
    return changed


def _int_member(obj, name: str) -> int | None:
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Write the failing test for backlog row W4-T17-08**

Triage row `W4-T17-08` (`engineering/pid/spec.py`, owner `task-12`): `dry_run` crossings count the spec's own lines only, unlabelled. Create `tests/test_pid_spec_crossings_scope.py`:

```python
"""Backlog W4-T17-08: the dry run's crossings count what the real run counts.

``pid_from_spec(dry_run=True)`` counted each planned line against the spec's
own earlier lines only, and did not say so; the real run counts against every
P&ID line already on the current layout as well (``draw_line`` reads
``existing_pid_lines``). On a sheet that already carried a line the two totals
disagreed. Both now count in one scope and report it as ``crossings_scope``.

Oracle: the spec's one line runs (5, 0) -> (95, 0) (two ``dcs`` instruments of
radius 5 at x = 0 and x = 100, ``route: direct``); a P&ID line from (50, -20) to
(50, 20) crosses it once, at (50, 0), strictly inside both segments.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from engineering.pid.spec import run_spec

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

SPEC = {
    "instruments": [
        {"id": "I1", "type": "dcs", "location": "primary", "x": 0, "y": 0},
        {"id": "I2", "type": "dcs", "location": "primary", "x": 100, "y": 0},
    ],
    "lines": [{"from": "I1", "to": "I2", "class": "electric", "route": "direct", "label": False}],
}
SCOPE = "spec_lines+current_space_pid_lines"


async def test_the_dry_run_counts_a_crossing_with_a_line_already_drawn(backend):
    await backend.entity_create_polyline([[50, -20], [50, 20]], layer="UTILITY-LINE")

    dry = await run_spec(backend, SPEC, dry_run=True)

    assert dry["crossings_scope"] == SCOPE
    assert dry["lines"][0]["vertices"] == [[5.0, 0.0], [95.0, 0.0]]
    assert dry["crossings_total"] == 1


async def test_the_dry_run_and_the_real_run_agree(backend):
    await backend.entity_create_polyline([[50, -20], [50, 20]], layer="UTILITY-LINE")

    dry = await run_spec(backend, SPEC, dry_run=True)
    real = await run_spec(backend, SPEC)

    assert real["crossings_scope"] == dry["crossings_scope"] == SCOPE
    assert real["crossings_total"] == dry["crossings_total"] == 1


async def test_a_line_on_a_non_pid_layer_is_not_a_crossing(backend):
    await backend.entity_create_polyline([[50, -20], [50, 20]], layer="0")

    dry = await run_spec(backend, SPEC, dry_run=True)

    assert dry["crossings_total"] == 0
```

```python
async def _add_drawing_crossings(backend: AutoCADBackend, planned: list[dict]) -> None:
    """Add each planned line's crossings with the P&ID lines already drawn."""
    existing = await existing_pid_lines(backend)
    if not existing:
        return
    others = [line["vertices"] for line in existing]
    bulges = [line["bulges"] for line in existing]
    for line in planned:
        vertices = [(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in line["vertices"]]
        line["crossings"] += count_crossings(vertices, others, bulges=bulges)
```

- [ ] **Step 17: Write the failing `cad_batch` preview and apply tests**

Create `tests/test_change_preview_tools.py` with its header and the `cad_batch` section (Steps 22, 27 and 31 append the other three sections):

```python
"""Previews and guarded applies on the four tools (spec section 5, Task 12).

``cad_batch``, ``drawing_refine``, ``text_find_replace`` and ``pid_from_spec``
return a ``preview`` on ``dry_run=True`` and, given that preview's ``revision``
(``expected_revision``) and/or ``preconditions``, apply only if nothing moved:
otherwise the call is refused with kind ``stale_revision`` and nothing changes.

Review Focus 5: an entity edited between preview and apply - through the
server (the revision moves) and directly in the document (only the exact
fingerprint moves). Both are refused, and the drawing is left as it was.

Expected fingerprints are recomputed here from ezdxf's ``TagCollector``
(``_oracle``); the routing region is computed by hand from the catalogue box of
the ``dcs`` instrument, (-5, -5, 5, 5).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import pytest
import pytest_asyncio
from ezdxf.lldxf.tagwriter import TagCollector
from fastmcp import Client, Context

import server
from backends.ezdxf_backend import EzdxfBackend
from engineering.validation import SpecError

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _oracle(entity, dxfversion: str) -> str:
    collector = TagCollector(dxfversion=dxfversion)
    entity.export_dxf(collector)
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for tag in collector.tags:
        digest.update(f"{tag[0]}\x1f{tag[1]!r}\x1e".encode())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _fingerprint(backend, handle: str) -> str:
    doc = backend._doc
    return _oracle(doc.entitydb[handle], doc.dxfversion)


def _revision(backend) -> int:
    return backend.document_identity()["revision"]


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def served(monkeypatch):
    """An in-memory client whose lifespan serves THIS headless backend, so a test
    can edit the document directly, the way an operator or a second program would."""
    backend = EzdxfBackend()
    await backend.connect()
    await backend.drawing_new()

    async def _make():
        return backend

    monkeypatch.setattr(server, "_make_backend", _make)
    async with Client(server.mcp) as client:
        yield client, backend


async def _payload(client, tool: str, args: dict) -> dict:
    return (await client.call_tool(tool, args)).structured_content


async def _refused(client, tool: str, args: dict) -> dict:
    result = await client.call_tool(tool, args, raise_on_error=False)
    assert result.is_error is True, "a stale apply is an error result, never a quiet pass"
    payload = result.structured_content
    assert payload["ok"] is False and payload["kind"] == "stale_revision"
    assert payload["tool"] == tool
    return payload


def _move(handle: str, dx: float = 5.0) -> dict:
    return {"tool": "entity_move", "args": {"handle": handle, "dx": dx, "dy": 0.0}}


def _start(backend, handle: str) -> tuple[float, float]:
    start = backend._doc.entitydb[handle].dxf.start
    return (start.x, start.y)


# -- cad_batch ----------------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_batch_dry_run_fingerprints_only_the_literal_handles(served):
    client, backend = served
    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    circle = await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 50, 5)
    steps = [
        _move(line.handle),
        {"tool": "entity_create_line", "args": {"x1": 0, "y1": 9, "x2": 9, "y2": 9}, "bind": "new"},
        {"tool": "entity_move", "args": {"handle": "$new", "dx": 1, "dy": 0}},
        {
            "tool": "point_intersection",
            "args": {"handle1": line.handle.lower(), "handle2": circle.handle},
        },
    ]

    result = await _payload(client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "dry_run": True})

    preview = result["preview"]
    assert result["executed"] == 0
    assert preview["revision"] == _revision(backend)
    assert preview["document_id"] == backend.document_identity()["document_id"]
    assert preview["preconditions"] == [
        {"handle": h, "fingerprint": _fingerprint(backend, h)}
        for h in sorted([line.handle, circle.handle])
    ], "a $reference and an entity a step creates have no precondition"
    assert [row["i"] for row in preview["planned"]] == [0, 1, 2, 3]


async def test_a_batch_applies_its_preview_when_nothing_moved(served):
    client, backend = served
    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    steps = [_move(line.handle)]
    preview = (await _payload(client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "dry_run": True}))["preview"]

    result = await _payload(
        client,
        "cad_batch",
        {
            "steps": steps,
            "expected_revision": preview["revision"],
            "preconditions": preview["preconditions"],
        },
    )

    assert result["ok"] is True and result["succeeded"] == 1
    assert _start(backend, line.handle) == (5.0, 0.0)
    assert result["revision"]["checked"] == preview["revision"]
    assert result["revision"]["after"] == _revision(backend)
    assert result["revision"]["interleaved"] == []


async def test_review_focus_5_a_server_edit_refuses_the_batch(served):
    client, backend = served
    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    steps = [_move(line.handle)]
    preview = (await _payload(client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "dry_run": True}))["preview"]

    await client.call_tool("entity_set_properties", {"handle": line.handle, "color": 1})
    revision = _revision(backend)

    payload = await _refused(
        client,
        "cad_batch",
        {
            "steps": steps,
            "expected_revision": preview["revision"],
            "preconditions": preview["preconditions"],
        },
    )

    assert payload["expected_revision"] == preview["revision"]
    assert payload["actual_revision"] == revision != preview["revision"]
    assert [row["handle"] for row in payload["changed"]] == [line.handle]
    assert _start(backend, line.handle) == (0.0, 0.0), "no step ran"
    assert _revision(backend) == revision, "the refusal changed nothing"


async def test_review_focus_5_a_direct_edit_refuses_the_batch(served):
    """The revision cannot see an edit that bypassed the server; the exact
    fingerprint does. A rollback batch is refused before its checkpoint opens."""
    client, backend = served
    line = await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    steps = [_move(line.handle)]
    preview = (await _payload(client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "dry_run": True}))["preview"]

    backend._doc.entitydb[line.handle].dxf.color = 5

    payload = await _refused(
        client,
        "cad_batch",
        {
            "steps": steps,
            "on_error": "rollback",
            "expected_revision": preview["revision"],
            "preconditions": preview["preconditions"],
        },
    )

    assert payload["expected_revision"] == payload["actual_revision"] == preview["revision"]
    (row,) = payload["changed"]
    assert row == {
        "handle": line.handle,
        "expected": preview["preconditions"][0]["fingerprint"],
        "actual": _fingerprint(backend, line.handle),
    }
    assert _start(backend, line.handle) == (0.0, 0.0)
    assert (await backend.system_status())["transaction_depth"] == 0, "no checkpoint opened"


async def test_a_change_between_two_steps_is_reported_as_interleaved(served, monkeypatch):
    """Each step is its own locked call. ``cad_batch`` awaits
    ``ctx.report_progress`` right before each step, so a call that lands there
    is exactly another client's change between two of ours."""
    client, backend = served
    steps = [
        {"tool": "entity_create_line", "args": {"x1": 0, "y1": 0, "x2": 1, "y2": 0}},
        {"tool": "entity_create_line", "args": {"x1": 0, "y1": 1, "x2": 1, "y2": 1}},
    ]
    preview = (await _payload(client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "dry_run": True}))["preview"]
    original = Context.report_progress

    async def _between(self, progress, total=None, message=None):
        if progress == 1:
            await backend.entity_create_circle(500, 500, 1)  # somebody else's call
        return await original(self, progress, total, message)

    monkeypatch.setattr(Context, "report_progress", _between)

    result = await _payload(
        client, "cad_batch", {"steps": steps, "expected_revision": preview["revision"]}
    )

    assert result["ok"] is True and result["succeeded"] == 2
    (row,) = result["revision"]["interleaved"]
    assert row["before"] == "step 1 (entity_create_line)"
    assert row["actual"] > row["expected"]
    assert result["revision"]["after"] == _revision(backend)


```

```python
def _batch_stale_of(exc: BaseException) -> dict | None:
    """The ``StaleRevisionError.to_dict()`` refusal ``exc`` carries, or None.

    By type, like the quarantine: a preview whose precondition no longer holds
    is neither an engine boundary nor a failure to act - the caller re-runs the
    dry run and applies its new preview (spec section 5, Task 12).
    """
    from engineering.changes.preview import StaleRevisionError

    for error in _batch_cause_chain(exc):
        if isinstance(error, StaleRevisionError):
            return error.to_dict()
    return None
```

Edit 1 - in `server.py`, replace:

```python
# ── schema validation (dry_run, and the pre-flight of every real run) ───────
```

with:

```python
#: An argument whose name says it carries entity handles: ``handle``,
#: ``handles``, ``handle1`` / ``handle2``, ``target_handle``,
#: ``overlay_handles``, ``circle_handle``, ... (every handle parameter the
#: registered tools declare).
_BATCH_HANDLE_ARG = re.compile(r"^(?:[a-z0-9_]+_)?handles?\d*$")


def _batch_literal_handles(args: dict) -> list[str]:
    """The literal entity handles in a step's handle parameters, upper-cased.

    A ``$reference`` is skipped: it resolves only when the step that binds it
    has run, so a dry run has nothing to fingerprint for it - and an entity a
    step creates does not exist yet.
    """
    found: list[str] = []
    for key, value in args.items():
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not _BATCH_HANDLE_ARG.match(key):
            continue
        for item in value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]:
            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() and not item.startswith(_BATCH_REF_SIGIL):
                found.append(item.strip().upper())
    return found


# ── schema validation (dry_run, and the pre-flight of every real run) ───────
```

```python
    ERRORS are typed, never text: each failed step carries error.kind - one of
```

```python
    PREVIEW AND APPLY. dry_run=True also returns `preview`: the document id and
    content revision, the exact fingerprint of every LITERAL handle in a handle
    parameter (a '$reference' resolves late and an entity a step creates has
    none) and the planned steps. Pass its `revision` as expected_revision and
    its `preconditions` back, and the batch runs only if nothing moved -
    otherwise it is refused with kind stale_revision and no step runs. A
    guarded batch reports `revision`: {checked, after, interleaved}; each step
    is its own locked call, so a change another call made between two steps is
    listed under `interleaved` (a rollback restores the checkpoint, that change
    included).
```

```python
@dataclass
class RefineResult:
    status: str
    initial_score: float
    final_score: float
    rounds: list[RefineRound]
    remaining_issues: list[dict]
    #: A dry run's ChangePreview (``ChangePreview.to_dict()``).
    preview: dict | None = None
    #: A dry run's answer to "would an apply at this min_score stop at once?".
    threshold_met: bool | None = None
    #: A guarded apply's ``RevisionWatch.report()``.
    revision: dict | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        payload = {
            "status": self.status,
            "initial_score": self.initial_score,
            "final_score": self.final_score,
            "rounds": [item.to_dict() for item in self.rounds],
            "remaining_issues": list(self.remaining_issues),
        }
        for key in ("preview", "threshold_met", "revision"):
            value = getattr(self, key)
            if value is not None:
                payload[key] = value
        return payload
```

```python
    *,
```

```python
) -> RefineResult:
    """Apply deterministic repairs without allowing quality to regress.

    ``dry_run`` returns the plan and its ChangePreview (``preview``: the
    revision and the exact fingerprints of every handle a planned repair
    touches) - also when the score already meets ``min_score``, which
    ``threshold_met`` then says. An apply given ``expected_revision`` and/or
    ``preconditions`` (a dry run's ``preview``) checks them before the first
    critique and raises ``StaleRevisionError`` (kind ``stale_revision``) with
    nothing changed when the document moved; its result carries ``revision``,
    the RevisionWatch report of changes interleaved between its own calls. A
    dry run ignores both.
    """
    max_rounds = max(1, min(3, int(max_rounds)))
    min_score = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(min_score)))
    registry = registry or RepairRegistry(defaults=True)
    allowed = set(allowed_repairs) if allowed_repairs is not None else None
    watch: RevisionWatch | None = None
    if not dry_run and (expected_revision is not None or preconditions is not None):
        await check_preconditions(
            backend, expected_revision=expected_revision, preconditions=preconditions
        )
        watch = await RevisionWatch.begin(backend)

    issues = await backend.drawing_critique(focus)
    initial_score, _initial_errors = _metrics(issues)
    rounds: list[RefineRound] = []
```

```python
    exact fingerprint of every handle a planned repair touches - also when the
    score already meets min_score: `threshold_met` then says an apply at that
    min_score would stop before repairing. Applied with the preview's

        _backend(ctx),
        max_rounds=max_rounds,
        min_score=min_score,
        focus=focus,
        allowed_repairs=allowed_repairs,
        dry_run=dry_run,
        expected_revision=expected_revision,
        preconditions=preconditions,
    )
    return result.to_dict()
```

```python
@cad_tool(summary="Find and replace text across the drawing.", cost="mutate")
@mcp.tool(
    annotations={"title": "Find and Replace Text", "destructiveHint": False},
    tags={"entity", "modify"},
)
async def text_find_replace(
    find: Annotated[str, "Literal text to search for (not a regex)."],
    replace: Annotated[str, "Replacement text."],
    layer: Annotated[str, "Restrict to one layer. Empty searches every layer."] = "",
    match_case: Annotated[bool, "Case-sensitive search."] = True,
    dry_run: Annotated[bool, "Report what would change without changing it."] = False,
    ctx: Context = None,
) -> dict:
    """Replace text in TEXT, MTEXT and block attributes (ATTRIB and ATTDEF).

    `searched_types` is on the response because "no matches" and "that type was
    never searched" are different answers. Block *definitions* are included, so
    the next insert does not reintroduce the old text. DIMENSION text is out of
    scope: its text field holds the `<>` override placeholder rather than the
    measurement, so editing it would break the association. A multi-line
    attribute is matched and rewritten as the whole value (`Line1\\PLine2`),
    the same value `block_get_attributes` reports and `block_explode` bursts.
    """
    await ctx.info(f"Replacing {find!r} with {replace!r}{' (dry run)' if dry_run else ''}")
    return await _backend(ctx).text_find_replace(find, replace, layer or None, match_case, dry_run)
```

with:

```python
async def _guarded_text_find_replace(
    backend, find, replace, layer, match_case, expected_revision, preconditions
) -> dict:
    """``text_find_replace`` applied only if its preview still holds (Task 12).

    The preconditions are checked, then the search runs again as a dry run: a
    match the preview did not list (a text added since) refuses the apply as
    surely as an edited one, because a precondition vouches only for the
    entities it names. Search and apply are two locked calls; a change another
    call makes before either refuses too, before anything is written.
    """
    from engineering.changes.preview import (
        RevisionWatch,
        StaleRevisionError,
        check_preconditions,
        parse_preconditions,
    )

    rows = parse_preconditions(preconditions)
    await check_preconditions(backend, expected_revision=expected_revision, preconditions=rows)
    watch = await RevisionWatch.begin(backend)

    async def _settled(label: str) -> None:
        if await watch.before(f"text_find_replace: {label}"):
            raise StaleRevisionError(
                "stale_revision: another call changed the document after the preconditions "
                "were checked; nothing was changed. Run the dry run again and apply its new "
                "preview.",
                expected=expected_revision,
                actual=watch.last,
            )

    await _settled("search")
    probe = await backend.text_find_replace(find, replace, layer, match_case, True)
    await watch.after()
    if not probe.get("ok"):
        return probe
    if preconditions is not None:
        listed = {row["handle"] for row in rows if "handle" in row}
        unlisted = sorted({str(row["handle"]).upper() for row in probe["entities"]} - listed)
        if unlisted:
            raise StaleRevisionError(
                f"stale_revision: {len(unlisted)} entity(ies) now match {find!r} that the "
                f"preview did not list ({', '.join(unlisted[:5])}); nothing was changed. Run "
                "the dry run again and apply its new preview.",
                expected=expected_revision,
                actual=watch.last,
                changed=[
                    {"handle": h, "expected": "not in the preview", "actual": "matches"}
                    for h in unlisted
                ],
            )
    await _settled("apply")
    result = await backend.text_find_replace(find, replace, layer, match_case, False)
    await watch.after()
    return {**result, "revision": watch.report()}


@cad_tool(summary="Find and replace text across the drawing.", cost="mutate")
@mcp.tool(
    annotations={"title": "Find and Replace Text", "destructiveHint": False},
    tags={"entity", "modify"},
)
async def text_find_replace(
    find: Annotated[str, "Literal text to search for (not a regex)."],
    replace: Annotated[str, "Replacement text."],
    layer: Annotated[str, "Restrict to one layer. Empty searches every layer."] = "",
    match_case: Annotated[bool, "Case-sensitive search."] = True,
    dry_run: Annotated[
        bool, "Report what would change, with a `preview` to apply it by; change nothing."
    ] = False,
    expected_revision: Annotated[
        int | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            ge=0,
            description=(
                "Apply only at this content revision (a dry run's `preview.revision`); "
                "otherwise refused stale_revision and nothing is replaced."
            ),
        ),
    ] = None,
    preconditions: Annotated[
        list[dict] | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description=(
                "A dry run's `preview.preconditions` ({handle, fingerprint} per match): apply "
                "only if each is unchanged and nothing else now matches; otherwise refused "
                "stale_revision and nothing is replaced."
            ),
        ),
    ] = None,
    ctx: Context = None,
) -> dict:
    """Replace text in TEXT, MTEXT and block attributes (ATTRIB and ATTDEF).

    Both engines search the same places (`searched_scope`): model space, every
    paper layout, every block definition - so the next insert does not
    reintroduce the old text - and the attributes of every block reference in
    any of them; an xref's block holds another file's text and is not searched.
    `searched_types` is on the response because "no matches" and "that type was
    never searched" are different answers. DIMENSION text is out of
    scope: its text field holds the `<>` override placeholder rather than the
    measurement, so editing it would break the association. A multi-line
    attribute is matched and rewritten as the whole value (`Line1\\PLine2`),
    the same value `block_get_attributes` reports and `block_explode` bursts.

    dry_run=True adds `preview`: the document's content revision, the exact
    fingerprint of every matched entity and the planned rows. Applied with that
    `revision` (expected_revision) and/or its `preconditions`, the search runs
    again first: an edited entity, a moved revision or a new match the preview
    did not list refuses the call with kind stale_revision and nothing is
    replaced. A guarded apply reports `revision` ({checked, after, interleaved}).
    """
    await ctx.info(f"Replacing {find!r} with {replace!r}{' (dry run)' if dry_run else ''}")
    backend = _backend(ctx)
    if dry_run:
        from engineering.changes.preview import make_preview

        result = await backend.text_find_replace(find, replace, layer or None, match_case, True)
        if not result.get("ok"):
            return result
        rows = list(result.get("entities") or [])
        preview = await make_preview(backend, handles=[row["handle"] for row in rows], planned=rows)
        return {**result, "preview": preview.to_dict()}
    if expected_revision is None and preconditions is None:
        return await backend.text_find_replace(find, replace, layer or None, match_case, False)
    return await _guarded_text_find_replace(
        backend, find, replace, layer or None, match_case, expected_revision, preconditions
    )
```

```python
def routing_region(norm: dict, planned: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, float, float, float] | None:
    """The WCS box a spec draws in: every item's catalogue box (``SymbolSpec.bbox``)
    placed at its insertion point, rotation and scale, and every planned line
    vertex. None for a spec with neither. ``pid_from_spec``'s region
    precondition fingerprints what lies in it."""
    xs: list[float] = []
    ys: list[float] = []
    for section in _SECTIONS:
        for item in norm[section]:
            x0, y0, x1, y1 = _spec_for(section, item).bbox
            angle = math.radians(float(item.get("rotation", 0.0)))
            cos_a, sin_a = math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)
            scale = float(item.get("scale", 1.0))
            for px, py in ((x0, y0), (x1, y0), (x1, y1), (x0, y1)):
                xs.append(item["x"] + scale * (px * cos_a - py * sin_a))
                ys.append(item["y"] + scale * (px * sin_a + py * cos_a))
    for line in planned:
        for vx, vy in line["vertices"]:
            xs.append(float(vx))
            ys.append(float(vy))
    if not xs:
        return None
    return (min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys))


def _planned_rows(norm: dict, planned: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """The preview's planned edits: one row per item placed, one per line drawn."""
    rows = [
        {"where": f"{section}[{i}]", "id": item["id"]}
        for section in _SECTIONS
        for i, item in enumerate(norm[section])
    ]
    rows.extend(
        {"where": line["where"], "from": line["from"], "to": line["to"], "class": line["class"]}
        for line in planned
    )
    return rows


def _match_region_rows(preconditions, region) -> None:
    """A region precondition must name this spec's routing region: one taken
    from another spec's preview would vouch for the wrong part of the sheet."""
    for index, row in enumerate(parse_preconditions(preconditions)):
        if "region" in row and (region is None or tuple(row["region"]) != tuple(region)):
            raise SpecError(
                f"preconditions[{index}].region: {row['region']} is not this spec's routing "
                f"region {None if region is None else list(region)}; take the dry run of "
                "this spec and apply its preview"
            )


async def _place(backend: AutoCADBackend, section: str, item: dict) -> dict:
```

```python
    ``dry_run`` routes from catalogue ports in memory and touches nothing.

```

with:

```python
async def run_spec(
    backend: AutoCADBackend,
    spec: dict,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    *,
    expected_revision: int | None = None,
    preconditions: list[dict] | None = None,
) -> dict:
    """Validate, then place every item and draw every line inside one transaction.

    ``dry_run`` routes from catalogue ports in memory and changes nothing; it
    reads the P&ID lines already on the current layout (``crossings_scope``)
    and returns a ChangePreview whose precondition is the fingerprint of the
    routing region (``routing_region``). The real run given
    ``expected_revision`` and/or ``preconditions`` (that preview) checks them
    before anything is planned or placed and raises ``StaleRevisionError``
    (kind ``stale_revision``) with nothing drawn when the document moved; its
    result then carries ``revision``, the RevisionWatch report.
    """
    norm = validate_spec(spec)
    if dry_run:
        planned = _plan_lines(norm)
        await _add_drawing_crossings(backend, planned)
        region = routing_region(norm, planned)
        preview = await make_preview(backend, region=region, planned=_planned_rows(norm, planned))
        return {
            "dry_run": True,
            "items": sum(len(norm[s]) for s in _SECTIONS),
            "lines": planned,
            "crossings_total": sum(p["crossings"] for p in planned),
            "crossings_scope": CROSSINGS_SCOPE,
            "region": None if region is None else list(region),
            "preview": preview.to_dict(),
        }
```

```python
                    placed = await _place(backend, section, item)
                except ValueError as exc:
                    raise ValueError(f"{section}[{i}]: {exc}") from exc
                if watch is not None:
                    await watch.after()
                handles[item["id"]] = placed["handle"]
```

with:

```python
    dangling ends or duplicate tags in the same round trip. `dry_run` returns
    the planned vertices and crossings without touching the drawing; crossings
    are counted, on both runs, against the spec's own lines and the P&ID lines
    already on the current layout (`crossings_scope`). The dry run also returns
    the routing `region` and a `preview` whose precondition is the fingerprint
    of every entity in that region. Applied with the preview's `revision`
    (expected_revision) and/or `preconditions`, the run is refused with kind
    stale_revision and nothing drawn when anything in the region (or the
    revision) changed; a region precondition taken from another spec is
    refused by path. A guarded run reports `revision` ({checked, after,
    interleaved}).
    """
    from engineering.pid.spec import run_spec

    await ctx.info("P&ID from spec" + (" (dry run)" if dry_run else ""))
    return await run_spec(
        _backend(ctx),
        spec,
        dry_run,
        expected_revision=expected_revision,
        preconditions=preconditions,
    )
```

**Files:**
- Create: `backends/entity_fields.py` (HATCH loops, DIMENSION fields, `ACADMCP_*` XDATA: one reader for both engines)
- Create: `engineering/verification/observe.py` (`observe()`: WCS features, exploded INSERTs, units)
- Modify: `engineering/understand/snapshot.py` (eight `EntityRecord` fields with defaults; `Snapshot.blocks`; `block_defs=` on `records_from_doc` / `_read` / `read_snapshot` / `take_snapshot`)
- Modify: `engineering/verification/types.py` (`ObservedFeature.type` gains `"spline"` and `"dimension"`), `tests/test_verification_types.py` (Task 11's `FEATURE_TYPES` pin moves with it)
- Modify: `backends/ezdxf_backend.py` (`_nesting_depths` moves to `entity_fields`; `_entity_info_dxf(..., detail=)` HATCH loops and DIMENSION fields; INSERT `mirrored` only on a flat frame; `hatch_loops` capability; `ocs_normalized` reason)
- Modify: `backends/com_backend.py` (`entity_get` reads HATCH loops / DIMENSION fields from the measured `Document.Export`; heavy 2D polyline stride 3; INSERT `mirrored` only on a flat frame; `hatch_loops` capability; `ocs_normalized` reason)
- Modify: `server.py` (`entity_get` docstring, SECTION 5 - banner count unchanged)
- Test: `tests/test_entity_fields.py`, `tests/test_snapshot_observed_fields.py`, `tests/test_verification_observe.py`, `tests/test_entity_get_detail.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `engineering/verification/types.py` (Task 11): `ObservedFeature(handle, type, space, layer, block_path, geometry, tags=(), approximation=None)`, `ObservedGeometry(features, declared_unit, inferred_unit, document_id, revision, source)`. W1 (landed): `EntityRecord.visible: bool = True`; `records_from_doc(doc, *, source, insert_boxes=True)`; `_read(path, *, source, insert_boxes=True)`; `take_snapshot(backend, path=None)` reading the headless document with `backend._async(..., readonly=True)`; `engineering/qa_scan.py` calls `_read(written, source=source, insert_boxes=False)`. Task 9: `document_identity() -> {"document_id", "revision", "name", "path", "backend"}`.
- Produces:
  - `EntityRecord` gains (all defaulted, hashable): `tilted: bool = False`, `ellipse: tuple | None = None` (`(centre, major_axis, ratio, start_param, end_param)`, counter-clockwise seen from +Z), `spline: tuple | None = None` (`(degree, control_points, fit_points, closed)`), `hatch_loops: tuple = ()` (per loop `(kind, flags, depth, vertices_or_edges, bulges)`), `hatch_style: int = 0`, `dimension: tuple | None = None` (`(dimtype, defpoints, measurement, text_override, dimstyle, dimlfac, angle)`), `transform: tuple | None = None` (INSERT affine `(a, b, c, d, e, f)`: `x' = a*x + c*y + e`, `y' = b*x + d*y + f`), `xdata: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()`.
  - `Snapshot.blocks: dict | None = None`; `records_from_doc(doc, *, source, insert_boxes=True, block_defs=False)`; `read_snapshot(path, *, block_defs=False)`; `take_snapshot(backend, path=None, *, block_defs=False)`.
  - `backends/entity_fields.py`: `hatch_loops(entity) -> tuple`, `loop_ring(loop) -> tuple[list[tuple[float, float, float]], bool]`, `nesting_depths(rings) -> list[int]`, `filled_area(loops, style=0) -> float | None`, `hatch_props(loops, style=0) -> dict`, `dimension_fields(entity) -> tuple`, `dimension_props(fields) -> dict`, `acadmcp_xdata(entity) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]`, `HATCH_STYLE_NAMES`, `DIMTYPE_NAMES`.
  - `engineering/verification/observe.py`: `observe(snap, *, explode_inserts: bool = True, document: dict | None = None) -> ObservedGeometry`; constants `TILTED = "tilted_plane"`, `NON_UNIFORM`, `NON_UNIFORM_BULGE`, `MAX_NESTING = 64`. Feature geometry keys: line `start`, `end`; circle `center`, `radius`; arc `center`, `radius`, `start_angle`, `end_angle` (degrees, counter-clockwise); ellipse `center`, `major_axis`, `ratio`, `start_param`, `end_param`; polyline `points`, `bulges`, `closed`; point `location`; spline `degree`, `control_points`, `fit_points`, `closed`; dimension `dimtype`, `defpoints`, `measurement`, `text_override`, `dimstyle`, `dimlfac`, `angle`, `text_midpoint`.
  - `entity_get` (both engines): a HATCH adds `hatch_style`, `loop_count`, `loops`, `area`, `area_exact`; a DIMENSION adds `dimension` (`dimension_props`); COM adds `detail_source: "dxf_export"`.
  - Deviations recorded against the skeleton: `EntityRecord.dimension` is a 7-tuple (`angle`, the DXF rotation a linear dimension measures along, is appended after `dimlfac`; Tasks 14 and 16 unpack seven fields); `EntityRecord` gains two fields the skeleton did not list, `hatch_style` and `transform`, both defaulted; `ObservedFeature.type` gains `"spline"` and `"dimension"` (Step 12); `observe(..., explode_inserts=True)` refuses a snapshot that holds an INSERT but was read without `block_defs=True`, so every observing caller (Tasks 14-17) reads with `block_defs=True`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test for the shared field reader**

Create `tests/test_entity_fields.py`:

```python
"""backends/entity_fields.py: HATCH loops, DIMENSION fields and ACADMCP XDATA (B16).

Every expected number is hand geometry: squares, a semicircle, a full circle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math

import ezdxf
import pytest

from backends.entity_fields import (
    acadmcp_xdata,
    dimension_fields,
    dimension_props,
    filled_area,
    hatch_loops,
    hatch_props,
    loop_ring,
    nesting_depths,
)


def _msp():
    doc = ezdxf.new()
    return doc, doc.modelspace()


def test_three_nested_squares_have_depths_0_1_2_and_fill_by_island_style():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path([(0, 0), (20, 0), (20, 20), (0, 20)], is_closed=True, flags=1)
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path([(4, 4), (16, 4), (16, 16), (4, 16)], is_closed=True, flags=0)
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path([(8, 8), (12, 8), (12, 12), (8, 12)], is_closed=True, flags=0)
    loops = hatch_loops(hatch)
    assert [loop[2] for loop in loops] == [0, 1, 2]
    # normal: 20x20 - 12x12 + 4x4; outer: 20x20 - 12x12; ignore: 20x20
    assert filled_area(loops, 0) == pytest.approx(400.0 - 144.0 + 16.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert filled_area(loops, 1) == pytest.approx(400.0 - 144.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert filled_area(loops, 2) == pytest.approx(400.0, abs=1e-9)


def test_a_bulged_outer_loop_and_a_full_circle_island_are_exact():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    # 20 x 10 rectangle whose right edge bows out as a semicircle of radius 5
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(
        [(0, 0, 0), (20, 0, 1.0), (20, 10, 0), (0, 10, 0)], is_closed=True, flags=1
    )
    hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=16).add_arc((5, 5), 2, 0, 360)
    loops = hatch_loops(hatch)
    outer, island = loops
    assert outer[0] == "polyline" and outer[2] == 0
    assert outer[4] == (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
    assert island[0] == "edges" and island[2] == 1
    ring, exact = loop_ring(island)
    assert exact is True
    assert ring == [(7.0, 5.0, 1.0), (3.0, 5.0, 1.0)]  # two semicircles
    expected = 200.0 + 12.5 * math.pi - 4.0 * math.pi
    assert filled_area(loops, 0) == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-9)


def test_an_edge_loop_of_lines_and_a_ccw_arc_is_a_bulge_ring():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    edges = hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=1)
    edges.add_line((30, 0), (40, 0))
    edges.add_arc((40, 5), 5, 270, 90, ccw=True)
    edges.add_line((40, 10), (30, 10))
    edges.add_line((30, 10), (30, 0))
    (loop,) = hatch_loops(hatch)
    ring, exact = loop_ring(loop)
    assert exact is True
    assert [(x, y) for x, y, _b in ring] == [(30.0, 0.0), (40.0, 0.0), (40.0, 10.0), (30.0, 10.0)]
    assert ring[1][2] == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-12)  # tan(180 deg / 4)
    assert filled_area((loop,), 0) == pytest.approx(100.0 + 12.5 * math.pi, abs=1e-9)


def test_a_mirrored_hatch_reports_wcs_loops_and_the_same_area():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    hatch.dxf.extrusion = (0.0, 0.0, -1.0)  # OCS x -> WCS -x
    edges = hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=1)
    edges.add_line((30, 0), (40, 0))
    edges.add_arc((40, 5), 5, 270, 90, ccw=True)
    edges.add_line((40, 10), (30, 10))
    edges.add_line((30, 10), (30, 0))
    (loop,) = hatch_loops(hatch)
    first, arc = loop[3][0], loop[3][1]
    assert first == ("line", (-30.0, 0.0), (-40.0, 0.0))
    # the arc's WCS image runs counter-clockwise 90 -> 270 and is walked clockwise
    assert arc == ("arc", (-40.0, 5.0), 5.0, 90.0, 270.0, False)
    assert filled_area((loop,), 0) == pytest.approx(100.0 + 12.5 * math.pi, abs=1e-9)


def test_a_clockwise_arc_edge_gets_a_negative_bulge():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    edges = hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=1)
    edges.add_arc((0, 0), 10, 0, 90, ccw=False)  # walked from 90 deg back to 0 deg
    edges.add_line((10, 0), (0, 0))
    edges.add_line((0, 0), (0, 10))
    (loop,) = hatch_loops(hatch)
    ring, _exact = loop_ring(loop)
    assert ring[0][0] == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12)
    assert ring[0][1] == pytest.approx(10.0, abs=1e-12)
    assert ring[0][2] == pytest.approx(-math.tan(math.radians(90.0) / 4.0), abs=1e-12)
    # quarter disc of radius 10
    assert filled_area((loop,), 0) == pytest.approx(25.0 * math.pi, abs=1e-9)


def test_an_ellipse_edge_is_sampled_and_says_it_is_not_exact():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=1).add_ellipse((0, 0), (10, 0), 0.5)
    (loop,) = hatch_loops(hatch)
    _ring, exact = loop_ring(loop)
    assert exact is False
    assert filled_area((loop,), 0) is None
    props = hatch_props((loop,), 0)
    assert props["area"] is None and props["area_exact"] is False
    assert props["loops"][0]["edges"][0]["type"] == "ellipse"


def test_nesting_depths_skip_loops_whose_boxes_do_not_contain():
    left = [(0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0), (10.0, 10.0), (0.0, 10.0)]
    right = [(20.0, 0.0), (30.0, 0.0), (30.0, 10.0), (20.0, 10.0)]
    inner = [(22.0, 2.0), (28.0, 2.0), (28.0, 8.0), (22.0, 8.0)]
    assert nesting_depths([left, right, inner]) == [0, 0, 1]


def test_hatch_props_names_every_field_entity_get_returns():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path([(0, 0), (10, 0), (10, 10), (0, 10)], is_closed=True, flags=1)
    props = hatch_props(hatch_loops(hatch), 0)
    assert props["hatch_style"] == "normal"
    assert props["loop_count"] == 1
    assert props["area"] == pytest.approx(100.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert props["area_exact"] is True
    row = props["loops"][0]
    assert row["kind"] == "polyline" and row["external"] is True and row["outermost"] is False
    assert row["depth"] == 0
    assert row["points"] == [[0.0, 0.0], [10.0, 0.0], [10.0, 10.0], [0.0, 10.0]]
    assert row["bulges"] == [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    assert row["area"] == pytest.approx(100.0, abs=1e-9)


def test_dimension_fields_keep_measurement_override_style_and_dimlfac_apart():
    doc, msp = _msp()
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(
        base=(0, 5), p1=(0, 0), p2=(42, 0), dimstyle="Standard", override={"dimlfac": 2.0}
    )
    dim.render()
    dim.dimension.dxf.text = "40"
    fields = dimension_fields(dim.dimension)
    assert fields == (
        0,
        ((0.0, 5.0), (0.0, 0.0), (42.0, 0.0), None, None),
        42.0,
        "40",
        "Standard",
        2.0,
        0.0,
    )
    props = dimension_props(fields)
    assert props["dimtype"] == "linear"
    assert props["text_override"] == "40"
    assert props["defpoints"][2] == [42.0, 0.0]
    assert props["defpoints"][3] is None


def test_dimlfac_falls_back_to_the_style_value():
    doc, msp = _msp()
    doc.dimstyles.get("Standard").dxf.dimlfac = 3.0
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(base=(0, 5), p1=(0, 0), p2=(10, 0), dimstyle="Standard")
    dim.render()
    assert dimension_fields(dim.dimension)[5] == 3.0


def test_a_diameter_dimension_keeps_its_two_chord_points():
    _doc, msp = _msp()
    dim = msp.add_diameter_dim(center=(0, 0), radius=5, angle=0, dimstyle="Standard")
    dim.render()
    dimtype, defpoints, measurement, override, _style, dimlfac, _angle = dimension_fields(
        dim.dimension
    )
    assert dimtype == 3
    assert defpoints[0] == (5.0, 0.0) and defpoints[3] == (-5.0, 0.0)
    assert measurement == 10.0 and override == "<>" and dimlfac == 1.0
    assert dimension_props(dimension_fields(dim.dimension))["text_override"] is None


def test_acadmcp_xdata_keeps_our_applications_only():
    doc, msp = _msp()
    for app in ("ACADMCP_MECH", "ACADMCP_ARCH_H", "OTHER_APP"):
        doc.appids.add(app)
    line = msp.add_line((0, 0), (1, 1))
    payload = {"v": 1, "kind": "feature", "part": "1A", "feature": "h1", "view": "front"}
    line.set_xdata("ACADMCP_MECH", [(1000, json.dumps(payload))])
    line.set_xdata("ACADMCP_ARCH_H", [(1000, "31"), (1000, "32")])  # joins to the number 3132
    line.set_xdata("OTHER_APP", [(1000, "x")])
    rows = dict(acadmcp_xdata(line))
    assert set(rows) == {"ACADMCP_MECH", "ACADMCP_ARCH_H"}
    assert json.loads(rows["ACADMCP_MECH"]) == payload
    assert json.loads(rows["ACADMCP_ARCH_H"]) == ["31", "32"]
    assert acadmcp_xdata(msp.add_line((0, 0), (2, 2))) == ()
```

```python
"""Exact HATCH loops, DIMENSION fields and ACADMCP_* XDATA, read once for both engines.

Both engines answer ``entity_get`` for a HATCH or a DIMENSION from this one
reader: the headless engine runs it on the live ``ezdxf`` entity, the live
engine on the record its measured ``Document.Export`` snapshot carries
(``engineering/understand/snapshot.py``). One reader, so the two engines
cannot disagree about a loop or a defpoint (v1.7.0, B16).

* A loop is ``(kind, flags, depth, vertices_or_edges, bulges)`` in WCS.
  ``kind`` "polyline" carries ``((x, y), ...)`` and the parallel bulges;
  ``kind`` "edges" carries edge tuples and no bulges -
  ``("line", start, end)``, ``("arc", centre, radius, start_deg, end_deg,
  ccw)`` (the arc is the counter-clockwise one from start to end; ``ccw`` is
  the direction the loop walks it), ``("ellipse", centre, major_axis, ratio,
  start_param, end_param, ccw)`` and ``("spline", degree, control_points)``.
* ``depth`` is how many other loops a loop sits inside, read off containment
  - the boundary-path flags mark a path external or outermost, never "two
  levels down", and depth is exactly where AutoCAD's three island styles stop
  agreeing. Hatch loops do not cross, so one vertex of the inner loop that is
  not on the other loop's edge settles it; a box test runs first, so a hatch
  with many loops does not pay a point-in-polygon walk per pair.
* A loop of lines and arcs is exact: its ring (vertices with bulges) IS the
  boundary and its area is closed-form (``engineering.measure``). An ellipse
  edge is sampled and a spline edge is its control polygon; such a loop says
  ``exact: False`` and reports no area.
* A mirrored frame (extrusion ``(0, 0, -1)``) reflects x: every point and
  arc is translated with ``backends/ocs.py``, and the walking direction of an
  arc or ellipse edge flips.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math

from backends.ocs import is_flat_frame, to_wcs_2d, wcs_arc_angles, wcs_bulge
from engineering.measure import polygon_area_perimeter

__all__ = [
    "DIMTYPE_NAMES",
    "HATCH_STYLE_NAMES",
    "acadmcp_xdata",
    "dimension_fields",
    "dimension_props",
    "filled_area",
    "hatch_loops",
    "hatch_props",
    "loop_ring",
    "nesting_depths",
]

#: AutoCAD's HPISLANDDETECTION, by the code DXF stores in group 75.
HATCH_STYLE_NAMES = {0: "normal", 1: "outer", 2: "ignore"}
#: DXF group 70's low bits (the flags 32 / 64 / 128 are masked off).
DIMTYPE_NAMES = {
    0: "linear",
    1: "aligned",
    2: "angular",
    3: "diameter",
    4: "radius",
    5: "angular_3p",
    6: "ordinate",
}
#: Samples per full turn of an ellipse edge (its loop then says it is not exact).
_ELLIPSE_SAMPLES = 72
_TAU = 2.0 * math.pi


def _xy(point) -> tuple[float, float]:
    return (float(point[0]), float(point[1]))


def _ocs_point(extrusion, x, y, z) -> tuple[float, float]:
    wx, wy = to_wcs_2d(extrusion, float(x), float(y), float(z))
    return (float(wx), float(wy))


def _edge(edge, extrusion, elevation: float, mirrored: bool) -> tuple:
    name = edge.type.name.lower()
    if name == "line":
        return (
            "line",
            _ocs_point(extrusion, edge.start[0], edge.start[1], elevation),
            _ocs_point(extrusion, edge.end[0], edge.end[1], elevation),
        )
    if name == "arc":
        centre = (float(edge.center[0]), float(edge.center[1]), elevation)
        start, end = wcs_arc_angles(
            extrusion, centre, float(edge.radius), float(edge.start_angle), float(edge.end_angle)
        )
        return (
            "arc",
            _ocs_point(extrusion, *centre),
            float(edge.radius),
            float(start),
            float(end),
            bool(edge.ccw) != mirrored,
        )
    if name == "ellipse":
        centre = _ocs_point(extrusion, edge.center[0], edge.center[1], elevation)
        tip = _ocs_point(
            extrusion,
            edge.center[0] + edge.major_axis[0],
            edge.center[1] + edge.major_axis[1],
            elevation,
        )
        major = (tip[0] - centre[0], tip[1] - centre[1])
        t0, t1 = float(edge.start_param), float(edge.end_param)
        if mirrored:  # the minor axis flips, so the parameter runs the other way
            t0, t1 = (-t1) % _TAU, (-t0) % _TAU
        return ("ellipse", centre, major, float(edge.ratio), t0, t1, bool(edge.ccw) != mirrored)
    points = tuple(_ocs_point(extrusion, p[0], p[1], elevation) for p in edge.control_points)
    return ("spline", int(edge.degree), points)


def _arc_point(centre, radius, deg) -> tuple[float, float]:
    rad = math.radians(deg)
    return (centre[0] + radius * math.cos(rad), centre[1] + radius * math.sin(rad))


def _edges_ring(edges) -> tuple[list[tuple[float, float, float]], bool]:
    ring: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = []
    exact = True
    for edge in edges:
        kind = edge[0]
        if kind == "line":
            ring.append((edge[1][0], edge[1][1], 0.0))
        elif kind == "arc":
            _kind, centre, radius, start, end, ccw = edge
            sweep = (end - start) % 360.0
            if sweep == 0.0:
                sweep = 360.0
            if sweep >= 360.0 - 1e-12:  # a full circle: two semicircles
                first = _arc_point(centre, radius, start)
                second = _arc_point(centre, radius, start + 180.0)
                sign = 1.0 if ccw else -1.0
                ring.append((first[0], first[1], sign))
                ring.append((second[0], second[1], sign))
                continue
            bulge = math.tan(math.radians(sweep) / 4.0)
            if ccw:
                x, y = _arc_point(centre, radius, start)
                ring.append((x, y, bulge))
            else:
                x, y = _arc_point(centre, radius, end)
                ring.append((x, y, -bulge))
        elif kind == "ellipse":
            exact = False
            _kind, centre, major, ratio, t0, t1, ccw = edge
            span = (t1 - t0) % _TAU or _TAU
            count = max(3, int(_ELLIPSE_SAMPLES * span / _TAU))
            minor = (-major[1] * ratio, major[0] * ratio)
            if ccw:
                params = [t0 + span * i / count for i in range(count)]
            else:
                params = [t1 - span * i / count for i in range(count)]
            for t in params:
                ring.append(
                    (
                        centre[0] + major[0] * math.cos(t) + minor[0] * math.sin(t),
                        centre[1] + major[1] * math.cos(t) + minor[1] * math.sin(t),
                        0.0,
                    )
                )
        else:  # a spline edge: its control polygon, said to be approximate
            exact = False
            ring.extend((x, y, 0.0) for x, y in edge[2])
    return ring, exact


def loop_ring(loop) -> tuple[list[tuple[float, float, float]], bool]:
    """``([(x, y, bulge), ...], exact)`` - the loop as a bulge ring in WCS."""
    kind, _flags, _depth, items, bulges = loop
    if kind == "polyline":
        return [(p[0], p[1], b) for p, b in zip(items, bulges, strict=True)], True
    return _edges_ring(items)


def _box(points) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    xs = [p[0] for p in points]
    ys = [p[1] for p in points]
    return (min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys))


def nesting_depths(rings: list[list[tuple[float, float]]]) -> list[int]:
    """How many other rings each ring sits inside (see the module docstring)."""
    from ezdxf import edgesmith
    from ezdxf.math import Vec2

    boxes = [_box(ring) if ring else None for ring in rings]
    polygons = [[Vec2(x, y) for x, y in ring] for ring in rings]
    depths = []
    for index, ring in enumerate(rings):
        depth = 0
        mine = boxes[index]
        for other, polygon in enumerate(polygons):
            theirs = boxes[other]
            if other == index or mine is None or theirs is None:
                continue
            if not (
                theirs[0] <= mine[0]
                and theirs[1] <= mine[1]
                and mine[2] <= theirs[2]
                and mine[3] <= theirs[3]
            ):
                continue
            for x, y in ring:
                verdict = edgesmith.is_point_in_polygon_2d(Vec2(x, y), polygon)
                if verdict != 0:
                    depth += verdict > 0
                    break
        depths.append(depth)
    return depths


def hatch_loops(entity) -> tuple:
    """Every boundary path of an ``ezdxf`` HATCH as a WCS loop tuple, with its depth."""
    extrusion = tuple(entity.dxf.get("extrusion", (0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    elevation = float(entity.dxf.get("elevation", (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))[2])
    mirrored = is_flat_frame(extrusion) and extrusion[2] < 0
    raw = []
    for path in entity.paths:
        flags = int(path.path_type_flags)
        if path.type.name.lower() == "polyline":
            vertices = [
                (float(v[0]), float(v[1]), float(v[2]) if len(v) > 2 else 0.0)
                for v in path.vertices
            ]
            points = tuple(_ocs_point(extrusion, x, y, elevation) for x, y, _b in vertices)
            bulges = tuple(float(wcs_bulge(extrusion, b)) for _x, _y, b in vertices)
            raw.append(("polyline", flags, points, bulges))
        else:
            edges = tuple(_edge(e, extrusion, elevation, mirrored) for e in path.edges)
            raw.append(("edges", flags, edges, ()))
    rings = [loop_ring((kind, flags, 0, items, bulges))[0] for kind, flags, items, bulges in raw]
    depths = nesting_depths([[(x, y) for x, y, _b in ring] for ring in rings])
    return tuple(
        (kind, flags, depth, items, bulges)
        for (kind, flags, items, bulges), depth in zip(raw, depths, strict=True)
    )


def _loop_area(loop) -> tuple[float | None, bool]:
    ring, exact = loop_ring(loop)
    if len(ring) < 2:
        return None, exact
    return abs(polygon_area_perimeter(ring, True)[0]), exact


def filled_area(loops, style: int = 0) -> float | None:
    """Outer loops minus their islands by AutoCAD's island style - the rule
    ``analysis_measure_entity`` applies. None when a counted loop is not exact."""
    name = HATCH_STYLE_NAMES.get(int(style), "normal")
    total = 0.0
    for loop in loops:
        depth = loop[2]
        if name == "ignore" and depth > 0:
            continue
        if name == "outer" and depth > 1:
            continue
        area, exact = _loop_area(loop)
        if area is None or not exact:
            return None
        total += -area if depth % 2 else area
    return abs(total)


def _edge_dict(edge) -> dict:
    kind = edge[0]
    if kind == "line":
        return {"type": "line", "start": list(edge[1]), "end": list(edge[2])}
    if kind == "arc":
        return {
            "type": "arc",
            "center": list(edge[1]),
            "radius": edge[2],
            "start_angle": edge[3],
            "end_angle": edge[4],
            "ccw": edge[5],
        }
    if kind == "ellipse":
        return {
            "type": "ellipse",
            "center": list(edge[1]),
            "major_axis": list(edge[2]),
            "ratio": edge[3],
            "start_param": edge[4],
            "end_param": edge[5],
            "ccw": edge[6],
        }
    return {"type": "spline", "degree": edge[1], "control_points": [list(p) for p in edge[2]]}


def hatch_props(loops, style: int = 0) -> dict:
    """``entity_get``'s HATCH fields: every loop in WCS with its depth and area,
    and the filled area by the hatch's own island style."""
    rows = []
    exact_all = True
    for loop in loops:
        kind, flags, depth, items, _bulges = loop
        ring, exact = loop_ring(loop)
        area, _ = _loop_area(loop)
        exact_all = exact_all and exact
        row = {
            "kind": kind,
            "flags": flags,
            "external": bool(flags & 1),
            "outermost": bool(flags & 16),
            "depth": depth,
            "points": [[x, y] for x, y, _b in ring],
            "bulges": [b for _x, _y, b in ring],
            "exact": exact,
            "area": area if exact else None,
        }
        if kind == "edges":
            row["edges"] = [_edge_dict(edge) for edge in items]
        rows.append(row)
    area = filled_area(loops, style)
    return {
        "hatch_style": HATCH_STYLE_NAMES.get(int(style), "normal"),
        "loop_count": len(rows),
        "loops": rows,
        "area": area,
        "area_exact": area is not None and exact_all,
    }


def dimension_fields(entity) -> tuple:
    """``(dimtype, defpoints, measurement, text_override, dimstyle, dimlfac, angle)``.

    ``defpoints`` are DXF groups 10, 13, 14, 15 (WCS) and 16 (OCS, translated),
    None where the dimension has none. ``measurement`` is what the dimension
    measures between its own defpoints (``get_measurement``), BEFORE DIMLFAC;
    ``dimlfac`` is the entity's override, else its style's value (None when
    the style cannot be read - an unknown factor is not 1). ``angle`` is DXF
    group 50, the direction a rotated linear dimension measures along: it is
    not recoverable from the defpoints.
    """
    from ezdxf.entities import DimStyleOverride

    dxf = entity.dxf
    extrusion = tuple(dxf.get("extrusion", (0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    points = []
    for name in ("defpoint", "defpoint2", "defpoint3", "defpoint4"):
        value = dxf.get(name)
        points.append(None if value is None else _xy(value))
    arc = dxf.get("defpoint5")
    points.append(None if arc is None else _ocs_point(extrusion, arc[0], arc[1], arc[2]))
    try:
        measured = entity.get_measurement()
        measurement = float(measured) if isinstance(measured, (int, float)) else None
    except Exception:  # an ordinate or a malformed dimension measures nothing here
        measurement = None
    try:
        dimlfac = float(DimStyleOverride(entity).get("dimlfac", 1.0))
    except Exception:  # the style cannot be read: the factor is unknown, not 1
        dimlfac = None
    return (
        int(dxf.get("dimtype", 0)) & 0x0F,
        tuple(points),
        measurement,
        str(dxf.get("text", "") or ""),
        str(dxf.get("dimstyle", "Standard")),
        dimlfac,
        float(dxf.get("angle", 0.0)),
    )


def dimension_props(fields) -> dict:
    """``entity_get``'s DIMENSION fields, from :func:`dimension_fields`."""
    dimtype, defpoints, measurement, override, dimstyle, dimlfac, angle = fields
    return {
        "dimtype": DIMTYPE_NAMES.get(dimtype, f"unknown:{dimtype}"),
        "dimtype_code": dimtype,
        "defpoints": [None if p is None else [p[0], p[1]] for p in defpoints],
        "measurement": measurement,
        "text_override": override if override not in ("", "<>") else None,
        "dimstyle": dimstyle,
        "dimlfac": dimlfac,
        "angle": angle,
    }


def acadmcp_xdata(entity) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
    """Every ``ACADMCP_*`` application on the entity as ``(app, json)``.

    A payload whose 1000 chunks join to a JSON object (``ACADMCP_MECH``,
    ``_ARCH``, ``_PID``, ...) is kept joined; anything else - a list of plain
    values such as ``ACADMCP_ARCH_H``'s owned handles, which could join into a
    JSON *number* ("31" + "32") - is kept as the JSON list of its values.
    """
    if entity.xdata is None:
        return ()
    rows = []
    for app in sorted(entity.xdata.data):
        if not str(app).startswith("ACADMCP_"):
            continue
        values = [str(tag.value) for tag in entity.get_xdata(app) if tag.code == 1000]
        joined = "".join(values)
        try:
            is_object = isinstance(json.loads(joined), dict)
        except ValueError:
            is_object = False
        rows.append((str(app), joined if is_object else json.dumps(values)))
    return tuple(rows)
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the failing test for the snapshot fields**

Create `tests/test_snapshot_observed_fields.py`:

```python
"""EntityRecord's v1.7.0 fields and Snapshot.blocks (B16): defaults, hashing, values.

Readers that ignore the new fields see no change: the defaults are empty, the
record stays hashable and diff.signature is the 1.6 tuple.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math

import ezdxf
import pytest

from engineering.understand.diff import signature
from engineering.understand.snapshot import (
    EntityRecord,
    read_snapshot,
    records_from_doc,
    take_snapshot,
)


def _doc():
    doc = ezdxf.new()
    doc.header["$INSUNITS"] = 4
    return doc, doc.modelspace()


def test_new_fields_default_to_nothing_and_the_record_stays_hashable():
    record = EntityRecord(
        handle="1A", type="LINE", layer="0", space="Model", points=((0.0, 0.0), (3.0, 4.0))
    )
    assert record.tilted is False
    assert record.ellipse is None
    assert record.spline is None
    assert record.hatch_loops == ()
    assert record.hatch_style == 0
    assert record.dimension is None
    assert record.transform is None
    assert record.xdata == ()
    twin = EntityRecord(
        handle="1A", type="LINE", layer="0", space="Model", points=((0.0, 0.0), (3.0, 4.0))
    )
    assert hash(record) == hash(twin)
    # diff.signature reads none of the new fields: the tuple 1.6 built
    assert signature(record) == (
        "LINE", "0", "Model", ((0, 0), (300, 400)), (), False, None, None, None, None,
        None, (), 0, (100, 100),
    )


def test_a_hatch_record_carries_its_loops_in_wcs_with_depth_and_style():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    hatch = msp.add_hatch()
    hatch.dxf.hatch_style = 1
    hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(
        [(0, 0, 0), (20, 0, 1.0), (20, 10, 0), (0, 10, 0)], is_closed=True, flags=1
    )
    hatch.paths.add_edge_path(flags=16).add_arc((5, 5), 2, 0, 360)
    (record,) = records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records
    assert record.hatch_style == 1
    outer, island = record.hatch_loops
    assert outer == (
        "polyline",
        3,
        0,
        ((0.0, 0.0), (20.0, 0.0), (20.0, 10.0), (0.0, 10.0)),
        (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
    )
    assert island == ("edges", 16, 1, (("arc", (5.0, 5.0), 2.0, 0.0, 360.0, True),), ())


def test_a_dimension_record_keeps_the_16_fields_and_adds_the_dimension_tuple():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(
        base=(0, 5), p1=(0, 0), p2=(42, 0), dimstyle="Standard", override={"dimlfac": 2.0}
    )
    dim.render()
    dim.dimension.dxf.text = "40"
    record = next(r for r in records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records if r.type == "DIMENSION")
    assert record.dimension == (
        0, ((0.0, 5.0), (0.0, 0.0), (42.0, 0.0), None, None), 42.0, "40", "Standard", 2.0, 0.0
    )
    assert record.text == "40"
    assert record.points[0] == (0.0, 5.0)


def test_a_mirrored_ellipse_runs_counter_clockwise_seen_from_plus_z():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    ellipse = msp.add_ellipse((0, 0), major_axis=(2, 0), ratio=0.5, start_param=0.0, end_param=1.0)
    ellipse.dxf.extrusion = (0.0, 0.0, -1.0)
    (record,) = records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records
    centre, major, ratio, start, end = record.ellipse
    assert centre == (0.0, 0.0) and major == (2.0, 0.0) and ratio == 0.5
    assert start == pytest.approx((-1.0) % math.tau, abs=1e-12)
    assert end == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12)
    # the point at the old parameter 1.0 is where the new start parameter points
    x = 2.0 * math.cos(start)
    y = 1.0 * math.sin(start)
    assert (x, y) == pytest.approx((2.0 * math.cos(1.0), -math.sin(1.0)), abs=1e-12)


def test_a_spline_record_carries_degree_points_and_closure():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    msp.add_spline(fit_points=[(0, 0), (10, 5), (20, 0)])
    (record,) = records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records
    degree, control, fit, closed = record.spline
    assert degree == 3
    assert fit == ((0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 5.0), (20.0, 0.0))
    assert closed is False
    assert record.points == control


def test_tilted_is_set_only_off_the_wcs_xy_plane():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    flat = msp.add_circle((0, 0), 1)
    mirrored = msp.add_circle((5, 0), 1)
    mirrored.dxf.extrusion = (0.0, 0.0, -1.0)
    tilted = msp.add_circle((10, 0), 1)
    tilted.dxf.extrusion = (0.5, 0.0, -math.sqrt(3.0) / 2.0)
    by_handle = {r.handle: r for r in records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records}
    assert by_handle[flat.dxf.handle].tilted is False
    assert by_handle[mirrored.dxf.handle].tilted is False
    assert by_handle[tilted.dxf.handle].tilted is True


def test_an_insert_record_carries_its_block_to_wcs_affine():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    block = doc.blocks.new("B", base_point=(1, 2))
    block.add_circle((3, 2), 1)
    msp.add_blockref("B", (10, 10), dxfattribs={"xscale": 2, "yscale": -1, "rotation": 30})
    (record,) = records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records
    c, s = math.cos(math.radians(30.0)), math.sin(math.radians(30.0))
    xscale, yscale = 2.0, -1.0
    a, b = xscale * c, xscale * s  # R(30) @ (xscale, 0)
    cc, d = -yscale * s, yscale * c  # R(30) @ (0, yscale)
    e = 10.0 - (a * 1.0 + cc * 2.0)  # insert - M @ base point
    f = 10.0 - (b * 1.0 + d * 2.0)
    assert record.transform == pytest.approx((a, b, cc, d, e, f), abs=1e-9)


def test_xdata_of_our_applications_rides_on_the_record():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    doc.appids.add("ACADMCP_MECH")
    line = msp.add_line((0, 0), (1, 0))
    payload = {"v": 1, "kind": "feature", "part": "P", "feature": "f", "view": "front"}
    line.set_xdata("ACADMCP_MECH", [(1000, json.dumps(payload))])
    (record,) = records_from_doc(doc, source="t").records
    ((app, text),) = record.xdata
    assert app == "ACADMCP_MECH" and json.loads(text) == payload


def test_block_definitions_are_read_only_on_request_and_never_enter_records():
    doc, msp = _doc()
    block = doc.blocks.new("HOLE")
    block.add_circle((0, 0), 5)
    block.add_attdef("TAG", (0, 0))
    msp.add_blockref("HOLE", (10, 0))
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(base=(0, 5), p1=(0, 0), p2=(10, 0), dimstyle="Standard")
    dim.render()
    plain = records_from_doc(doc, source="t")
    full = records_from_doc(doc, source="t", block_defs=True)
    assert plain.blocks is None
    assert full.records == plain.records
    assert "HOLE" in full.blocks
    # no layout block, no *D block holding the dimension's rendering
    assert not [n for n in full.blocks if n.upper().startswith(("*D", "*MODEL", "*PAPER"))]
    (circle,) = full.blocks["HOLE"]
    assert circle.type == "CIRCLE" and circle.space == "HOLE" and circle.radius == 5.0


def test_read_snapshot_passes_block_defs_through(tmp_path):
    doc, msp = _doc()
    doc.blocks.new("B").add_line((0, 0), (1, 0))
    msp.add_blockref("B", (0, 0))
    path = tmp_path / "blocks.dxf"
    doc.saveas(path)
    assert read_snapshot(str(path)).blocks is None
    assert "B" in read_snapshot(str(path), block_defs=True).blocks


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_take_snapshot_passes_block_defs_through_on_the_headless_engine(backend):
    backend._doc.blocks.new("B").add_line((0, 0), (1, 0))
    await backend.entity_create_block_ref("B", 0.0, 0.0)
    snap = await take_snapshot(backend, block_defs=True)
    assert "B" in snap.blocks
    assert (await take_snapshot(backend)).blocks is None
```
