- [ ] **Step 15: Write the failing test for the binder**

Create `tests/test_verification_binding.py`:

```python
"""engineering/verification/binding.py (B17): the requirement's feature, or an honest no.

Review Focus 7: two identical holes where the requirement names one, and a copied
feature tag, are `ambiguous` with both candidates - never a pick. Review Focus 8:
a dimension whose text says 40 over geometry measuring 42, and a DIMLFAC of 2,
are findings, with the geometric value, the dimension's own measurement and the
displayed text kept apart.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from statistics import NormalDist

import pytest

from engineering.mech.draw import draw_part
from engineering.mech.part import build_part
from engineering.understand.snapshot import take_snapshot
from engineering.verification.binding import (
    WILSON_Z,
    bind,
    bind_dimension,
    feature_of,
    wilson_interval,
)
from engineering.verification.observe import observe
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet

PLATE2 = {
    "kind": "prismatic",
    "name": "plate",
    "material": "aluminium",
    "thickness": 8.0,
    "outline": [[0.0, 0.0], [80.0, 0.0], [80.0, 50.0], [0.0, 50.0]],
    "features": [
        {"kind": "hole", "id": "h1", "x": 40.0, "y": 25.0, "diameter": 10.0},
        {"kind": "hole", "id": "h2", "x": 60.0, "y": 25.0, "diameter": 10.0},
    ],
}
HOLE_WINDOW = {"type": "circle", "radius_min": 4.999, "radius_max": 5.001}


def _sel(kind, **value):
    return FeatureSelector(kind=kind, value=tuple(sorted(value.items())))


def _req(rid, quantity, *selectors, nominal=None):
    target = (("unit", "mm"),) if nominal is None else (("nominal", nominal), ("unit", "mm"))
    return Requirement(
        id=rid,
        quantity=quantity,
        target=target,
        features=tuple(selectors),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("label", rid),),
    )


def _bind(observed, *requirements, policy="strict"):
    return bind(RequirementSet(requirements=tuple(requirements), units="mm"), observed, policy=policy)


async def _observed(backend):
    snap = await take_snapshot(backend, block_defs=True)
    return observe(snap, document={"document_id": "doc", "revision": 7})


async def _circle_at(backend, x, y):
    for info in await backend.entity_list(type_filter="CIRCLE", limit=1000):
        if info.properties["center"] == [x, y]:
            return info.handle
    raise AssertionError(f"no circle at {(x, y)}")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_feature_tag_binds_the_one_hole_it_names(backend):
    drawn = await draw_part(backend, build_part(PLATE2), at=(0.0, 0.0), dimension=False)
    h1 = await _circle_at(backend, 40.0, 25.0)
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    (binding,) = _bind(
        observed,
        _req("bore", "diameter", _sel("feature_tag", feature="h1", part=drawn["part_id"], type="circle")),
    )
    assert binding.status == "bound"
    (ref,) = binding.refs
    assert ref.handles == (h1,) and ref.feature == "h1"
    assert ref.document_id == "doc" and ref.revision == 7 and ref.space == "Model"
    assert "one candidate" in binding.reason
    assert dict(feature_of(ref, observed).geometry)["radius"] == 5.0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_two_identical_holes_named_by_size_are_ambiguous_never_a_pick(backend):
    await draw_part(backend, build_part(PLATE2), at=(0.0, 0.0), dimension=False)
    h1 = await _circle_at(backend, 40.0, 25.0)
    h2 = await _circle_at(backend, 60.0, 25.0)
    (binding,) = _bind(await _observed(backend), _req("bore", "diameter", _sel("predicate", **HOLE_WINDOW)))
    assert binding.status == "ambiguous"
    assert binding.refs == ()
    assert sorted(ref.handles[0] for ref in binding.candidates) == sorted([h1, h2])
    assert "2 equal candidates, none chosen" in binding.reason


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_copied_feature_tag_is_ambiguous(backend):
    drawn = await draw_part(backend, build_part(PLATE2), at=(0.0, 0.0), dimension=False)
    h1 = await _circle_at(backend, 40.0, 25.0)
    copy = await backend.entity_copy(h1, 0.0, 100.0)
    (binding,) = _bind(
        await _observed(backend),
        _req("bore", "diameter", _sel("feature_tag", feature="h1", part=drawn["part_id"], type="circle")),
    )
    assert binding.status == "ambiguous"
    assert {ref.handles[0] for ref in binding.candidates} == {h1, copy.handle}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_handle_that_is_not_there_is_unknown(backend):
    await backend.entity_create_circle(0.0, 0.0, 5.0)
    (binding,) = _bind(await _observed(backend), _req("x", "radius", _sel("handle", handle="FFFFF")))
    assert binding.status == "unknown" and binding.refs == () and "no candidate" in binding.reason


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_count_counts_every_match_and_zero_is_a_result(backend):
    await draw_part(backend, build_part(PLATE2), at=(0.0, 0.0), dimension=False)
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    counted, absent = _bind(
        observed,
        _req("holes", "count", _sel("predicate", **HOLE_WINDOW)),
        _req("no_ellipse", "absence", _sel("predicate", type="ellipse")),
    )
    assert counted.status == "bound" and len(counted.refs) == 2
    assert absent.status == "bound" and absent.refs == ()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_lenient_binds_congruent_candidates_and_still_refuses_unequal_ones(backend):
    await draw_part(backend, build_part(PLATE2), at=(0.0, 0.0), dimension=False)
    (binding,) = _bind(
        await _observed(backend),
        _req("bores", "diameter", _sel("predicate", **HOLE_WINDOW)),
        policy="lenient",
    )
    assert binding.status == "bound" and len(binding.refs) == 2
    assert "every one measured (lenient)" in binding.reason
    await backend.entity_create_circle(200.0, 0.0, 6.0)
    wide = {"type": "circle", "radius_min": 4.0, "radius_max": 7.0}
    (binding,) = _bind(
        await _observed(backend), _req("bores", "diameter", _sel("predicate", **wide)), policy="lenient"
    )
    assert binding.status == "ambiguous"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_drawing_without_tags_falls_back_to_the_geometric_predicate(backend):
    plain = await backend.entity_create_circle(10.0, 10.0, 5.0)
    fallback = tuple(sorted({"type": "circle", "radius_min": 4.999, "radius_max": 5.001}.items()))
    (binding,) = _bind(
        await _observed(backend),
        _req("bore", "diameter", _sel("feature_tag", feature="h1", fallback=fallback)),
    )
    assert binding.status == "bound" and binding.refs[0].handles == (plain.handle,)
    assert "fell back to the geometric predicate" in binding.reason


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_an_arch_element_selects_the_entities_its_record_owns(backend):
    face = await backend.entity_create_line(0.0, 0.0, 4000.0, 0.0)
    back = await backend.entity_create_line(0.0, 200.0, 4000.0, 200.0)
    end = await backend.entity_create_line(4000.0, 0.0, 4000.0, 200.0)
    await backend.entity_create_line(0.0, 500.0, 10.0, 500.0)  # not the wall's
    record = json.dumps({"v": 1, "kind": "wall", "id": "W1"}, separators=(",", ":"))
    await backend.entity_set_xdata(face.handle, "ACADMCP_ARCH", [record])
    await backend.entity_set_xdata(face.handle, "ACADMCP_ARCH_H", [back.handle, end.handle])
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    counted, distance = _bind(
        observed,
        _req("faces", "count", _sel("arch_element", element="wall:W1", type="line")),
        _req("thickness", "distance", _sel("arch_element", element="wall:W1", type="line")),
    )
    assert {ref.handles[0] for ref in counted.refs} == {face.handle, back.handle, end.handle}
    assert distance.status == "ambiguous"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_an_exploded_feature_is_bound_by_handle_and_block_path(backend):
    block = backend._doc.blocks.new("HOLE")
    circle = block.add_circle((0, 0), 5)
    first = await backend.entity_create_block_ref("HOLE", 0.0, 0.0)
    second = await backend.entity_create_block_ref("HOLE", 100.0, 0.0)
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    named, bare = _bind(
        observed,
        _req("a", "radius", _sel("handle", handle=circle.dxf.handle, block_path=(second.handle,))),
        _req("b", "radius", _sel("handle", handle=circle.dxf.handle)),
    )
    assert named.status == "bound" and named.refs[0].block_path == (second.handle,)
    assert dict(feature_of(named.refs[0], observed).geometry)["center"] == (100.0, 0.0)
    assert bare.status == "unknown"  # the definition's own handle is in no layout
    assert first.handle != second.handle


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_text_override_of_40_over_geometry_of_42_is_a_finding(backend):
    msp = backend._doc.modelspace()
    msp.add_line((0, 0), (0, 20))
    msp.add_line((42, 0), (42, 20))
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(base=(0, 30), p1=(0, 0), p2=(42, 0), dimstyle="Standard")
    dim.render()
    dim.dimension.dxf.text = "40"
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    (dimension,) = [f for f in observed.features if f.type == "dimension"]
    result = bind_dimension(dimension, observed, epsilon=1e-9)
    assert result["status"] == "bound" and len(result["refs"]) == 2
    assert result["geometric_value"] == 42.0
    assert result["dimension_measurement"] == 42.0
    assert result["displayed_text"] == "40" and result["displayed_value"] == 40.0
    assert result["mismatch"] is True
    assert result["findings"] == ["text_override_disagrees_with_geometry"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_dimlfac_of_2_over_21_of_geometry_is_a_finding(backend):
    msp = backend._doc.modelspace()
    msp.add_line((100, 0), (100, 20))
    msp.add_line((121, 0), (121, 20))
    dim = msp.add_linear_dim(
        base=(100, 30), p1=(100, 0), p2=(121, 0), dimstyle="Standard", override={"dimlfac": 2.0}
    )
    dim.render()
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    (dimension,) = [f for f in observed.features if f.type == "dimension"]
    result = bind_dimension(dimension, observed, epsilon=1e-9)
    assert result["geometric_value"] == 21.0
    assert result["dimension_measurement"] == 21.0
    assert result["dimlfac"] == 2.0
    assert result["displayed_value"] == 42.0  # 21 x 2: the sheet says 42
    assert result["findings"] == ["dimlfac_scales_the_displayed_value"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_rotated_and_aligned_dimensions_measure_along_their_own_direction(backend):
    msp = backend._doc.modelspace()
    msp.add_point((0, 0))
    msp.add_point((30, 40))
    msp.add_linear_dim(base=(0, 60), p1=(0, 0), p2=(30, 40), angle=0, dimstyle="Standard").render()
    msp.add_aligned_dim(p1=(0, 0), p2=(30, 40), distance=10, dimstyle="Standard").render()
    observed = await _observed(backend)
    values = sorted(
        bind_dimension(f, observed, epsilon=1e-9)["geometric_value"]
        for f in observed.features
        if f.type == "dimension"
    )
    assert values == [pytest.approx(30.0, abs=1e-9), pytest.approx(50.0, abs=1e-9)]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_diameter_dimension_binds_its_circle_and_a_selector_follows_it(backend):
    msp = backend._doc.modelspace()
    circle = msp.add_circle((200, 0), 10)
    msp.add_circle((300, 0), 10)
    dim = msp.add_diameter_dim(center=(200, 0), radius=10, angle=45, dimstyle="Standard")
    dim.render()

    result = bind_dimension(dimension, observed, epsilon=1e-9)

    assert result["geometric_value"] == pytest.approx(20.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert result["mismatch"] is False
    (binding,) = _bind(
        observed, _req("bore", "diameter", _sel("dimension", handle=dim.dimension.dxf.handle))
    )
    assert binding.status == "bound" and binding.refs[0].handles == (circle.dxf.handle,)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_an_angular_dimension_binds_both_legs(backend):

    leg2 = msp.add_line((400, 0), (400, 20))

        base=(410, 10), line1=((400, 0), (420, 0)), line2=((400, 0), (400, 20)), dimstyle="Standard"
    ).render()

    assert result["geometric_value"] == pytest.approx(90.0, abs=1e-9)


def test_wilson_interval_matches_its_closed_forms():
    z = NormalDist().inv_cdf(0.975)
    assert WILSON_Z == pytest.approx(z, abs=1e-12)
    low, high = wilson_interval(10, 10)
    assert low == pytest.approx(10.0 / (10.0 + z * z), abs=1e-12)  # p = 1: n / (n + z^2)
    assert high == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-12)
    low, high = wilson_interval(0, 10)
    assert low == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12)
    assert high == pytest.approx(z * z / (10.0 + z * z), abs=1e-12)  # p = 0: z^2 / (n + z^2)
    assert wilson_interval(0, 0) == (0.0, 1.0)
    low, high = wilson_interval(8, 10)  # the textbook 8/10 case
    assert (low, high) == (pytest.approx(0.4902, abs=1e-4), pytest.approx(0.9433, abs=1e-4))

async def test_an_unknown_policy_is_refused(backend):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="policy"):
        bind(RequirementSet(requirements=(), units="mm"), await _observed(backend), policy="greedy")
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Write the held-out set and its failing gate**

Create `tests/fixtures/heldout_dims.py`:

```python
"""A held-out binding set from a generator family the development fixtures never use (B29).

The development fixtures of the verification engine are parts drawn by the
mechanical generator: one part model, its views, its feature tags. This family
is built differently on purpose - raw ``ezdxf`` plates with holes, no part
model, no view engine - so a binder tuned on the development fixtures meets
geometry it was not tuned on:

* each plate is drawn either straight into model space or as a block INSERTed
  with a rotation, a mirror (x scale -1) and a uniform scale of 1 or 2;
* holes have distinct diameters, except on "twin" plates, which carry two
  holes of one diameter - a requirement naming "the hole of that diameter on
  this plate" must then abstain;
* holes carry ``ACADMCP_MECH`` feature tags written with ``set_xdata``; on
  "copied" plates the block is inserted twice, so the copy carries the same
  tag - a requirement naming that tag must abstain;
* every model-space plate carries a diameter DIMENSION on its first hole.

Every truth is computed from what the generator placed - a hole's WCS centre
is its placement applied to its local centre by :func:`_place`, written here -
never read back from the binder. Seeded and synthetic: no client data.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import random
from dataclasses import dataclass

import ezdxf

from engineering.verification.binding import wilson_interval
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement

__all__ = [
    "SEED",
    "TARGET_COVERAGE",
    "TARGET_PRECISION",
    "HeldoutCase",
    "build_heldout",
    "score",
]

#: The seed of the published held-out set. Changing it makes a new set.
SEED = 20261024
PLATES = 48
SPACING = 2000.0
COPY_OFFSET = 900.0
DIAMETERS = (6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 12.0, 16.0, 20.0)
#: The B29 targets on the supported native subset.
TARGET_PRECISION = 0.98
TARGET_COVERAGE = 0.80


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HeldoutCase:
    """One requirement and what it must bind to.

    ``truth`` is ``((block_path, handle), ...)``, one per selector, or None
    when the right answer is to abstain (the requirement cannot name one
    feature).
    """

    case_id: str
    requirement: Requirement
    truth: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, ...], str], ...] | None


def _place(local, placement) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Plate coordinates to WCS: mirror x, scale, rotate, translate - the order
    an INSERT applies them. ``placement`` None: the plate was drawn in place."""
    if placement is None:
        return (float(local[0]), float(local[1]))
    x, y = float(local[0]), float(local[1])
    if placement["mirror"]:
        x = -x
    x, y = x * placement["scale"], y * placement["scale"]
    c = math.cos(math.radians(placement["rotation"]))
    s = math.sin(math.radians(placement["rotation"]))
    ox, oy = placement["origin"]
    return (ox + c * x - s * y, oy + s * x + c * y)


def _requirement(case_id: str, kind: str, value: dict, diameter: float) -> Requirement:
    return Requirement(
        id=case_id,
        quantity="diameter",
        target=(("nominal", diameter), ("unit", "mm")),
        features=(FeatureSelector(kind=kind, value=tuple(sorted(value.items()))),),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("document", "heldout"), ("label", case_id)),
    )


def _tag(entity, part: str, feature: str) -> None:
    payload = json.dumps(
        {"feature": feature, "kind": "feature", "part": part, "v": 1, "view": "plan"},
        separators=(",", ":"),
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    entity.set_xdata("ACADMCP_MECH", [(1000, payload)])


def _box(points, pad: float) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    xs = [p[0] for p in points]
    ys = [p[1] for p in points]
    return (min(xs) - pad, min(ys) - pad, max(xs) + pad, max(ys) + pad)


def build_heldout(seed: int = SEED, plates: int = PLATES):
    """``(doc, cases)``: the drawing and every case with its truth."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    doc = ezdxf.new("R2018")
    doc.header["$INSUNITS"] = 4
    doc.appids.add("ACADMCP_MECH")
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    cases: list[HeldoutCase] = []
    for index in range(plates):
        part = f"P{index}"
        origin = ((index % 8) * SPACING, (index // 8) * SPACING)
        as_block = rng.random() < 0.5
        rotation = rng.choice((0.0, 30.0, 45.0, 90.0, 137.0, 210.0))
        mirror = as_block and rng.random() < 0.5
        scale = rng.choice((1.0, 2.0)) if as_block else 1.0
        twin = rng.random() < 0.15
        copied = as_block and not twin and rng.random() < 0.15
        count = rng.randint(3, 5)
        sizes = rng.sample(DIAMETERS, count)
        if twin:
            sizes[1] = sizes[0]
        width, height = 60.0 * count, 120.0
        if as_block:
            name = f"PLATE_{index}"
            space = doc.blocks.new(name)
            base = (0.0, 0.0)
            placement = {"origin": origin, "rotation": rotation, "mirror": mirror, "scale": scale}
        else:
            space = msp
            base = origin
            placement = None
        corners = [
            (base[0] + x, base[1] + y) for x, y in ((0, 0), (width, 0), (width, height), (0, height))
        ]
        space.add_lwpolyline(corners, close=True)
        holes = []
        for k, diameter in enumerate(sizes):
            centre = (base[0] + 30.0 + 60.0 * k, base[1] + 40.0 + 40.0 * (k % 2))
            circle = space.add_circle(centre, diameter / 2.0)
            _tag(circle, part, f"H{k}")
            holes.append((circle.dxf.handle, centre, diameter))
        path: tuple[str, ...] = ()
        if as_block:
            attribs = {"rotation": rotation, "xscale": -scale if mirror else scale, "yscale": scale}
            path = (msp.add_blockref(name, origin, dxfattribs=attribs).dxf.handle,)
            if copied:
                msp.add_blockref(name, (origin[0], origin[1] + COPY_OFFSET), dxfattribs=attribs)
        plate_region = _box([_place(c, placement) for c in corners], 1.0)
        for k, (handle, centre, diameter) in enumerate(holes):
            cx, cy = _place(centre, placement)
            radius = diameter / 2.0 * scale
            margin = 15.0 * scale
            truth = ((path, handle),)
            twins = sum(1 for _h, _c, d in holes if d == diameter) > 1
            window = {"type": "circle", "radius_min": radius - 1e-6, "radius_max": radius + 1e-6}
            region = (cx - margin, cy - margin, cx + margin, cy + margin)
            cases.append(
                HeldoutCase(
                    f"{part}-H{k}-region",
                    _requirement(
                        f"{part}-H{k}-region", "predicate", {**window, "region": region}, 2.0 * radius
                    ),
                    truth,
                )
            )
            cases.append(
                HeldoutCase(
                    f"{part}-H{k}-size",
                    _requirement(
                        f"{part}-H{k}-size",
                        "predicate",
                        {**window, "region": plate_region},
                        2.0 * radius,
                    ),
                    None if twins else truth,
                )
            )
            cases.append(
                HeldoutCase(
                    f"{part}-H{k}-tag",
                    _requirement(
                        f"{part}-H{k}-tag",
                        "feature_tag",
                        {"feature": f"H{k}", "part": part, "type": "circle"},
                        2.0 * radius,
                    ),
                    None if copied else truth,
                )
            )
            cases.append(
                HeldoutCase(
                    f"{part}-H{k}-handle",
                    _requirement(
                        f"{part}-H{k}-handle",
                        "handle",
                        {"handle": handle, "block_path": path},
                        2.0 * radius,
                    ),
                    truth,
                )
            )
        if not as_block:
            handle, centre, diameter = holes[0]
            angle = rng.choice((0.0, 45.0, 120.0))
            dimension = msp.add_diameter_dim(center=centre, radius=diameter / 2.0, angle=angle)
            dimension.render()
            dim_handle = dimension.dimension.dxf.handle
            cases.append(
                HeldoutCase(
                    f"{part}-dim",
                    _requirement(f"{part}-dim", "dimension", {"handle": dim_handle}, diameter),
                    (((), handle),),
                )
            )
    return doc, tuple(cases)


def score(cases, bindings) -> dict:
    """Eligible / attempted / accepted / abstained, precision and coverage.

    ``attempted``: the binder bound the case. ``accepted``: an attempted case
    whose refs are exactly its truth (binding a must-abstain case is an attempt
    that is never accepted). ``precision = accepted / attempted`` and
    ``coverage = attempted / eligible``, each with its Wilson 95 % interval.
    Abstaining on everything gives coverage 0 and never meets the target.
    """
    by_id = {b.requirement_id: b for b in bindings}
    eligible = len(cases)
    attempted = accepted = abstained = abstained_correctly = 0
    for case in cases:
        binding = by_id[case.case_id]
        if binding.status != "bound":
            abstained += 1
            abstained_correctly += case.truth is None
            continue
        attempted += 1
        got = tuple((tuple(ref.block_path), ref.handles[0]) for ref in binding.refs)
        if case.truth is not None and got == case.truth:
            accepted += 1
    precision = accepted / attempted if attempted else 0.0
    coverage = attempted / eligible if eligible else 0.0
    p_low, p_high = wilson_interval(accepted, attempted)
    c_low, c_high = wilson_interval(attempted, eligible)
    return {
        "eligible": eligible,
        "attempted": attempted,
        "accepted": accepted,
        "abstained": abstained,
        "must_abstain": sum(case.truth is None for case in cases),
        "abstained_correctly": abstained_correctly,
        "precision": {"value": precision, "low": p_low, "high": p_high},
        "coverage": {"value": coverage, "low": c_low, "high": c_high},
        "meets_target": precision >= TARGET_PRECISION and coverage >= TARGET_COVERAGE,
    }
```

Create `tests/test_verification_heldout.py`:

```python
"""B29: the binder on a held-out set from a different generator family.

The report gives eligible, attempted, accepted and abstained counts, precision
and coverage with Wilson 95 % intervals; the target is >= 98 % precision and
>= 80 % coverage, and abstaining on everything does not meet it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect

import pytest

from engineering.understand.snapshot import records_from_doc
from engineering.verification.binding import bind
from engineering.verification.observe import observe
from engineering.verification.types import Binding, RequirementSet
from tests.fixtures import heldout_dims
from tests.fixtures.heldout_dims import TARGET_COVERAGE, TARGET_PRECISION, build_heldout, score


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def heldout():
    doc, cases = build_heldout()
    observed = observe(records_from_doc(doc, source="heldout", block_defs=True))
    bindings = bind(RequirementSet(requirements=tuple(c.requirement for c in cases), units="mm"), observed)
    return doc, cases, bindings


def test_the_held_out_family_is_not_the_development_generator():
    source = inspect.getsource(heldout_dims)
    assert "engineering.mech" not in source
    assert "draw_part" not in source and "draw_prims" not in source


def test_the_generator_s_truth_is_where_ezdxf_puts_the_hole(heldout):
    doc, cases, _bindings = heldout
    checked = 0
    for case in cases:
        if not case.case_id.endswith("-region") or not case.truth[0][0]:
            continue
        (path, handle), = case.truth
        insert = doc.entitydb[path[0]]
        circle = doc.entitydb[handle]
        expected = insert.matrix44().transform(circle.dxf.center)
        region = dict(case.requirement.features[0].value)["region"]
        centre = ((region[0] + region[2]) / 2.0, (region[1] + region[3]) / 2.0)
        assert centre == pytest.approx((expected.x, expected.y), abs=1e-9)
        checked += 1
    assert checked > 50


def test_the_binder_meets_the_b29_targets(heldout):
    _doc, cases, bindings = heldout
    report = score(cases, bindings)
    assert report["eligible"] == len(cases) == report["attempted"] + report["abstained"]
    assert report["must_abstain"] > 0
    # Review Focus 7: every case that names no single feature is abstained on
    assert report["abstained_correctly"] == report["must_abstain"]
    assert report["precision"]["value"] >= TARGET_PRECISION
    assert report["coverage"]["value"] >= TARGET_COVERAGE
    for key in ("precision", "coverage"):
        assert report[key]["low"] <= report[key]["value"] <= report[key]["high"]
    assert report["meets_target"] is True


def test_abstaining_on_everything_does_not_meet_the_target(heldout):
    _doc, cases, _bindings = heldout
    silent = tuple(
        Binding(requirement_id=c.case_id, status="unknown", refs=(), candidates=(), reason="abstain")
        for c in cases
    )
    report = score(cases, silent)
    assert report["attempted"] == 0 and report["coverage"]["value"] == 0.0
    assert report["meets_target"] is False


def test_a_binder_that_picks_the_first_candidate_is_caught(heldout):
    _doc, cases, bindings = heldout
    picked = tuple(
        Binding(
            requirement_id=b.requirement_id,
            status="bound",
            refs=b.candidates[:1],
            candidates=b.candidates,
            reason="first pick",
        )
        if b.status == "ambiguous"
        else b
        for b in bindings
    )
    report = score(cases, picked)
    assert report["accepted"] < report["attempted"]
```

- [ ] **Step 18: Create `engineering/verification/binding.py`**

```python
"""Binding: which CAD feature a requirement is about - or an honest "cannot tell".

``bind(rs, observed)`` resolves every requirement's selectors against the
observed geometry (``engineering/verification/observe.py``) and returns one
:class:`Binding` per requirement. A selector resolves to its candidates; the
binding keeps them all, says which it bound and why, and **never picks between
equals** (v1.7.0, B17):

* exactly one candidate per selector -> ``bound``;
* two or more -> ``ambiguous`` with every candidate listed (a copied feature
  tag, two identical holes where the requirement names one) - never a random
  or a first-found pick;
* none -> ``unknown``.

Selectors (``FeatureSelector.kind`` and the keys of its ``value``):

* ``handle`` - ``{"handle", "block_path"?}``: that entity; an exploded one
  needs its ``block_path`` (the INSERT handles, outermost first).
* ``feature_tag`` - ``{"feature", "part"?, "view"?, "type"?, "fallback"?}``:
  the entities whose ``ACADMCP_MECH`` feature tag matches. A drawing made
  before 1.7.0 carries no tags; ``fallback`` (a predicate's pairs) is then
  used, and the reason says so.
* ``arch_element`` - ``{"element": "<kind>:<id>", "type"?}``: the entities an
  ``ACADMCP_ARCH`` record owns - its carrier and its ``ACADMCP_ARCH_H`` list.
* ``predicate`` - ``{"type"?, "layer"?, "space"?, "region"?: (x0, y0, x1, y1),
  "radius_min"?, "radius_max"?, "length_min"?, "length_max"?}``: geometry in
  a window; every anchor point (ends, centre, vertices) must lie in the region.
* ``dimension`` - ``{"handle"}``: the features a DIMENSION's defpoints sit on
  (:func:`bind_dimension`).

For ``count`` and ``absence`` a selector's matches are the set being counted:
every match is bound and none is chosen, so zero matches is a result
(``bound`` with no refs), not ``unknown``.

``policy="lenient"`` binds *all* candidates of a radius / diameter selector
when they are congruent (equal radii within epsilon) - still not a pick: the
evaluator measures each. ``"strict"`` (the default) calls them ambiguous.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import re

from engineering.verification.types import Binding, FeatureRef

__all__ = [
    "WILSON_Z",
    "bind",
    "bind_dimension",
    "feature_of",
    "feature_points",
    "wilson_interval",
]

#: The two-sided 95 % normal quantile.
WILSON_Z = 1.959963984540054
_COUNTING = ("count", "absence")
_RADIAL = ("radius", "diameter")
_NUMBER = re.compile(r"[-+]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?")


def wilson_interval(successes: int, trials: int, z: float = WILSON_Z) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """The Wilson score interval of a proportion; ``(0.0, 1.0)`` for no trials."""
    if trials <= 0:
        return (0.0, 1.0)
    p = successes / trials
    z2 = z * z
    denominator = 1.0 + z2 / trials
    centre = (p + z2 / (2.0 * trials)) / denominator
    half = z * math.sqrt(p * (1.0 - p) / trials + z2 / (4.0 * trials * trials)) / denominator
    return (max(0.0, centre - half), min(1.0, centre + half))


def _numbers(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None:
        return
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        yield float(value)
    elif isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        for item in value:
            yield from _numbers(item)


def _epsilon_for(observed) -> float:
    """Length epsilon in drawing units: 1e-9 of the largest coordinate, at least 1e-9."""
    largest = 1.0
    for feature in observed.features:
        for _key, value in feature.geometry:
            for x in _numbers(value):
                largest = max(largest, abs(x))
    return 1e-9 * largest


def _tag(feature, app: str) -> dict:
    for name, text in feature.tags:
        if name == app:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(text)
            except ValueError:
                return {}
            return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {"values": payload}
    return {}


def _ref(feature, observed) -> FeatureRef:
    return FeatureRef(
        document_id=observed.document_id,
        revision=observed.revision,
        space=feature.space,
        block_path=tuple(feature.block_path),
        handles=(feature.handle,),
        feature=_tag(feature, "ACADMCP_MECH").get("feature") or None,
    )


def feature_of(ref: FeatureRef, observed):
    """The observed feature a single-handle reference names, or None."""
    for feature in observed.features:
        if (
            feature.handle == ref.handles[0]
            and tuple(feature.block_path) == tuple(ref.block_path)
            and feature.space == ref.space
        ):
            return feature
    return None


def _order(feature) -> tuple:
    return (feature.space, tuple(feature.block_path), feature.handle)


def _type_ok(feature, wanted) -> bool:
    return wanted is None or feature.type == str(wanted)


def _inside(point, region) -> bool:
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = (float(v) for v in region)
    return min(x0, x1) <= point[0] <= max(x0, x1) and min(y0, y1) <= point[1] <= max(y0, y1)


def _anchor_points(feature) -> list:
    g = dict(feature.geometry)
    if feature.type == "line":
        return [g["start"], g["end"]]
    if feature.type in ("circle", "arc", "ellipse"):
        return [g["center"]]
    if feature.type == "point":
        return [g["location"]]
    if feature.type == "polyline":
        return list(g["points"])
    if feature.type == "spline":
        return list(g["control_points"])
    return []


def _predicate_matches(feature, crit: dict) -> bool:
    if feature.type == "dimension" and crit.get("type") != "dimension":
        return False
    if not _type_ok(feature, crit.get("type")):
        return False
    if "layer" in crit and feature.layer != str(crit["layer"]):
        return False
    if "space" in crit and feature.space != str(crit["space"]):
        return False
    g = dict(feature.geometry)
    if "radius_min" in crit or "radius_max" in crit:
        if "radius" not in g:
            return False
        radius = float(g["radius"])
        if "radius_min" in crit and radius < float(crit["radius_min"]):
            return False
        if "radius_max" in crit and radius > float(crit["radius_max"]):
            return False
    if "length_min" in crit or "length_max" in crit:
        if feature.type != "line":
            return False
        length = math.dist(g["start"], g["end"])
        if "length_min" in crit and length < float(crit["length_min"]):
            return False
        if "length_max" in crit and length > float(crit["length_max"]):
            return False
    if "region" in crit:
        points = _anchor_points(feature)
        if not points or not all(_inside(p, crit["region"]) for p in points):
            return False
    return True


def _resolve(selector, observed, epsilon: float) -> tuple[list, str, bool]:
    """``(candidate features, reason, together)`` for one selector. ``together``:
    the features are bound jointly (a dimension's ends), not alternatives."""
    crit = dict(selector.value)
    kind = selector.kind
    features = list(observed.features)
    if kind == "handle":
        handle = str(crit.get("handle", "")).upper()
        path = tuple(crit.get("block_path", ()))
        found = [f for f in features if f.handle.upper() == handle and tuple(f.block_path) == path]
        where = f" under {' > '.join(path)}" if path else ""
        return found, f"handle {handle}{where}", False
    if kind == "feature_tag":
        found = []
        for f in features:
            tag = _tag(f, "ACADMCP_MECH")
            if tag.get("kind") != "feature" or tag.get("feature") != crit.get("feature"):
                continue
            if "part" in crit and tag.get("part") != crit["part"]:
                continue
            if "view" in crit and tag.get("view") != crit["view"]:
                continue
            if _type_ok(f, crit.get("type")):
                found.append(f)
        reason = f"feature tag {crit.get('feature')!r}"
        if not found and "fallback" in crit:
            fallback = dict(crit["fallback"])
            found = [f for f in features if _predicate_matches(f, fallback)]
            reason += (
                " is on no entity (a pre-1.7 drawing carries no tags); "
                "fell back to the geometric predicate"
            )
        return found, reason, False
    if kind == "arch_element":
        element = str(crit.get("element", ""))
        owned: set[str] = set()
        for f in features:
            record = _tag(f, "ACADMCP_ARCH")
            if f"{record.get('kind')}:{record.get('id')}" != element:
                continue
            owned.add(f.handle.upper())
            owned.update(str(h).upper() for h in _tag(f, "ACADMCP_ARCH_H").get("values", []))
        found = [
            f for f in features if f.handle.upper() in owned and _type_ok(f, crit.get("type"))
        ]
        return found, f"arch element {element}", False
    if kind == "predicate":
        return [f for f in features if _predicate_matches(f, crit)], "geometric predicate", False
    if kind == "dimension":
        handle = str(crit.get("handle", "")).upper()
        dims = [f for f in features if f.type == "dimension" and f.handle.upper() == handle]
        if not dims:
            return [], f"no DIMENSION {handle}", False
        result = bind_dimension(dims[0], observed, epsilon=epsilon)
        reason = f"DIMENSION {handle}: {result['reason']}"
        if result["status"] not in ("bound", "ambiguous"):
            return [], reason, False
        found = [feature_of(ref, observed) for ref in result["refs"]]
        # A bound dimension's features are drawn to TOGETHER (both ends of a
        # length), not alternatives; coincident circles under it are.
        return [f for f in found if f is not None], reason, result["status"] == "bound"
    raise ValueError(f"bind: unknown selector kind {kind!r}")


def _congruent(found, epsilon: float) -> bool:
    radii = [dict(f.geometry).get("radius") for f in found]
    if any(r is None for r in radii):
        return False
    return max(radii) - min(radii) <= epsilon


def _bind_one(requirement, observed, policy: str, epsilon: float) -> Binding:
    refs: list[FeatureRef] = []
    candidates: list[FeatureRef] = []
    reasons: list[str] = []
    status = "bound"
    counting = requirement.quantity in _COUNTING
    for selector in requirement.features:
        found, reason, together = _resolve(selector, observed, epsilon)
        found = sorted(found, key=_order)
        mine = [_ref(f, observed) for f in found]
        candidates.extend(mine)
        if counting:
            refs.extend(mine)
            reasons.append(f"{reason}: {len(found)} counted")
        elif together and found:
            refs.extend(mine)
            reasons.append(f"{reason}: {len(found)} feature(s) the dimension is drawn to")
        elif not found:
            if status == "bound":
                status = "unknown"
            reasons.append(f"{reason}: no candidate")
        elif len(found) == 1:
            refs.extend(mine)
            reasons.append(f"{reason}: one candidate")
        elif policy == "lenient" and requirement.quantity in _RADIAL and _congruent(found, epsilon):
            refs.extend(mine)
            reasons.append(
                f"{reason}: {len(found)} congruent candidates, every one measured (lenient)"
            )
        else:
            status = "ambiguous"
            reasons.append(f"{reason}: {len(found)} equal candidates, none chosen")
    if not requirement.features:
        status = "unknown"
        reasons.append("the requirement names no feature")
    if status != "bound":
        refs = []
    return Binding(
        requirement_id=requirement.id,
        status=status,
        refs=tuple(refs),
        candidates=tuple(candidates),
        reason="; ".join(reasons),
    )


def bind(rs, observed, *, policy: str = "strict", epsilon: float | None = None) -> tuple:
    """One :class:`Binding` per requirement of ``rs``, in order."""
    if policy not in ("strict", "lenient"):
        raise ValueError(f"bind: policy must be 'strict' or 'lenient', got {policy!r}")
    eps = float(epsilon) if epsilon is not None else _epsilon_for(observed)
    return tuple(_bind_one(r, observed, policy, eps) for r in rs.requirements)


# -- native dimensions ----------------------------------------------------------


def feature_points(feature) -> list:
    """Every point a dimension can be drawn to: ends, centres, quadrants, locations."""
    g = dict(feature.geometry)
    if feature.type == "line":
        return [g["start"], g["end"]]
    if feature.type == "point":
        return [g["location"]]
    if feature.type in ("circle", "arc"):
        cx, cy = g["center"]
        r = float(g["radius"])
        points = [(cx, cy), (cx + r, cy), (cx, cy + r), (cx - r, cy), (cx, cy - r)]
        if feature.type == "arc":
            for deg in (g["start_angle"], g["end_angle"]):
                rad = math.radians(deg)
                points.append((cx + r * math.cos(rad), cy + r * math.sin(rad)))
        return points
    if feature.type == "polyline":
        return list(g["points"])
    return []


def _at(point, observed, epsilon: float) -> list:
    return sorted(
        (
            f
            for f in observed.features
            if f.type != "dimension"
            and any(math.dist(point, p) <= epsilon for p in feature_points(f))
        ),
        key=_order,
    )


def _circles_at(centre, radius, observed, epsilon: float) -> list:
    return sorted(
        (
            f
            for f in observed.features
            if f.type in ("circle", "arc")
            and math.dist(dict(f.geometry)["center"], centre) <= epsilon
            and abs(float(dict(f.geometry)["radius"]) - radius) <= epsilon
        ),
        key=_order,
    )


def _legs(a, b, observed, epsilon: float) -> list:
    out = []
    for f in observed.features:
        if f.type != "line":
            continue
        g = dict(f.geometry)
        start, end = g["start"], g["end"]
        forward = math.dist(start, a) <= epsilon and math.dist(end, b) <= epsilon
        backward = math.dist(start, b) <= epsilon and math.dist(end, a) <= epsilon
        if forward or backward:
            out.append(f)
    return out


def _line_angle(a, b) -> float:
    return math.degrees(math.atan2(b[1] - a[1], b[0] - a[0])) % 180.0


def _displayed(override: str, measurement, dimlfac) -> tuple[str, float | None]:
    """What the sheet shows: the override (``<>`` standing for the value), else
    the measurement times DIMLFAC; and the first number in it."""
    if measurement is None or dimlfac is None:
        value = None
        text = ""
    else:
        value = float(measurement) * float(dimlfac)
        text = f"{value:.10g}"
    if override and override != "<>":
        text = override.replace("<>", text) if "<>" in override else override
        found = _NUMBER.search(text.replace("\\P", " "))
        value = float(found.group().replace(",", ".")) if found else None
    return text, value


def _fields(dimension_record) -> tuple:
    if hasattr(dimension_record, "dimension"):
        return dimension_record.dimension
    g = dict(dimension_record.geometry)
    return (
        g["dimtype"],
        g["defpoints"],
        g["measurement"],
        g["text_override"],
        g["dimstyle"],
        g["dimlfac"],
        g["angle"],
    )


def bind_dimension(dimension_record, observed, *, epsilon: float) -> dict:
    """What a DIMENSION is drawn to, and whether what it shows is what is there.

    ``dimension_record`` is a ``dimension`` :class:`ObservedFeature` (or an
    ``EntityRecord`` carrying ``dimension``). Its defpoints are matched to
    feature points within ``epsilon``. ``geometric_value`` is measured on the
    matched geometry, ``dimension_measurement`` is the dimension's own
    measurement (before DIMLFAC), ``displayed_text`` / ``displayed_value`` what
    the sheet shows. A text override or a DIMLFAC that makes the sheet disagree
    with the geometry is a finding - ``mismatch`` - never a pass.

    Linear (type 0, measured along DXF angle 50) and aligned (1) dimensions
    are matched by their two measured points; diameter (3) and radius (4) by
    the one circle or arc their chord points describe; two-line angular (2) by
    its two legs. Other types answer ``unsupported``.
    """
    fields = _fields(dimension_record)
    dimtype, defpoints, measurement, override, _style, dimlfac, angle = fields
    refs: list = []
    geometric = None
    status = "bound"
    if dimtype in (0, 1):
        first = _at(defpoints[1], observed, epsilon) if defpoints[1] else []
        second = _at(defpoints[2], observed, epsilon) if defpoints[2] else []
        if not first or not second:
            status, reason = "unknown", "a measured point lies on no feature"
        else:
            refs = [_ref(f, observed) for f in dict.fromkeys(first + second)]
            p1, p2 = defpoints[1], defpoints[2]
            if dimtype == 0:
                ux, uy = math.cos(math.radians(angle)), math.sin(math.radians(angle))
                geometric = abs((p2[0] - p1[0]) * ux + (p2[1] - p1[1]) * uy)
            else:
                geometric = math.dist(p1, p2)
            reason = "both measured points lie on features"
    elif dimtype in (3, 4):
        if dimtype == 3:
            a, b = defpoints[0], defpoints[3]
            centre = ((a[0] + b[0]) / 2.0, (a[1] + b[1]) / 2.0)
            radius = math.dist(a, b) / 2.0
        else:
            centre, radius = defpoints[0], math.dist(defpoints[0], defpoints[3])
        found = _circles_at(centre, radius, observed, epsilon)
        if not found:
            status, reason = "unknown", "no circle or arc under the dimension"
        elif len(found) > 1:
            status, reason = "ambiguous", f"{len(found)} coincident circles under the dimension"
            refs = [_ref(f, observed) for f in found]
        else:
            refs = [_ref(found[0], observed)]
            r = float(dict(found[0].geometry)["radius"])
            geometric = 2.0 * r if dimtype == 3 else r
            reason = "the dimension is drawn on one circle"
    elif dimtype == 2:
        first = _legs(defpoints[1], defpoints[2], observed, epsilon)
        second = _legs(defpoints[3], defpoints[0], observed, epsilon)
        if len(first) != 1 or len(second) != 1:
            status, reason = "unknown", "the angle's legs are not single lines"
        else:
            refs = [_ref(first[0], observed), _ref(second[0], observed)]
            g1, g2 = dict(first[0].geometry), dict(second[0].geometry)
            raw = abs(_line_angle(g1["start"], g1["end"]) - _line_angle(g2["start"], g2["end"]))
            raw %= 180.0
            target = measurement if measurement is not None else raw
            geometric = min((raw, 180.0 - raw), key=lambda v: abs(v - target))
            reason = "both legs are lines"
    else:
        status, reason = "unsupported", f"dimension type {dimtype} is not bound"
    text, shown = _displayed(override, measurement, dimlfac)
    findings: list[str] = []
    if geometric is not None:
        if shown is None or abs(shown - geometric) > epsilon:
            if override and override != "<>":
                findings.append("text_override_disagrees_with_geometry")
            elif dimlfac not in (None, 1.0):
                findings.append("dimlfac_scales_the_displayed_value")
            else:
                findings.append("displayed_value_disagrees_with_geometry")
        if measurement is None or abs(float(measurement) - geometric) > epsilon:
            findings.append("measurement_disagrees_with_geometry")
    return {
        "handle": dimension_record.handle,
        "dimtype": dimtype,
        "status": status,
        "refs": tuple(refs),
        "geometric_value": geometric,
        "dimension_measurement": measurement,
        "dimlfac": dimlfac,
        "displayed_text": text,
        "displayed_value": shown,
        "text_override": override if override not in ("", "<>") else None,
        "findings": findings,
        "mismatch": bool(findings),
        "reason": reason,
    }
```

```bash
The mechanical generators write an ACADMCP_MECH feature record
({kind: "feature", part, feature, view}) on every entity they draw, in the
create call itself: entity_create_line / circle / arc / polyline / text /
hatch take xdata= on both engines (validated by prepare_xdata before anything
is created; one lock, one undo step, no extra COM round trip). Primitives
carry a feature field (default ""); views tag each feature's primitives and
keep the tag through mirrors and details. A copy carries its tag.

engineering/verification/binding.py binds requirements by handle, feature tag
(with a geometric fallback for 1.6 drawings), arch element, geometric
predicate and DIMENSION. Every binding keeps its candidates and its reason;
two equal candidates are ambiguous with both, never a pick; none is unknown.
bind_dimension matches defpoints to feature points and keeps the geometric
value, the dimension's own measurement and the displayed text apart: a text
override of 40 over 42 of geometry, or a DIMLFAC of 2, is a finding.
```

- Modify: `backends/contracts/premium.py` (`point_intersection_detail`: the on-segment flags; `point_intersection` keeps its tuple)
- Modify: `server.py` (`point_intersection` tool, SECTION 13 - no banner count, no new tool)
- Modify: `discovery/aliases.py` (`point_intersection` synonyms)
- Test: `tests/test_verification_measure.py`, `tests/test_verification_metamorphic.py`, `tests/test_point_intersection_segments.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11 `engineering/verification/types.py`: `Requirement(id, quantity, target, features, provenance, source, datum=None, manufacturing_tolerance=None, verification_epsilon=None)`, `RequirementSet(requirements, units, legacy=None)`, `FeatureSelector(kind, value)`, `Binding(requirement_id, status, refs, candidates, reason)`, `FeatureRef(document_id, revision, space, block_path, handles, feature)`, `ObservedFeature(handle, type, space, layer, block_path, geometry, tags=(), approximation=None)`, `ObservedGeometry(features, declared_unit, inferred_unit, document_id, revision, source)`, `Measurement(requirement_id, value, unit, method, residual, status, detail=())`. Task 13: `observe(snap, *, explode_inserts=True, document=None)`, `records_from_doc(doc, *, source, insert_boxes=True, block_defs=False)`, the feature geometry keys (line `start`/`end`; circle `center`/`radius`; arc `center`/`radius`/`start_angle`/`end_angle` in degrees, counter-clockwise; ellipse `center`/`major_axis`/`ratio`/`start_param`/`end_param`; point `location`; spline any keys), `approximation` containing `"tilted_plane"` for a tilted feature. Task 14: `feature_of(ref, observed) -> ObservedFeature | None`, `bind(rs, observed, *, policy="strict", epsilon=None)`, private `_epsilon_for(observed)`. `engineering/understand/units.py::MM_PER_UNIT`. `backends/contracts/premium.py::point_intersection(handle1, handle2, ref_x=None, ref_y=None) -> tuple[float, float]` (unchanged signature).
- Produces:
  - `default_epsilon(observed: ObservedGeometry) -> float` (drawing units: `RELATIVE_EPSILON * max(1, largest |coordinate|)`)
  - `evaluate(req: Requirement, binding: Binding, observed: ObservedGeometry, *, epsilon: float | None = None) -> Measurement`; `ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG = 1e-7`; `RELATIVE_EPSILON = 1e-9`
  - target keys read: `nominal`, `unit`, `exact`, `min`, `max`; `manufacturing_tolerance` keys `upper`, `lower` (signed deviations, requirement unit); `verification_epsilon` in the requirement's unit (degrees for angular quantities)
  - `Measurement.method` values: `point_point`, `point_line`, `line_line`, `circle_radius`, `arc_radius`, `ellipse_radius`, `circle_diameter`, `arc_diameter`, `ellipse_diameter`, `line_line_angle`, `arc_sweep`, `centre_distance`, `point_distance`, `line_line_parallel`, `line_line_perpendicular`, `line_horizontal`, `line_vertical`, `line_circle_tangency`, `circle_circle_tangency`, `arc_continuity`, `count`, `absence`, `binding`, `units`, `tilted_plane`, `spline`, and `<quantity>_<type>` for an unsupported pair; `Measurement.detail` keys: `basis` (`manufacturing_tolerance` | `verification_epsilon`), `epsilon`, `degenerate`, `binding`, `units`, `radii`, `features`
  - `point_intersection_detail(handle1, handle2, ref_x=None, ref_y=None) -> dict` `{"x", "y", "on_first", "on_second", "on_both", "candidates"}`; the `point_intersection` tool returns the same keys

Create `tests/test_verification_measure.py`:

```python
"""engineering/verification/measure.py (B18, B19, B20): every quantity against hand geometry.

The features are written out by hand; every expected value is worked in the
comment beside it, never taken from a generator or from the module under test.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

from engineering.verification.measure import (
    ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG,
    RELATIVE_EPSILON,
    default_epsilon,
    evaluate,
)
from engineering.verification.types import (
    Binding,
    FeatureRef,
    ObservedFeature,
    ObservedGeometry,
    Requirement,
)


def _f(handle, kind, approximation=None, **geometry):
    return ObservedFeature(
        handle=handle,
        type=kind,
        space="Model",
        layer="0",
        block_path=(),
        geometry=tuple(geometry.items()),
        approximation=approximation,
    )


def line(handle, a, b):
    return _f(handle, "line", start=a, end=b)


def circle(handle, centre, radius, **extra):
    return _f(handle, "circle", center=centre, radius=radius, **extra)


def arc(handle, centre, radius, a0, a1):
    return _f(handle, "arc", center=centre, radius=radius, start_angle=a0, end_angle=a1)


def point(handle, at):
    return _f(handle, "point", location=at)


def drawing(*features, unit="mm"):
    return ObservedGeometry(
        features=tuple(features),
        declared_unit=unit,
        inferred_unit=unit,
        document_id="doc",
        revision=1,
        source="test",
    )


def _binding(rid, handles, status):
    refs = tuple(
        FeatureRef(
            document_id="doc", revision=1, space="Model", block_path=(), handles=(h,), feature=None
        )
        for h in handles
    )
    return Binding(
        requirement_id=rid,
        status=status,
        refs=refs if status == "bound" else (),
        candidates=refs,
        reason="test",
    )


def req(quantity, *, nominal=None, unit="mm", tol=None, eps=None, **counts):
    target = {} if unit is None else {"unit": unit}
    if nominal is not None:
        target["nominal"] = nominal
    target.update(counts)
    return Requirement(
        id="r",
        quantity=quantity,
        target=tuple(sorted(target.items())),
        features=(),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(),
        manufacturing_tolerance=None if tol is None else tuple(sorted(tol.items())),
        verification_epsilon=eps,
    )


def measure(requirement, observed, *handles, status="bound"):
    return evaluate(requirement, _binding(requirement.id, handles, status), observed)


def test_point_to_point_distance_is_the_3_4_5_triangle():
    observed = drawing(point("P", (3.0, 4.0)), circle("C", (0.0, 0.0), 1.0))
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=5.0), observed, "P", "C")
    assert (m.value, m.residual, m.unit, m.method, m.status) == (5.0, 0.0, "mm", "point_point", "pass")
    assert dict(m.detail)["basis"] == "verification_epsilon"


def test_point_to_line_distance_is_the_perpendicular():
    # |(x2-x1)(y1-py) - (x1-px)(y2-y1)| / L = |3*(0+3) - (0-4)*4| / 5 = 25 / 5
    observed = drawing(line("L", (0.0, 0.0), (3.0, 4.0)), point("P", (4.0, -3.0)))
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=5.0), observed, "P", "L")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(5.0, abs=1e-9) and m.method == "point_line"
    assert m.status == "pass"


def test_line_to_line_distance_needs_parallel_lines():
    a = line("A", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0))
    b = line("B", (0.0, 7.5), (10.0, 7.5))
    slanted = line("S", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 1.0))
    observed = drawing(a, b, slanted)
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=7.5), observed, "A", "B")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(7.5, abs=1e-9) and m.method == "line_line"
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=7.5), observed, "A", "S")
    assert m.status == "unknown" and dict(m.detail)["degenerate"] == "lines are not parallel"


def test_the_manufacturing_tolerance_decides_pass_and_fail():
    observed = drawing(line("A", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)), line("B", (0.0, 7.5), (10.0, 7.5)))
    tight = measure(req("distance", nominal=7.4, tol={"upper": 0.05, "lower": -0.05}), observed, "A", "B")
    assert tight.status == "fail" and tight.residual == pytest.approx(0.1, abs=1e-9)
    assert dict(tight.detail)["basis"] == "manufacturing_tolerance"
    loose = measure(req("distance", nominal=7.4, tol={"upper": 0.2, "lower": -0.2}), observed, "A", "B")
    assert loose.status == "pass"


def test_radius_and_diameter_of_circles_and_arcs():
    observed = drawing(circle("C", (0.0, 0.0), 12.5), arc("A", (0.0, 0.0), 3.0, 0.0, 90.0))
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=12.5), observed, "C")
    assert (m.value, m.method, m.status) == (12.5, "circle_radius", "pass")
    m = measure(req("diameter", nominal=25.0), observed, "C")
    assert (m.value, m.method, m.status) == (25.0, "circle_diameter", "pass")
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=3.0), observed, "A")
    assert (m.value, m.method) == (3.0, "arc_radius")


def test_an_ellipse_is_not_a_circle_unless_its_ratio_is_one():
    flat = _f("E", "ellipse", center=(0.0, 0.0), major_axis=(10.0, 0.0), ratio=0.5,
              start_param=0.0, end_param=2.0 * math.pi)
    round_ = _f("R", "ellipse", center=(0.0, 0.0), major_axis=(6.0, 8.0), ratio=1.0,
                start_param=0.0, end_param=2.0 * math.pi)
    observed = drawing(flat, round_)
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=10.0), observed, "E")
    assert m.status == "unsupported" and m.method == "radius_of_ellipse"
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=10.0), observed, "R")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(10.0, abs=1e-9)  # |(6, 8)|


def test_two_bound_radii_that_differ_are_ambiguous_never_averaged():
    observed = drawing(circle("A", (0.0, 0.0), 5.0), circle("B", (20.0, 0.0), 5.1))
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=5.0), observed, "A", "B")
    assert m.status == "ambiguous" and m.value is None


def test_the_angle_between_lines_and_its_supplement():
    observed = drawing(line("X", (0.0, 0.0), (1.0, 0.0)), line("S", (0.0, 0.0), (1.0, math.sqrt(3.0))))
    m = measure(req("angle", nominal=60.0, unit="deg"), observed, "X", "S")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(60.0, abs=1e-9) and m.unit == "deg" and m.status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("angle", nominal=120.0, unit="deg"), observed, "X", "S")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(120.0, abs=1e-9) and m.status == "pass"


def test_an_arc_s_sweep_is_an_angle():
    observed = drawing(arc("A", (0.0, 0.0), 5.0, 30.0, 300.0))
    m = measure(req("angle", nominal=270.0, unit="deg"), observed, "A")
    assert (m.value, m.method, m.status) == (270.0, "arc_sweep", "pass")


def test_concentric_and_coincident():
    observed = drawing(
        circle("A", (5.0, 5.0), 3.0),
        circle("B", (5.0, 5.0), 1.0),
        circle("C", (5.0, 5.002), 1.0),
        point("P", (5.0, 5.0)),
    )
    m = measure(req("concentric"), observed, "A", "B")
    assert (m.value, m.method, m.status) == (0.0, "centre_distance", "pass")
    m = measure(req("concentric"), observed, "A", "C")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(0.002, abs=1e-9) and m.status == "fail"
    m = measure(req("concentric", tol={"upper": 0.01}), observed, "A", "C")
    assert m.status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("coincident"), observed, "P", "A")
    assert (m.value, m.method, m.status) == (0.0, "point_distance", "pass")


def test_parallel_perpendicular_horizontal_vertical():
    one_degree = line("D", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 10.0 * math.tan(math.radians(1.0))))
    half_degree = line("H", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 10.0 * math.tan(math.radians(0.5))))
    observed = drawing(
        line("A", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)),
        line("B", (0.0, 7.5), (10.0, 7.5)),
        line("U", (0.0, 0.0), (0.0, 5.0)),
        line("V", (3.0, 0.0), (3.0, 9.0)),
        one_degree,
        half_degree,
    )
    assert measure(req("parallel", unit=None), observed, "A", "B").status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("parallel", unit=None), observed, "A", "D")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-9) and m.status == "fail"
    m = measure(req("perpendicular", unit=None), observed, "A", "U")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12) and m.status == "pass"
    assert measure(req("horizontal", unit=None), observed, "A").status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("horizontal", unit=None), observed, "H")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(0.5, abs=1e-9) and m.status == "fail"
    assert measure(req("vertical", unit=None), observed, "V").status == "pass"


def test_line_circle_tangency_exact_near_and_secant():
    observed = drawing(
        circle("C", (0.0, 0.0), 10.0),
        line("T", (-20.0, 10.0), (20.0, 10.0)),  # distance 10 = r
        line("N", (-20.0, 10.000001), (20.0, 10.000001)),  # misses by 1e-6
        line("S", (-20.0, 9.999), (20.0, 9.999)),  # cuts by 1e-3
    )
    # default epsilon: 1e-9 x the largest coordinate (20) = 2e-8
    assert default_epsilon(observed) == pytest.approx(2e-8, abs=1e-20)
    m = measure(req("tangent"), observed, "T", "C")
    assert (m.method, m.status) == ("line_circle_tangency", "pass")
    m = measure(req("tangent"), observed, "N", "C")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(1e-6, abs=1e-12) and m.status == "fail"
    assert measure(req("tangent", tol={"upper": 1e-5}), observed, "N", "C").status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("tangent"), observed, "S", "C")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(1e-3, abs=1e-12) and m.status == "fail"


def test_circle_circle_tangency_external_internal_near_and_coincident():
    observed = drawing(
        circle("A", (0.0, 0.0), 3.0),
        circle("B", (5.0, 0.0), 2.0),  # d = 5 = 3 + 2: external
        circle("O", (0.0, 0.0), 5.0),
        circle("I", (2.0, 0.0), 3.0),  # d = 2 = 5 - 3: internal
        circle("N", (5.000001, 0.0), 2.0),  # misses by 1e-6
        circle("Z", (0.0, 0.0), 3.0),  # A again
    )
    assert measure(req("tangent"), observed, "A", "B").status == "pass"
    assert measure(req("tangent"), observed, "O", "I").status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("tangent"), observed, "A", "N")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(1e-6, abs=1e-12) and m.status == "fail"
    m = measure(req("tangent"), observed, "A", "Z")
    assert m.status == "unknown" and dict(m.detail)["degenerate"] == "coincident circles"


def test_arc_continuity_smooth_and_kinked():
    smooth = drawing(line("L", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)), arc("A", (10.0, 5.0), 5.0, 270.0, 90.0))
    m = measure(req("tangent"), smooth, "L", "A")
    assert m.method == "arc_continuity" and m.value == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert m.status == "pass"
    kinked = drawing(line("L", (0.0, -1.0), (10.0, 0.0)), arc("A", (10.0, 5.0), 5.0, 270.0, 90.0))
    m = measure(req("tangent"), kinked, "L", "A")
    # the arc's tangent at (10, 0) is +x; the line runs along (10, 1)
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(math.degrees(math.atan2(1.0, 10.0)), abs=1e-9)
    assert m.status == "fail"


def test_counts_exact_at_least_at_most_and_absence():
    observed = drawing(*(circle(f"C{i}", (10.0 * i, 0.0), 2.0) for i in range(3)))
    handles = ("C0", "C1", "C2")
    m = measure(req("count", unit=None, exact=3), observed, *handles)
    assert (m.value, m.residual, m.unit, m.status) == (3.0, 0.0, "count", "pass")
    assert measure(req("count", unit=None, min=4), observed, *handles).status == "fail"
    assert measure(req("count", unit=None, max=3), observed, *handles).status == "pass"
    assert measure(req("count", unit=None, min=1, max=2), observed, *handles).status == "fail"
    assert measure(req("absence", unit=None), observed).status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("absence", unit=None), observed, "C0")
    assert (m.value, m.status) == (1.0, "fail")


def test_a_zero_length_line_is_degenerate():
    observed = drawing(line("Z", (1.0, 1.0), (1.0, 1.0)), point("P", (0.0, 0.0)))
    m = measure(req("horizontal", unit=None), observed, "Z")
    assert m.status == "unknown" and dict(m.detail)["degenerate"] == "zero-length line"
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=1.0), observed, "P", "Z")
    assert m.status == "unknown"


def test_splines_and_tilted_planes_are_unsupported():
    observed = drawing(
        _f("S", "spline", degree=3, control_points=((0.0, 0.0), (1.0, 1.0)), fit_points=(), closed=False),
        circle("T", (0.0, 0.0), 5.0, approximation="tilted_plane"),
    )
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=5.0), observed, "T")
    assert (m.status, m.method, m.value) == ("unsupported", "tilted_plane", None)
    m = measure(req("count", unit=None, exact=1), observed, "S")
    assert m.status == "pass"  # a count of splines is a count
    m = measure(req("distance", nominal=1.0), observed, "S", "T")
    assert m.status == "unsupported"


def test_an_unbound_requirement_is_never_a_pass():
    observed = drawing(circle("A", (0.0, 0.0), 5.0), circle("B", (20.0, 0.0), 5.0))
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=5.0), observed, "A", "B", status="ambiguous")
    assert (m.status, m.value, m.method) == ("ambiguous", None, "binding")
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=5.0), observed, status="unknown")
    assert (m.status, m.value) == ("unknown", None)


def test_lengths_convert_between_the_drawing_and_the_requirement_unit():
    inches = drawing(circle("C", (0.0, 0.0), 1.0), unit="in")
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=25.4, unit="mm"), inches, "C")
    assert m.value == pytest.approx(25.4, abs=1e-12) and m.unit == "mm" and m.status == "pass"
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=1.0, unit="in"), inches, "C")
    assert m.value == 1.0 and m.status == "pass"
    unitless = drawing(circle("C", (0.0, 0.0), 1.0), unit=None)
    m = measure(req("radius", nominal=1.0, unit="mm"), unitless, "C")
    assert m.status == "unknown" and m.method == "units"


def test_a_requirement_s_own_epsilon_overrides_the_default():
    observed = drawing(line("A", (0.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)), line("B", (0.0, 7.5), (10.0, 7.5)))
    # residual 1e-7 against the default 1e-9 x 10 = 1e-8: fail; against 1e-6: pass
    assert measure(req("distance", nominal=7.5000001), observed, "A", "B").status == "fail"
    assert measure(req("distance", nominal=7.5000001, eps=1e-6), observed, "A", "B").status == "pass"


def test_default_epsilon_scales_with_the_drawing():
    assert default_epsilon(drawing(point("P", (0.5, 0.25)))) == RELATIVE_EPSILON
    big = drawing(line("L", (-1000.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)))
    assert default_epsilon(big) == pytest.approx(1000.0 * RELATIVE_EPSILON, abs=1e-18)
    assert ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG == 1e-7
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing metamorphic test**

Create `tests/test_verification_metamorphic.py`:

```python
"""Metamorphic evaluation (B18-B20): translate, rotate, mirror, scale and mm <-> inch.

One drawing, measured as drawn and after every transform of the drawing (ezdxf's
own `transform`, so mirrored arcs really get a -Z extrusion), with the
requirement transformed alongside where its numbers carry a length. The
verdicts must not move. The expected verdicts are worked by hand from the
geometry below.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import ezdxf
import pytest
from ezdxf.math import Matrix44

from engineering.understand.snapshot import records_from_doc
from engineering.verification.binding import bind
from engineering.verification.measure import evaluate
from engineering.verification.observe import observe
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet

EXPECTED = {
    "d_pp": "pass",  # centre (40, 15) to point (70, 15): 30
    "d_pl": "pass",  # point (70, 15) to the x axis: 15
    "d_ll": "pass",  # y = 0 and y = 30: 30
    "d_ll_bad": "fail",  # ... asked for 31
    "r1": "pass",  # radius 10
    "dia": "pass",  # diameter 8
    "ang": "pass",  # x axis and a 30 degree line
    "conc": "pass",  # both circles centred on (40, 15)
    "par": "pass",
    "perp": "pass",  # x axis and the y axis
    "perp_bad": "fail",  # x axis and the 30 degree line: 60 off
    "tan": "pass",  # y = 25 touches the r 10 circle at (40, 25)
    "tan_near": "fail",  # y = 25.001 misses it by 1e-3
    "tan_arc": "pass",  # the arc continues y = 0 at (100, 0)
    "cnt": "pass",
}


def _build(matrix=None, units=4):
    doc = ezdxf.new()
    doc.header["$INSUNITS"] = units
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    h = {
        "L1": msp.add_line((0, 0), (100, 0)).dxf.handle,
        "L2": msp.add_line((0, 30), (100, 30)).dxf.handle,
        "L3": msp.add_line((0, 0), (0, 30)).dxf.handle,
        "L4": msp.add_line((0, 0), (100, 100 * math.tan(math.radians(30)))).dxf.handle,
        "C1": msp.add_circle((40, 15), 10).dxf.handle,
        "C2": msp.add_circle((40, 15), 4).dxf.handle,
        "T1": msp.add_line((20, 25), (60, 25)).dxf.handle,
        "T2": msp.add_line((20, 25.001), (60, 25.001)).dxf.handle,
        "A1": msp.add_arc((100, 15), 15, 270, 90).dxf.handle,
        "P1": msp.add_point((70, 15)).dxf.handle,
    }
    if matrix is not None:
        for entity in msp:
            entity.transform(matrix)
    return doc, h


def _requirements(h, k=1.0, unit="mm"):
    def r(rid, quantity, names, **target):
        tol = target.pop("tol", None)
        return Requirement(
            id=rid,
            quantity=quantity,
            target=tuple(sorted(target.items())),
            features=tuple(
                FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", h[n]),)) for n in names
            ),
            provenance="explicit",
            source=(("label", rid),),
            manufacturing_tolerance=tol,
        )

    band = (("lower", -0.01 * k), ("upper", 0.01 * k))
    degrees = (("lower", -0.01), ("upper", 0.01))
    return RequirementSet(
        requirements=(
            r("d_pp", "distance", ["C1", "P1"], nominal=30.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("d_pl", "distance", ["P1", "L1"], nominal=15.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("d_ll", "distance", ["L1", "L2"], nominal=30.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("d_ll_bad", "distance", ["L1", "L2"], nominal=31.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("r1", "radius", ["C1"], nominal=10.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("dia", "diameter", ["C2"], nominal=8.0 * k, unit=unit, tol=band),
            r("ang", "angle", ["L1", "L4"], nominal=30.0, unit="deg", tol=degrees),
            r("conc", "concentric", ["C1", "C2"], unit=unit),
            r("par", "parallel", ["L1", "L2"]),
            r("perp", "perpendicular", ["L1", "L3"]),
            r("perp_bad", "perpendicular", ["L1", "L4"]),
            r("tan", "tangent", ["T1", "C1"], unit=unit),
            r("tan_near", "tangent", ["T2", "C1"], unit=unit),
            r("tan_arc", "tangent", ["L1", "A1"], unit=unit),
            r("cnt", "count", ["C1", "C2"], exact=2),
        ),
        units=unit,
    )


def _verdicts(doc, rs):
    observed = observe(records_from_doc(doc, source="t", block_defs=True))
    bindings = bind(rs, observed)
    return {r.id: evaluate(r, b, observed).status for r, b in zip(rs.requirements, bindings, strict=True)}


def test_the_drawing_as_drawn_gives_the_hand_worked_verdicts():
    doc, h = _build()
    assert _verdicts(doc, _requirements(h)) == EXPECTED


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "matrix",
    [
        Matrix44.translate(1234.5, -987.25, 0.0),
        Matrix44.z_rotate(math.radians(37.0)),
        Matrix44.scale(-1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        Matrix44.chain(
            Matrix44.scale(1.0, -1.0, 1.0),
            Matrix44.z_rotate(math.radians(-61.0)),
            Matrix44.translate(10.0, 20.0, 0.0),
        ),
    ],
    ids=["translate", "rotate", "mirror", "mirror_rotate_translate"],
)
def test_rigid_motions_and_mirrors_keep_every_verdict(matrix):
    doc, h = _build(matrix)
    assert _verdicts(doc, _requirements(h)) == EXPECTED


def test_a_uniform_scale_with_the_requirement_scaled_keeps_every_verdict():
    doc, h = _build(Matrix44.scale(3.0, 3.0, 1.0))
    assert _verdicts(doc, _requirements(h, k=3.0)) == EXPECTED


def test_mm_to_inch_on_drawing_and_requirement_together_keeps_every_verdict():
    doc, h = _build(Matrix44.scale(1 / 25.4, 1 / 25.4, 1.0), units=1)
    assert _verdicts(doc, _requirements(h, k=1 / 25.4, unit="in")) == EXPECTED


def test_an_inch_drawing_measured_against_a_mm_requirement_keeps_every_verdict():
    doc, h = _build(Matrix44.scale(1 / 25.4, 1 / 25.4, 1.0), units=1)
    assert _verdicts(doc, _requirements(h)) == EXPECTED
```
