- [ ] **Step 4: Create `engineering/verification/measure.py`**

```python
"""Evaluators: one requirement, measured on CAD-read geometry only (v1.7.0, B18-B20).

``evaluate(requirement, binding, observed)`` returns a :class:`Measurement`:
the value in the requirement's unit, the method, the residual and a status.
Nothing here reads a label, a stored model or the numbers a caller typed -
only the observed features the binding names.

Status:

* ``ambiguous`` / ``unknown`` - the binding was not ``bound``: never ``pass``;
* ``unsupported`` - a spline, a feature in a tilted plane, an ellipse where a
  circle is needed, or a type the quantity cannot read;
* ``unknown`` - a degenerate case the quantity is undefined for (a zero-length
  line, two non-parallel lines asked for a distance, coincident circles asked
  for tangency) or a length requirement on a drawing whose unit is unknown;
* ``pass`` / ``fail`` - against the requirement's own manufacturing tolerance
  (``upper`` / ``lower``: signed deviations in the requirement's unit; a
  constraint reads ``upper`` as its allowed deviation) when it has one, else
  against the verification epsilon. ``detail["basis"]`` says which.

Quantities: ``distance`` (point-point, point-line - the perpendicular to the
line through the segment - and line-line, which must be parallel), ``radius``
and ``diameter`` (circle, arc, or an ellipse of ratio 1; several bound radii
must agree or the answer is ``ambiguous``, never an average), ``angle`` (two
lines, or an arc's sweep), ``concentric``, ``coincident`` (centres and
points), ``parallel``, ``perpendicular``, ``horizontal``, ``vertical``
(degrees off), ``tangent`` (line-circle and circle-circle by distance;
G1 continuity by the angle between the tangents when an arc shares an end
with the other curve), ``count`` (``exact`` / ``min`` / ``max``) and
``absence``. A point-like feature is a POINT or the centre of a circle or arc.

Units: the drawing's unit is ``observed.inferred_unit``, else its
``declared_unit``; a length is converted to the requirement's unit with the
exact factors of ``engineering/understand/units.py::MM_PER_UNIT``. Two
undirected lines meet at an angle and at its supplement; ``angle`` reports the
one nearer the nominal - the same pair of lines, not a choice between
features - and ``parallel`` / ``perpendicular`` read the acute angle.

Epsilons: lengths ``RELATIVE_EPSILON`` x the largest coordinate (at least
1e-9 drawing units); angles ``ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG``. A requirement's
``verification_epsilon`` replaces either, in its own unit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

from engineering.understand.units import MM_PER_UNIT
from engineering.verification.binding import feature_of
from engineering.verification.types import Measurement

__all__ = ["ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG", "RELATIVE_EPSILON", "default_epsilon", "evaluate"]

#: Angular verification epsilon, degrees: rotating exact geometry leaves about
#: 1e-12 degrees of noise; a drawn error is orders of magnitude larger.
ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG = 1e-7
#: Relative length epsilon: this fraction of the largest coordinate.
RELATIVE_EPSILON = 1e-9

_ANGULAR = ("angle", "parallel", "perpendicular", "horizontal", "vertical")


def _numbers(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None:
        return
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        yield float(value)
    elif isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        for item in value:
            yield from _numbers(item)


def default_epsilon(observed) -> float:
    """The length epsilon in drawing units: ``RELATIVE_EPSILON`` x the largest
    absolute coordinate of any feature, never below ``RELATIVE_EPSILON``."""
    largest = 1.0
    for feature in observed.features:
        for _key, value in feature.geometry:
            for x in _numbers(value):
                largest = max(largest, abs(x))
    return RELATIVE_EPSILON * largest


def _result(req, value, unit, method, residual, status, **detail) -> Measurement:
    return Measurement(
        requirement_id=req.id,
        value=value,
        unit=unit,
        method=method,
        residual=residual,
        status=status,
        detail=tuple(sorted(detail.items())),
    )


def _drawing_unit(observed) -> str | None:
    return observed.inferred_unit or observed.declared_unit


def _factor(observed, unit: str | None) -> float | None:
    """Drawing units -> requirement units, or None when either is unknown."""
    if unit is None:
        return 1.0
    drawing = _drawing_unit(observed)
    if drawing not in MM_PER_UNIT or unit not in MM_PER_UNIT:
        return None
    return MM_PER_UNIT[drawing] / MM_PER_UNIT[unit]


def _unsupported(features) -> str | None:
    for feature in features:
        if feature.approximation and "tilted_plane" in feature.approximation:
            return "tilted_plane"
        if feature.type == "spline":
            return "spline"
    return None


def _g(feature) -> dict:
    return dict(feature.geometry)


def _direction(line) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
    g = _g(line)
    dx = g["end"][0] - g["start"][0]
    dy = g["end"][1] - g["start"][1]
    length = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    if length == 0.0:
        return None
    return (dx / length, dy / length)


def _acute(u, v) -> float:
    """The acute angle between two undirected directions, degrees in [0, 90]."""
    cross = abs(u[0] * v[1] - u[1] * v[0])
    dot = abs(u[0] * v[0] + u[1] * v[1])
    return math.degrees(math.atan2(cross, dot))


def _point_of(feature) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
    g = _g(feature)
    if feature.type == "point":
        return tuple(g["location"])
    if feature.type in ("circle", "arc"):
        return tuple(g["center"])
    return None


def _point_line(point, line) -> float:
    g = _g(line)
    (x1, y1), (x2, y2) = g["start"], g["end"]
    length = math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1)
    return abs((x2 - x1) * (y1 - point[1]) - (x1 - point[0]) * (y2 - y1)) / length


def _within(req, value: float, nominal: float | None, eps: float) -> tuple[str, float | None, str]:
    """``(status, residual, basis)`` of a dimensional value against its target."""
    tol = dict(req.manufacturing_tolerance or ())
    if nominal is None:
        return "unknown", None, "no_nominal"
    residual = value - nominal
    if "upper" in tol or "lower" in tol:
        upper = float(tol.get("upper", 0.0))
        lower = float(tol.get("lower", 0.0))
        ok = lower - eps <= residual <= upper + eps
        return ("pass" if ok else "fail"), residual, "manufacturing_tolerance"
    return ("pass" if abs(residual) <= eps else "fail"), residual, "verification_epsilon"


def _deviation(req, value: float, eps: float) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """``(status, basis)`` of a constraint whose ideal value is 0."""
    tol = dict(req.manufacturing_tolerance or ())
    if "upper" in tol:
        ok = value <= float(tol["upper"]) + eps
        return ("pass" if ok else "fail"), "manufacturing_tolerance"
    return ("pass" if value <= eps else "fail"), "verification_epsilon"


def _counted(req, features) -> Measurement:
    target = dict(req.target)
    count = len(features)
    if req.quantity == "absence":
        status = "pass" if count == 0 else "fail"
        return _result(req, float(count), "count", "absence", float(count), status)
    ok = True
    if "exact" in target:
        ok = ok and count == int(target["exact"])
    if "min" in target:
        ok = ok and count >= int(target["min"])
    if "max" in target:
        ok = ok and count <= int(target["max"])
    residual = float(count - int(target["exact"])) if "exact" in target else None
    return _result(req, float(count), "count", "count", residual, "pass" if ok else "fail")


def _radial(req, features, nominal, factor, eps, unit) -> Measurement:
    radii = []
    for feature in features:
        g = _g(feature)
        if feature.type in ("circle", "arc"):
            radii.append(float(g["radius"]))
        elif feature.type == "ellipse" and abs(float(g["ratio"]) - 1.0) <= 1e-12:
            radii.append(math.hypot(*g["major_axis"]))
        else:
            return _result(req, None, unit, f"{req.quantity}_of_{feature.type}", None, "unsupported")
    if max(radii) - min(radii) > eps / factor:
        return _result(req, None, unit, req.quantity, None, "ambiguous", radii=tuple(radii))
    value = radii[0] * factor * (2.0 if req.quantity == "diameter" else 1.0)
    status, residual, basis = _within(req, value, nominal, eps)
    method = f"{features[0].type}_{req.quantity}"
    return _result(req, value, unit, method, residual, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)


def _distance(req, a, b, nominal, factor, eps, unit) -> Measurement:
    pa, pb = _point_of(a), _point_of(b)
    if pa is not None and pb is not None:
        raw, method = math.dist(pa, pb), "point_point"
    elif pa is not None or pb is not None:
        at, line = (pa, b) if pa is not None else (pb, a)
        if line.type != "line":
            return _result(req, None, unit, f"distance_point_{line.type}", None, "unsupported")
        if _direction(line) is None:
            return _result(
                req, None, unit, "point_line", None, "unknown", degenerate="zero-length line"
            )
        raw, method = _point_line(at, line), "point_line"
    elif a.type == "line" and b.type == "line":
        u, v = _direction(a), _direction(b)
        if u is None or v is None:
            return _result(
                req, None, unit, "line_line", None, "unknown", degenerate="zero-length line"
            )
        if _acute(u, v) > ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG:
            return _result(
                req, None, unit, "line_line", None, "unknown", degenerate="lines are not parallel"
            )
        raw, method = _point_line(_g(a)["start"], b), "line_line"
    else:
        return _result(req, None, unit, f"distance_{a.type}_{b.type}", None, "unsupported")
    value = raw * factor
    status, residual, basis = _within(req, value, nominal, eps)
    return _result(req, value, unit, method, residual, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)


def _angular(req, features, nominal) -> Measurement:
    q = req.quantity
    if req.verification_epsilon is not None:
        eps = float(req.verification_epsilon)
    else:
        eps = ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG
    if q in ("horizontal", "vertical"):
        if len(features) != 1 or features[0].type != "line":
            return _result(req, None, "deg", q, None, "unsupported")
        u = _direction(features[0])
        if u is None:
            return _result(req, None, "deg", q, None, "unknown", degenerate="zero-length line")
        axis = (1.0, 0.0) if q == "horizontal" else (0.0, 1.0)
        off = _acute(u, axis)
        status, basis = _deviation(req, off, eps)
        return _result(req, off, "deg", f"line_{q}", off, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    if q == "angle" and len(features) == 1 and features[0].type == "arc":
        g = _g(features[0])
        sweep = (g["end_angle"] - g["start_angle"]) % 360.0 or 360.0
        status, residual, basis = _within(req, sweep, nominal, eps)
        return _result(req, sweep, "deg", "arc_sweep", residual, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    if len(features) != 2 or any(f.type != "line" for f in features):
        return _result(req, None, "deg", q, None, "unsupported")
    u, v = _direction(features[0]), _direction(features[1])
    if u is None or v is None:
        return _result(req, None, "deg", q, None, "unknown", degenerate="zero-length line")
    acute = _acute(u, v)
    if q == "parallel":
        status, basis = _deviation(req, acute, eps)
        method = "line_line_parallel"
        return _result(req, acute, "deg", method, acute, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    if q == "perpendicular":
        off = 90.0 - acute
        status, basis = _deviation(req, off, eps)
        method = "line_line_perpendicular"
        return _result(req, off, "deg", method, off, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    if nominal is None:
        value = acute
    else:
        value = min((acute, 180.0 - acute), key=lambda c: abs(c - nominal))
    status, residual, basis = _within(req, value, nominal, eps)
    return _result(req, value, "deg", "line_line_angle", residual, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)


def _ends(feature) -> list:
    g = _g(feature)
    if feature.type == "line":
        return [g["start"], g["end"]]
    if feature.type == "arc":
        cx, cy = g["center"]
        r = float(g["radius"])
        return [
            (
                cx + r * math.cos(math.radians(g[key])),
                cy + r * math.sin(math.radians(g[key])),
            )
            for key in ("start_angle", "end_angle")
        ]
    return []


def _shared_end(a, b, eps: float):
    if "arc" not in (a.type, b.type):
        return None
    for p in _ends(a):
        for q in _ends(b):
            if math.dist(p, q) <= eps:
                return p
    return None


def _tangent_dir(feature, at) -> tuple[float, float]:
    if feature.type == "line":
        return _direction(feature)
    cx, cy = _g(feature)["center"]
    rx, ry = at[0] - cx, at[1] - cy
    length = math.hypot(rx, ry)
    return (-ry / length, rx / length)


def _tangent(req, a, b, factor, eps, unit) -> Measurement:
    kinds = {a.type, b.type}
    if a.type == "line" and b.type == "line":
        return _result(req, None, unit, "tangent_line_line", None, "unsupported")
    if not kinds <= {"line", "circle", "arc"}:
        return _result(req, None, unit, f"tangent_{a.type}_{b.type}", None, "unsupported")
    if (a.type == "line" and _direction(a) is None) or (b.type == "line" and _direction(b) is None):
        return _result(req, None, unit, "tangent", None, "unknown", degenerate="zero-length line")
    shared = _shared_end(a, b, eps / factor)
    if shared is not None:
        off = _acute(_tangent_dir(a, shared), _tangent_dir(b, shared))
        status, basis = _deviation(req, off, ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG)
        return _result(
            req, off, "deg", "arc_continuity", off, status, basis=basis, epsilon=ANGLE_EPSILON_DEG
        )
    if "line" in kinds:
        line, round_ = (a, b) if a.type == "line" else (b, a)
        g = _g(round_)
        value = abs(_point_line(g["center"], line) - float(g["radius"])) * factor
        status, basis = _deviation(req, value, eps)
        method = "line_circle_tangency"
        return _result(req, value, unit, method, value, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    ga, gb = _g(a), _g(b)
    d = math.dist(ga["center"], gb["center"])
    r1, r2 = float(ga["radius"]), float(gb["radius"])
    if d <= eps / factor and abs(r1 - r2) <= eps / factor:
        return _result(
            req, None, unit, "circle_circle_tangency", None, "unknown", degenerate="coincident circles"
        )
    value = min(abs(d - (r1 + r2)), abs(d - abs(r1 - r2))) * factor
    status, basis = _deviation(req, value, eps)
    method = "circle_circle_tangency"
    return _result(req, value, unit, method, value, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)


def evaluate(req, binding, observed, *, epsilon: float | None = None) -> Measurement:
    """Measure one requirement on the features its binding names.

    ``epsilon`` (drawing units) replaces :func:`default_epsilon`; a
    requirement's own ``verification_epsilon`` (its unit) wins over both.
    """
    target = dict(req.target)
    unit = target.get("unit")
    nominal = float(target["nominal"]) if target.get("nominal") is not None else None
    quantity = req.quantity
    if binding.status != "bound":
        status = "ambiguous" if binding.status == "ambiguous" else "unknown"
        return _result(req, None, unit or "", "binding", None, status, binding=binding.reason)
    features = [feature_of(ref, observed) for ref in binding.refs]
    if any(f is None for f in features):
        return _result(
            req, None, unit or "", "binding", None, "unknown",
            binding="a bound feature is not in this geometry",
        )
    if quantity in ("count", "absence"):
        return _counted(req, features)
    why = _unsupported(features)
    if why:
        return _result(req, None, unit or "", why, None, "unsupported")
    if quantity in _ANGULAR:
        return _angular(req, features, nominal)
    factor = _factor(observed, unit)
    if factor is None:
        return _result(
            req, None, unit or "", "units", None, "unknown", units="the drawing unit is unknown"
        )
    if req.verification_epsilon is not None:
        eps = float(req.verification_epsilon)
    else:
        eps = (epsilon if epsilon is not None else default_epsilon(observed)) * factor
    out_unit = unit or (_drawing_unit(observed) or "")
    if quantity in ("radius", "diameter"):
        return _radial(req, features, nominal, factor, eps, out_unit)
    if len(features) != 2:
        return _result(req, None, out_unit, quantity, None, "unknown", features=len(features))
    a, b = features
    if quantity == "distance":
        return _distance(req, a, b, nominal, factor, eps, out_unit)
    if quantity in ("concentric", "coincident"):
        pa, pb = _point_of(a), _point_of(b)
        round_pair = a.type in ("circle", "arc") and b.type in ("circle", "arc")
        if pa is None or pb is None or (quantity == "concentric" and not round_pair):
            return _result(req, None, out_unit, f"{quantity}_{a.type}_{b.type}", None, "unsupported")
        value = math.dist(pa, pb) * factor
        status, basis = _deviation(req, value, eps)
        method = "centre_distance" if quantity == "concentric" else "point_distance"
        return _result(req, value, out_unit, method, value, status, basis=basis, epsilon=eps)
    if quantity == "tangent":
        return _tangent(req, a, b, factor, eps, out_unit)
    return _result(req, None, out_unit, quantity, None, "unsupported")
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Add `point_intersection_detail` to `backends/contracts/premium.py`**

Add above the class that holds `point_intersection` (module level, after the imports):

```python
#: Slack on a segment's parameter: an intersection computed at a drawn end
#: lands within it.
_ON_SEGMENT_TOL = 1e-9


def _on_entity(kind: str, props: dict, point) -> bool:
    """Does ``point`` - already on the infinite line, or on the circle - lie on
    the drawn entity? A LINE checks the parameter along its segment."""
    if kind != "LINE":
        return True
    x1, y1 = float(props["start"][0]), float(props["start"][1])
    x2, y2 = float(props["end"][0]), float(props["end"][1])
    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
    length2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if length2 == 0.0:
        return math.dist((x1, y1), point) <= _ON_SEGMENT_TOL
    t = ((point[0] - x1) * dx + (point[1] - y1) * dy) / length2
    return -_ON_SEGMENT_TOL <= t <= 1.0 + _ON_SEGMENT_TOL
```

Replace the whole `point_intersection` method with:

```python
    async def point_intersection(
        self,
        handle1: str,
        handle2: str,
        ref_x: float | None = None,
        ref_y: float | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Return the intersection point of two geometry entities.

        Supports LINE-LINE, LINE-CIRCLE, and CIRCLE-CIRCLE cases. When
        multiple intersections exist (e.g. two circles), ``ref_x``/``ref_y``
        selects the candidate nearest to that reference point. If omitted the
        first candidate is returned. ``point_intersection_detail`` also says
        whether the point lies on both drawn entities.

        Raises RuntimeError when no real intersection exists or entity types
        are unsupported.
        """
        found = await self.point_intersection_detail(handle1, handle2, ref_x, ref_y)
        return (found["x"], found["y"])

    async def point_intersection_detail(
        self,
        handle1: str,
        handle2: str,
        ref_x: float | None = None,
        ref_y: float | None = None,
    ) -> dict:
        """The intersection point and whether it lies on both drawn entities (v1.7.0, B18).

        A LINE is intersected as the infinite line through it, so the point may
        lie beyond a segment's ends: ``on_first`` / ``on_second`` say whether it
        lies on each drawn segment (its parameter along the segment within
        [0, 1], to 1e-9); a CIRCLE always holds its own intersection points.
        Returns ``{"x", "y", "on_first", "on_second", "on_both", "candidates"}``.
        Refusals are ``point_intersection``'s.
        """
        info1 = await self.entity_get(handle1)
        info2 = await self.entity_get(handle2)
        t1, t2 = info1.type.upper(), info2.type.upper()
        props1, props2 = info1.properties, info2.properties

        candidates: list[tuple[float, float]] = []

        if t1 == "LINE" and t2 == "LINE":
            # Line-line intersection via parametric form
            x1, y1 = props1["start"][0], props1["start"][1]
            x2, y2 = props1["end"][0], props1["end"][1]
            x3, y3 = props2["start"][0], props2["start"][1]
            x4, y4 = props2["end"][0], props2["end"][1]
            dx1, dy1 = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
            dx2, dy2 = x4 - x3, y4 - y3
            denom = dx1 * dy2 - dy1 * dx2
            if abs(denom) < 1e-12:
                raise RuntimeError("point_intersection: lines are parallel or coincident.")
            t = ((x3 - x1) * dy2 - (y3 - y1) * dx2) / denom
            candidates = [(x1 + t * dx1, y1 + t * dy1)]

        elif {t1, t2} == {"LINE", "CIRCLE"}:
            line_p = props1 if t1 == "LINE" else props2
            circ_p = props2 if t1 == "LINE" else props1
            lx1, ly1 = line_p["start"][0], line_p["start"][1]
            lx2, ly2 = line_p["end"][0], line_p["end"][1]
            cx, cy = circ_p["center"][0], circ_p["center"][1]
            r = circ_p["radius"]
            dx, dy = lx2 - lx1, ly2 - ly1
            fx, fy = lx1 - cx, ly1 - cy
            a = dx * dx + dy * dy
            b = 2.0 * (fx * dx + fy * dy)
            c = fx * fx + fy * fy - r * r
            disc = b * b - 4.0 * a * c
            if disc < 0:
                raise RuntimeError("point_intersection: line does not intersect circle.")
            sq = math.sqrt(disc)
            for sign in (1, -1):
                t = (-b + sign * sq) / (2.0 * a)
                candidates.append((lx1 + t * dx, ly1 + t * dy))

        elif t1 == "CIRCLE" and t2 == "CIRCLE":
            cx1, cy1, r1 = props1["center"][0], props1["center"][1], props1["radius"]
            cx2, cy2, r2 = props2["center"][0], props2["center"][1], props2["radius"]
            dx, dy = cx2 - cx1, cy2 - cy1
            d = math.hypot(dx, dy)
            if d < 1e-12:
                raise RuntimeError("point_intersection: circles are concentric.")
            if d > r1 + r2 + 1e-9 or d < abs(r1 - r2) - 1e-9:
                raise RuntimeError("point_intersection: circles do not intersect.")
            a = (r1 * r1 - r2 * r2 + d * d) / (2.0 * d)
            h_sq = r1 * r1 - a * a
            h = math.sqrt(max(h_sq, 0.0))
            mx = cx1 + a * dx / d
            my = cy1 + a * dy / d
            candidates = [
                (mx + h * dy / d, my - h * dx / d),
                (mx - h * dy / d, my + h * dx / d),
            ]

        else:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"point_intersection: unsupported entity combination {t1}+{t2}. "
                "Supported: LINE-LINE, LINE-CIRCLE, CIRCLE-CIRCLE."
            )

        if len(candidates) > 1 and ref_x is not None and ref_y is not None:
            chosen = min(
                candidates,
                key=lambda p: (p[0] - float(ref_x)) ** 2 + (p[1] - float(ref_y)) ** 2,
            )
        else:
            chosen = candidates[0]
        on_first = _on_entity(t1, props1, chosen)
        on_second = _on_entity(t2, props2, chosen)
        return {
            "x": float(chosen[0]),
            "y": float(chosen[1]),
            "on_first": on_first,
            "on_second": on_second,
            "on_both": on_first and on_second,
            "candidates": len(candidates),
        }
```

In `server.py` replace the `point_intersection` registration with:

```python
@cad_tool(summary="Find where two lines or circles cross, exactly.", cost="read")
@mcp.tool(
    annotations={"title": "Point: Intersection (deterministic)", "readOnlyHint": True},
    tags={"premium", "snap"},
)
async def point_intersection(
    handle1: Annotated[str, "First entity handle (LINE or CIRCLE)"],
    handle2: Annotated[str, "Second entity handle (LINE or CIRCLE)"],
    ref_x: Annotated[
        float | None, "Reference X to pick nearest candidate when multiple exist"
    ] = None,
    ref_y: Annotated[float | None, "Reference Y"] = None,
    ctx: Context = None,
) -> dict:
    """Compute the intersection of two geometry entities (LINE-LINE, LINE-CIRCLE,
    CIRCLE-CIRCLE). When two candidates exist, ref_x/ref_y selects the nearest.

    Returns {x, y, on_first, on_second, on_both, candidates}. A LINE is
    intersected as the infinite line through it, so the point can lie beyond
    a drawn end: `on_first` / `on_second` say whether it lies on each drawn
    segment (a CIRCLE always holds its own intersection points) and `on_both`
    whether on both - check it before using the point as a corner.
    `candidates` is how many intersections existed. Refused, with the case
    named: parallel or coincident lines, a line that misses the circle,
    concentric circles, circles that do not meet, any other entity pair.
    """
    found = await _backend(ctx).point_intersection_detail(handle1, handle2, ref_x, ref_y)
    return {key: found[key] for key in ("x", "y", "on_first", "on_second", "on_both", "candidates")}
```

Create `tests/test_verification_report.py`:

```python
"""Task 16 (B22, F02): the acceptance report and verify_requirements.

Every expected number is hand geometry written here, never read back from the
module under test: a circle of radius r has diameter 2r, so a bore drawn at
r = 20 is Ø40 and one drawn at r = 21 is Ø42 (delta +2 against a nominal 40).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import ezdxf
import pytest

from engineering.layers import ensure_engineering_layers, ensure_standard_linetypes
from engineering.verification.report import (
    SCHEMA_VERSION,
    attach_dimension_evidence,
    build_report,
    document_identity_of,
    mark_stale,
    render_markdown,
    summarize,
    verify_requirements,
)
from engineering.verification.types import (
    Binding,
    FeatureRef,
    FeatureSelector,
    Measurement,
    ObservedGeometry,
    Requirement,
    RequirementSet,
)

OBSERVED = ObservedGeometry(
    features=(),
    declared_unit="mm",
    inferred_unit="mm",
    document_id="d1",
    revision=3,
    source="memory:untitled",
)
DOCUMENT = {
    "document_id": "d1",
    "revision": 3,
    "name": "untitled.dxf",
    "path": None,
    "backend": "ezdxf",
}
DIAMETER_40 = (("nominal", 40.0), ("unit", "mm"))


def _req(rid, handle, *, quantity="diameter", target=DIAMETER_40, tolerance=None):
    return Requirement(
        id=rid,
        quantity=quantity,
        target=target,
        features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", handle),)),),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("document", "sheet-1"),),
        manufacturing_tolerance=tolerance,
    )


def _ref(handle):
    return FeatureRef(
        document_id="d1",
        revision=3,
        space="Model",
        block_path=(),
        handles=(handle,),
        feature=None,
    )


def _bound(rid, handle):
    return Binding(
        requirement_id=rid,
        status="bound",
        refs=(_ref(handle),),
        candidates=(),
        reason=f"handle {handle}",
    )


def _unbound(rid, reason):
    return Binding(requirement_id=rid, status="unknown", refs=(), candidates=(), reason=reason)


def _measured(rid, value, status, method="circle_diameter"):
    return Measurement(
        requirement_id=rid, value=value, unit="mm", method=method, residual=None, status=status
    )


def _seven():
    """Seven requirements, one per way a row can end (statuses assigned by hand)."""
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(
            _req("R1", "2A"),  # Ø40 measured 40: pass
            _req("R2", "2B"),  # Ø40 measured 42: fail, delta +2
            _req("R3", "FFFF0", quantity="distance", target=(("nominal", 100.0), ("unit", "mm"))),
            _req("R4", "2C", quantity="radius", target=(("nominal", 5.0), ("unit", "mm"))),
            _req("R5", "2D", quantity="count", target=(("exact", 6),)),
            _req("R6", "2E"),  # nothing was produced for it at all
            _req("R7", "2F"),  # unbound, yet an evaluator said pass
        ),
        units="mm",
    )
    bindings = (
        _bound("R1", "2A"),
        _bound("R2", "2B"),
        _unbound("R3", "no entity has handle FFFF0"),
        _bound("R4", "2C"),
        Binding(
            requirement_id="R5",
            status="ambiguous",
            refs=(),
            candidates=(_ref("2D"), _ref("3D")),
            reason="two equal candidates",
        ),
        _unbound("R7", "no entity has handle 2F"),
    )
    measurements = (
        _measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),
        _measured("R2", 42.0, "fail"),
        _measured("R3", None, "unknown", method="binding"),
        _measured("R4", None, "unsupported", method="spline"),
        _measured("R5", None, "unknown", method="count"),
        _measured("R7", 40.0, "pass"),
    )
    return rs, bindings, measurements


def _rows(report):
    return {row["id"]: row for row in report["rows"]}


def test_every_requirement_gets_one_row_and_the_statuses_add_up():
    rs, bindings, measurements = _seven()
    report = build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT)
    assert report["schema_version"] == SCHEMA_VERSION == 1
    assert [row["id"] for row in report["rows"]] == ["R1", "R2", "R3", "R4", "R5", "R6", "R7"]
    assert report["counts"] == {
        "required": 7,
        "pass": 1,
        "fail": 1,
        "unknown": 3,
        "unsupported": 1,
        "ambiguous": 1,
    }
    statuses = ("pass", "fail", "unknown", "unsupported", "ambiguous")
    assert sum(report["counts"][status] for status in statuses) == 7
    assert report["status"] == "fail"
    assert report["accepted"] is False
    assert report["coverage"] == {"required": 7, "measured": 2, "ratio": 2 / 7}
    assert report["requirements_digest"] == rs.digest()
    assert report["evidence"]["document_id"] == "d1"
    assert report["evidence"]["revision"] == 3


def test_no_silent_pass():
    rs, bindings, measurements = _seven()
    rows = _rows(build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT))
    assert (rows["R3"]["status"], rows["R3"]["kind"]) == ("unknown", "missing_requirement")
    assert (rows["R4"]["status"], rows["R4"]["kind"]) == ("unsupported", "unsupported_measurement")
    assert (rows["R5"]["status"], rows["R5"]["kind"]) == ("ambiguous", "ambiguous_binding")
    assert len(rows["R5"]["binding"]["candidates"]) == 2
    assert (rows["R6"]["status"], rows["R6"]["kind"]) == ("unknown", "missing_requirement")
    assert rows["R6"]["measured"] is None and rows["R6"]["binding"] is None
    assert (rows["R7"]["status"], rows["R7"]["kind"]) == ("unknown", "missing_requirement"), (
        "an evaluator's pass on an unbound requirement is not a pass"
    )
    assert rows["R7"]["delta"] is None


def test_a_row_carries_target_measured_delta_basis_handles_and_source():
    rs, bindings, measurements = _seven()
    rows = _rows(build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT))
    r2 = rows["R2"]
    assert r2["target"] == {"nominal": 40.0, "unit": "mm"}
    assert r2["measured"]["value"] == 42.0
    assert r2["measured"]["unit"] == "mm"
    assert abs(r2["delta"] - 2.0) <= 1e-9  # 42 - 40
    assert r2["tolerance_basis"] == "verification_epsilon"
    assert r2["handles"] == ["2B"]
    assert r2["source"] == {"document": "sheet-1"}
    assert r2["provenance"] == "explicit"
    assert rows["R1"]["delta"] == 0.0  # 40 - 40


def test_a_manufacturing_tolerance_with_its_source_is_the_basis():
    tolerance = (("lower", -0.1), ("source", "drawing note 3"), ("upper", 0.1))
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", "2A", tolerance=tolerance),), units="mm")
    report = build_report(
        rs,
        (_bound("R1", "2A"),),
        (_measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),),
        observed=OBSERVED,
        document=DOCUMENT,
    )
    row = report["rows"][0]
    assert row["tolerance_basis"] == "manufacturing_tolerance"
    assert row["tolerance"] == {
        "manufacturing_tolerance": {"lower": -0.1, "source": "drawing note 3", "upper": 0.1},
        "verification_epsilon": None,
    }


def test_an_empty_set_verifies_nothing_and_carries_the_legacy_dimensions():
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(), units="mm", legacy=(("dimensions", (("bore", 40.0),)),)
    )
    report = build_report(rs, (), (), observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT)
    assert report["status"] == "no_requirements"
    assert report["accepted"] is False
    assert report["counts"]["required"] == 0
    assert report["coverage"]["ratio"] is None
    assert report["legacy"] == {"dimensions": [["bore", 40.0]]}


def test_every_requirement_passing_is_accepted():
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", "2A"),), units="mm")
    report = build_report(
        rs, (_bound("R1", "2A"),), (_measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),), observed=OBSERVED,
        document=DOCUMENT,
    )
    assert (report["status"], report["accepted"], report["blocking"]) == ("pass", True, [])


def test_the_evidence_goes_stale_when_the_revision_moves():
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", "2A"),), units="mm")
    report = build_report(
        rs, (_bound("R1", "2A"),), (_measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),), observed=OBSERVED,
        document=DOCUMENT,
    )
    fresh = mark_stale(report, DOCUMENT)
    assert (fresh["stale"], fresh["accepted"]) == (False, True)
    moved = mark_stale(report, {**DOCUMENT, "revision": 4})
    assert moved["stale"] is True and moved["accepted"] is False
    assert "3" in moved["stale_reason"] and "4" in moved["stale_reason"]
    assert all(row["stale"] for row in moved["rows"])
    other = mark_stale(report, {**DOCUMENT, "document_id": "d2"})
    assert other["stale"] is True and "d2" in other["stale_reason"]


def test_the_evidence_goes_stale_when_the_artifact_changes(tmp_path):
    artifact = tmp_path / "part.dxf"
    artifact.write_bytes(b"0\nEOF\n")
    record = {
        "path": str(artifact),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"0\nEOF\n").hexdigest(),
        "size_bytes": 6,
    }
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", "2A"),), units="mm")
    report = build_report(
        rs,
        (_bound("R1", "2A"),),
        (_measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),),
        observed=OBSERVED,
        document={"document_id": None, "revision": None, "name": "part.dxf", "path": str(artifact)},
        artifact=record,
    )
    assert mark_stale(report, None)["stale"] is False
    artifact.write_bytes(b"0\nSECTION\n0\nEOF\n")
    changed = mark_stale(report, None)
    assert changed["stale"] is True and "changed after it was measured" in changed["stale_reason"]
    artifact.unlink()
    assert "is gone" in mark_stale(report, None)["stale_reason"]


def test_a_dimension_that_disagrees_with_its_geometry_is_a_blocking_finding():
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", "2A"),), units="mm")
    report = build_report(
        rs, (_bound("R1", "2A"),), (_measured("R1", 40.0, "pass"),), observed=OBSERVED,
        document=DOCUMENT,
    )
    agreeing = attach_dimension_evidence(
        report,
        {
            "R1": {
                "handle": "3A",
                "refs": (),
                "geometric_value": 40.0,
                "dimension_measurement": 40.0,
                "displayed_text": "40",
                "mismatch": False,
            }
        },
    )
    assert agreeing["findings"] == [] and agreeing["accepted"] is True
    lying = attach_dimension_evidence(
        report,
        {
            "R1": {
                "handle": "3A",
                "refs": (),
                "geometric_value": 42.0,
                "dimension_measurement": 42.0,
                "displayed_text": "40",
                "mismatch": True,
            }
        },
    )
    (finding,) = lying["findings"]
    assert (finding["kind"], finding["severity"], finding["requirement"]) == (
        "dimension_mismatch",
        "error",
        "R1",
    )
    assert lying["rows"][0]["dimension"]["displayed_text"] == "40"
    assert lying["accepted"] is False
    assert "dimension_mismatch: R1" in lying["blocking"]


def test_markdown_lists_every_row_and_the_denominators():
    rs, bindings, measurements = _seven()
    text = render_markdown(
        build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT)
    )
    assert text.startswith("# Acceptance report\n")
    assert "| 7 | 1 | 1 | 3 | 1 | 1 |" in text
    assert (
        "| R1 | diameter | 40 mm | 40 | +0 | verification_epsilon | pass | 2A | document=sheet-1 |"
        in text
    )
    assert (
        "| R2 | diameter | 40 mm | 42 | +2 | verification_epsilon | fail | 2B | document=sheet-1 |"
        in text
    )
    assert (
        "| R5 | count | exactly 6 | - | - | verification_epsilon | ambiguous | - | document=sheet-1 |"
        in text
    )
    assert "## Not accepted because" in text


def test_summarize_keeps_the_verdict_and_drops_the_rows():
    rs, bindings, measurements = _seven()
    summary = summarize(
        build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, observed=OBSERVED, document=DOCUMENT)
    )
    assert set(summary) == {
        "status",
        "accepted",
        "stale",
        "stale_reason",
        "counts",
        "coverage",
        "blocking",
        "requirements_digest",
    }
    assert summary["status"] == "fail"


# -- on real CAD geometry (headless engine) ------------------------------------------


async def _open_part(backend, tmp_path):
    """A 200 x 100 frame, a Ø40 bore (r = 20) and a Ø42 bore (r = 21), in mm."""
    base = tmp_path / "base.dxf"
    ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4).saveas(base)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(base))
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    frame = await backend.entity_create_polyline(
        [[0, 0], [200, 0], [200, 100], [0, 100]], closed=True, layer="GEOMETRY"
    )
    bore = await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 50, 20, layer="GEOMETRY")
    wide = await backend.entity_create_circle(150, 50, 21, layer="GEOMETRY")
    return {"frame": frame.handle, "bore": bore.handle, "wide": wide.handle}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_verify_requirements_measures_the_real_circles(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(
            _req("R1", handles["bore"]),
            _req("R2", handles["wide"]),
            _req("R3", "FFFF0"),
        ),
        units="mm",
    )
    report = await verify_requirements(backend, rs)
    rows = _rows(report)
    assert rows["R1"]["status"] == "pass"
    assert abs(rows["R1"]["measured"]["value"] - 40.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 20
    assert rows["R2"]["status"] == "fail"
    assert abs(rows["R2"]["measured"]["value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 21
    assert abs(rows["R2"]["delta"] - 2.0) <= 1e-9
    assert rows["R2"]["handles"] == [handles["wide"]]
    assert (rows["R3"]["status"], rows["R3"]["kind"]) == ("unknown", "missing_requirement")
    assert report["counts"] == {
        "required": 3,
        "pass": 1,
        "fail": 1,
        "unknown": 1,
        "unsupported": 0,
        "ambiguous": 0,
    }
    identity = await document_identity_of(backend)
    assert report["evidence"]["document_id"] == identity["document_id"]
    assert report["evidence"]["revision"] == identity["revision"]
    assert report["stale"] is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_evidence_goes_stale_when_the_drawing_changes(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", handles["bore"]),), units="mm")
    report = await verify_requirements(backend, rs)
    assert report["accepted"] is True
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0, layer="GEOMETRY")
    later = mark_stale(report, await document_identity_of(backend))
    assert later["stale"] is True
    assert later["accepted"] is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_dimension_that_says_40_over_42_fails_and_is_a_finding(backend, tmp_path):
    """Spec §8.2 item 8: the text says 40, the geometry measures 42, DIMLFAC is 2."""
    doc = ezdxf.new("R2010", setup=True, units=4)
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    msp.add_lwpolyline([(0, 0), (200, 0), (200, 100), (0, 100)], close=True)
    msp.add_circle((50, 50), 21)  # measures Ø42
    dim = msp.add_diameter_dim(
        center=(50, 50),
        radius=21,
        angle=0,  # defpoints on the 0 and 180 degree quadrants: (71, 50) and (29, 50)
        text="40",
        override={"dimlfac": 2.0},
        dimstyle="EZ_RADIUS",
    )
    dim.render()
    path = tmp_path / "dim.dxf"
    doc.saveas(path)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(path))
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(
            Requirement(
                id="RD",
                quantity="diameter",
                target=DIAMETER_40,
                features=(
                    FeatureSelector(kind="dimension", value=(("handle", dim.dimension.dxf.handle),)),
                ),
                provenance="explicit",
                source=(("label", "Ø40"),),
            ),
        ),
        units="mm",
    )
    report = await verify_requirements(backend, rs)
    (row,) = report["rows"]
    assert row["status"] == "fail"
    assert abs(row["measured"]["value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9  # the geometry: 2 x 21
    assert abs(row["delta"] - 2.0) <= 1e-9
    assert row["dimension"]["displayed_text"] == "40"
    assert abs(row["dimension"]["geometric_value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9
    assert row["dimension"]["dimension_measurement"] is not None
    assert row["dimension"]["mismatch"] is True
    assert [finding["kind"] for finding in report["findings"]] == ["dimension_mismatch"]
    assert report["accepted"] is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_an_artifact_is_measured_on_the_file_not_on_the_document(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    artifact = tmp_path / "artifact.dxf"
    await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(artifact))
    edited = ezdxf.readfile(artifact)
    edited.entitydb[handles["bore"]].dxf.radius = 21.0  # the saved bore is Ø42 now
    edited.saveas(artifact)
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", handles["bore"]),), units="mm")
    on_document = await verify_requirements(backend, rs)
    on_file = await verify_requirements(backend, rs, artifact_path=str(artifact))
    assert _rows(on_document)["R1"]["status"] == "pass"
    row = _rows(on_file)["R1"]
    assert row["status"] == "fail"
    assert abs(row["measured"]["value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 21
    assert on_file["artifact"]["sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert on_file["document"]["document_id"] is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_drawing_with_a_block_reference_is_measured_not_refused(backend, tmp_path):
    """Task 13's observe() refuses a snapshot holding an INSERT without its block
    definitions; the document and the artifact are both read with block_defs=True."""
    doc = ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4)
    mark = doc.blocks.new("MARK")
    mark.add_circle((0, 0), 5)
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    bore = msp.add_circle((50, 50), 20)  # Ø40: 2 x 20
    msp.add_blockref("MARK", (150, 50))
    path = tmp_path / "with_block.dxf"
    doc.saveas(path)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(path))
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_req("R1", bore.dxf.handle),), units="mm")
    on_document = await verify_requirements(backend, rs)
    on_file = await verify_requirements(backend, rs, artifact_path=str(path))
    assert _rows(on_document)["R1"]["status"] == "pass"
    assert _rows(on_file)["R1"]["status"] == "pass"
```

Expected: FAIL — collection error `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'engineering.verification.report'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `engineering/verification/report.py`**

```python
"""Acceptance report: every requirement measured on the CAD geometry, with its evidence.

Spec §5 Task 16 (B22, F02). One row per requirement - target, measured value,
delta, tolerance basis, status, the handles it was measured on and where the
requirement came from - and the denominators ``required`` / ``pass`` /
``fail`` / ``unknown`` / ``unsupported`` / ``ambiguous``. The five statuses
always add up to ``required``.

No silent pass:

* a requirement with no binding or no measurement is ``unknown``
  (``kind: missing_requirement``), whatever else was produced;
* a binding that found nothing is ``unknown`` even when an evaluator said
  ``pass``;
* a binding with two equal candidates is ``ambiguous``
  (``kind: ambiguous_binding``) - never a pick;
* a measurement the evaluators cannot make (a spline, a tilted plane) is
  ``unsupported`` (``kind: unsupported_measurement``).

The report as a whole is ``pass`` only when every requirement passes, ``fail``
when any fails, ``incomplete`` otherwise and ``no_requirements`` for a set
without a typed requirement (a legacy ``dimensions`` dict is carried read-only
and verifies nothing). ``accepted`` is true only for a ``pass`` report that is
not stale and carries no ``error`` finding; ``blocking`` says why it is not. A
DIMENSION whose displayed text or own measurement disagrees with the geometry
it annotates (a text override, a DIMLFAC) is an ``error`` finding
``dimension_mismatch``: the geometry decides the row, the finding keeps the
annotation honest.

Evidence is stale when what it was measured on moved: :func:`mark_stale`
compares the report's evidence (the document id and content revision at the
time of measurement, or an artifact's sha256) with the present.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import dataclasses
import hashlib
import inspect
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from engineering.understand.snapshot import Snapshot, take_snapshot
from engineering.verification.binding import bind, bind_dimension
from engineering.verification.measure import default_epsilon, evaluate
from engineering.verification.observe import observe
from engineering.verification.types import (
    AcceptanceItem,
    Binding,
    FeatureRef,
    Measurement,
    ObservedGeometry,
    Requirement,
    RequirementSet,
)

__all__ = [
    "AMBIGUOUS_BINDING",
    "BINDING_POLICIES",
    "DIMENSION_MISMATCH",
    "MISSING_REQUIREMENT",
    "SCHEMA_VERSION",
    "STATUSES",
    "UNSUPPORTED_MEASUREMENT",
    "MeasuredRun",
    "acceptance_items",
    "add_finding",
    "attach_dimension_evidence",
    "build_report",
    "dimension_evidence",
    "document_identity_of",
    "mark_stale",
    "measure_requirements",
    "render_markdown",
    "sha256_file",
    "summarize",
    "verify_requirements",
]

SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
STATUSES = ("pass", "fail", "unknown", "unsupported", "ambiguous")
BINDING_POLICIES = ("strict", "lenient")
MISSING_REQUIREMENT = "missing_requirement"
AMBIGUOUS_BINDING = "ambiguous_binding"
UNSUPPORTED_MEASUREMENT = "unsupported_measurement"
DIMENSION_MISMATCH = "dimension_mismatch"


def sha256_file(path) -> str:
    """The sha256 of a file, read in 1 MB chunks."""
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _jsonable(value):
    """``value`` as plain JSON: tuples become lists, dataclasses dicts, NaN a string."""
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, str, int)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return value if math.isfinite(value) else repr(value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(key): _jsonable(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return _jsonable(dataclasses.asdict(value))
    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        return [_jsonable(item) for item in value]
    return str(value)


def _number(value) -> float | None:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return None
    number = float(value)
    return number if math.isfinite(number) else None


def _ref_dict(ref: FeatureRef) -> dict:
    return {
        "space": ref.space,
        "block_path": list(ref.block_path),
        "handles": list(ref.handles),
        "feature": ref.feature,
    }


def _row_status(
    requirement: Requirement, binding: Binding | None, measurement: Measurement | None
) -> tuple[str, str | None, str]:
    """``(status, kind, reason)`` of one row - the no-silent-pass rules."""
    if (
        binding is None
        or measurement is None
        or binding.requirement_id != requirement.id
        or measurement.requirement_id != requirement.id
    ):
        return (
            "unknown",
            MISSING_REQUIREMENT,
            "no binding or no measurement was produced for this requirement",
        )
    if binding.status == "ambiguous":
        return "ambiguous", AMBIGUOUS_BINDING, binding.reason
    if binding.status != "bound":
        return "unknown", MISSING_REQUIREMENT, binding.reason
    if measurement.status == "unsupported":
        return (
            "unsupported",
            UNSUPPORTED_MEASUREMENT,
            f"{measurement.method} is not a measurement the evaluators make",
        )
    if measurement.status == "ambiguous":
        return "ambiguous", AMBIGUOUS_BINDING, binding.reason
    if measurement.status in ("pass", "fail"):
        return measurement.status, None, binding.reason
    return "unknown", None, f"the evaluator could not decide ({measurement.method})"


def _delta(requirement: Requirement, measurement: Measurement | None, status: str):
    """measured - target, for a measured row (pass or fail) only."""
    if status not in ("pass", "fail") or measurement is None:
        return None
    value = _number(measurement.value)
    target = dict(requirement.target)
    reference = _number(target.get("nominal"))
    if reference is None:
        reference = _number(target.get("exact"))
    if value is None or reference is None:
        return None
    return value - reference


def _tolerance_basis(requirement: Requirement) -> str:
    if requirement.manufacturing_tolerance:
        return "manufacturing_tolerance"
    return "verification_epsilon"


def acceptance_items(rs: RequirementSet, bindings, measurements) -> tuple[AcceptanceItem, ...]:
    """One ``AcceptanceItem`` per requirement; a missing binding or measurement
    is filled with an explicit ``unknown`` placeholder, never left out."""
    by_binding = {b.requirement_id: b for b in bindings}
    by_measure = {m.requirement_id: m for m in measurements}
    items = []
    for requirement in rs.requirements:
        binding = by_binding.get(requirement.id)
        measurement = by_measure.get(requirement.id)
        status, _kind, _reason = _row_status(requirement, binding, measurement)
        if binding is None:
            binding = Binding(
                requirement_id=requirement.id,
                status="unknown",
                refs=(),
                candidates=(),
                reason="no binding was produced for this requirement",
            )
        if measurement is None:
            measurement = Measurement(
                requirement_id=requirement.id,
                value=None,
                unit=str(dict(requirement.target).get("unit", "")),
                method="none",
                residual=None,
                status="unknown",
            )
        items.append(
            AcceptanceItem(
                requirement=requirement,
                binding=binding,
                measurement=measurement,
                delta=_delta(requirement, measurement, status),
                tolerance_basis=_tolerance_basis(requirement),
            )
        )
    return tuple(items)


def _row(requirement: Requirement, binding: Binding | None, measurement: Measurement | None):
    status, kind, reason = _row_status(requirement, binding, measurement)
    refs = binding.refs if binding is not None else ()
    tolerance = requirement.manufacturing_tolerance
    return {
        "id": requirement.id,
        "quantity": requirement.quantity,
        "target": _jsonable(dict(requirement.target)),
        "measured": None
        if measurement is None
        else {
            "value": _number(measurement.value),
            "unit": measurement.unit,
            "method": measurement.method,
            "residual": _number(measurement.residual),
            "detail": _jsonable(dict(measurement.detail)),
        },
        "delta": _delta(requirement, measurement, status),
        "tolerance_basis": _tolerance_basis(requirement),
        "tolerance": {
            "manufacturing_tolerance": _jsonable(dict(tolerance)) if tolerance else None,
            "verification_epsilon": requirement.verification_epsilon,
        },
        "status": status,
        "kind": kind,
        "reason": reason,
        "binding": None
        if binding is None
        else {
            "status": binding.status,
            "reason": binding.reason,
            "candidates": [_ref_dict(ref) for ref in binding.candidates],
        },
        "features": [_ref_dict(ref) for ref in refs],
        "handles": sorted({handle for ref in refs for handle in ref.handles}),
        "source": _jsonable(dict(requirement.source)),
        "provenance": requirement.provenance,
        "datum": requirement.datum,
        "stale": False,
    }


def _overall(counts: dict) -> str:
    if counts["required"] == 0:
        return "no_requirements"
    if counts["fail"]:
        return "fail"
    if counts["pass"] == counts["required"]:
        return "pass"
    return "incomplete"


def _settle(report: dict) -> dict:
    """Recompute ``blocking`` and ``accepted`` from the rows, findings and staleness."""
    blocking: list[str] = []
    if report["status"] == "no_requirements":
        blocking.append("no_requirements: the set holds no typed requirement")
    blocking.extend(
        f"{row['status']}: {row['id']}" for row in report["rows"] if row["status"] != "pass"
    )
    blocking.extend(
        f"{finding['kind']}: {finding['requirement']}"
        for finding in report["findings"]
        if finding.get("severity") == "error"
    )
    if report["stale"]:
        blocking.append(f"stale: {report['stale_reason']}")
    report["blocking"] = blocking
    report["accepted"] = report["status"] == "pass" and not blocking
    return report


def build_report(rs, bindings, measurements, *, observed, document, artifact=None) -> dict:
    """The acceptance report (JSON, ``schema_version`` 1) of one measurement run."""
    by_binding = {b.requirement_id: b for b in bindings}
    by_measure = {m.requirement_id: m for m in measurements}
    rows = [
        _row(requirement, by_binding.get(requirement.id), by_measure.get(requirement.id))
        for requirement in rs.requirements
    ]
    counts = {"required": len(rows), **{status: 0 for status in STATUSES}}
    for row in rows:
        counts[row["status"]] += 1
    measured = counts["pass"] + counts["fail"]
    identity = dict(document or {})
    report = {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "status": _overall(counts),
        "accepted": False,
        "stale": False,
        "stale_reason": None,
        "counts": counts,
        "coverage": {
            "required": counts["required"],
            "measured": measured,
            "ratio": measured / counts["required"] if counts["required"] else None,
        },
        "rows": rows,
        "findings": [],
        "blocking": [],
        "document": _jsonable(identity),
        "evidence": {
            "document_id": identity.get("document_id"),
            "revision": identity.get("revision"),
            "source": observed.source,
            "measured_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        },
        "units": {
            "requirements": rs.units,
            "declared": observed.declared_unit,
            "inferred": observed.inferred_unit,
        },
        "requirements_digest": rs.digest(),
        "legacy": _jsonable(dict(rs.legacy)) if rs.legacy else None,
        "artifact": _jsonable(dict(artifact)) if artifact else None,
    }
    return _settle(report)


def add_finding(
    report: dict, *, kind: str, requirement: str, message: str, severity: str = "error"
) -> dict:
    """A copy of ``report`` with one more finding; an ``error`` finding blocks acceptance."""
    out = dict(report)
    out["findings"] = [
        *report["findings"],
        {"requirement": requirement, "kind": kind, "severity": severity, "message": message},
    ]
    return _settle(out)


def attach_dimension_evidence(report: dict, evidence: dict[str, dict]) -> dict:
    """Each row's DIMENSION evidence (``bind_dimension``), and a ``dimension_mismatch``
    finding where the dimension's text or own measurement disagrees with the geometry."""
    out = dict(report)
    out["rows"] = [dict(row) for row in report["rows"]]
    out["findings"] = list(report["findings"])
    for row in out["rows"]:
        item = evidence.get(row["id"])
        if item is None:
            continue
        row["dimension"] = _jsonable(dict(item))
        if item.get("mismatch"):
            out = add_finding(
                out,
                kind=DIMENSION_MISMATCH,
                requirement=row["id"],
                message=(
                    f"dimension {item.get('handle')} displays {item.get('displayed_text')!r}; "
                    f"its own measurement is {item.get('dimension_measurement')} and the "
                    f"geometry measures {item.get('geometric_value')}"
                ),
            )
    return _settle(out)


def mark_stale(report: dict, identity: dict | None) -> dict:
    """A copy of ``report``, stale when what it was measured on has moved.

    ``identity`` is the document as it is now (``document_identity_of``); None
    skips that comparison. An artifact's file is re-hashed whenever the report
    names one.
    """
    reasons: list[str] = []
    evidence = report.get("evidence") or {}
    if identity is not None and evidence.get("document_id") is not None:
        if identity.get("document_id") != evidence["document_id"]:
            reasons.append(
                f"measured on document {evidence['document_id']}; the current document is "
                f"{identity.get('document_id')}"
            )
        elif identity.get("revision") != evidence.get("revision"):
            reasons.append(
                f"the evidence was measured at revision {evidence.get('revision')} and the "
                f"document is at revision {identity.get('revision')}"
            )
    artifact = report.get("artifact") or {}
    if artifact.get("path") and artifact.get("sha256"):
        path = Path(artifact["path"])
        if not path.is_file():
            reasons.append(f"the artifact {path.name} is gone")
        elif sha256_file(path) != artifact["sha256"]:
            reasons.append(f"the artifact {path.name} changed after it was measured")
    out = dict(report)
    out["rows"] = [dict(row, stale=bool(reasons)) for row in report["rows"]]
    out["stale"] = bool(reasons)
    out["stale_reason"] = "; ".join(reasons) or None
    return _settle(out)


def summarize(report: dict) -> dict:
    """The verdict without the rows - what a manifest or a finalize payload carries."""
    return {
        "status": report["status"],
        "accepted": report["accepted"],
        "stale": report["stale"],
        "stale_reason": report["stale_reason"],
        "counts": dict(report["counts"]),
        "coverage": dict(report["coverage"]),
        "blocking": list(report["blocking"]),
        "requirements_digest": report["requirements_digest"],
    }


def _fmt(value) -> str:
    number = _number(value)
    if number is not None:
        return format(number, ".6g")
    return "-" if value is None else str(value)


def _signed(value) -> str:
    number = _number(value)
    return "-" if number is None else format(number, "+.6g")


def _target_text(target: dict) -> str:
    unit = str(target.get("unit") or "")
    if target.get("nominal") is not None:
        return f"{_fmt(target['nominal'])} {unit}".strip()
    if target.get("exact") is not None:
        return f"exactly {_fmt(target['exact'])}"
    low, high = target.get("min"), target.get("max")
    if low is not None and high is not None:
        return f"{_fmt(low)} to {_fmt(high)}"
    if low is not None:
        return f"at least {_fmt(low)}"
    if high is not None:
        return f"at most {_fmt(high)}"
    return "-"


def _cell(text) -> str:
    return str(text).replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ")


def _markdown_row(row: dict) -> str:
    measured = row["measured"]
    source = ", ".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in row["source"].items()) or "-"
    cells = [
        row["id"],
        row["quantity"],
        _target_text(row["target"]),
        _fmt(measured["value"]) if measured else "-",
        _signed(row["delta"]),
        row["tolerance_basis"],
        row["status"],
        ", ".join(row["handles"]) or "-",
        source,
    ]
    return "| " + " | ".join(_cell(cell) for cell in cells) + " |"


def render_markdown(report: dict) -> str:
    """The report as Markdown: the verdict, the denominators, one table row per requirement."""
    counts = report["counts"]
    lines = [
        "# Acceptance report",
        "",
        f"Status: **{report['status']}** - accepted: **{'yes' if report['accepted'] else 'no'}**",
        "",
        "| required | pass | fail | unknown | unsupported | ambiguous |",
        "|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|",
        f"| {counts['required']} | {counts['pass']} | {counts['fail']} | {counts['unknown']} "
        f"| {counts['unsupported']} | {counts['ambiguous']} |",
        "",
        "| id | quantity | target | measured | delta | basis | status | handles | source |",
        "|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|---|",
    ]
    lines.extend(_markdown_row(row) for row in report["rows"])
    if report["stale"]:
        lines += ["", f"**Stale evidence:** {report['stale_reason']}"]
    if report["findings"]:
        lines += ["", "## Findings", ""]
        lines.extend(
            f"- {finding['kind']} ({finding['requirement']}): {finding['message']}"
            for finding in report["findings"]
        )
    if report["blocking"]:
        lines += ["", "## Not accepted because", ""]
        lines.extend(f"- {reason}" for reason in report["blocking"])
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


async def document_identity_of(backend) -> dict:
    """``backend.document_identity()`` as a plain dict. The skeleton pins it
    synchronous; an awaitable is awaited, so either landing works."""
    value = backend.document_identity()
    if inspect.isawaitable(value):
        value = await value
    return dict(value or {})


def dimension_evidence(rs: RequirementSet, snap: Snapshot, observed: ObservedGeometry) -> dict:
    """``{requirement id: bind_dimension evidence}`` for each requirement that names
    a DIMENSION (a ``dimension`` selector carrying its ``handle``)."""
    records = {rec.handle: rec for rec in snap.records if rec.type == "DIMENSION"}
    out: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for requirement in rs.requirements:
        for selector in requirement.features:
            if selector.kind != "dimension":
                continue
            handle = dict(selector.value).get("handle")
            record = records.get(str(handle)) if handle is not None else None
            if record is None:
                continue
            epsilon = requirement.verification_epsilon
            if epsilon is None:
                epsilon = default_epsilon(observed)
            item = dict(bind_dimension(record, observed, epsilon=epsilon))
            item["handle"] = record.handle
            out[requirement.id] = item
            break
    return out


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasuredRun:
    """One read, binding and measurement of a requirement set."""

    snapshot: Snapshot
    observed: ObservedGeometry
    bindings: tuple[Binding, ...]
    measurements: tuple[Measurement, ...]
    document: dict
    artifact: dict | None
    dimensions: dict


def _measure(rs, snap, document, binding_policy):
    observed = observe(snap, explode_inserts=True, document=document)
    bindings = tuple(bind(rs, observed, policy=binding_policy))
    by_id = {binding.requirement_id: binding for binding in bindings}
    measurements = tuple(
        evaluate(requirement, by_id[requirement.id], observed, epsilon=requirement.verification_epsilon)
        for requirement in rs.requirements
        if requirement.id in by_id
    )
    return observed, bindings, measurements, dimension_evidence(rs, snap, observed)


async def measure_requirements(
    backend, rs: RequirementSet, *, binding_policy: str = "strict", artifact_path=None
) -> MeasuredRun:
    """Read the drawing once (the current document, or the file at ``artifact_path``),
    observe it, bind every requirement and measure it. The arithmetic runs off the
    event loop."""
    if binding_policy not in BINDING_POLICIES:
        raise ValueError(
            f"binding_policy must be one of {', '.join(BINDING_POLICIES)}; got {binding_policy!r}"
        )
    if artifact_path is None:
        document = await document_identity_of(backend)
        snap = await take_snapshot(backend, block_defs=True)
        artifact = None
    else:
        target = Path(artifact_path)
        snap = await take_snapshot(backend, str(target), block_defs=True)
        document = {
            "document_id": None,
            "revision": None,
            "name": target.name,
            "path": str(target),
            "backend": "file",
        }
        artifact = {
            "path": str(target),
            "sha256": await asyncio.to_thread(sha256_file, target),
            "size_bytes": target.stat().st_size,
        }
    observed, bindings, measurements, dimensions = await asyncio.to_thread(
        _measure, rs, snap, document, binding_policy
    )
    return MeasuredRun(
        snapshot=snap,
        observed=observed,
        bindings=bindings,
        measurements=measurements,
        document=document,
        artifact=artifact,
        dimensions=dimensions,
    )


async def verify_requirements(backend, rs, *, binding_policy="strict", artifact_path=None) -> dict:
    """Measure ``rs`` and report it. On the current document the evidence is the
    document's id and revision, checked again after the measurement (an edit that
    landed meanwhile makes the report stale); with ``artifact_path`` it is the
    file's sha256."""
    run = await measure_requirements(
        backend, rs, binding_policy=binding_policy, artifact_path=artifact_path
    )
    report = build_report(
        rs,
        run.bindings,
        run.measurements,
        observed=run.observed,
        document=run.document,
        artifact=run.artifact,
    )
    report = attach_dimension_evidence(report, run.dimensions)
    identity = None if artifact_path is not None else await document_identity_of(backend)
    return mark_stale(report, identity)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing delivery acceptance tests**

Create `tests/test_delivery_acceptance.py`:

```python
"""Task 16: drawing_deliver(requirements=) and verify_manifest.

A requirement that does not pass blocks delivery whatever the drafting score
(status failed_acceptance), and verify_manifest recomputes every hash the
manifest recorded: an artifact edited after the manifest was written is stale
(spec §8.2 item 9). Ø40 is a circle of r = 20, Ø42 one of r = 21.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import ezdxf
import pytest

from engineering.delivery import deliver_drawing, verify_manifest
from engineering.layers import ensure_engineering_layers, ensure_standard_linetypes
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet


def _sha(path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _diameter(rid, handle, nominal=40.0):
    return Requirement(
        id=rid,
        quantity="diameter",
        target=(("nominal", nominal), ("unit", "mm")),
        features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", handle),)),),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("document", "synthetic-sheet"),),
    )


async def _open_part(backend, tmp_path):
    """A 200 x 100 frame, a Ø40 bore (r = 20) and a Ø42 bore (r = 21), in mm."""
    base = tmp_path / "base.dxf"
    ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4).saveas(base)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(base))
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    frame = await backend.entity_create_polyline(
        [[0, 0], [200, 0], [200, 100], [0, 100]], closed=True, layer="GEOMETRY"
    )
    bore = await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 50, 20, layer="GEOMETRY")
    wide = await backend.entity_create_circle(150, 50, 21, layer="GEOMETRY")
    return {"frame": frame.handle, "bore": bore.handle, "wide": wide.handle}


async def _clean(backend):
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 40, 0, layer="GEOMETRY")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_failed_requirement_blocks_delivery_whatever_the_score(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(_diameter("R1", handles["bore"]), _diameter("R2", handles["wide"])),
        units="mm",
    )
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False, requirements=rs
    )
    assert result.status == "failed_acceptance"
    data = result.to_dict()
    assert data["drafting_quality"] == data["score"]
    assert data["geometry_acceptance"]["status"] == "fail"
    assert data["geometry_acceptance"]["counts"] == {
        "required": 2,
        "pass": 1,
        "fail": 1,
        "unknown": 0,
        "unsupported": 0,
        "ambiguous": 0,
    }
    assert data["coverage"] == {"required": 2, "measured": 2, "ratio": 1.0}
    assert "fail: R2" in data["blocking"]
    manifest = json.loads((bundle / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert manifest["status"] == "failed_acceptance"
    assert manifest["acceptance"]["report"]["sha256"] == _sha(bundle / "acceptance.json")
    report = json.loads((bundle / "acceptance.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert [row["status"] for row in report["rows"]] == ["pass", "fail"]
    assert abs(report["rows"][1]["measured"]["value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 21
    assert (bundle / "acceptance.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").startswith("# Acceptance report")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_requirements_that_pass_deliver_success(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R1", handles["bore"]),), units="mm")
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, tmp_path / "bundle", formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False,
        requirements=rs,
    )
    assert result.status == "success"
    data = result.to_dict()
    assert data["geometry_acceptance"]["accepted"] is True
    assert data["artifact_integrity"]["ok"] is True
    assert data["blocking"] == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_without_requirements_the_result_is_unchanged(backend, tmp_path):
    await _clean(backend)
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False
    )
    assert set(result.to_dict()) == {
        "status",
        "output_dir",
        "manifest_path",
        "artifacts",
        "score",
        "parity",
    }
    manifest = json.loads((bundle / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert "acceptance" not in manifest
    assert not (bundle / "acceptance.json").exists()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_validation_failure_comes_first_and_every_reason_is_listed(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    await backend.construction_xline(0, 0, 45)  # construction_left: a critique error
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R2", handles["wide"]),), units="mm")
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, tmp_path / "bundle", formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, requirements=rs
    )
    assert result.status == "failed_validation"
    blocking = result.to_dict()["blocking"]
    assert any(reason.startswith("critique:") for reason in blocking)
    assert "fail: R2" in blocking


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_verify_manifest_rehashes_and_reports_stale_and_missing(backend, tmp_path):
    await _clean(backend)
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    await deliver_drawing(backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False)
    clean = verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))
    assert clean["ok"] is True
    assert (clean["stale"], clean["missing"], clean["inconsistent"]) == ([], [], [])
    assert {row["filename"] for row in clean["checked"]} == {"drawing.dxf", "validation.json"}
    with (bundle / "drawing.dxf").open("ab") as stream:
        stream.write(b"999\nedited after the manifest was written\n")
    edited = verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))
    assert edited["ok"] is False
    assert [row["filename"] for row in edited["stale"]] == ["drawing.dxf"]
    assert edited["stale"][0]["sha256"] == _sha(bundle / "drawing.dxf")
    (bundle / "validation.json").unlink()
    gone = verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))
    assert [row["filename"] for row in gone["missing"]] == ["validation.json"]


def test_verify_manifest_refuses_a_file_that_is_not_a_manifest(tmp_path):
    text = tmp_path / "manifest.json"
    text.write_text("not json", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not a readable delivery manifest"):
        verify_manifest(str(text))
    text.write_text(json.dumps({"status": "success"}), encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no artifacts list"):
        verify_manifest(str(text))


def test_verify_manifest_never_reads_outside_its_folder(tmp_path):
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    bundle.mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "secret.dxf").write_bytes(b"outside")
    manifest = {
        "status": "success",
        "artifacts": [{"format": "dxf", "filename": "../secret.dxf", "sha256": "0" * 64}],
    }
    (bundle / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest), encoding="utf-8")
    result = verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))
    assert result["ok"] is False
    assert result["checked"] == []
    assert [row["field"] for row in result["inconsistent"]] == ["filename"]
```
