```python
    parity: dict
    #: Set only when the delivery verified requirements (B22); a delivery
    #: without them returns exactly the six fields above.
    geometry_acceptance: dict | None = None
    drafting_quality: dict | None = None
    artifact_integrity: dict | None = None
    coverage: dict | None = None
    blocking: list[str] | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        data = {
            "status": self.status,
            "output_dir": self.output_dir,
            "manifest_path": self.manifest_path,
            "artifacts": list(self.artifacts),
            "score": dict(self.score),
            "parity": dict(self.parity),
        }
        if self.geometry_acceptance is not None:
            data["geometry_acceptance"] = dict(self.geometry_acceptance)
            data["drafting_quality"] = dict(self.drafting_quality or {})
            data["artifact_integrity"] = dict(self.artifact_integrity or {})
            data["coverage"] = dict(self.coverage or {})
            data["blocking"] = list(self.blocking or [])
        return data
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Add `verify_manifest` and the acceptance helper**

In `engineering/delivery.py` replace

```python
async def _reopen_parity(source: dict, dxf_path: Path) -> dict:
```

with

```python
def _manifest_records(manifest: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Every file record a manifest hashed: artifacts, validation, acceptance report."""
    records = [dict(item) for item in manifest.get("artifacts") or []]
    if manifest.get("validation_artifact"):
        records.append(dict(manifest["validation_artifact"]))
    acceptance = manifest.get("acceptance") or {}
    for key in ("report", "markdown"):
        if acceptance.get(key):
            records.append(dict(acceptance[key]))
    return records


def verify_manifest(path: str) -> dict:
    """Recompute every hash a delivery manifest recorded (B23 made observable).

    ``stale``: a file whose sha256 no longer matches (it was edited after the
    manifest was written). ``missing``: a recorded file that is gone.
    ``skipped``: a record that was never created (an unsupported format).
    ``inconsistent``: a record that cannot be trusted - a file name that is
    not a plain name inside the bundle is never read. ``ok`` only when all
    three of stale / missing / inconsistent are empty. Refuses (ValueError) a
    file that is not JSON or holds no ``artifacts`` list.
    """
    manifest_path = Path(path)
    try:
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: not a readable delivery manifest ({exc})") from exc
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict) or not isinstance(manifest.get("artifacts"), list):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: not a delivery manifest (no artifacts list)")
    base = manifest_path.parent
    checked: list[dict] = []
    stale: list[dict] = []
    missing: list[dict] = []
    skipped: list[dict] = []
    inconsistent: list[dict] = []
    for record in _manifest_records(manifest):
        filename = str(record.get("filename") or "")
        recorded = record.get("sha256")
        row = {"filename": filename, "format": record.get("format")}
        if not filename or recorded is None:
            skipped.append({**row, "status": record.get("status")})
            continue
        if Path(filename).name != filename or filename in (".", ".."):
            inconsistent.append(
                {**row, "field": "filename", "reason": "not a plain file name inside the bundle"}
            )
            continue
        target = base / filename
        if not target.is_file():
            missing.append(row)
            continue
        row = {**row, "recorded_sha256": recorded, "sha256": _sha256(target)}
        checked.append(row)
        if row["sha256"] != recorded:
            stale.append(row)
    return {
        "ok": not (stale or missing or inconsistent),
        "manifest": str(manifest_path),
        "status": manifest.get("status"),
        "checked": checked,
        "stale": stale,
        "missing": missing,
        "skipped": skipped,
        "inconsistent": inconsistent,
    }


async def _acceptance(
    backend: AutoCADBackend,
    requirements: RequirementSet,
    destination: Path,
    identity_at_start: dict | None,
) -> dict:
    """Measure ``requirements``, write acceptance.json / acceptance.md, hash both."""
    from engineering.verification.report import mark_stale, render_markdown, verify_requirements

    report = await verify_requirements(backend, requirements)
    if identity_at_start is not None:
        report = mark_stale(report, identity_at_start)
    json_path = destination / "acceptance.json"
    md_path = destination / "acceptance.md"
    json_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    md_path.write_text(render_markdown(report), encoding="utf-8")
    return {
        "report": report,
        "json": _artifact_record("json", json_path, status="created"),
        "markdown": _artifact_record("md", md_path, status="created"),
    }


async def _reopen_parity(source: dict, dxf_path: Path) -> dict:
```

```python
"""Task 16: drawing_verify_requirements, and drawing_finalize / drawing_deliver with
requirements, on the headless engine. Ø40 is a circle of r = 20, Ø42 one of r = 21.

The raw requirement JSON below is Task 11's raw shape written as a client sends
it - `{"units", "items": [...]}` (`parse_requirements` reads `items`, not
`requirements`); `_raw` / `_diameter_row` are the one place to change if Task 11
landed another spelling (the landed parser wins).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import ezdxf
import pytest
from fastmcp.exceptions import ToolError

import server
from discovery.aliases import aliases_for
from engineering.delivery import deliver_drawing
from engineering.layers import ensure_engineering_layers, ensure_standard_linetypes
from engineering.verification.requirements import store_requirements
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet


class _FakeCtx:
    """What the tools touch on a Context: the backend, info and warning."""

    def __init__(self, backend):
        self.lifespan_context = {"backend": backend}

    async def info(self, message):
        pass

    async def warning(self, message):
        pass


def _raw(*rows) -> dict:
    return {"units": "mm", "items": list(rows)}


def _diameter_row(rid, handle, nominal=40.0) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": rid,
        "quantity": "diameter",
        "target": {"nominal": nominal, "unit": "mm"},
        "features": [{"kind": "handle", "handle": handle}],
        "provenance": "explicit",
        "source": {"document": "synthetic-sheet", "label": rid},
    }


async def _open_part(backend, tmp_path):
    """A 200 x 100 frame, a Ø40 bore (r = 20) and a Ø42 bore (r = 21), in mm."""
    base = tmp_path / "base.dxf"
    ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4).saveas(base)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(base))
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    frame = await backend.entity_create_polyline(
        [[0, 0], [200, 0], [200, 100], [0, 100]], closed=True, layer="GEOMETRY"
    )
    bore = await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 50, 20, layer="GEOMETRY")
    wide = await backend.entity_create_circle(150, 50, 21, layer="GEOMETRY")
    return {"frame": frame.handle, "bore": bore.handle, "wide": wide.handle}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_requirements_are_verified_on_the_current_document(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    report = await server.drawing_verify_requirements(
        requirements=_raw(
            _diameter_row("R1", handles["bore"]), _diameter_row("R2", handles["wide"])
        ),
        ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
    )
    assert report["mode"] == "document"
    assert report["status"] == "fail"
    rows = {row["id"]: row for row in report["rows"]}
    assert abs(rows["R1"]["measured"]["value"] - 40.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 20
    assert abs(rows["R2"]["measured"]["value"] - 42.0) <= 1e-9  # 2 x 21
    assert abs(rows["R2"]["delta"] - 2.0) <= 1e-9


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_use_stored_reads_the_requirements_the_drawing_carries(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(
        requirements=(
            Requirement(
                id="R1",
                quantity="diameter",
                target=(("nominal", 40.0), ("unit", "mm")),
                features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", handles["bore"]),)),),
                provenance="explicit",
                source=(("document", "synthetic-sheet"),),
            ),
        ),
        units="mm",
    )
    await store_requirements(backend, rs)
    report = await server.drawing_verify_requirements(use_stored=True, ctx=_FakeCtx(backend))
    assert (report["status"], report["accepted"]) == ("pass", True)
    assert report["requirements_digest"] == rs.digest()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_refusals_come_before_any_work(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    row = _diameter_row("R1", handles["bore"])
    cases = [
        ({"requirements": _raw(row), "use_stored": True}, "invalid_args: pass requirements or"),
        ({}, "missing_requirement: nothing to verify"),
        ({"use_stored": True}, "missing_requirement: this drawing stores no requirements"),
        ({"requirements": _raw(row), "binding_policy": "fuzzy"}, "binding_policy"),
        (
            {"requirements": _raw(row), "report_path": str(tmp_path / "report.txt")},
            r"\.json or \.md",
        ),
        (
            {"manifest_path": str(tmp_path / "manifest.json"), "use_stored": True},
            "manifest_path is its own mode",
        ),
        (
            {"requirements": _raw(row), "artifact_path": str(tmp_path / "absent.dxf")},
            "artifact_path: no such file",
        ),
        ({"manifest_path": str(tmp_path / "absent.json")}, "manifest_path: no such file"),
    ]
    for kwargs, pattern in cases:
        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match=pattern):
            await server.drawing_verify_requirements(ctx=_FakeCtx(backend), **kwargs)
    assert not (tmp_path / "report.txt").exists()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_report_path_writes_json_or_markdown(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    requirements = _raw(_diameter_row("R1", handles["bore"]))
    as_json = await server.drawing_verify_requirements(
        requirements=requirements,
        report_path=str(tmp_path / "acceptance.json"),
        ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
    )
    written = json.loads((tmp_path / "acceptance.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert written["rows"] == as_json["rows"]
    assert as_json["report_path"] == str((tmp_path / "acceptance.json").resolve())
    await server.drawing_verify_requirements(
        requirements=requirements,
        report_path=str(tmp_path / "acceptance.md"),
        ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
    )
    assert (tmp_path / "acceptance.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").startswith(
        "# Acceptance report"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_manifest_mode_reports_an_artifact_edited_after_delivery(backend, tmp_path):
    await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    await deliver_drawing(backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False)
    clean = await server.drawing_verify_requirements(
        manifest_path=str(bundle / "manifest.json"), ctx=_FakeCtx(backend)
    )
    assert (clean["mode"], clean["ok"]) == ("manifest", True)
    with (bundle / "drawing.dxf").open("ab") as stream:
        stream.write(b"999\nedited\n")
    edited = await server.drawing_verify_requirements(
        manifest_path=str(bundle / "manifest.json"), ctx=_FakeCtx(backend)
    )
    assert edited["ok"] is False
    assert [row["filename"] for row in edited["stale"]] == ["drawing.dxf"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_finalize_with_requirements_adds_the_acceptance_fields(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    saved = tmp_path / "part.dxf"
    payload = await server.drawing_finalize(
        save_path=str(saved),
        requirements=_raw(_diameter_row("R1", handles["bore"])),
        ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
    )
    assert payload["geometry_acceptance"]["accepted"] is True
    assert payload["drafting_quality"] == payload["score"]
    assert payload["coverage"] == {"required": 1, "measured": 1, "ratio": 1.0}
    integrity = payload["artifact_integrity"]
    assert integrity["sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(saved.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_finalize_fails_acceptance_whatever_the_score(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="failed_acceptance") as raised:
        await server.drawing_finalize(
            save_path=str(tmp_path / "part.dxf"),
            requirements=_raw(_diameter_row("R2", handles["wide"])),
            ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
        )
    assert "fail: R2" in str(raised.value)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_finalize_without_requirements_is_unchanged(backend, tmp_path):
    await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    payload = await server.drawing_finalize(
        save_path=str(tmp_path / "part.dxf"), ctx=_FakeCtx(backend)
    )
    assert "score" in payload
    assert "geometry_acceptance" not in payload and "coverage" not in payload


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_finalize_refuses_requirements_that_do_not_parse_before_saving(backend, tmp_path):
    await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    saved = tmp_path / "part.dxf"
    with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="invalid_args: requirements"):
        await server.drawing_finalize(
            save_path=str(saved),
            requirements={"units": "mm", "items": [{"id": "R1"}]},
            ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
        )
    assert not saved.exists()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_drawing_deliver_tool_reports_failed_acceptance(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    result = await server.drawing_deliver(
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "bundle"),
        formats=["dxf"],
        min_score=0,
        strict_critique=False,
        requirements=_raw(_diameter_row("R2", handles["wide"])),
        ctx=_FakeCtx(backend),
    )
    assert result["status"] == "failed_acceptance"
    assert result["geometry_acceptance"]["counts"]["fail"] == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_tool_is_registered_core_lean_and_in_section_26():
    tools = {tool.name: tool for tool in await server._registered_tools()}
    tool = tools["drawing_verify_requirements"]
    assert tool.annotations.readOnlyHint is False
    assert tool.annotations.destructiveHint is False

    groups = await server._tool_groups()
    assert "drawing_verify_requirements" in groups["understanding"]
    record = aliases_for("drawing_verify_requirements")
    assert record is not None and "verify requirements" in record.synonyms
```

```python
@cad_tool(
    summary="Verify source requirements on the CAD geometry: pass/fail/unknown each, with evidence.",
    cost="safe",
)
@mcp.tool(
    annotations={
        "title": "Understand: Verify Requirements",
        "readOnlyHint": False,
        "destructiveHint": False,
    },
    tags={"understand", "analysis", "validation"},
)
async def drawing_verify_requirements(
    requirements: Annotated[
        dict | list | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description="Typed requirements ({units, items: [{id, quantity, target, "
            "features, provenance, source, ...}]}) or a legacy {dimensions: {...}} carried "
            "read-only.",
        ),
    ] = None,
    use_stored: Annotated[
        bool,
        Field(
            default=False,
            description="Verify the set drawing_plan(persist_requirements=True) stored in the "
            "drawing.",
        ),
    ] = False,
    binding_policy: Annotated[
        str,
        Field(
            default="strict",
            description="strict or lenient: how a requirement is bound to its feature. Two "
            "equal candidates are ambiguous under both.",
        ),
    ] = "strict",
    artifact_path: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description="A saved file of this drawing (.dxf; a .dwg only on a live seat) to "
            "measure instead of the open document - read fresh, never the model in memory.",
        ),
    ] = None,
    manifest_path: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description="A drawing_deliver manifest.json: re-hash its artifacts and list the "
            "stale and missing ones. Its own mode.",
        ),
    ] = None,
    report_path: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description="Also write the report here: .json, or .md (Markdown; not in manifest "
            "mode).",
        ),
    ] = None,
    ctx: Context = None,
) -> dict:
    """Measure source requirements on the CAD geometry and say, per requirement,
    pass / fail / unknown / unsupported / ambiguous - with the evidence.

    Each requirement is bound to the real feature (by handle, feature tag, arch
    element, geometric predicate or DIMENSION; two equal candidates are
    `ambiguous`, never a pick) and measured on the geometry read from the drawing
    - never on a label, a stored model or the numbers that were typed in. A row
    gives target, measured, delta, tolerance basis, status, handles and source;
    `counts` gives the denominators (required = pass + fail + unknown +
    unsupported + ambiguous). `status` is `pass` only when every requirement
    passes; `accepted` also needs fresh evidence and no `dimension_mismatch`
    finding (a DIMENSION whose text or DIMLFAC disagrees with the geometry).

    Modes:
    - `requirements` ({"units": ..., "items": [typed requirements]}, or a
      legacy {"dimensions": {...}} that is carried read-only and verifies
      nothing) or `use_stored=True`: measured on
      the current document. `evidence` records its id and content revision; a
      later revision makes the report stale.
    - `artifact_path`: measured on that saved file, read fresh.
    - `manifest_path`: re-hashes a drawing_deliver bundle and lists every
      artifact whose sha256 no longer matches (`stale`) or that is gone
      (`missing`).

    `report_path` writes the report as JSON (.json) or Markdown (.md); nothing
    else is written, and the drawing never is.

    Refused before any work: `requirements` together with `use_stored`
    (invalid_args); neither of them without `manifest_path`
    (missing_requirement); `use_stored` on a drawing that stores no
    requirements (missing_requirement); requirements that do not parse
    (invalid_args, the offending path named); a `binding_policy` other than
    strict / lenient; `manifest_path` together with `requirements`, `use_stored`
    or `artifact_path`; an `artifact_path` or `manifest_path` that does not
    exist; a manifest that is not a delivery manifest; a `report_path` not
    ending in .json or .md (only .json in manifest mode); a DWG artifact on the
    headless engine (capability `dwg`). A quarantined document is refused.
    """
    from engineering.delivery import verify_manifest
    from engineering.verification.report import (
        BINDING_POLICIES,
        render_markdown,
        verify_requirements,
    )
    from engineering.verification.requirements import load_requirements, parse_requirements

    report_target = None
    if report_path is not None:
        report_target = validate_path(report_path, allow_write=True)
        allowed = (".json",) if manifest_path is not None else (".json", ".md")
        if report_target.suffix.lower() not in allowed:
            raise ToolError(
                "invalid_args: report_path must end in .json or .md (only .json in manifest "
                f"mode); got {report_target.name!r}"
            )
    if binding_policy not in BINDING_POLICIES:
        raise ToolError(
            f"invalid_args: binding_policy must be one of {', '.join(BINDING_POLICIES)}; "
            f"got {binding_policy!r}"
        )
    backend = _backend(ctx)

    if manifest_path is not None:
        if requirements is not None or use_stored or artifact_path is not None:
            raise ToolError(
                "invalid_args: manifest_path is its own mode - it re-hashes a delivery's "
                "artifacts. Verify requirements in a separate call (requirements= or "
                "use_stored=True, optionally artifact_path=)."
            )
        manifest = validate_path(manifest_path)
        if not manifest.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"manifest_path: no such file {str(manifest)!r}")
        try:
            result = await asyncio.to_thread(verify_manifest, str(manifest))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ToolError(f"invalid_args: {exc}") from exc
        result = {"mode": "manifest", **result}
        if report_target is not None:
            report_target.write_text(
                json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8"
            )
            result["report_path"] = str(report_target)
        return result

    if requirements is not None and use_stored:
        raise ToolError("invalid_args: pass requirements or use_stored=True, not both")
    if requirements is None and not use_stored:
        raise ToolError(
            "missing_requirement: nothing to verify - pass requirements, use_stored=True "
            "(the set drawing_plan(persist_requirements=True) stored) or manifest_path"
        )
    if use_stored:
        rs = await load_requirements(backend)
        if rs is None:
            raise ToolError(
                "missing_requirement: this drawing stores no requirements - persist them with "
                "drawing_plan(..., persist_requirements=True) or pass requirements"
            )
    else:
        try:
            rs = parse_requirements(requirements)
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ToolError(f"invalid_args: requirements: {exc}") from exc
    artifact = None
    if artifact_path is not None:
        artifact = validate_path(artifact_path)
        if not artifact.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"artifact_path: no such file {str(artifact)!r}")
    await ctx.info(
        f"drawing_verify_requirements: {len(rs.requirements)} requirement(s) on "
        f"{artifact or 'the current document'}"
    )
    try:
        report = await verify_requirements(
            backend,
            rs,
            binding_policy=binding_policy,
            artifact_path=str(artifact) if artifact is not None else None,
        )
    except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
        raise ToolError(str(exc)) from exc
    report["mode"] = "artifact" if artifact is not None else "document"
    if report_target is not None:
        if report_target.suffix.lower() == ".md":
            text = render_markdown(report)
        else:
            text = json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
        report_target.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        report["report_path"] = str(report_target)
    return report
```

with

```python
    expected: Annotated[
        dict | None,
        Field(default=None, description="Optional validator expectations for the drawing."),
    ] = None,
    requirements: Annotated[
        dict | list | None,
        Field(
            default=None,
            description="Source requirements to verify (the drawing_verify_requirements "
            "shape). A requirement that does not pass makes the status failed_acceptance, "
            "whatever the drafting score.",
        ),
    ] = None,
    ctx: Context = None,
) -> dict:
    """Create a hashed, validated delivery bundle and verify DXF save/reopen parity.

    The result status is ``success``, ``failed_validation``, ``failed_export`` or
    ``failed_acceptance``. Failure intentionally keeps all generated artifacts for
    diagnosis.

    With ``requirements`` every requirement is measured on the CAD geometry
    (drawing_verify_requirements' engine); the bundle gains ``acceptance.json`` and
    ``acceptance.md``, the result ``geometry_acceptance``, ``drafting_quality`` (the
    unchanged score), ``artifact_integrity``, ``coverage`` and ``blocking``, and a
    requirement that does not pass, stale evidence or a dimension that disagrees
    with its geometry makes the status ``failed_acceptance`` whatever the score
    (``failed_export`` and ``failed_validation`` are reported first). Refused before
    any work: requirements that do not parse (invalid_args).
    """
    from engineering import deliver_drawing

    rs = None
    if requirements is not None:
        from engineering.verification.requirements import parse_requirements

        try:
            rs = parse_requirements(requirements)
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ToolError(f"invalid_args: requirements: {exc}") from exc
    destination = validate_path(output_dir, allow_write=True)
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        _backend(ctx),
        destination,
        formats=formats,
        min_score=min_score,
        strict_critique=strict_critique,
        expected=expected,
        requirements=rs,
    )
    return result.to_dict()
```

```bash
feat(verify): the acceptance report and drawing_verify_requirements (B22, F02)

engineering/verification/report.py turns bindings and measurements into one row per requirement - target, measured, delta, tolerance basis, status, handles, source - with the denominators required / pass / fail / unknown / unsupported / ambiguous (they always add up), as JSON (schema_version 1) and Markdown. **Files:**
```
- Create: `engineering/verification/artifact.py`

- Modify: `engineering/understand/snapshot.py` (`dxf_version_of`)
- Modify: `engineering/understand/diff.py` (`read_revision(..., insert_boxes=True)`)
- Modify: `backends/ezdxf_backend.py` (`drawing_export_dxf` writes a copy)
- Modify: `backends/com_backend.py` (`drawing_export_dxf` through `Document.Export`)
- Modify: `backends/contracts/drawing.py` (`drawing_export_dxf` docstring)
- Modify: `server.py` (`drawing_export_dxf` / `drawing_deliver` / `drawing_verify_requirements` docstrings, `drawing_finalize` re-verifies the saved file)
- Modify: `README.md` (collected-tests figure retaken), `benchmarks/results/published/ab-v1.6.0-vs-v1.7.0.json` (regenerated)
- Test: `tests/test_export_no_repoint.py`, `tests/test_delivery_parity.py`, `tests/test_verification_artifact.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 16: `measure_requirements(backend, rs, *, binding_policy="strict", artifact_path=None) -> MeasuredRun`, `MeasuredRun`, `build_report(...)`, `attach_dimension_evidence`, `add_finding`, `dimension_evidence`, `mark_stale`, `summarize`, `render_markdown`, `document_identity_of`, `sha256_file`, `BINDING_POLICIES`, `verify_manifest(path) -> dict`, `DeliveryResult` acceptance fields.
  - Task 9: `backends.com_backend._acad_doc()` — inside a `ComBackend._run` job it returns the bound `Document` through Task 9's per-job guard (`_JobGuard` → `ComBackend._guard(self, app)`) or raises `ActiveDocumentChangedError`; `backend.document_identity()` (synchronous); `drawing_export_dxf` is not in `_MUTATING_METHODS` (an export never moves the revision).
  - Tasks 11/13/14/15: `Binding`, `FeatureRef`, `RequirementSet`, `ObservedFeature` (`handle`, `type`, `space`, `layer`, `block_path`, `geometry`, `tags`), `observe`, `bind`, `default_epsilon`, `evaluate`.
  - W1 / 1.6, with Task 13's `block_defs=` keyword: `take_snapshot(backend, path=None, *, block_defs=False)`, `read_snapshot(path, *, block_defs=False)` (every snapshot this task observes is read with `block_defs=True`; `observe` refuses an INSERT without its block definitions), `records_from_doc(..., insert_boxes=)`, `diff_snapshots`, `block_signatures`, `block_changes`, `CATEGORIES`, `DrawingValidator`.
- Produces:

The report adds `rebinding: {"handles_stable", "rows": [{"id", "method", "status", "reason"}]}`, `comparison` (before / after per requirement, or None) and `source_document`.

Create `tests/test_export_no_repoint.py`:

```python
"""Task 17 (B23 residue): exporting a DXF never re-points the open document.

`drawing_save_as` keeps its re-pointing semantics; `drawing_export_dxf` writes a
copy on the headless engine and goes through `Document.Export` on the live one.
The live fake models only members measured on AutoCAD 2026 (25.1s) on
2026-10-24 in a scratch document: `Export(<base>, "DXF", <selection set>)`
wrote `<base>.DXF` (upper-case extension, AC1032) and left `Name`, `FullName`
and `Saved` as they were. It has no `SaveAs`: a call to it is the bug.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ezdxf
import pytest

import backends.com_backend as cb
from backends.base import UnsupportedCapabilityError
from backends.com_backend import ComBackend
from backends.quarantine import DaemonExecutor
from engineering.understand.snapshot import dxf_version_of
from engineering.verification.report import document_identity_of


def test_dxf_version_of_reads_the_acadver_header(tmp_path):
    for release, code in (("R2010", "AC1024"), ("R2018", "AC1032")):  # the DXF reference's codes
        path = tmp_path / f"{release}.dxf"
        ezdxf.new(release).saveas(path)
        assert dxf_version_of(path) == code
    text = tmp_path / "note.dxf"
    text.write_text("not a drawing", encoding="utf-8")
    assert dxf_version_of(text) is None
    assert dxf_version_of(tmp_path / "absent.dxf") is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_headless_export_writes_a_copy_and_leaves_the_document_alone(backend, tmp_path):
    await backend.entity_create_circle(0, 0, 20)
    before_info = await backend.drawing_info()
    before = await document_identity_of(backend)
    target = tmp_path / "copy.dxf"
    result = await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(target))
    after_info = await backend.drawing_info()
    after = await document_identity_of(backend)
    assert (after_info.full_path, after_info.saved) == ("", False)
    assert (after_info.full_path, after_info.saved) == (before_info.full_path, before_info.saved)
    assert after["revision"] == before["revision"]
    assert result["repointed"] is False
    assert result["method"] == "ezdxf.write"
    assert result["dxf_version"] == "AC1024"  # drawing_new writes R2010
    assert ezdxf.readfile(target).modelspace().query("CIRCLE")[0].dxf.radius == 20.0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_headless_export_still_refuses_a_dwg_name(backend, tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(UnsupportedCapabilityError):
        await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(tmp_path / "copy.dwg"))


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_save_as_keeps_re_pointing_the_document(backend, tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "saved.dxf"
    await backend.drawing_save_as(str(target), "dxf")
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    assert (info.full_path, info.saved) == (str(target), True)


class _Selection:
    def __init__(self, log):
        self.log = log

    def Select(self, mode):  # noqa: N802 - the ActiveX member name
        self.log.append(("Select", mode))

    def Delete(self):  # noqa: N802
        self.log.append(("Delete",))


class _SelectionSets:
    def __init__(self, log):
        self.log = log

    def Add(self, name):  # noqa: N802
        self.log.append(("Add", name))
        return _Selection(self.log)


class _Document:
    """The measured members only: Name, FullName, Saved, SelectionSets, Export."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.Name = "Drawing7.dwg"
        self.FullName = ""
        self.Saved = False
        self.log: list[tuple] = []
        self.SelectionSets = _SelectionSets(self.log)

    def Export(self, base, extension, selection):  # noqa: N802
        self.log.append(("Export", extension))
        if extension != "DXF":
            raise ValueError("Invalid argument")  # what ".dxf" got on AutoCAD 2026
        doc = ezdxf.new("R2018", units=4)
        doc.modelspace().add_circle((0, 0), 20)
        doc.saveas(base + ".DXF")


@pytest.fixture
def com_backend(monkeypatch):
    def _off_limits():
        raise AssertionError("this test must never reach the live AutoCAD")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cb, "_acad_app", _off_limits)
    backend = ComBackend()
    backend._executor = DaemonExecutor(max_workers=1, name="test-sta")  # no CoInitialize
    backend._connected = True
    yield backend
    backend._executor.shutdown(wait=False)
    backend._executor = None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_export_goes_through_document_export_and_renames_the_dxf(
    com_backend, tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    fake = _Document()
    # _acad_doc() is where Task 9 runs the bound-document guard inside a job;
    # standing in for it here keeps the guard - and the live seat - out of the test.
    monkeypatch.setattr(cb, "_acad_doc", lambda: fake)
    target = tmp_path / "drawing.dxf"
    result = await com_backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(target))
    assert sorted(path.name for path in tmp_path.iterdir()) == ["drawing.dxf"]
    assert (fake.Name, fake.FullName, fake.Saved) == ("Drawing7.dwg", "", False)
    assert ("Export", "DXF") in fake.log
    assert fake.log[-1] == ("Delete",)
    assert result["method"] == "Document.Export"
    assert result["repointed"] is False
    assert result["dxf_version"] == "AC1032"  # R2018, what AutoCAD 2026's Export wrote
    assert result["document"] == {"name": "Drawing7.dwg", "full_name": ""}
    assert ezdxf.readfile(target).modelspace().query("CIRCLE")[0].dxf.radius == 20.0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_export_refuses_a_name_that_is_not_dxf(com_backend, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    fake = _Document()
    monkeypatch.setattr(cb, "_acad_doc", lambda: fake)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="name the file .dxf"):
        await com_backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(tmp_path / "drawing.dwg"))
    assert fake.log == []
```

```python
_ACADVER = re.compile(rb"\$ACADVER\s+1\s+(AC\d{4})")


def dxf_version_of(path) -> str | None:
    """The ``$ACADVER`` of an ASCII DXF (``AC1024`` = R2010, ``AC1032`` = R2018),
    read from the first 16 KB; None for a missing file, a binary DXF or anything
    without the header variable."""
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as stream:
            head = stream.read(16384)
    except OSError:
        return None
    match = _ACADVER.search(head)
    return match.group(1).decode("ascii") if match else None


def length_of(rec: EntityRecord) -> float:
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Make the headless export write a copy**

In `backends/ezdxf_backend.py` replace

```python
    async def drawing_export_dxf(self, path: str) -> dict:
        return await self.drawing_save_as(path, "dxf")
```

with

```python
    async def drawing_export_dxf(self, path: str) -> dict:
        """A DXF copy of the active document; the document is not re-pointed.

        ``doc.saveas`` (``drawing_save_as``) sets ``doc.filename`` and this
        backend's path and saved flag; an export must do neither (v1.7.0,
        B23), so the document is written through ``doc.write`` into a stream
        this method opens, with ``Drawing.save``'s own encoding rule
        (``output_encoding``, errors ``dxfreplace``). Not a content change:
        the revision does not move. A ``.dwg`` name is refused (``dwg``).
        """
        if _is_dwg_path(path):
            raise _refuse_dwg("drawing_export_dxf", path)

        def _sync():
            doc = self._require_doc()
            self._refuse_path_held_elsewhere("drawing_export_dxf", path)
            with open(path, "wt", encoding=doc.output_encoding, errors="dxfreplace") as stream:
                doc.write(stream)
            return {
                "ok": True,
                "path": path,
                "format": "dxf",
                "method": "ezdxf.write",
                "dxf_version": doc.dxfversion,
                "document": {
                    "name": Path(self._doc_path).name if self._doc_path else "untitled.dxf",
                    "full_name": self._doc_path or "",
                },
                "repointed": False,
            }

        return await self._async(_sync)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Make the live export go through `Document.Export`**

In `backends/com_backend.py` replace

```python
    async def drawing_export_dxf(self, path: str) -> dict:
        return await self.drawing_save_as(path, "dxf")
```

with

```python
    async def drawing_export_dxf(self, path: str) -> dict:
        """A DXF copy through ``Document.Export``; the open document is not re-pointed.

        ``SaveAs(path, 61)`` - what this method did until 1.7.0 - makes the
        open document the DXF. ``Export`` does not. Measured on AutoCAD 2026
        (25.1s) on 2026-10-24 in a scratch document: ``Export(<base>, "DXF",
        <selection set of all>)`` writes ``<base>.DXF`` (upper-case extension,
        ``$ACADVER`` AC1032 = R2018) and leaves ``Name``, ``FullName`` and
        ``Saved`` as they were; ``".dxf"`` as the extension argument is
        refused. The file is written into a private folder beside the target
        and renamed onto it, so the name is the caller's and a file already
        there is replaced in one step - AutoCAD is never asked about it. The
        version reported is the one read back from the file's header.
        """
        target = Path(path)
        if target.suffix.lower() != ".dxf":
            raise ValueError(
                f"drawing_export_dxf writes DXF; name the file .dxf, got {target.name!r}"
            )

        def _sync():
            import shutil
            import tempfile

            from engineering.understand.snapshot import dxf_version_of

            doc = _acad_doc()  # inside a job: the bound document, guarded (Task 9)
            name, full_name = str(doc.Name), str(doc.FullName)
            folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".acadmcp_export_", dir=str(target.parent))
            try:
                selection = doc.SelectionSets.Add(f"_EXP_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}")
                try:
                    selection.Select(_AC_SELECTION_SET_ALL)
                    # "DXF", never ".dxf" (measured: "Invalid argument").
                    doc.Export(os.path.join(folder, "export"), "DXF", selection)
                finally:
                    try:
                        selection.Delete()
                    except Exception:  # a selection set left behind is harmless; the export is not
                        pass
                written = sorted(Path(folder).glob("export.*"))
                if not written:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"AutoCAD reported the export of {name} done but wrote no file"
                    )
                os.replace(written[0], target)
            finally:
                shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)
            return {
                "ok": True,
                "path": str(target),
                "format": "dxf",
                "method": "Document.Export",
                "dxf_version": dxf_version_of(target),
                "document": {"name": name, "full_name": full_name},
                "repointed": False,
            }

        return await self._run(_sync)
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the failing parity, validator and manifest tests**

Create `tests/test_delivery_parity.py`:

```python
"""Task 17 (B21 / B23 residue): delivery parity over every space, the delivery
context of the validator, the manifest's new fields, verify_manifest's
consistency checks and the build stamp.

Parity expectations come from engineering/understand/diff.py's documented
rules, worked by hand below: the default move tolerance is 2 % of the robust
diagonal of the model-space anchors, and a pure translation is ``moved_by``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import ezdxf
import pytest

from engineering import delivery
from engineering.delivery import compare_artifacts, deliver_drawing, verify_manifest
from engineering.layers import ensure_engineering_layers, ensure_standard_linetypes
from engineering.understand.diff import block_signatures, read_revision
from engineering.understand.snapshot import records_from_doc
from engineering.validator import DrawingValidator
from engineering.verification.report import document_identity_of
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet
from version import __version__


def _sha(path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _sheet():
    """Model space: a 200 x 100 frame, a hole r = 10 at (50, 50), a BOLT insert at
    (150, 50); Layout1: one line (10, 10)-(60, 10)."""
    doc = ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4)
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    msp.add_lwpolyline([(0, 0), (200, 0), (200, 100), (0, 100)], close=True)
    hole = msp.add_circle((50, 50), 10)
    doc.blocks.new("BOLT").add_circle((0, 0), 3)
    msp.add_blockref("BOLT", (150, 50))
    note = doc.layouts.get("Layout1").add_line((10, 10), (60, 10))
    return doc, hole, note


def _revision(doc):
    return records_from_doc(doc, source="test", insert_boxes=False), block_signatures(doc, tol=1e-6)


def _diameter(rid, handle, nominal=40.0):
    return Requirement(
        id=rid,
        quantity="diameter",
        target=(("nominal", nominal), ("unit", "mm")),
        features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", handle),)),),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("document", "synthetic-sheet"),),
    )


async def _open_part(backend, tmp_path):
    """A 200 x 100 frame, a Ø40 bore (r = 20) and a Ø42 bore (r = 21), in mm."""
    base = tmp_path / "base.dxf"
    ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4).saveas(base)
    await backend.drawing_open(str(base))
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    frame = await backend.entity_create_polyline(
        [[0, 0], [200, 0], [200, 100], [0, 100]], closed=True, layer="GEOMETRY"
    )
    bore = await backend.entity_create_circle(50, 50, 20, layer="GEOMETRY")
    wide = await backend.entity_create_circle(150, 50, 21, layer="GEOMETRY")
    return {"frame": frame.handle, "bore": bore.handle, "wide": wide.handle}


def test_an_identical_read_is_parity_over_every_space():
    doc, _hole, _note = _sheet()
    result = compare_artifacts(_revision(doc), _revision(doc))
    assert result["ok"] is True and result["differences"] == []
    summary = result["summary"]
    assert (summary["changed"], summary["added"], summary["removed"]) == (0, 0, 0)
    assert result["counts"]["source"] == {"Layout1": 1, "Model": 3}
    assert result["blocks"]["compared"] is True


def test_a_hole_moved_inside_the_outline_breaks_parity():
    doc, hole, _note = _sheet()
    source = _revision(doc)
    hole.dxf.center = (50.5, 50.0, 0.0)
    result = compare_artifacts(source, _revision(doc))
    assert result["ok"] is False
    # model-space anchors x: 50 / 50.5, 100, 150 on both sides -> robust box
    # x 50..150, y 50..50, diagonal 100, move_tol = 0.02 x 100 = 2.0 >= 0.5,
    # so the hole is paired and reported as moved, not as removed + added
    assert result["move_tol"] == 2.0
    summary = result["summary"]
    assert (summary["changed"], summary["added"], summary["removed"]) == (1, 0, 0)
    (row,) = next(item for item in result["differences"] if item["field"] == "changed")["rows"]
    assert row["changes"] == {"moved_by": [0.5, 0.0]}


def test_a_change_on_a_paper_layout_breaks_parity():
    doc, _hole, note = _sheet()
    source = _revision(doc)
    note.dxf.end = (60.0, 12.0, 0.0)
    result = compare_artifacts(source, _revision(doc))
    assert result["ok"] is False
    (row,) = next(item for item in result["differences"] if item["field"] == "changed")["rows"]
    assert row["space"] == "Layout1"


def test_a_redrawn_block_definition_breaks_parity():
    doc, _hole, _note = _sheet()
    source = _revision(doc)
    doc.blocks.get("BOLT").query("CIRCLE")[0].dxf.radius = 4.0
    result = compare_artifacts(source, _revision(doc))
    assert result["ok"] is False
    assert result["summary"]["changed"] == 0, "every INSERT record is unchanged"
    assert {"field": "blocks_changed", "names": ["BOLT"]} in result["differences"]


def test_read_revision_can_skip_insert_boxes(tmp_path):
    doc, _hole, _note = _sheet()
    path = tmp_path / "sheet.dxf"
    doc.saveas(path)
    snap, blocks = read_revision(str(path), tol=1e-6, insert_boxes=False)
    (insert,) = [record for record in snap.records if record.type == "INSERT"]
    assert insert.bbox is None and snap.insert_boxes is False
    assert set(blocks) == {"BOLT"}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_saved_step_checks_the_delivered_dxf(backend, tmp_path):
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 10, 0)
    delivered = tmp_path / "drawing.dxf"
    ezdxf.new("R2010").saveas(delivered)
    result = await DrawingValidator().run(backend, delivered_path=str(delivered))
    codes = {finding.code for finding in result.findings}
    assert "not_saved" not in codes and "no_path" not in codes
    missing = await DrawingValidator().run(backend, delivered_path=str(tmp_path / "absent.dxf"))
    assert "delivery_artifact_missing" in {finding.code for finding in missing.findings}
    empty = tmp_path / "empty.dxf"
    empty.write_bytes(b"")
    unreadable = await DrawingValidator().run(backend, delivered_path=str(empty))
    assert "delivery_artifact_unreadable" in {finding.code for finding in unreadable.findings}
    legacy = await DrawingValidator().run(backend)
    assert "not_saved" in {finding.code for finding in legacy.findings}


def test_the_git_sha_is_the_server_checkout_not_the_working_directory(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    root = Path(delivery.__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    expected = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "-C", str(root), "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    ).stdout.strip()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)  # not a git checkout
    assert delivery._git_sha() == expected


def test_the_git_sha_is_none_outside_a_checkout(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(delivery, "_PACKAGE_ROOT", tmp_path)
    assert delivery._git_sha() is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_delivery_never_re_points_the_document_and_records_where_it_was(
    backend, tmp_path
):
    await ensure_standard_linetypes(backend)
    await ensure_engineering_layers(backend)
    await backend.entity_create_line(0, 0, 40, 0, layer="GEOMETRY")
    identity = await document_identity_of(backend)
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False
    )
    assert result.status == "success"
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    assert (info.full_path, info.saved) == ("", False)
    manifest = json.loads((bundle / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert manifest["schema_version"] == "1.1"
    assert manifest["source_document"] == {"name": "untitled.dxf", "path": None, "saved": False}
    assert manifest["dxf_export"] == {
        "method": "ezdxf.write",
        "dxf_version": "AC1024",
        "repointed": False,
    }
    assert manifest["document"]["document_id"] == identity["document_id"]
    assert manifest["document"]["revision"] == identity["revision"]
    assert manifest["build"] == {"version": __version__, "git_sha": manifest["git_sha"]}
    assert manifest["parity"]["ok"] is True
    assert manifest["parity"]["counts"]["source"] == {"Model": 1}
    assert manifest["parity"]["summary"]["unchanged"] == 1
    assert (manifest["requirements_hash"], manifest["report_hash"]) == (None, None)
    codes = {finding["code"] for finding in manifest["validator"]["findings"]}
    assert "not_saved" not in codes and "no_path" not in codes


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_requirements_are_measured_on_the_delivered_dxf(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R1", handles["bore"]),), units="mm")
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    result = await deliver_drawing(
        backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False, requirements=rs
    )
    assert result.status == "success"
    report = json.loads((bundle / "acceptance.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert Path(report["artifact"]["path"]).resolve() == (bundle / "drawing.dxf").resolve()
    assert report["artifact"]["sha256"] == _sha(bundle / "drawing.dxf")
    assert report["rebinding"]["handles_stable"] is True
    assert [row["method"] for row in report["rebinding"]["rows"]] == ["handle"]
    assert report["comparison"] == [
        {
            "id": "R1",
            "before": {"status": "pass", "value": 40.0},
            "after": {"status": "pass", "value": 40.0},
            "changed": False,
        }
    ]
    manifest = json.loads((bundle / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert manifest["requirements_hash"] == rs.digest()
    assert manifest["report_hash"] == _sha(bundle / "acceptance.json")
    assert manifest["source_document"]["path"] == str(tmp_path / "base.dxf")
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    assert info.full_path == str(tmp_path / "base.dxf"), "the delivery re-pointed the document"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_verify_manifest_checks_the_report_and_requirements_hashes(backend, tmp_path):
    handles = await _open_part(backend, tmp_path)
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R1", handles["bore"]),), units="mm")
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    await deliver_drawing(
        backend, bundle, formats=["dxf"], min_score=0, strict_critique=False, requirements=rs
    )
    assert verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))["ok"] is True
    report_path = bundle / "acceptance.json"
    report = json.loads(report_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    report["requirements_digest"] = "0" * 64
    report_path.write_text(json.dumps(report), encoding="utf-8")
    checked = verify_manifest(str(bundle / "manifest.json"))
    assert checked["ok"] is False
    assert [row["filename"] for row in checked["stale"]] == ["acceptance.json"]
    assert [row["field"] for row in checked["inconsistent"]] == ["requirements_hash"]
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Replace `engineering/delivery.py`**

Replace the whole file with (it keeps every Task 16 addition; `drawing_snapshot` and `compare_drawing_snapshots` stay exported for their callers):

```python
"""Auditable drawing delivery: artifacts, hashes, all-spaces reopen parity, acceptance.

``deliver_drawing`` writes the bundle (``drawing.dxf`` always, PDF / PNG on
request), validates and critiques the drawing, reads the delivered DXF back and
compares it with a fresh serialisation of the source document over every space
(:func:`compare_artifacts`: ``diff_snapshots`` + ``block_signatures``), and -
with requirements - verifies them on the delivered DXF
(``engineering.verification.artifact.verify_artifact``). ``manifest.json``
records every artifact's sha256, the source document (id, revision, path and
saved flag), the DXF export (method and version), the acceptance summary with
the requirements digest and the report hash, and the build (version, and the
git SHA of the server's own checkout when there is one).

Exporting never re-points the document (v1.7.0, B23): the live engine writes
through ``Document.Export``, the headless one writes a copy; the open document
keeps its name, path and saved flag, and those go into the manifest. The
validator's "saved" step checks the delivered DXF in this context.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

from backends.quarantine import DocumentQuarantineError
from version import __version__

from .scoring import combine
from .validator import DrawingValidator

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from backends.base import AutoCADBackend
    from engineering.understand.snapshot import Snapshot
    from engineering.verification.types import RequirementSet


_FORMAT_FILENAMES = {
    "dxf": "drawing.dxf",
    "pdf": "drawing.pdf",
    "png": "preview.png",
}
#: 1.1 adds document, source_document, dxf_export, build, requirements_hash,
#: report_hash, blocking and (with requirements) acceptance.
MANIFEST_SCHEMA = "1.1"
#: Delivery parity compares coordinates within this distance (drawing units).
PARITY_TOL = 1e-6
#: Rows of each parity difference kept in the manifest; the counts are complete.
PARITY_ROWS = 50
#: The server package's own root: the repository in a source checkout,
#: site-packages in a wheel (no .git there, so no SHA).
_PACKAGE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


@dataclass
class DeliveryResult:
    status: str
    output_dir: str
    manifest_path: str
    artifacts: list[dict]
    score: dict
    parity: dict
    #: Set only when the delivery verified requirements (B22); a delivery
    #: without them returns exactly the six fields above.
    geometry_acceptance: dict | None = None
    drafting_quality: dict | None = None
    artifact_integrity: dict | None = None
    coverage: dict | None = None
    blocking: list[str] | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        data = {
            "status": self.status,
            "output_dir": self.output_dir,
            "manifest_path": self.manifest_path,
            "artifacts": list(self.artifacts),
            "score": dict(self.score),
            "parity": dict(self.parity),
        }
        if self.geometry_acceptance is not None:
            data["geometry_acceptance"] = dict(self.geometry_acceptance)
            data["drafting_quality"] = dict(self.drafting_quality or {})
            data["artifact_integrity"] = dict(self.artifact_integrity or {})
            data["coverage"] = dict(self.coverage or {})
            data["blocking"] = list(self.blocking or [])
        return data


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _git_sha() -> str | None:
    """The commit of the server's own checkout, or None.

    Read at the package's root, never in the working directory: a server
    started from another repository used to stamp that repository's HEAD into
    the manifest. A wheel has no ``.git`` beside the package, and a checkout
    whose top level is not the package root (a wheel installed into a venv
    that lives inside some other repository) is not ours: both give None, and
    the manifest carries the version alone.
    """
    root = _PACKAGE_ROOT
    if not (root / ".git").exists():
        return None
    try:
        top = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(root), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=2,
        ).stdout.strip()
        if not top or Path(top).resolve() != root.resolve():
            return None
        sha = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(root), "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=2,
        ).stdout.strip()
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    return sha or None


async def drawing_snapshot(backend: AutoCADBackend) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the stable structural fields used for save/reopen comparison."""
    await backend._ensure_document_state()
    entities = await backend.entity_list(limit=100_000)
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    listed_entity_count = len(entities)
    reported_entity_count = int(info.entity_count)
    return {
        "entity_count": reported_entity_count,
        "listed_entity_count": listed_entity_count,
        "inventory_complete": listed_entity_count == reported_entity_count,
        "types": dict(sorted(Counter(item.type for item in entities).items())),
        "layers": dict(sorted(Counter(item.layer for item in entities).items())),
        "extents_min": [float(value) for value in info.extents_min],
        "extents_max": [float(value) for value in info.extents_max],
    }


def compare_drawing_snapshots(
    source: dict[str, Any],
    reopened: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    tolerance: float = 1e-6,
) -> dict:
    """Compare counts exactly and drawing extents within ``tolerance``."""
    differences: list[dict] = []
    for label, snapshot in (("source", source), ("reopened", reopened)):
        if snapshot.get("inventory_complete") is False:
            differences.append(
                {
                    "field": f"{label}_inventory_complete",
                    "entity_count": snapshot.get("entity_count"),
                    "listed_entity_count": snapshot.get("listed_entity_count"),
                }
            )
    for field in ("entity_count", "types", "layers"):
        if source.get(field) != reopened.get(field):
            differences.append(
                {"field": field, "source": source.get(field), "reopened": reopened.get(field)}
            )
    for field in ("extents_min", "extents_max"):
        left = source.get(field, [])
        right = reopened.get(field, [])
        equal = len(left) == len(right) and all(
            abs(float(a) - float(b)) <= tolerance for a, b in zip(left, right, strict=True)
        )
        if not equal:
            differences.append({"field": field, "source": left, "reopened": right})
    return {
        "ok": not differences,
        "tolerance": tolerance,
        "source": source,
        "reopened": reopened,
        "differences": differences,
    }


def _artifact_record(fmt: str, path: Path, *, status: str, error: str | None = None) -> dict:
    record = {
        "format": fmt,
        "filename": path.name,
        "status": status,
        "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size if path.is_file() else 0,
        "sha256": _sha256(path) if path.is_file() else None,
    }
    if error:
        record["error"] = error
    return record


def _space_counts(snap: Snapshot) -> dict[str, int]:
    return dict(sorted(Counter(record.space for record in snap.records).items()))


def compare_artifacts(source, reopened, *, tol: float = PARITY_TOL) -> dict:
    """Delivery parity over every space: zero changed, added or removed entities
    within ``tol``, the same entity count per space, the same block definitions.

    ``source`` and ``reopened`` are ``(Snapshot, block signatures)`` pairs
    (``read_revision``). A hole moved inside the outline is a ``changed`` row
    (``moved_by``) - the 1.6 counts-and-extents comparison let it through.
    """
    from engineering.understand.diff import CATEGORIES, block_changes, diff_snapshots

    old_snap, old_blocks = source
    new_snap, new_blocks = reopened
    diff = diff_snapshots(old_snap, new_snap, tol=tol)
    blocks = block_changes(old_blocks, new_blocks)
    counts = {"source": _space_counts(old_snap), "reopened": _space_counts(new_snap)}
    differences: list[dict] = []
    for category in CATEGORIES:
        rows = diff[category]
        if rows:
            differences.append(
                {"field": category, "count": len(rows), "rows": rows[:PARITY_ROWS]}
            )
    if counts["source"] != counts["reopened"]:
        differences.append({"field": "counts", **counts})
    if not blocks.get("compared"):
        differences.append({"field": "blocks", "reason": blocks.get("reason")})
    else:
        for key in ("added", "removed", "changed"):
            if blocks[key]:
                differences.append({"field": f"blocks_{key}", "names": list(blocks[key])})
    return {
        "ok": not differences,
        "tolerance": tol,
        "move_tol": diff["move_tol"],
        "handles_stable": diff["handles_stable"],
        "summary": diff["summary"],
        "counts": counts,
        "blocks": blocks,
        "differences": differences,
    }


def _parity_failure(field: str, error: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "ok": False,
        "tolerance": PARITY_TOL,
        "move_tol": None,
        "handles_stable": None,
        "summary": None,
        "counts": None,
        "blocks": None,
        "differences": [{"field": field, "error": error}],
    }


async def source_revision(
    backend: AutoCADBackend, *, tol: float = PARITY_TOL
) -> tuple[Snapshot, dict[str, str]]:
    """A fresh serialisation of the open document, read back: the parity source.

    Both engines write the document through ``drawing_export_dxf`` (which never
    re-points it) into a private folder and read that file. Not the model in
    memory: ezdxf leaves unset DXF defaults unset in memory and writes them out
    (an MTEXT without ``char_height`` reads 0.0 in memory and 2.5 after a write
    - measured on 2026-10-24), so an in-memory source would differ from every
    honest copy.
    """
    from engineering.understand.diff import read_revision

    folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="acadmcp_parity_")
    try:
        target = Path(folder) / "source.dxf"
        await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(target))
        return await asyncio.to_thread(
            read_revision, str(target), tol=tol, insert_boxes=False
        )
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)


def _manifest_records(manifest: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Every file record a manifest hashed: artifacts, validation, acceptance report."""
    records = [dict(item) for item in manifest.get("artifacts") or []]
    if manifest.get("validation_artifact"):
        records.append(dict(manifest["validation_artifact"]))
    acceptance = manifest.get("acceptance") or {}
    for key in ("report", "markdown"):
        if acceptance.get(key):
            records.append(dict(acceptance[key]))
    return records


def verify_manifest(path: str) -> dict:
    """Recompute every hash a delivery manifest recorded (B23 made observable).

    ``stale``: a file whose sha256 no longer matches (it was edited after the
    manifest was written). ``missing``: a recorded file that is gone.
    ``skipped``: a record that was never created (an unsupported format).
    ``inconsistent``: a record that cannot be trusted - a file name that is
    not a plain name inside the bundle (never read), a ``report_hash`` that is
    not the acceptance report's recorded sha256, or a ``requirements_hash``
    that is not the digest the acceptance report says it measured. ``ok`` only
    when stale, missing and inconsistent are all empty. Refuses (ValueError) a
    file that is not JSON or holds no ``artifacts`` list.
    """
    manifest_path = Path(path)
    try:
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: not a readable delivery manifest ({exc})") from exc
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict) or not isinstance(manifest.get("artifacts"), list):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: not a delivery manifest (no artifacts list)")
    base = manifest_path.parent
    checked: list[dict] = []
    stale: list[dict] = []
    missing: list[dict] = []
    skipped: list[dict] = []
    inconsistent: list[dict] = []
    for record in _manifest_records(manifest):
        filename = str(record.get("filename") or "")
        recorded = record.get("sha256")
        row = {"filename": filename, "format": record.get("format")}
        if not filename or recorded is None:
            skipped.append({**row, "status": record.get("status")})
            continue
        if Path(filename).name != filename or filename in (".", ".."):
            inconsistent.append(
                {**row, "field": "filename", "reason": "not a plain file name inside the bundle"}
            )
            continue
        target = base / filename
        if not target.is_file():
            missing.append(row)
            continue
        row = {**row, "recorded_sha256": recorded, "sha256": _sha256(target)}
        checked.append(row)
        if row["sha256"] != recorded:
            stale.append(row)
    report_record = (manifest.get("acceptance") or {}).get("report")
    if manifest.get("report_hash") is not None and (
        not report_record or report_record.get("sha256") != manifest["report_hash"]
    ):
        inconsistent.append(
            {
                "field": "report_hash",
                "reason": "the manifest's report_hash is not the acceptance report's sha256",
            }
        )
    if manifest.get("requirements_hash") is not None and report_record:
        report_file = base / str(report_record.get("filename") or "")
        digest = None
        if Path(str(report_record.get("filename") or "")).name == report_record.get(
            "filename"
        ) and report_file.is_file():
            try:
                digest = json.loads(report_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get(
                    "requirements_digest"
                )
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
                digest = None
        if digest != manifest["requirements_hash"]:
            inconsistent.append(
                {
                    "field": "requirements_hash",
                    "manifest": manifest["requirements_hash"],
                    "report": digest,
                }
            )
    return {
        "ok": not (stale or missing or inconsistent),
        "manifest": str(manifest_path),
        "status": manifest.get("status"),
        "checked": checked,
        "stale": stale,
        "missing": missing,
        "skipped": skipped,
        "inconsistent": inconsistent,
    }


async def _acceptance(
    backend: AutoCADBackend,
    requirements: RequirementSet,
    destination: Path,
    identity_at_start: dict,
    delivered: Path | None,
) -> dict:
    """Measure ``requirements`` on the delivered DXF (read fresh, re-bound from the
    document), write acceptance.json / acceptance.md, hash both. Without a DXF
    the document is measured and the report says why."""
    from engineering.verification.artifact import verify_artifact
    from engineering.verification.report import (
        add_finding,
        mark_stale,
        render_markdown,
        verify_requirements,
    )

    if delivered is not None:
        try:
            report = await verify_artifact(str(delivered), requirements, backend=backend)
        except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
            report = await verify_requirements(backend, requirements)
            report = add_finding(
                report,
                kind="artifact_unread",
                requirement="*",
                message=f"the delivered DXF could not be read fresh: {exc}",
            )
    else:
        report = await verify_requirements(backend, requirements)
        report = add_finding(
            report,
            kind="artifact_unread",
            requirement="*",
            message="no canonical DXF was written, so nothing delivered was measured",
        )
    report = mark_stale(report, identity_at_start)
    json_path = destination / "acceptance.json"
    md_path = destination / "acceptance.md"
    json_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    md_path.write_text(render_markdown(report), encoding="utf-8")
    return {
        "report": report,
        "json": _artifact_record("json", json_path, status="created"),
        "markdown": _artifact_record("md", md_path, status="created"),
    }


async def deliver_drawing(
    backend: AutoCADBackend,
    output_dir: str | Path,
    *,
    formats: list[str] | None = None,
    min_score: float = 95.0,
    strict_critique: bool = True,
    expected: dict | None = None,
    requirements: RequirementSet | None = None,
) -> DeliveryResult:
    """Export, validate, hash, read back and (with requirements) verify a bundle.

    Status: ``failed_export`` (an artifact was not written), then
    ``failed_validation`` (validator, critique, score or parity), then
    ``failed_acceptance`` (a requirement did not pass on the delivered DXF, the
    evidence went stale, or a dimension disagrees with its geometry), else
    ``success``. ``blocking`` lists every reason, whichever status won. The
    open document is never re-pointed or saved.
    """
    from engineering.verification.report import document_identity_of, summarize

    requested = list(
        dict.fromkeys(item.lower().strip() for item in (formats or ["dxf", "pdf", "png"]))
    )
    unknown = [item for item in requested if item not in _FORMAT_FILENAMES]
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported delivery format(s): {', '.join(unknown)}")
    if not 0 <= float(min_score) <= 100:
        raise ValueError("min_score must be between 0 and 100")

    destination = Path(output_dir).resolve()
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if not destination.is_dir():
        raise ValueError(f"Delivery output is not a directory: {destination}")

    started = time.perf_counter()
    timings: dict[str, float] = {}
    artifacts: list[dict] = []
    capabilities = backend.capabilities().to_dict()
    feature_caps = capabilities["features"]

    identity = await document_identity_of(backend)
    info = await backend.drawing_info()
    source_document = {
        "name": info.name,
        "path": info.full_path or None,
        "saved": bool(info.saved),
    }
    source = None
    source_error = None
    source_started = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        source = await source_revision(backend)
    except DocumentQuarantineError:
        raise  # a RuntimeError subclass: a quarantined document is refused, never delivered
    except (ValueError, OSError, RuntimeError) as exc:
        source_error = str(exc) or type(exc).__name__
    timings["source_ms"] = round((time.perf_counter() - source_started) * 1000, 2)

    dxf_export = None
    # DXF is always emitted: it is the canonical, re-readable parity artifact.
    export_formats = ["dxf", *(item for item in requested if item != "dxf")]
    for fmt in export_formats:
        artifact_path = destination / _FORMAT_FILENAMES[fmt]
        capability = feature_caps.get(fmt, {"supported": False, "reason": "not_declared"})
        export_started = time.perf_counter()
        if not capability.get("supported"):
            artifacts.append(
                _artifact_record(
                    fmt,
                    artifact_path,
                    status="unsupported",
                    error=str(capability.get("reason") or "backend does not support format"),
                )
            )
            continue
        try:
            if fmt == "dxf":
                exported = await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(artifact_path))
                dxf_export = {
                    "method": exported.get("method"),
                    "dxf_version": exported.get("dxf_version"),
                    "repointed": exported.get("repointed"),
                }
            elif fmt == "pdf":
                await backend.drawing_export_pdf(str(artifact_path))
            else:
                png = await backend.view_screenshot()
                if not png:
                    raise RuntimeError("backend returned no PNG bytes")
                artifact_path.write_bytes(png)
            artifacts.append(_artifact_record(fmt, artifact_path, status="created"))
        except Exception as exc:
            artifacts.append(_artifact_record(fmt, artifact_path, status="failed", error=str(exc)))
        finally:
            timings[f"export_{fmt}_ms"] = round((time.perf_counter() - export_started) * 1000, 2)

    delivered = destination / _FORMAT_FILENAMES["dxf"]
    validation_started = time.perf_counter()
    # One run context: the validator and the critique share one all-spaces read.
    qa_shared: dict = {}
    validation = await DrawingValidator().run(
        backend, expected=expected or {}, shared=qa_shared, delivered_path=str(delivered)
    )
    critique_issues = await backend.drawing_critique(focus=None, shared=qa_shared)
    critique_summary = {severity: 0 for severity in ("error", "warning", "info")}
    for issue in critique_issues:
        critique_summary[issue.severity] += 1

    dxf_record = next(item for item in artifacts if item["format"] == "dxf")
    if dxf_record["status"] != "created":
        parity = _parity_failure("dxf", "canonical DXF was not created")
    elif source is None:
        parity = _parity_failure(
            "source", f"the source document could not be serialised: {source_error}"
        )
    else:
        from engineering.understand.diff import read_revision

        try:
            reopened = await asyncio.to_thread(
                read_revision, str(delivered), tol=PARITY_TOL, insert_boxes=False
            )
            parity = compare_artifacts(source, reopened)
        except ValueError as exc:
            parity = _parity_failure("reopen", str(exc))

    score = combine(
        validation.summary,
        critique_summary,
        {"error": 0 if parity["ok"] else 1},
    )
    timings["validation_ms"] = round((time.perf_counter() - validation_started) * 1000, 2)

    acceptance = None
    if requirements is not None:
        acceptance_started = time.perf_counter()
        acceptance = await _acceptance(
            backend,
            requirements,
            destination,
            identity,
            delivered if dxf_record["status"] == "created" else None,
        )
        timings["acceptance_ms"] = round((time.perf_counter() - acceptance_started) * 1000, 2)

    failed_export = any(item["status"] != "created" for item in artifacts)
    blocking_issues = [
        issue
        for issue in critique_issues
        if issue.severity == "error" or (strict_critique and issue.severity != "info")
    ]
    critique_blocked = bool(blocking_issues)
    validation_blocked = (
        not validation.ok
        or not parity["ok"]
        or score["score"] < float(min_score)
        or critique_blocked
    )
    blocking: list[str] = []
    if failed_export:
        blocking.append(
            "export: "
            + ", ".join(
                f"{item['format']} {item['status']}"
                for item in artifacts
                if item["status"] != "created"
            )
        )
    if not validation.ok:
        blocking.append(f"validator: {validation.summary['error']} error(s)")
    if not parity["ok"]:
        blocking.append("parity: the delivered DXF differs from the source document")
    if score["score"] < float(min_score):
        blocking.append(f"score: {score['score']} < min_score {float(min_score)}")
    if critique_blocked:
        blocking.append(f"critique: {len(blocking_issues)} blocking issue(s)")
    acceptance_blocked = acceptance is not None and not acceptance["report"]["accepted"]
    if acceptance is not None:
        blocking.extend(acceptance["report"]["blocking"])
    if failed_export:
        status = "failed_export"
    elif validation_blocked:
        status = "failed_validation"
    elif acceptance_blocked:
        status = "failed_acceptance"
    else:
        status = "success"

    validation_payload = {
        "validator": validation.to_dict(),
        "critique": [issue.to_dict() for issue in critique_issues],
        "critique_summary": critique_summary,
        "score": score,
        "parity": parity,
    }
    validation_path = destination / "validation.json"
    validation_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(validation_payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    plan = backend.get_plan_spec()
    git_sha = _git_sha()
    timings["total_ms"] = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 2)
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": MANIFEST_SCHEMA,
        "status": status,
        "version": __version__,
        "git_sha": git_sha,
        "build": {"version": __version__, "git_sha": git_sha},
        "generated_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "backend": backend.name,
        "capabilities": capabilities,
        "document": identity,
        "source_document": source_document,
        "dxf_export": dxf_export,
        "spec_hash": plan.get("spec_hash") if plan else None,
        "requirements_hash": requirements.digest() if requirements is not None else None,
        "report_hash": acceptance["json"]["sha256"] if acceptance is not None else None,
        "requested_formats": requested,
        "min_score": float(min_score),
        "strict_critique": strict_critique,
        "score": score,
        "validator": validation.to_dict(),
        "critique": validation_payload["critique"],
        "critique_summary": critique_summary,
        "parity": parity,
        "blocking": blocking,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
        "validation_artifact": _artifact_record("json", validation_path, status="created"),
        "timings_ms": timings,
    }
    if acceptance is not None:
        manifest["acceptance"] = {
            **summarize(acceptance["report"]),
            "report": acceptance["json"],
            "markdown": acceptance["markdown"],
        }
    manifest_path = destination / "manifest.json"
    manifest_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    result = DeliveryResult(
        status=status,
        output_dir=str(destination),
        manifest_path=str(manifest_path),
        artifacts=artifacts,
        score=score,
        parity=parity,
    )
    if acceptance is not None:
        report = acceptance["report"]
        result.geometry_acceptance = {
            **summarize(report),
            "report_file": acceptance["json"]["filename"],
        }
        result.drafting_quality = dict(score)
        result.artifact_integrity = {
            "ok": not failed_export and parity["ok"],
            "parity_ok": parity["ok"],
            "artifacts": [
                {
                    "format": item["format"],
                    "filename": item["filename"],
                    "status": item["status"],
                    "sha256": item["sha256"],
                }
                for item in artifacts
            ],
            "parity": {
                "ok": parity["ok"],
                "summary": parity["summary"],
                "counts": parity["counts"],
                "blocks": parity["blocks"],
            },
            "dxf_export": dxf_export,
            "reverified": report.get("artifact"),
        }
        result.coverage = dict(report["coverage"])
        result.blocking = list(blocking)
    return result
```
