```python
"""Task 17 (B21): requirements measured again on the saved file, read fresh.

Spec §8.2 item 9: a save that renumbers handles is re-bound by feature tag,
then by geometry; a copied feature tag makes the binding ambiguous, which is
`unknown` - never a pick. Ø40 is a circle of r = 20, a pin of r = 5 is Ø10.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import ezdxf
import pytest

import server
from backends.base import UnsupportedCapabilityError
from engineering.mech.xdata import encode
from engineering.layers import ensure_engineering_layers, ensure_standard_linetypes
from engineering.understand.diff import diff_snapshots
from engineering.understand.snapshot import read_snapshot
from engineering.verification import artifact
from engineering.verification.artifact import ArtifactReadError, verify_artifact
from engineering.verification.report import verify_requirements
from engineering.verification.types import FeatureSelector, Requirement, RequirementSet

TAG = {"kind": "feature", "part": "P1", "feature": "bore", "view": "front"}


class _FakeCtx:
    def __init__(self, backend):
        self.lifespan_context = {"backend": backend}

    async def info(self, message):
        pass

    async def warning(self, message):
        pass


def _diameter(rid, handle, nominal=40.0):
    return Requirement(
        id=rid,
        quantity="diameter",
        target=(("nominal", nominal), ("unit", "mm")),
        features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", handle),)),),
        provenance="explicit",
        source=(("document", "synthetic-sheet"),),
    )


def _raw_diameter(rid, handle, nominal=40.0) -> dict:
    return {
        "units": "mm",
        "items": [
            {
                "id": rid,
                "quantity": "diameter",
                "target": {"nominal": nominal, "unit": "mm"},
                "features": [{"kind": "handle", "handle": handle}],
                "provenance": "explicit",
                "source": {"document": "synthetic-sheet"},
            }
        ],
    }


def _write(path, circles) -> dict:
    """A 200 x 100 frame, then ``circles`` in creation order: (name, cx, cy, r, tagged).
    Returns ``{name: handle}``; the frame is always created first."""
    doc = ezdxf.new("R2010", units=4)
    doc.appids.add("ACADMCP_MECH")
    msp = doc.modelspace()
    handles = {
        "frame": msp.add_lwpolyline(
            [(0, 0), (200, 0), (200, 100), (0, 100)], close=True
        ).dxf.handle
    }
    for name, cx, cy, radius, tagged in circles:
        circle = msp.add_circle((cx, cy), radius)
        if tagged:
            rows = encode(TAG, kinds=("feature",))
            circle.set_xdata("ACADMCP_MECH", [row for row in rows if row[0] != 1001])
        handles[name] = circle.dxf.handle
    doc.saveas(path)
    return handles


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_save_that_renumbers_handles_is_re_bound_by_geometry(backend, tmp_path):
    source = _write(
        tmp_path / "a.dxf",
        [("bore", 50, 50, 20, False), ("pin", 150, 50, 5, False)],
    )
    saved = _write(
        tmp_path / "b.dxf",
        [("pin", 150, 50, 5, False), ("bore", 50, 50, 20, False)],
    )
    assert saved["pin"] == source["bore"], "the save gave the bore's handle to the pin"
    await backend.drawing_open(str(tmp_path / "a.dxf"))
    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R1", source["bore"]),), units="mm")
    report = await verify_artifact(str(tmp_path / "b.dxf"), rs, backend=backend)
    (row,) = report["rows"]
    assert row["status"] == "pass"
    assert abs(row["measured"]["value"] - 40.0) <= 1e-9  # the bore, 2 x 20 - not the pin
    assert row["handles"] == [saved["bore"]]
    assert report["rebinding"]["handles_stable"] is False
    assert [item["method"] for item in report["rebinding"]["rows"]] == ["geometry"]
    assert report["comparison"] == [
        {
            "id": "R1",
            "before": {"status": "pass", "value": 40.0},
            "after": {"status": "pass", "value": 40.0},
            "changed": False,
        }
    ]
    # the stability evidence, by hand: frame, bore and pin match exactly and only
    # the frame kept its handle - 1 of 3 is less than half
    diff = diff_snapshots(read_snapshot(str(tmp_path / "a.dxf")), read_snapshot(str(tmp_path / "b.dxf")))
    assert diff["handle_evidence"] == {"exact_matches": 3, "kept_handle": 1}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_without_the_source_the_handle_is_bound_afresh(backend, tmp_path):

        [("bore", 50, 50, 20, False), ("pin", 150, 50, 5, False)],

    rs = RequirementSet(requirements=(_diameter("R1", source["bore"]),), units="mm")
    report = await verify_artifact(str(tmp_path / "b.dxf"), rs)
    (row,) = report["rows"]

    assert row["status"] == "fail"
    assert [item["method"] for item in report["rebinding"]["rows"]] == ["fresh"]
    assert report["comparison"] is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_copied_feature_tag_is_unknown_never_a_pick(backend, tmp_path):
    source = _write(
        tmp_path / "a.dxf",
        [("bore", 50, 50, 20, True), ("pin", 150, 80, 5, False)],

    saved = _write(

    assert report["rebinding"]["handles_stable"] is False
    assert [item["method"] for item in report["rebinding"]["rows"]] == ["tag"]
    assert report["status"] == "incomplete"
    assert report["accepted"] is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_verify_requirements_on_an_artifact_goes_through_verify_artifact(backend, tmp_path):
    source = _write(tmp_path / "a.dxf", [("bore", 50, 50, 20, False), ("pin", 150, 50, 5, False)])

@pytest.mark.asyncio

    dwg = tmp_path / "part.dwg"
    dwg.write_bytes(b"AC1032 synthetic")

class _ComLike:
    name = "com"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_live_dwg_is_read_from_a_private_copy(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    saved = tmp_path / "part.dwg"
    saved.write_bytes(b"AC1032 synthetic")
    fresh = tmp_path / "fresh.dxf"
    _write(fresh, [("bore", 50, 50, 20, False)])
    seen: dict = {}

    async def fake_take_snapshot(backend, path=None, *, block_defs=False):
        seen["path"] = Path(path)
        seen["bytes"] = Path(path).read_bytes()
        seen["block_defs"] = block_defs
        return read_snapshot(str(fresh), block_defs=block_defs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(artifact, "take_snapshot", fake_take_snapshot)
    snap, method = await artifact._fresh_snapshot(saved, _ComLike())
    assert seen["path"].name == "part.dwg"
    assert seen["path"].parent != saved.parent, "AutoCAD opens a copy, never the saved file"
    assert seen["bytes"] == b"AC1032 synthetic"
    assert seen["block_defs"] is True, "observe() needs the block definitions to explode INSERTs"
    assert not seen["path"].exists(), "the private copy is deleted after the read"
    assert method == "private copy, Documents.Open read-only + Document.Export"
    assert len(snap.records) == 2


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_dwg_that_cannot_be_copied_is_a_named_refusal(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    saved = tmp_path / "part.dwg"
    saved.write_bytes(b"AC1032 synthetic")

    def locked(src, dst):
        raise PermissionError(13, "The process cannot access the file")

    monkeypatch.setattr(artifact.shutil, "copy2", locked)
    with pytest.raises(ArtifactReadError, match="private copy"):
        await artifact._fresh_snapshot(saved, _ComLike())
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Write `engineering/verification/artifact.py`**

```python
"""Saved-artifact verification: the requirements measured again on the file itself.

Spec §5 Task 17 (B21). ``verify_artifact`` reads the saved file fresh - a DXF
through ``ezdxf.readfile``, a DWG on the live engine through a private copy
that AutoCAD opens read-only, exports and closes - and never the model in
memory. Every requirement is measured again on that read.

With the source document at hand (``backend``), each requirement's features
are re-bound from the source binding, one feature at a time:

1. by handle - only while handles look stable (``diff_snapshots``'
   ``handles_stable``: at least one exact match and at least half of them kept
   their handle) and only onto a feature of the same type, space and
   ``ACADMCP_*`` tags; a save that renumbers handles makes a handle name a
   stranger, so this stage then does not run;
2. by feature tag - the same ``ACADMCP_*`` payload on exactly one feature of
   the same type;
3. by geometry - exactly one feature of the same type, space and nesting depth
   whose geometry equals the source's within the epsilon.

Two candidates at a stage - a copied feature tag, two identical holes - make
the requirement ``unknown`` with both candidates listed: never a pick. Nothing
found is ``unknown`` too. Without ``backend`` the requirements are bound afresh
on the file. The report carries the re-binding (the method per requirement)
and - with ``backend`` - the before / after comparison and the source document
as its evidence, so ``mark_stale`` can tell when the document moved after the
artifact was written.

The DWG route copies the file first because a fresh read must not be the
document AutoCAD already has open (``take_snapshot`` would export that one,
unsaved edits included). Whether AutoCAD lets another process copy a DWG it
holds open is the live measurement of Task 17's live step; a copy that fails
is an ``ArtifactReadError`` naming the reason, never a silent fallback.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import math
import shutil
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from backends.capability import UnsupportedCapabilityError
from engineering.understand.diff import diff_snapshots
from engineering.understand.snapshot import Snapshot, dxf_version_of, read_snapshot, take_snapshot
from engineering.verification.binding import bind
from engineering.verification.measure import default_epsilon, evaluate
from engineering.verification.observe import observe
from engineering.verification.report import (
    BINDING_POLICIES,
    MeasuredRun,
    attach_dimension_evidence,
    build_report,
    dimension_evidence,
    mark_stale,
    measure_requirements,
    sha256_file,
)
from engineering.verification.types import Binding, FeatureRef, ObservedGeometry, RequirementSet

__all__ = ["ArtifactReadError", "rebind", "verify_artifact"]


class ArtifactReadError(ValueError):
    """A saved artifact could not be read fresh; the message says why."""


async def _fresh_snapshot(target: Path, backend) -> tuple[Snapshot, str]:
    """``(snapshot, how it was read)`` of the file at ``target``, never of a model in memory."""
    suffix = target.suffix.lower()
    if suffix == ".dxf":
        return await asyncio.to_thread(read_snapshot, str(target), block_defs=True), "ezdxf.readfile"
    if suffix != ".dwg":
        raise ValueError(f"{target}: a saved artifact is a .dxf or a .dwg, not {suffix or 'this'}")
    if backend is None or backend.name != "com":
        raise UnsupportedCapabilityError(
            "dwg",
            f"{target} is a DWG, which the headless engine cannot read. Verify the drawing's "
            ".dxf copy, or run the server on the live engine (AUTOCAD_MCP_BACKEND=com).",
        )
    folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="acadmcp_verify_")
    try:
        copy = Path(folder) / target.name
        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(shutil.copy2, target, copy)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ArtifactReadError(
                f"{target}: could not take a private copy of the saved file to read it fresh "
                f"({exc}); AutoCAD may hold it locked - save a .dxf copy and verify that"
            ) from exc
        snap = await take_snapshot(backend, str(copy), block_defs=True)
        return snap, "private copy, Documents.Open read-only + Document.Export"
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)


def _same(a, b, eps: float) -> bool:
    if isinstance(a, bool) or isinstance(b, bool):
        return a == b
    if isinstance(a, (int, float)) and isinstance(b, (int, float)):
        fa, fb = float(a), float(b)
        return math.isfinite(fa) and math.isfinite(fb) and abs(fa - fb) <= eps
    if isinstance(a, (tuple, list)) and isinstance(b, (tuple, list)):
        return len(a) == len(b) and all(_same(x, y, eps) for x, y in zip(a, b, strict=True))
    return a == b


def _same_geometry(f, g, eps: float) -> bool:
    return (
        f.type == g.type
        and f.space == g.space
        and len(f.block_path) == len(g.block_path)
        and _same(f.geometry, g.geometry, eps)
    )


def _rebind_feature(f, after: ObservedGeometry, *, handles_stable: bool, by_key: dict, eps: float):
    """``(feature or None, method, candidates)`` for one source feature."""
    if handles_stable:
        g = by_key.get((f.handle, f.block_path))
        if g is not None and g.type == f.type and g.space == f.space and g.tags == f.tags:
            return g, "handle", ()
    if f.tags:
        tagged = [g for g in after.features if g.tags == f.tags and g.type == f.type]
        if len(tagged) == 1:
            return tagged[0], "tag", ()
        if len(tagged) > 1:
            return None, "tag", tuple(tagged)
    shaped = [g for g in after.features if _same_geometry(f, g, eps)]
    if len(shaped) == 1:
        return shaped[0], "geometry", ()
    if len(shaped) > 1:
        return None, "geometry", tuple(shaped)
    return None, "none", ()


def _ref_of(feature) -> FeatureRef:
    return FeatureRef(
        document_id=None,
        revision=None,
        space=feature.space,
        block_path=feature.block_path,
        handles=(feature.handle,),
        feature=None,
    )


def rebind(
    rs: RequirementSet,
    before: MeasuredRun | None,
    after_snap: Snapshot,
    after_observed: ObservedGeometry,
    *,
    binding_policy: str = "strict",
) -> tuple[tuple[Binding, ...], list[dict], bool | None]:
    """``(bindings on the saved file, one re-binding row per requirement, handles_stable)``."""
    fresh = {b.requirement_id: b for b in bind(rs, after_observed, policy=binding_policy)}
    bindings: list[Binding] = []
    rows: list[dict] = []
    if before is None:
        for requirement in rs.requirements:
            binding = fresh.get(requirement.id)
            if binding is not None:
                bindings.append(binding)
            rows.append(
                {
                    "id": requirement.id,
                    "method": "fresh",
                    "status": binding.status if binding is not None else "unknown",
                    "reason": binding.reason if binding is not None else "no binding produced",
                }
            )
        return tuple(bindings), rows, None
    handles_stable = bool(diff_snapshots(before.snapshot, after_snap)["handles_stable"])
    by_key = {(g.handle, g.block_path): g for g in after_observed.features}
    before_by_key = {(f.handle, f.block_path): f for f in before.observed.features}
    eps = default_epsilon(after_observed)
    prior_bindings = {b.requirement_id: b for b in before.bindings}
    for requirement in rs.requirements:
        prior = prior_bindings.get(requirement.id)
        if prior is None or prior.status != "bound":
            binding = fresh.get(requirement.id)
            if binding is not None:
                bindings.append(binding)
            rows.append(
                {
                    "id": requirement.id,
                    "method": "fresh",
                    "status": binding.status if binding is not None else "unknown",
                    "reason": "the source binding was not bound; bound afresh on the file",
                }
            )
            continue
        refs: list[FeatureRef] = []
        methods: set[str] = set()
        problem = None
        candidates: list = []
        for ref in prior.refs:
            if not ref.handles:
                refs.append(ref)
                continue
            matched = []
            for handle in ref.handles:
                feature = before_by_key.get((handle, ref.block_path))
                if feature is None:
                    problem = f"feature {handle} of the source is not in its observed geometry"
                    break
                found, how, cands = _rebind_feature(
                    feature, after_observed, handles_stable=handles_stable, by_key=by_key, eps=eps
                )
                methods.add(how)
                if found is None:
                    candidates.extend(cands)
                    problem = (
                        f"{len(cands)} features of the saved file match {handle} by {how}"
                        if cands
                        else f"no feature of the saved file matches {handle}"
                    )
                    break
                matched.append(found)
            if problem is not None:
                break
            refs.append(
                FeatureRef(
                    document_id=None,
                    revision=None,
                    space=matched[0].space,
                    block_path=matched[0].block_path,
                    handles=tuple(g.handle for g in matched),
                    feature=ref.feature,
                )
            )
        if problem is not None:
            binding = Binding(
                requirement_id=requirement.id,
                status="unknown",
                refs=(),
                candidates=tuple(_ref_of(g) for g in candidates),
                reason=f"re-binding on the saved file failed: {problem}",
            )
        else:
            binding = Binding(
                requirement_id=requirement.id,
                status="bound",
                refs=tuple(refs),
                candidates=(),
                reason=f"re-bound on the saved file by {', '.join(sorted(methods))}",
            )
        bindings.append(binding)
        rows.append(
            {
                "id": requirement.id,
                "method": "+".join(sorted(methods)) or "none",
                "status": binding.status,
                "reason": binding.reason,
            }
        )
    return tuple(bindings), rows, handles_stable


def _compare(before: dict, after: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Per requirement: the status and value on the document and on the saved file."""
    after_rows = {row["id"]: row for row in after["rows"]}
    out = []
    for row in before["rows"]:
        other = after_rows.get(row["id"])
        if other is None:
            continue
        old = (row["measured"] or {}).get("value")
        new = (other["measured"] or {}).get("value")
        moved = (old is None) != (new is None) or (
            old is not None and new is not None and abs(new - old) > 1e-9 * max(1.0, abs(old))
        )
        out.append(
            {
                "id": row["id"],
                "before": {"status": row["status"], "value": old},
                "after": {"status": other["status"], "value": new},
                "changed": row["status"] != other["status"] or moved,
            }
        )
    return out


async def verify_artifact(
    path: str, rs: RequirementSet, *, backend=None, binding_policy: str = "strict"
) -> dict:
    """The acceptance report of ``rs`` measured on the saved file at ``path``.

    Refuses a missing file (FileNotFoundError), a suffix other than .dxf / .dwg
    (ValueError), a DWG headlessly (``dwg`` capability) and a DWG the live
    engine cannot copy (``ArtifactReadError``). An unreadable DXF is refused by
    ``read_snapshot`` with the reason.
    """
    if binding_policy not in BINDING_POLICIES:
        raise ValueError(
            f"binding_policy must be one of {', '.join(BINDING_POLICIES)}; got {binding_policy!r}"
        )
    target = Path(path)
    if not target.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{path}: no such file")
    sha = await asyncio.to_thread(sha256_file, target)
    after_snap, read_method = await _fresh_snapshot(target, backend)
    document = {
        "document_id": None,
        "revision": None,
        "name": target.name,
        "path": str(target),
        "backend": "file",
    }
    before = None
    if backend is not None:
        before = await measure_requirements(backend, rs, binding_policy=binding_policy)

    def _work():
        observed = observe(after_snap, explode_inserts=True, document=document)
        bindings, rebinding, stable = rebind(
            rs, before, after_snap, observed, binding_policy=binding_policy
        )
        by_id = {binding.requirement_id: binding for binding in bindings}
        measurements = tuple(
            evaluate(
                requirement,
                by_id[requirement.id],
                observed,
                epsilon=requirement.verification_epsilon,
            )
            for requirement in rs.requirements
            if requirement.id in by_id
        )
        dimensions = (
            dimension_evidence(rs, after_snap, observed) if before is None or stable else {}
        )
        return observed, bindings, measurements, rebinding, stable, dimensions

    observed, bindings, measurements, rebinding, stable, dimensions = await asyncio.to_thread(
        _work
    )
    record = {
        "path": str(target),
        "sha256": sha,
        "size_bytes": target.stat().st_size,
        "read": read_method,
        "dxf_version": dxf_version_of(target) if target.suffix.lower() == ".dxf" else None,
    }
    report = build_report(
        rs, bindings, measurements, observed=observed, document=document, artifact=record
    )
    report = attach_dimension_evidence(report, dimensions)
    report["rebinding"] = {"handles_stable": stable, "rows": rebinding}
    if before is not None:
        before_report = build_report(
            rs,
            before.bindings,
            before.measurements,
            observed=before.observed,
            document=before.document,
        )
        report["comparison"] = _compare(before_report, report)
        report["source_document"] = dict(before.document)
        report["evidence"] = {
            **report["evidence"],
            "document_id": before.document.get("document_id"),
            "revision": before.document.get("revision"),
        }
    else:
        report["comparison"] = None
        report["source_document"] = None
    return mark_stale(report, None)
```

```python
"""Task 17 live check: Export never re-points, verify_artifact reads the DXF fresh,
and the DWG route reads a private copy. Our own document only, closed by its path."""

import asyncio
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))

from backends.com_backend import ComBackend  # noqa: E402
from engineering.verification.artifact import verify_artifact  # noqa: E402
from engineering.verification.types import (  # noqa: E402
    FeatureSelector,
    Requirement,
    RequirementSet,
)


async def main() -> None:
    out = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    backend = ComBackend()
    await backend.connect()
    result: dict = {}
    ours = None
    try:
        created = await backend.drawing_new()
        ours = created["name"]
        circle = await backend.entity_create_circle(50.0, 50.0, 20.0)
        before = await backend.drawing_info()
        result["export"] = await backend.drawing_export_dxf(str(out / "drawing.dxf"))
        after = await backend.drawing_info()
        result["unchanged"] = (before.name, before.full_path, before.saved) == (
            after.name,
            after.full_path,
            after.saved,
        )
        rs = RequirementSet(
            requirements=(
                Requirement(
                    id="R1",
                    quantity="diameter",
                    target=(("nominal", 40.0), ("unit", "mm")),
                    features=(FeatureSelector(kind="handle", value=(("handle", circle.handle),)),),
                    provenance="explicit",
                    source=(("document", "live-smoke"),),
                ),
            ),
            units="mm",
        )
        dxf_report = await verify_artifact(str(out / "drawing.dxf"), rs, backend=backend)
        result["dxf"] = {
            "status": dxf_report["status"],
            "measured": dxf_report["rows"][0]["measured"]["value"],
            "rebinding": dxf_report["rebinding"],
        }
        await backend.drawing_save_as(str(out / "part.dwg"), "dwg")
        ours = str(out / "part.dwg")
        try:
            dwg_report = await verify_artifact(str(out / "part.dwg"), rs, backend=backend)
            result["dwg"] = {
                "status": dwg_report["status"],
                "read": dwg_report["artifact"]["read"],
            }
        except Exception as exc:  # the measurement is the outcome, either way
            result["dwg"] = {"refused": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
    finally:
        try:
            if ours is not None:
                await backend.document_close(name_or_path=ours, discard=True)
        finally:
            await backend.disconnect()
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=1, default=str))


asyncio.run(main())
```

```bash
git add engineering/verification/artifact.py engineering/verification/report.py engineering/delivery.py engineering/validator.py engineering/understand/snapshot.py engineering/understand/diff.py backends/ezdxf_backend.py backends/com_backend.py backends/contracts/drawing.py server.py README.md benchmarks/results/published/ab-v1.6.0-vs-v1.7.0.json tests/test_verification_artifact.py tests/test_delivery_parity.py tests/test_export_no_repoint.py
git -c user.name="Umut CAN" -c user.email="umutcan.edizaslan@gmail.com" commit -F - <<'EOF'
feat(verify): requirements measured again on the saved file; delivery parity over every space; exports never re-point (B21, B23)

engineering/verification/artifact.py::verify_artifact reads the saved file fresh (a DXF through ezdxf, a DWG on the live engine through a private copy AutoCAD opens read-only) and measures every requirement again. Each feature is re-bound from the source binding: by handle only while handles are stable and the type, space and ACADMCP tags agree, then by feature tag, then by geometry; two candidates are unknown, never a pick. A save that gave the bore's handle to a pin re-binds the bore by geometry (Ø40, pass) and a copied feature tag is unknown with both candidates (spec §8.2 item 9). The report carries the re-binding, the before / after comparison and the source document as its evidence; verify_requirements(artifact_path=) and drawing_verify_requirements route through it, and drawing_finalize(requirements=) measures its saved file again.

drawing_export_dxf never re-points the document on either engine: headless, ezdxf writes a copy (doc.filename, the path and the saved flag untouched; no revision bump); live, Document.Export into a private folder beside the target, renamed onto it (measured on AutoCAD 2026 25.1s, 2026-10-24: <base>.DXF, AC1032, Name / FullName / Saved unchanged). drawing_save_as keeps re-pointing. Delivery parity is now diff_snapshots + block_signatures over every space against a fresh serialisation of the document (an in-memory source differs from every honest copy: an MTEXT without char_height reads 0.0 in memory and 2.5 after a write), so a hole moved inside the outline, a changed paper-space line or a redrawn block fails it. The validator's saved and path steps check the delivered DXF in the delivery context. The manifest (schema 1.1) gains the document id and revision, the source document's path and saved flag, the DXF export method and version, the build, the requirements hash and the report hash; verify_manifest checks both hashes against the acceptance report. _git_sha reads the server package's own checkout and gives None in a wheel.
```
